![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/3bd7f56b774085836bad4ca4a98d26ad6aa7de878090e7264bfbe709bec7fad7.jpg)



图9.43


![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/2b51cf65b1a637cd6919f954cd9b333d3248e37fa214d1d4da54406a75f1ce51.jpg)


![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/af8c98ebb2cf282eca1f484b587056cd31e29158e6c390b7eed473b8247fcac2.jpg)



图9.44


对悬臂用达朗贝尔原理, 解除 O 点处的约束, $X_{0}$ 、 $Y_{0}$ 和 $M_{0}$ 均视为主动力, x、y、 $\alpha$ 为广义坐标.

$$
\delta W = (X _ {o} + f) \delta x + (Y _ {o} - N) \delta y + (N x - M _ {o}) \delta \alpha = 0
$$

$\delta x, \delta y, \delta \alpha$ 均独立，得

$$
X _ {0} = - f, \quad Y _ {0} = N, \quad M _ {0} = N _ {x}
$$

对圆柱用达朗贝尔原理, 因需求 f 和 N, 暂不考虑纯滚动这个约束, 也不考虑 y 方向对圆柱质心的约束,

$$
\delta W = (P \sin \alpha - \frac {P}{g} \ddot {x} - f) \delta x + (N - P \cos \alpha) \delta y + \left(f r - \frac {1}{2} \frac {P}{g} r ^ {2} \ddot {\varphi}\right) \delta \varphi = 0
$$

其中用了圆柱对其对称轴的转动惯量 $I = \frac{1}{2}\frac{P}{g} r^2, r$ 是圆柱的半径.

$\delta x, \delta y, \delta \varphi$ 均独立，得

$$
\frac {P}{g} \ddot {x} = P \sin \alpha - f\tag{1}
$$

$$
N = P \cos \alpha
$$

$$
\frac {1}{2} \frac {P}{g} r ^ {2} \ddot {\varphi} = f r\tag{2}
$$

再用纯滚动得到的约束关系，

$$
\dot {x} - r \dot {\varphi} = 0, \quad \ddot {x} = r \ddot {\varphi}
$$

与式(1)、(2)联立可解出

所以

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & f = \frac {1}{3} P \sin \alpha \\ & X _ {O} = - \frac {1}{3} P \sin \alpha , Y _ {O} = P \cos \alpha \\ & M _ {O} = P x \cos \alpha \end{array}
$$

9.2.6 一个质量为 $m$ 的质点通过一根无重量的不可伸长的绳子系在半径为 $R$ 、竖直放置的固定圆柱边缘，绳子紧缠在圆柱上，质点与圆柱相接触。给质点一个沿径向方向的水平冲量，使质点获得一个初速度 $v_0$ ，质点被约束在与圆柱垂直的光滑水平面内运动。用达朗贝尔原理求以后未缠在圆柱体上的绳子长度 $l$ 与时间 $t$ 的关系。

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/414d88106a61e7223848d375f1b194450460dde5eaf2d67dbf85489d64097f9c.jpg)


解 方法一: 考虑以未缠在圆柱上的绳子为参考系, 其原点在绳子的未缠部分与尚缠在圆柱上部分的分界点, 质点与此原点的距离 l 是随时间变化的, 有速度 i 和加速度 i. 这个参考系相对于静止的惯性参考系既有平动, 又有转动, 取坐标系的极轴总平行于 t = 0 时圆柱的切线方向, 在图 9.45 中画出了在 $t (\neq 0)$ 时刻坐标系的极轴. 这个坐标系的运动是参考系的平动部分, 图中还画出了质点的对静参考系的绝对加速度的各个组成部分, i 是相对加速度 (对(动) 参考系的加速度) $R\ddot{\psi}$ 、 $R\dot{\psi}^{2}$ 是与参考系的平动关联的牵连加

图9.45

速度， $l\ddot{\theta}$ 、 $l\dot{\theta}^{2}$ 是与参考系的转动关联的牵连加速度，与质点所在位置有关，故画在质点处，而 $R\ddot{\psi}$ 、 $R\dot{\psi}^{2}$ 与质点所在位置无关，故画在坐标系的原点 $O'$ 处。由于参考系有转动，转动角速度为 $\dot{\theta}$ ，方向垂直纸面向下，质点对参考系有速度 $\dot{l}$ ，故有科里奥利加速度 $2\dot{l}\dot{\theta}$ 。

取极坐标系的 $l, \theta$ 为广义坐标，质点所受的主动力有绳子张力 $T$ 和图中所画的与各加速度分量相应的达朗贝尔惯性力，分别为质量与加速度的乘积的负值，各力的正向规定与图中所画的各加速度方向相反。

$$
\delta W = (m R \ddot {\psi} + m l \dot {\theta} ^ {2} - m \ddot {l} - T) \delta l + (m R \dot {\psi} ^ {2} - m l \ddot {\theta} - 2 m \dot {l} \dot {\theta}) l \delta \theta = 0
$$

因为 $\delta l, \delta \theta$ 相互独立，可得

$$
m (R \dot {\psi} + l \dot {\theta} ^ {2} - \ddot {l}) - T = 0\tag{1}
$$

$$
R \dot {\psi} ^ {2} - l \dot {\theta} - 2 \dot {l} \dot {\theta} = 0\tag{2}
$$

$\theta$ 角的两条边与 $\psi$ 角的两条边分别垂直，

$$
\theta = \psi\tag{3}
$$

又有约束关系

$$
l = R \psi\tag{4}
$$

从式(3)、(4)得

$$
R \dot {\psi} = \ddot {l}, \quad l \dot {\theta} ^ {2} = \frac {1}{R ^ {2}} l \dot {l} ^ {2}
$$

$$
R \dot {\psi} ^ {2} = \frac {1}{R} \dot {l} ^ {2}, \quad l \ddot {\theta} = \frac {1}{R} l \ddot {l}, \quad 2 \dot {l} \dot {\theta} = \frac {2}{R} \dot {l} ^ {2}
$$

代入式(1)、(2)得

$$
T = \frac {1}{R ^ {2}} l i ^ {2}\tag{5}
$$

$$
l \ddot {l} + \dot {l} ^ {2} = 0\tag{6}
$$

用 $\dot{l}=\frac{d\dot{l}}{dl}\dot{l}$ ，可将式(6)积分，得

$$
i \dot {l} = C
$$

因为 t=0 时, l=0, $\dot{l}=\infty$ , 不能由此定出 C.

再用式(3)、(4)，

所以

$$
\begin{array}{r l} C & = l \dot {l} = l R \dot {\psi} = l R \dot {\theta} = R (l \dot {\theta}) _ {t = 0} = R v _ {0} \\ & \quad l \dot {l} = R v _ {0} \\ & \quad \int_ {0} ^ {l} l \mathrm{d} l = \int_ {0} ^ {t} R v _ {0} \mathrm{d} t \\ & \frac {1}{2} l ^ {2} = R v _ {0} t, \qquad l = \sqrt {2 R v _ {0} t} \end{array}
$$

方法二:用静参考系,取静坐标系 Oxy,O 是 t=0 时质点所在位置,x 轴沿切线方向,即图 9.45 中 t=0 时的极轴,y 轴沿圆柱的半径方向,向外为正.t 时刻,质点的 x、y 坐标为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {x = x _ {\sigma} + l \cos \theta = - R \sin \psi + l \cos \theta} \\ & {y = y _ {\sigma} + l \sin \theta = - R + R \cos \psi + l \sin \theta} \\ & {\dot {x} = - R \cos \psi \dot {\psi} + \dot {l} \cos \theta - l \sin \theta \dot {\theta}} \\ & {\ddot {x} = - R \cos \psi \ddot {\psi} + R \cos \psi \dot {\psi} ^ {2} + \ddot {l} \cos \theta - \dot {l} \dot {\theta} l \sin \theta} \\ & {- \dot {l} \dot {\theta} \sin \theta - l \cos \theta \dot {\theta} ^ {2} - l \sin \theta \ddot {\theta}} \\ & {\dot {y} = - R \sin \psi \dot {\psi} + \dot {l} \sin \theta + l \cos \theta \dot {\theta}} \\ & {\ddot {y} = - R \cos \psi \psi^ {2} - R \sin \psi \psi^ {\prime \prime} + \ddot {l} \sin \theta + \dot {l} \cos \theta \dot {\theta}} \\ & {+ \dot {l} \dot {\theta} \cos \theta + \dot {l} \dot {\theta} \cos \theta - l \dot {\theta} ^ {2} \sin \theta} \end{array}\tag{7}
$$

(8) 

$$
\begin{array}{l} \delta W = (- T \cos \theta - m \ddot {x}) \delta x + (- T \sin \theta - m \ddot {y}) \delta y = 0 \\ m \ddot {x} = - T \cos \theta \\ m \ddot {y} = - T \sin \theta \end{array}\tag{9}
$$

(10) 

式(9) $\times\sin\theta-$ 式(10) $\times\cos\theta$ ,得

$$
\ddot {x} \sin \theta - \dot {y} \cos \theta = 0\tag{11}
$$

将式(7)、(8)代入式(11)，并用 $\psi=\theta$ ，化简后得

$$
R \dot {\psi} ^ {2} - l \ddot {\theta} - 2 \dot {l} \dot {\theta} = 0
$$

正是方法一中的式(2)，以下与方法一的做法相同.

## 9.3 拉格朗日方程

9.3.1 一个质量为 $m$ 的质点在平面上运动, 不采用极坐标 $r, \varphi$ , 而用 $r$ 和 $\sin \varphi$ 为广义坐标, 写出此质点的动能.

解 $x = r\cos \varphi, y = r\sin \varphi$ 今用 $r$ 和 $q = \sin \varphi$ 为广义坐标，

$$
\begin{array}{l} {x = r \cos \varphi = r \sqrt {1 - q ^ {2}}} \\ {y = r \sin \varphi = r q} \\ {\dot {x} = \dot {r} \sqrt {1 - q ^ {2}} - \frac {r q \dot {q}}{\sqrt {1 - q ^ {2}}}} \\ {\dot {y} = \dot {r} q + r \dot {q}} \\ {T = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2}) = \frac {1}{2} m \left[ \left(\dot {r} \sqrt {1 - q ^ {2}} - \frac {r q \dot {q}}{\sqrt {1 - q ^ {2}}}\right) ^ {2} + (\dot {r} q + r \dot {q}) ^ {2} \right]} \\ {\quad = \frac {1}{2} m \left(\dot {r} ^ {2} + \frac {r ^ {2} \dot {q} ^ {2}}{1 - q ^ {2}}\right)} \end{array}
$$

9.3.2 在一根拉紧的弹性绳上等距离(距离为 a)地连着小球,两端固定,平衡时,弹性绳是水平的(重力可忽略不计),小球仅限于垂直方向运动,位移很小,即图 9.46 中 $y_{1}$ 、 $y_{2}$ 、 $\cdots$ 、 $y_{5}$ 均为小量,平衡时绳子张力为 $\tau$ ,写出系统的势能.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/f26a4093862cb834be0fca4c9f59efd5482020278a7d30f634c93d2310456aa5.jpg)



图9.46


解 考虑球 1、球 2 沿 y 方向的受力 $f_{1}$ 、 $f_{2}$ ，图 9.47(a)、(b) 分别画出了球 1 和球 2 受两边绳子张力的情况，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} f _ {1} & = f _ {1 0} + f _ {1 2} = - T _ {1 0} \sin \alpha - T _ {1 2} \sin \beta \\ & = - T _ {1 0} \cos \alpha \tan \alpha - T _ {1 2} \cos \beta \cdot \tan \beta \end{array}
$$


(b)


![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/28a798f99c397adce2e783894062a433b230f6dff3f643a4fec6aaa81273f94d.jpg)



(a)


![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/8391fc9dca96a5730353a57e5952831879bc5ad46cae856cf7d0934a14e7620e.jpg)



图9.47


$\alpha, \beta$ 均为小量. 又因水平方向合力为零，

所以

$$
\begin{array}{c} T _ {1 0} \cos \alpha = T _ {1 2} \cos \beta = \tau \\ f _ {1} = - \tau \tan \alpha - \tau \tan \beta = - \tau \frac {y _ {1}}{a} - \tau \frac {y _ {1} - y _ {2}}{a} \end{array}
$$

同样可写出球2受到左边绳子在 $y$ 方向的力为

$$
f _ {2 1} = \tau   {\frac {y _ {1} - y _ {2}}{a}} \quad {\text {或}} \quad - \tau   {\frac {y _ {2} - y _ {1}}{a}}
$$

对于 $f_{10}$ 可引入势能

$$
V _ {1 0} = \frac {\tau}{2 a} y _ {1} ^ {2}
$$

$$
f _ {1 0} = - \frac {\partial V _ {1 0}}{\partial y _ {1}} = - \frac {\tau}{a} y _ {1}
$$

对于 $f_{12}$ 和 $f_{21}$ 可引入势能

$$
\begin{array}{l} V _ {1 2} = \frac {\tau}{2 a} (y _ {1} - y _ {2}) ^ {2} \\ f _ {1 2} = - \frac {\partial V _ {1 2}}{\partial y _ {1}} = - \frac {\tau}{a} (y _ {1} - y _ {2}) \\ f _ {2 1} = - \frac {\partial V _ {1 2}}{\partial y _ {2}} = \frac {\tau}{a} (y _ {1} - y _ {2}) \end{array}
$$

照此办法可得系统的势能为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {V = V _ {1 0} + V _ {1 2} + V _ {2 3} + V _ {3 4} + V _ {4 5} + V _ {5 6}} \\ & {\quad = \frac {\tau}{2 a} y _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {\tau}{2 a} (y _ {1} - y _ {2}) ^ {2}} \\ & {\quad \quad + \frac {\tau}{2 a} (y _ {2} - y _ {3}) ^ {2} + \frac {\tau}{2 a} (y _ {3} - y _ {4}) ^ {2} + \frac {\tau}{2 a} (y _ {4} - y _ {5}) ^ {2} + \frac {\tau}{2 a} y _ {5} ^ {2}} \\ & {\quad = \frac {\tau}{a} (y _ {1} ^ {2} + y _ {2} ^ {2} + \dots + y _ {5} ^ {2} - y _ {1} y _ {2} - y _ {2} y _ {3} - y _ {3} y _ {4} - y _ {4} y _ {5}).} \end{array}
$$

9.3.3 劲度系数分别为 $k_{i}(i = 1,2,3,4)$ 的四根弹簧，原长分别为 $l_{i0}$ ，一端连在 $P$ 点，另一端分别连在固定点 $A,B,C,D$ . 取 $x,y$ 坐标，其原点是四个弹簧系统的平衡位置， $A,B,C,D$ 和 $P$ 点的坐标分别为 $(x_{1},y_{1}),(x_{2},y_{2}),(x_{3},y_{3}),(x_{4},y_{4})$ 和 $(x,y)$ ，平衡时，即 $P$ 位于原点时，各弹簧的长度分别为 $l_{i},\alpha_{i},\beta_{i}$ 分别是 $OA,OB,OC,OD$ 的方向余弦，即

$$
\alpha_ {i} = \frac {x _ {i}}{l _ {i}}, \quad \beta_ {i} = \frac {y _ {i}}{l _ {i}}
$$

$P$ 点偏离原点很小, 即 $x, y$ 均为小量. 证明弹簧系统的势能的近似表达式为

$$
V = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {4} \left[ \frac {1}{2} k _ {i} (x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) - \frac {k _ {i} l _ {i 0}}{2 l _ {i}} (\beta_ {i} x - \alpha_ {i} y) ^ {2} \right]
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/d37c5f327c7052b7d6a919ca7386a91f894d00bc034174a8c1bf11d0799491a3.jpg)



图9.48


证明 $V = \sum_{i=1}^{4} V_i = \frac{1}{2} \sum_{i=1}^{4} k_i \left[ \sqrt{(l_i \alpha_i - x)^2 + (l_i \beta_i - y)^2} - l_{i0} \right]^2$ 

对

$$
V _ {i} = \frac {1}{2} k _ {i} \left[ \sqrt {\left(l _ {i} \alpha_ {i} - x\right) ^ {2} + \left(l _ {i} \beta_ {i} - y\right) ^ {2}} - l _ {i 0} \right] ^ {2}
$$

在 $(0,0)$ 处作泰勒展开，保留到二级小量，且选择 $V_{i}(0,0)=0$ ，得

同样可得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {V _ {i} (x, y) \approx \frac {\partial V _ {i}}{\partial x} \Big | _ {(0, 0)} x + \frac {\partial V _ {i}}{\partial y} \Big | _ {(0, 0)} y + \frac {1}{2} \Big [ \frac {\partial^ {2} V}{\partial x ^ {2}} \Big | _ {(0, 0)} x ^ {2}} \\ & {\qquad + 2 \frac {\partial^ {2} V}{\partial x \partial y} \Big | _ {(0, 0)} x y + \frac {\partial^ {2} V}{\partial y ^ {2}} \Big | _ {(0, 0)} y _ {2} \Big ]} \\ & {\frac {\partial V _ {i}}{\partial x} = k _ {i} \Big [ \sqrt {(l _ {i} \alpha_ {i} - x) ^ {2} + (l _ {i} \beta_ {i} - y) ^ {2}} - l _ {i 0} \Big ] \frac {(- 1) (l _ {i} \alpha_ {i} - x)}{\sqrt {(l _ {i} x _ {i} - x) ^ {2} + (l _ {i} \beta_ {i} - y) ^ {2}}}} \\ & {\qquad = k _ {i} \Big [ - (l _ {i} \alpha_ {i} - x) + \frac {l _ {i 0} (l _ {i} \alpha_ {i} - x)}{\sqrt {(l _ {i} \alpha_ {i} - x) ^ {2} + (l _ {i} \beta_ {i} - y) ^ {2}}} \Big ]} \\ & {\qquad \frac {\partial V _ {i}}{\partial x} \Big | _ {(0, 0)} = - k _ {i} (l _ {i} - l _ {i 0}) \alpha_ {i}} \\ & {\text {可得} \quad \frac {\partial V _ {i}}{\partial y} \Big | _ {(0, 0)} = - k _ {i} (l _ {i} - l _ {i 0}) \beta_ {i}} \\ & {\qquad \frac {\partial^ {2} V _ {i}}{\partial x \partial y} = k _ {i} l _ {i 0} (l _ {i} \alpha_ {i} - x) \cdot \frac {(- 2) (l _ {i} \beta_ {i} - y) (- 1)}{2 [ (l _ {i} \alpha_ {i} - x) ^ {2} + (l _ {i} \beta_ {i} - y) ^ {2} ] ^ {3 / 2}}} \\ & {\qquad = \frac {k _ {i} l _ {i 0} (l _ {i} \alpha - x) (l _ {i} \beta_ {i} - y)}{[ (l _ {i} \alpha_ {i} - x) ^ {2} + (l _ {i} \beta_ {i} - y) ^ {2} ] ^ {3 / 2}}} \\ & {\qquad \frac {\partial^ {2} V}{\partial x \partial y} \Big | _ {(0, 0)} = \frac {k _ {i} l _ {i 0}}{l _ {i}} \alpha_ {i} \beta_ {i}} \\ & {\frac {\partial^ {2} V}{\partial x ^ {2}} = k _ {i} \left\{1 - \frac {l _ {i 0}}{[ (l _ {i} \alpha_ {i} - x) ^ {2} + (l _ {i} \beta_ {i} - y) ^ {2} ] ^ {1 / 2}} + \frac {l _ {i 0} (l _ {i} \alpha_ {i} - x) ^ {2}}{[ (l _ {i} \alpha_ {i} - x) ^ {2} + (l _ {i} \beta_ {i} - y) ^ {2} ] ^ {3 / 2}} \right\}.} \end{array}
$$

$$
\left. \frac {\partial^ {2} V}{\partial x ^ {2}} \right| _ {(0, 0)} = k _ {i} \left(1 - \frac {l _ {i 0}}{l _ {i}} + \frac {l _ {i 0}}{l _ {i}} \alpha_ {i} ^ {2}\right) = k _ {i} \left(1 - \frac {l _ {i 0}}{l _ {i}} \beta_ {i} ^ {2}\right)
$$

同样可得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\left. \frac {\partial^ {2} V _ {i}}{\partial y ^ {2}} \right| _ {(0, 0)} = k _ {i} \Big (1 - \frac {l _ {i 0}}{l _ {i}} \alpha_ {i} ^ {2} \Big)} \\ & {\qquad V _ {i} = - k _ {i} (l _ {i} - l _ {i 0}) (\alpha_ {i} x + \beta_ {i} y) + \frac {1}{2} \Big [ k _ {i} \Big (1 - \frac {l _ {i 0}}{l _ {i}} \beta_ {i} ^ {2} \Big) x ^ {2}} \\ & {\qquad + 2 k _ {i} \frac {l _ {i 0}}{l _ {i}} \alpha_ {i} \beta_ {i} x y + k _ {i} \Big (1 - \frac {l _ {i 0}}{l _ {i}} \alpha_ {i} ^ {2} \Big) y ^ {2} \Big ]} \\ & {\qquad = - k _ {i} (l _ {i} - l _ {i 0}) (\alpha_ {i} x + \beta_ {i} y) + \frac {1}{2} k _ {i} (x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) - \frac {k _ {i} l _ {i 0}}{2 l _ {i}} (\beta_ {i} x - \alpha_ {i} y) ^ {2}} \\ & {\qquad V = - \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {4} k _ {i} (l _ {i} - l _ {i 0}) (\alpha_ {i} x + \beta_ {i} y) + \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {4} \Big [ \frac {1}{2} k _ {i} (x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) - \frac {k _ {i} l _ {i 0}}{2 l _ {i}} (\beta_ {i} x - \alpha_ {i} y) ^ {2} \Big ]} \\ & {\qquad = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {4} \Big [ \frac {1}{2} k _ {i} (x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) - \frac {k _ {i} l _ {i 0}}{l _ {i}} (\beta_ {i} x - \alpha_ {i} y) ^ {2} \Big ]} \end{array}
$$

这里用了在 $(0,0)$ 处是平衡位置.

$$
\sum_ {i = 1} ^ {4} k _ {i} (l _ {i} - l _ {i 0}) \alpha_ {i} = 0, \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {4} k _ {i} (l _ {i} - l _ {i 0}) \beta_ {i} = 0
$$

9.3.4 双摆中质量为 $m_{1}$ 的质点与劲度系数分别为 $k_{1}$ 和 $k_{2}$ 、原长分别为 $l_{10}$ 和 $l_{20}$ 的两弹簧相连，弹簧的另一端分别固定于 $A$ 和 $B, m_{1}, m_{2}$ 两质点和两弹簧处在同一竖直平面内运动，当 $m_{1}$ 处于 $O$ 点时，且 $\varphi = 0$ ，双摆处于平衡位置，弹簧处于拉紧情况。求两弹簧的势能及其在 $\theta$ 很小时的近似表达式。

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/f7106a8b718e7a23e9c0c3fdf797ec1f660bda9560b7af77d3ab17461e4c3e16.jpg)



图9.49


解 两弹簧的势能为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} V & = \frac {1}{2} k _ {1} \left[ \sqrt {(x _ {1} - x) ^ {2} + (y _ {1} - y) ^ {2}} - l _ {1 0} \right] ^ {2} \\ & \quad + \frac {1}{2} k _ {2} \left[ \sqrt {(x _ {2} - x) ^ {2} + (y _ {2} - y) ^ {2}} - l _ {2 0} \right] ^ {2} \\ & x = r _ {1} \sin \theta , \quad y = r _ {1} (1 - \cos \theta) \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} V = & \frac {1}{2} k _ {1} \left[ \sqrt {(x _ {1} - r _ {1} \sin \theta) ^ {2} + (y _ {1} - r _ {1} + r _ {1} \cos \theta) ^ {2}} - l _ {1 0} \right] ^ {2} \\ & + \frac {1}{2} k _ {2} \left[ \sqrt {(x _ {2} - r _ {1} \sin \theta) ^ {2} + (y _ {2} - r _ {1} + r _ {1} \cos \theta) ^ {2}} - l _ {2 0} \right] ^ {2} \end{array}
$$

当 $\theta$ 很小时, 可用上题结果, 但连在 $(0,0)$ 点即 $\theta = 0$ 处, 有 $\frac{\partial V}{\partial x} = 0$ , 而 $\frac{\partial V}{\partial y} \neq 0$ (因为 $V$ 中没包括重力势能), 所以

这里

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {V = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} \left[ - k _ {i} (l _ {i} - l _ {i 0}) \beta_ {i} y + \frac {1}{2} k _ {i} (x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) - \frac {k _ {i} l _ {i 0}}{2 l _ {i}} (\beta_ {i} x - \alpha_ {i} y) ^ {2} \right]} \\ & {\alpha_ {i} = \frac {x _ {i}}{l _ {i}}, \qquad \beta_ {i} = \frac {y _ {i}}{l _ {i}}} \\ & {\quad x = r _ {1} \sin \theta \approx r _ {1} \theta} \\ & {\quad y = r _ {1} (1 - \cos \theta) \approx \frac {1}{2} r _ {1} \theta^ {2}} \\ & {\quad x ^ {2} + y ^ {2} \approx r _ {1} ^ {2} \theta^ {2}} \\ & {\quad (\beta_ {i} x - \alpha_ {i} y) ^ {2} \approx \left(\beta_ {i} r _ {1} \theta - \alpha_ {i} \cdot \frac {1}{2} r _ {1} \theta^ {2}\right) ^ {2} \approx \beta_ {i} r _ {1} ^ {2} \theta^ {2} = \frac {y _ {i} ^ {2}}{l _ {i} ^ {2}} r _ {1} ^ {2} \theta^ {2}} \\ & {\quad V = \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} \left[ - k _ {i} \frac {l _ {i} - l _ {i 0}}{2 l _ {i}} y _ {i} r _ {1} \theta^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k _ {i} r _ {1} ^ {2} \theta^ {2} - \frac {k _ {i} l _ {i 0}}{2 l _ {i} ^ {3}} y _ {i} ^ {2} r _ {1} ^ {2} \theta^ {2} \right]} \\ & {\quad = \left[ \frac {1}{2} (k _ {1} + k _ {2}) r _ {1} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} \sum_ {i = 1} ^ {2} k _ {i} \left(\frac {l _ {i} - l _ {i 0}}{l _ {i}} y _ {i} r _ {1} + \frac {l _ {i 0}}{l _ {i} ^ {3}} y _ {i} ^ {2} r _ {1} ^ {2}\right) \right] \theta^ {2}} \end{array}
$$

所以

9.3.5 9.2.6 题的系统有几个自由度? 是否保守系? 选用适当的广义坐标, 由拉格朗日方程得到运动微分方程.

解 9.2.6 题所述系统只有一个自由度. 用解 9.2.6 题的方法二,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\dot {x} = - R \mathrm{cos} \psi \dot {\psi} + \dot {l} \mathrm{cos} \theta - l \dot {\theta} \mathrm{sin} \theta} \\ & {\dot {y} = - R \mathrm{sin} \psi \dot {\psi} + \dot {l} \mathrm{sin} \theta + l \dot {\theta} \mathrm{cos} \theta} \\ & {\delta x = - R \mathrm{cos} \psi \delta \psi + \mathrm{cos} \theta \delta l - l \mathrm{sin} \theta \delta \theta} \\ & {\delta y = - R \mathrm{sin} \psi \delta \psi + \mathrm{sin} \theta \delta l + l \mathrm{cos} \theta \delta \theta} \end{array}
$$

$\delta x, \delta y$ 不是相互独立的，或 $\delta \psi, \delta l, \delta \theta$ 不是都独立的，有下列两个约束关系：

$$
\begin{array}{l} l = R \psi , \qquad \psi = \theta \\ \delta l = R \delta \psi = R \delta \theta , \qquad \delta \psi = \delta \theta \end{array}
$$

今取 $\theta$ 为广义坐标，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \delta x = - R \cos \theta \delta \theta + R \cos \theta \delta \theta - l \sin \theta \delta \theta = - l \sin \theta \delta \theta \\ \delta y = - R \sin \theta \delta \theta + R \sin \theta \delta \theta + l \cos \theta \delta \theta = l \cos \theta \delta \theta \end{array}
$$

拉格朗日函数为

$$
L = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2}) = \frac {1}{2} m R ^ {2} \theta^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2}
$$

这里又用了 $l = R\psi, \psi = \theta$ 两个约束关系.

系统是否保守系,要看用什么参考系.对静参考系, $\frac{\partial L}{\partial t}=0$ ,或者说有稳定约束,坐标变换关系 $(x, y$ 与 $\theta$ 的变换关系) 不显含时间, 又不存在主动力 (绳子张力是约束力), 系统是保守系. 可对于 9.2.6 题解中所用的对静参考系既有平动又有转动的参考系 (方法一), 若采用同样的广义坐标, 仍可得到相同的运动微分方程, 但拉格朗日函数不一样, 受力情况不一样, 做虚功的惯性力不是保守力, 因比是非保守系.

用静参考系，已得

$$
L = \frac {1}{2} m R ^ {2} \theta^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2}
$$

$$
\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\theta}} = m R ^ {2} \dot {\theta} \dot {\theta} ^ {2}, \quad \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\theta}} = m R ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} \dot {\theta}
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\theta}}\right) = m R ^ {2} \theta^ {2} \ddot {\theta} + 2 m R ^ {2} \theta \dot {\theta} ^ {2}
$$

由

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\theta}}\right) - \frac {\partial L}{\partial \theta} = 0
$$

得

$$
\theta \dot {g} + \dot {\theta} ^ {2} = 0
$$

如用 $l = R\psi = R\theta$ ，改用 $l$ 作广义坐标，上式可改为

$$
l \ddot {l} + l ^ {\cdot 2} = 0
$$

即解 9.2.6 题中的(6)式.

9.3.6 一个珠子套在一条光滑的锥形螺旋线上, 在重力的作用下运动. 用柱坐标, 锥形螺旋线方程为

$$
\rho = a z, \quad \varphi = - b z
$$

a、b 为常量，z 轴竖直向上. 证明：珠子的运动微分方程为

$$
\ddot {z} \left(a ^ {2} + 1 + a ^ {2} b ^ {2} z ^ {2}\right) + a ^ {2} b ^ {2} z \dot {z} = - g
$$

证明 设珠子质量为 $m$ 

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {\rho} ^ {2} + \rho^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2}) = \frac {1}{2} m (a ^ {2} + 1 + a ^ {2} b ^ {2} z ^ {2}) \dot {z} ^ {2}
$$

$$
V = m g z
$$

$$
L = T - V = \frac {1}{2} m (a ^ {2} + 1 + a ^ {2} b ^ {2} z ^ {2}) \dot {z} ^ {2} - m g z
$$

$$
\frac {\partial L}{\partial z} = m a ^ {2} b ^ {2} z \dot {z} ^ {2} - m g
$$

$$
\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {z}} = m (a ^ {2} + 1 + a ^ {2} b ^ {2} z ^ {2}) \dot {z}
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {z}}\right) = m (a ^ {2} + 1 + a ^ {2} b ^ {2} z ^ {2}) \ddot {z} + 2 m a ^ {2} b ^ {2} z \dot {z} ^ {2}
$$

由

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {z}}\right) - \frac {\partial L}{\partial z} = 0
$$

得

$$
(a ^ {2} + 1 + a ^ {2} b ^ {2} z ^ {2}) \ddot {z} + a ^ {2} b ^ {2} z \dot {z} ^ {2} = - g
$$

9.3.7 三个相互啮合的齿轮 $G_{1}, G_{2}, G_{3}$ 通过三个扭转弹簧与三个圆盘 $D_{4}, D_{5}, D_{6}$ 耦合，如图9.50所示。三个齿轮的半径分别为 $r_{1}, r_{2}, r_{3}$ ，围绕对称轴的转动惯量分别为 $I_{1}$ 、

$I_{2}$ 、 $I_{3}$ ，三个圆盘绕其对称轴的转动惯量分别为 $I_{4}$ 、 $I_{5}$ 、 $I_{6}$ ，它们偏离平衡位置的转角分别为 $\theta_{1}$ 、 $\theta_{2}$ 、 $\cdots$ 、 $\theta_{6}$ ，如图9.50所示。三个扭转弹簧扭转单位角度时受到的扭力矩大小分别为 $k_{1}$ 、 $k_{2}$ 、 $k_{3}$ ，求此系统的拉格朗日函数。

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/9be381f78cddb0464bd1a19db0e563ca69763c43870c0b5157282c0730a48a4e.jpg)



图9.50


$$
T = \frac {1}{2} I _ {1} \dot {\theta} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} I _ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} I _ {3} \dot {\theta} _ {3} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} I _ {4} \dot {\theta} _ {4} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} I _ {5} \dot {\theta} _ {5} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} I _ {6} \dot {\theta} _ {6} ^ {2}
$$

三个齿轮相互啮合,接触点无相对运动,

$$
\begin{array}{r l r} & {r _ {1} \dot {\theta} _ {1} = r _ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {2},} & {r _ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {2} = r _ {3} \dot {\theta} _ {3}} \\ & {\dot {\theta} _ {1} = \frac {r _ {2}}{r _ {1}} \dot {\theta} _ {2},} & {\dot {\theta} _ {3} = \frac {r _ {2}}{r _ {3}} \dot {\theta} _ {2}} \end{array}
$$

取平衡位置时，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\theta_ {1} = \theta_ {2} = \theta_ {3} = \theta_ {4} = \theta_ {5} = \theta_ {6} = 0} \\ & {\theta_ {1} = \frac {r _ {2}}{r _ {1}} \theta_ {2}, \quad \theta_ {3} = \frac {r _ {2}}{r _ {3}} \theta_ {2}} \\ & {T = \frac {1}{2} I _ {1} \Big (\frac {r _ {2}}{r _ {1}} \Big) ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} I _ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} I _ {3} \Big (\frac {r _ {2}}{r _ {3}} \Big) ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} I _ {4} \dot {\theta} _ {4} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} I _ {5} \dot {\theta} _ {5} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} I _ {6} \dot {\theta} _ {6} ^ {2}} \\ & {\qquad = \frac {1}{2} r _ {2} ^ {2} \Big (\frac {I _ {1}}{r _ {1} ^ {2}} + \frac {I _ {2}}{r _ {2} ^ {2}} + \frac {I _ {3}}{r _ {3} ^ {2}} \Big) \dot {\theta} _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} I _ {4} \dot {\theta} _ {4} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} I _ {5} \dot {\theta} _ {5} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} I _ {6} \dot {\theta} _ {6} ^ {2}} \\ & {V = \frac {1}{2} k _ {1} (\theta_ {4} - \theta_ {2}) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k _ {2} (\theta_ {1} - \theta_ {5}) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k _ {3} (\theta_ {3} - \theta_ {6}) ^ {2}} \\ & {\qquad = \frac {1}{2} k _ {1} (\theta_ {4} - \theta_ {2}) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k _ {2} \Big (\frac {r _ {2}}{r _ {1}} \theta_ {2} - \theta_ {5}) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k _ {3} \Big (\frac {r _ {2}}{r _ {3}} \theta_ {2} - \theta_ {6} \Big) ^ {2}} \\ & {\qquad = \frac {1}{2} k _ {1} (\theta_ {4} - \theta_ {2}) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k _ {2} r _ {2} ^ {2} \Big (\frac {\theta_ {2}}{r _ {1}} - \frac {\theta_ {5}}{r _ {2}} \Big) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k _ {3} r _ {2} ^ {2} \Big (\frac {\theta_ {2}}{r _ {3}} - \frac {\theta_ {6}}{r _ {2}} \Big) ^ {2}} \\ & {\quad L = T - V = \frac 12 r _ {2} ^ {2} \Big (\frac I{r _ {1} ^ {2}} + \frac I{r _ {2} ^ {2}} + \frac I{r _ {3} ^ {2}} \Big) \dot {\theta} _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac 12 I _ {4} \dot {\theta} _ {4} ^ {2} + \frac 12 I _ {5} \dot {\theta} _ {5} ^ {2} + \frac 12 I _ {6} \dot {\theta} _ {6} ^ {2}} \\ & {-   \frac 12 k _ {1} (\theta_ {4} - \theta_ {2}) ^ {2} -   \frac 12 k _ {2} r _ {2} ^ {2}   \Big (\frac {\theta_ {2}}{r _ {1}} - \frac {\theta_ {5}}{r _ {2}}   \Big) ^ {2} -   \frac 12 k _ {3} r _ {2} ^ {2}   \Big (\frac {\theta_ {2}}{r _ {3}} - \frac {\theta_ {6}}{r _ {2}}   \Big) ^ {2}} \end{array}
$$

9.3.8 如图 9.51, 一个质量为 $m$ 、半径为 $a$ 的均质薄圆盘的质心 $C$ 固连一根不计质

量的、长为 $b$ 的细杆 $AC$ , 细杆垂直于盘面. 此系统放在粗糙的、倾角为 $\alpha$ 的斜面上, 杆的 $A$ 端在斜面上的 $O$ 点保持不动, 圆盘沿半径为 $R = (a^2 + b^2)^{1/2}$ 的圆周在平衡位置附近做小振动(纯滚动). 选 $O$ 点为坐标系原点, $\zeta$ 轴垂直于斜面, $\eta$ 轴沿斜面向上, 固连于圆盘的动坐标系原点也在 $O$ 点, $AC$ 方向为 $z$ 轴. 求:

(1) 系统的动能；

(2) 系统的势能；

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/e751b85d08b4d133b1095286b733a9662019a7637fea732cda647624ff0ddb4c.jpg)


（3）证明欧拉角 $\varphi$ 的运动方程如同一个角频率为 $\Omega = \left(\frac{4gR\sin\alpha}{a^2 + 6b^2}\right)^{1 / 2}$ 的单摆.

图9.51

解 (1) $T = \frac{1}{2} (I_1\omega_x^2 + I_2\omega_y^2 + I_3\omega_z^2)$ 

$$
\begin{array}{l} I _ {1} = I _ {2} = \frac {1}{4} m a ^ {2} + m b ^ {2} \\ I _ {3} = \frac {1}{2} m a ^ {2} \\ \omega_ {z} = \dot {\psi} + \dot {\varphi} \cos \theta = \dot {\psi} + \dot {\varphi} \sin \beta \end{array}
$$

圆盘与斜面的接触点速度为零

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & b \sin \theta \dot {\varphi} + a \omega_ {z} = 0 \\ & b \cos \beta \dot {\varphi} + a (\dot {\psi} + \dot {\varphi} \sin \beta) = 0 \end{array}
$$

将 $\cos \beta = \frac{b}{\sqrt{a^2 + b^2}}$ ， $\sin \beta = \frac{a}{\sqrt{a^2 + b^2}}$ 代入上式，可得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\dot {\psi} = - \frac {\sqrt {a ^ {2} + b ^ {2}}}{a} \dot {\varphi}} \\ & {w _ {z} = - \frac {\sqrt {a ^ {2} + b ^ {2}}}{a} \dot {\varphi} + \dot {\varphi} \frac {a}{\sqrt {a ^ {2} + b ^ {2}}} = - \frac {b ^ {2}}{a \sqrt {a ^ {2} + b ^ {2}}} \dot {\varphi}} \\ & {\sqrt {\omega_ {x} ^ {2} + \omega_ {y} ^ {2}} = \omega_ {z} \tan \beta = \frac {a}{b} \omega_ {z} = - \frac {b}{\sqrt {a ^ {2} + b ^ {2}}} \dot {\varphi}} \\ & {\quad T = \frac {1}{2} \left(\frac {1}{4} m a ^ {2} + m b ^ {2}\right) (\omega_ {x} ^ {2} + \omega_ {y} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{2} \cdot \frac {1}{2} m a ^ {2} \omega_ {z} ^ {2}} \\ & {\quad = \frac {1}{8} (m a ^ {2} + 4 m b ^ {2}) \cdot \frac {b ^ {2}}{a ^ {2} + b ^ {2}} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{4} m a ^ {2} \cdot \frac {b ^ {4}}{a ^ {2} (a ^ {2} + b ^ {2})} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}} \\ & {\quad = \frac {1}{8} m \frac {(a ^ {2} + 6 b ^ {2}) b ^ {2}}{a ^ {2} + b ^ {2}} \dot {\varphi}} \\ & {\quad V = m g \eta_ {c} \sin \alpha + C} \end{array}\tag{2}
$$

取平衡位置处为势能零点,由此定出 C 值.

$$
\eta_ {C} = - b \cos \varphi \cos \beta = - \frac {b ^ {2}}{\sqrt {a ^ {2} + b ^ {2}}} \cos \varphi
$$

$$
V = - m g \frac {b ^ {2}}{\sqrt {a ^ {2} + b ^ {2}}} \sin \alpha \cos \varphi + C
$$

平衡位置处, $\varphi=0$ 

$$
0 = - m g \frac {b ^ {2}}{\sqrt {a ^ {2} + b ^ {2}}} \sin \alpha + C
$$

$$
C = m g \frac {b ^ {2}}{\sqrt {a ^ {2} + b ^ {2}}} \sin \alpha
$$

$$
V = \frac {m g b ^ {2}}{\sqrt {a ^ {2} + b ^ {2}}} \sin \alpha (1 - \cos \varphi)
$$

在平衡位置附近做小振动时， $\varphi$ 很小，

$$
\cos \varphi \approx 1 - \frac {1}{2} \varphi^ {2}
$$

$$
V = \frac {m g b ^ {2} \sin \alpha}{2 \sqrt {a ^ {2} + b ^ {2}}} \varphi^ {2}
$$

(3) 

$$
L = T - V = \frac {m (a ^ {2} + 6 b ^ {2}) b ^ {2}}{8 (a ^ {2} + b ^ {2})} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} - \frac {m g b ^ {2} \sin \alpha}{2 \sqrt {a ^ {2} + b ^ {2}}} \varphi^ {2}
$$

由

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\varphi}}\right) - \frac {\partial L}{\partial \varphi} = 0
$$

$$
\frac {m (a ^ {2} + 6 b ^ {2}) b ^ {2}}{4 (a ^ {2} + b ^ {2})} \ddot {\varphi} + \frac {m g b ^ {2} \sin \alpha}{\sqrt {a ^ {2} + b ^ {2}}} \varphi = 0
$$

欧拉角 $\varphi$ 满足的微分方程和单摆满足的微分方程一样. 角频率 $\Omega$ 为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \Omega & = \left[ \frac {m g b ^ {2} \sin \alpha}{\sqrt {a ^ {2} + b ^ {2}}} \bigg / \frac {m (a ^ {2} + 6 b ^ {2}) b ^ {2}}{4 (a ^ {2} + b ^ {2})} \right] ^ {1 / 2} \\ & = \left(\frac {4 g \sqrt {a ^ {2} + b ^ {2}} \sin \alpha}{a ^ {2} + 6 b ^ {2}}\right) ^ {1 / 2} = \left(\frac {4 g R \sin \alpha}{a ^ {2} + 6 b ^ {2}}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \end{array}
$$

9.3.9 一个质量为 m 的质点受到势能为 $V(r)$ (r 是球坐标) 的力作用.

(1) 用一个绕 z 轴以角速度 $\omega$ 转动的球坐标系写出其拉格朗日函数；

(2) 证明上面写出的拉格朗日函数和用上述的转动参考系引入与速度有关的广义势 (它来自惯性离轴力和科里奥利力) 的拉格朗日函数是相同的.

解 （1）参考系仍是静止的惯性参考系，但用的广义坐标是转动的球坐标系 r、θ 和 φ。质点的绝对速度为

$$
\begin{array}{l} \boldsymbol {v} = \boldsymbol {v} ^ {\prime} + \omega \times \boldsymbol {r} = \dot {r} \boldsymbol {e} _ {r} + r \dot {\theta} \boldsymbol {e} _ {\theta} + r \sin \theta \dot {\varphi} \boldsymbol {e} _ {\varphi} + \omega \times r \boldsymbol {e} _ {r} \\ \omega = \omega (\cos \theta \boldsymbol {e} _ {r} - \sin \theta \boldsymbol {e} _ {\theta}) \end{array}
$$

所以

$$
\textbf {v} = \dot {r} \textbf {e} _ {r} + r \dot {\theta} \textbf {e} _ {\theta} + (r \dot {\varphi} \sin \theta + \omega r \sin \theta) \textbf {e} _ {\varphi}
$$

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} m \boldsymbol {v} \cdot \boldsymbol {v} = \frac {1}{2} m \left[ \dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + (r \dot {\varphi} \sin \theta + \omega r \sin \theta) ^ {2} \right]
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta + \omega^ {2} r ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta + 2 \omega r ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta \dot {\varphi}) \\ & L = T - V = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta) - V (r) \\ & + \frac {1}{2} m (\omega^ {2} r ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta + 2 \omega r ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta \dot {\varphi}) \end{array}
$$

(2)采用绕 $z$ 轴以角速度 $\omega$ 转动的参考系，采用球坐标 $r, \theta, \varphi$ 为广义坐标，

$$
\begin{array}{l} T = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}) \\ V = V (r) \end{array}
$$

引入广义势 $U$ 

$$
U = - \frac {1}{2} m (\omega^ {2} r ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta + 2 \omega r ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta \dot {\varphi})
$$

则 $L = T - V - U$ ，与用静参考系写出的拉格朗日函数相同.或反过来讲，采用同样的广义坐标，不论用什么参考系，应有相同的拉格朗日函数，由此需引入上述广义势.

采用转动参考系,必须引入惯性力,它们是惯性离轴力和科里奥利力,因此需引入的广义势必来自这两个力.下面来验证这一点.

先由广义势 U 求广义力.

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {Q _ {r} = - \frac {\partial U}{\partial r} + \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial U}{\partial \dot {r}}\right) = m \omega^ {2} r \sin^ {2} \theta + 2 m \omega r \sin^ {2} \theta \dot {\varphi}} \\ & {Q _ {\theta} = - \frac {\partial U}{\partial \theta} + \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial U}{\partial \dot {\theta}}\right) = m \omega^ {2} r ^ {2} \sin \theta \cos \theta + 2 m r ^ {2} \sin \theta \cos \theta \dot {\varphi}} \\ & {Q _ {\varphi} = - \frac {\partial U}{\partial \varphi} + \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial U}{\partial \dot {\varphi}}\right) = - \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} (m \omega r ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta)} \\ & {\qquad = - 2 m \omega r \dot {r} \sin^ {2} \theta - 2 m \omega r ^ {2} \sin \theta \cos \theta \dot {\theta}} \end{array}
$$

再由惯性离轴力和科里奥利力求广义力

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\text {主离轴力和科里奥利力求广义力}} \\ & {- m \pmb {\omega} \times (\pmb {\omega} \times \pmb {r}) = m \omega^ {2} r \sin^ {2} \theta \pmb {e} _ {r} + m \omega^ {2} r \sin \theta \cos \theta \pmb {e} _ {\theta}} \\ & {- 2 m \pmb {\omega} \times \pmb {v} = - 2 m \omega (\cos_ {\theta} \pmb {e} _ {r} - \sin \theta \pmb {e} _ {\theta}) \times (\dot {r}   \pmb {e} _ {r} + r \dot {\theta}   \pmb {e} _ {\theta}} \\ & {\qquad + r \sin \theta \dot {\varphi}   \pmb {e} _ {\varphi})} \\ & {\qquad = 2 m \omega r \sin^ {2} \theta \dot {\varphi}   \pmb {e} _ {r} + 2 m \omega r \sin \theta \cos \theta \dot {\varphi}   \pmb {e} _ {\theta}} \\ & {\qquad + (- 2 m \omega \dot {r}   \sin \theta - 2 m \omega r \dot {\theta}   \cos \theta) \pmb {e} _ {\varphi}} \end{array}
$$

惯性离轴力和科里奥利力的合力的三个分量为：

$$
\begin{array}{l} F _ {r} = m \omega^ {2} r \sin^ {2} \theta + 2 m \omega r \sin^ {2} \dot {\theta} \dot {\varphi} \\ F _ {\theta} = m \omega^ {2} r \sin \theta \cos \theta + 2 m \omega r \sin \theta \cos \dot {\theta} \dot {\varphi} \\ F _ {\varphi} = - 2 m \omega \dot {r} \sin \theta - 2 m \omega r \dot {\theta} \cos \theta \end{array}
$$

用球坐标,力和广义力的关系为

$$
Q _ {r} = F _ {r}, \quad Q _ {\theta} = r F _ {r}, \quad Q _ {\varphi} = r \sin \theta F _ {\varphi}
$$

与由广义势算出的广义力完全一致.

9.3.10 一个由 $x, y, z$ 轴构成的直角坐标系，相对于惯性系以匀角速 $\omega$ 绕 $z$ 轴转动。一个质量为 $m$ 的质点在势能 $V(x, y, z)$ 的力的作用下运动。试用 $x, y, z$ 为广义坐标，写出此质点的拉格朗日函数及拉格朗日方程，并证明这些方程与在惯性参考系中这个质点受到一个力 $-\nabla V$ 及另一个可用广义势 $U$ 导出的力作用下的运动微分方程是一样的，写出这个广义势。

解 方法一:用惯性参考系,但用转动的 x、y、z 作为广义坐标,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\pmb {v} = \dot {x} \pmb {i} + \dot {y} \pmb {j} + \dot {z} \pmb {k} + \omega \pmb {k} \times (x \pmb {i} + y \pmb {j} + z \pmb {k})} \\ & {\qquad = (\dot {x} - \omega y) \pmb {i} + (\dot {y} + \omega x) \pmb {j} + \dot {z} \pmb {k}} \\ & {T = \frac {1}{2} m v ^ {2} = \frac {1}{2} m [ (\dot {x} - \omega y) ^ {2} + (\dot {y} + \omega x) ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2} ]} \\ & {\qquad = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{2} m \omega^ {2} (x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) + m \omega (x \dot {y} - y \dot {x})} \\ & {L = T - V} \\ & {\qquad = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{2} m \omega^ {2} (x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) + m \omega (x \dot {y} - y \dot {x}) - V (x, y, z)} \\ & {\qquad \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {x}} = m \dot {x} - m \omega y, \quad \frac {\partial L}{\partial x} = m \omega^ {2} x + m \omega \dot {y} - \frac {\partial V}{\partial x}} \\ & {\qquad \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {y}} = m \dot {y} + m \omega x   \frac {\partial L}{\partial y} = m \omega^ {2} y - m \omega \dot {x} - \frac {\partial V}{\partial y}} \\ & {\qquad \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {z}} = m \dot {z}, \quad \frac {\partial L}{\partial z} = - \frac {\partial V}{\partial z}} \end{array}
$$

由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L}{\partial q_i}\right) - \frac{\partial L}{\partial q_i} = 0$ ，得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & m \ddot {x} = - \frac {\partial V}{\partial x} + m \omega^ {2} x + 2 m \omega \dot {y} \\ & m \ddot {y} = - \frac {\partial V}{\partial y} + m \omega^ {2} y - 2 m \omega \dot {x} \\ & m \ddot {z} = - \frac {\partial V}{\partial z} \end{array}
$$

用上述绕 z 轴以角速度 $\omega$ 转动的 x、y、z 坐标系作为参考系，

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2}), \quad V = V (x, y, z)
$$

用同样的广义坐标,把惯性力用广义势来处理,可有同样的拉格朗日函数.

$$
L = T - V - U = \frac {1}{2} m \left(\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2}\right) - V (x, y, z) - U
$$

与上面写出的 L 比较, 可得

$$
U = - \frac {1}{2} m \omega^ {2} (x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) - m \omega (x \dot {y} - y \dot {x})
$$

显然，用此广义势可得广义力

$$
Q _ {x} = - \frac {\partial U}{\partial x} + \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial U}{\partial \dot {x}}\right) = m \omega^ {2} x + 2 m \omega \dot {y}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} Q _ {y} = - \frac {\partial U}{\partial y} + \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial U}{\partial \dot {y}}\right) = m \omega^ {2} y - 2 m \omega \dot {x} \\ Q _ {z} = - \frac {\partial U}{\partial z} + \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial U}{\partial \dot {z}}\right) = 0 \end{array}
$$

这些广义力正是惯性离轴力和科里奥利力引起的，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} - m \omega \times (\omega \times r) - 2 m \omega \times v & \\ = m \omega^ {2} x i + m \omega^ {2} y j + 2 m \omega \dot {y} i - 2 m \omega \dot {x} j & \\ = (m \omega^ {2} x + 2 m \omega \dot {y}) i + (m \omega^ {2} y - 2 m \omega \dot {x}) j & \end{array}
$$

可见,引入的广义势正是用来处理惯性离轴力和科里奥利力这两个惯性力的.

方法二:广义势 U 的表达式可以写成不同的表达式. 只要满足下列三式即可:

$$
\begin{array}{l} - \frac {\partial U}{\partial x} + \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial U}{\partial \dot {x}}\right) = m \omega^ {2} x + 2 m \omega \dot {y} \\ - \frac {\partial U}{\partial y} + \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial U}{\partial \dot {y}}\right) = m \omega^ {2} y - 2 m \omega \dot {x} \\ - \frac {\partial U}{\partial z} + \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial U}{\partial \dot {z}}\right) = 0 \end{array}
$$

把 $U$ 写成 $U = U_{1} + U_{2}$ ，其中 $U_{1} = -\frac{1}{2} m\omega^{2}(x^{2} + y^{2})$ ；它是惯性离轴力势能.则 $U_{2}$ 应满足

$$
\begin{array}{l} - \frac {\partial U _ {2}}{\partial x} + \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial U _ {2}}{\partial \dot {x}}\right) = 2 m \omega \dot {y} \\ - \frac {\partial U _ {2}}{\partial y} + \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial U _ {2}}{\partial \dot {y}}\right) = - 2 m \omega \dot {x} \\ - \frac {\partial U _ {2}}{\partial z} + \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial U _ {2}}{\partial \dot {z}}\right) = 0 \end{array}
$$

$2m\pmb{v} \times \pmb{\omega}$ 可与 $q\pmb{v} \times \pmb{B}$ 相比拟, $\pmb{B} = \nabla \times \pmb{A}$ , 其中 $\pmb{A}$ 是矢势. 可以证明, $q\pmb{v} \times \pmb{B} = q\pmb{v} \times (\nabla \times \pmb{A})$ 的广义势为 $U = -q\pmb{v} \cdot \pmb{A}$ 

今 $\omega = \omega k$ ，也可写成 $\omega = \nabla \times A$ 的形式， $A = -\omega y i, \bar{A} = \omega x \bar{j}$ 或 $\bar{A} = \frac{1}{2} \omega (-y i + x j)$ ，都可得到 $\nabla \times A = \omega k$ 。第三个 $A$ 是第一、第二个 $A$ 各取一半相加而得，由此可见还可以写出无穷个 $A$ 的表达式，例如第一个的 $\frac{1}{n}$ 与第二个的 $\frac{n-1}{n}$ 之和。

$$
U _ {2} = - 2 m \boldsymbol {v} \cdot \boldsymbol {A}
$$

可写成 $U_{2} = 2m\omega y\dot{x}, U_{2} = -2m\omega x\dot{y}, U_{2} = m\omega (\dot{x}y - \dot{x}y)$ 等，

因而 $U = U_{1} + U_{2}$ 可写成不同的表达式. 方法一写出的 $U$ 是这里 $U_{2}$ 的第三个写法.

9.3.11 质量为 $m$ 、荷电为 $q$ 的粒子在柱坐标中有轴对称的电场 $E = \frac{E_0}{r} e_r$ 和均匀磁场 $B = B_0 k, E_0, B_0$ 均为常量。求此粒子的拉格朗日函数和运动微分方程。

解 方法一: 引入电磁场的标势 $\phi$ 和矢势 A, 今电场、磁场都不随时间而变, 故有

$$
\pmb {E} = - \nabla \phi , \qquad \pmb {B} = \nabla \times \pmb {A}
$$

用柱坐标，

可取

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\nabla \phi = \frac {\partial \phi}{\partial r} e _ {r} + \frac {1}{r} \frac {\partial \phi}{\partial \varphi} e _ {\varphi} + \frac {\partial \phi}{\partial z} k} \\ & {\nabla \times A = \left(\frac {1}{r} \frac {\partial A _ {z}}{\partial \varphi} - \frac {\partial A _ {\varphi}}{\partial z}\right) e _ {r} + \left(\frac {\partial A _ {r}}{\partial z} - \frac {\partial A _ {z}}{\partial r}\right) e _ {\varphi} + \left[ \frac {1}{r} \frac {\partial (r A _ {\varphi})}{\partial r} - \frac {1}{r} \frac {\partial A _ {r}}{\partial \varphi} \right] k} \\ & {\qquad \phi = - E _ {0} \mathrm{ln} r, \quad A = \frac {1}{2} r B _ {0} e _ {\varphi}} \\ & {U = q \phi - q v \cdot A = - q E _ {0} \mathrm{ln} r - q (\dot {r} e _ {r} + r \dot {\varphi} e _ {\varphi} + \dot {z} k) \cdot \frac {1}{2} r B _ {0} e _ {\varphi}} \\ & {\qquad = - q E _ {0} \mathrm{ln} r - \frac {1}{2} q B _ {0} r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi}} \\ & {T = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2})} \\ & {L = T - U = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2}) + q E _ {0} \mathrm{ln} r + \frac {1}{2} q B _ {0} r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi}} \\ & {\qquad \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \Big (\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {r}} \Big) - \frac {\partial L}{\partial r} = 0, \quad \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \Big (\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\varphi}} \Big) - \frac {\partial L}{\partial \varphi} = 0, \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \Big (\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {z}} \Big) - \frac {\partial L}{\partial z} = 0} \end{array}
$$

由

可得运动微分方程为

$$
\begin{array}{l} m \ddot {r} - m r \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} - \frac {q E _ {0}}{r} - q B _ {0} r \dot {\varphi} = 0 \\ m r ^ {2} \ddot {\varphi} + 2 m r \dot {r} \dot {\varphi} + q B _ {0} r \dot {r} = 0 \\ m \ddot {z} = 0 \end{array}
$$

方法二：先用直角坐标的旋度表达式求 A，再变换为柱坐标的表达式，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \mathbf {A} = - B _ {0} y \mathbf {i} = - B _ {0} r \sin \varphi (\cos \varphi \mathbf {e} _ {r} - \sin \varphi \mathbf {e} _ {\varphi}) \\ U = q \phi - q ^ {*} A = - q E _ {0} \ln r + q B _ {0} r \sin \varphi (\dot {r} \cos \varphi - r \dot {\varphi} \sin \varphi) \\ L = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2}) + q E _ {0} \ln r - q B _ {0} r \sin \varphi (\dot {r} \cos \varphi - r \dot {\varphi} \sin \varphi) \end{array}
$$

以下略,可得与方法一相同的运动微分方程.

9.3.12 当电磁场的矢势和标势受到规范变换：

$\pmb{A}$ 换为

$\phi$ 换为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \boldsymbol {A} ^ {\prime} & = \boldsymbol {A} + \nabla \Lambda \\ \phi^ {\prime} & = \phi - \frac {\partial \Lambda}{\partial t} \end{array}
$$

时, 它们所描述的电磁场不变, 可是拉格朗日函数显然要改变. 证明: 用新的拉格朗日函数仍然给出与原来相同的运动微分方程.

证明 只要证明规范变换后的广义势得到的广义力仍和变换前的一样,就证明了可得相同的运动微分方程.

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {U = q \phi^ {\prime} - q \dot {\boldsymbol {r}} \cdot \boldsymbol {A} ^ {\prime}} \\ & {\qquad = q \phi - q \frac {\partial \Lambda}{\partial t} - q \dot {\boldsymbol {r}} \cdot \boldsymbol {A} - q \dot {\boldsymbol {r}} \cdot \nabla \Lambda} \end{array}
$$

要证明附加项

$$
U ^ {*} = - q \frac {\partial \Lambda}{\partial t} - q \dot {r} \cdot \nabla \Lambda = - q \frac {\partial \Lambda}{\partial t} - q \left(\dot {x} \frac {\partial \Lambda}{\partial x} + \dot {y} \frac {\partial \Lambda}{\partial y} + \dot {z} \frac {\partial \Lambda}{\partial z}\right)
$$

对广义力没有贡献，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\frac {\partial U ^ {*}}{\partial \dot {x}} = - q \frac {\partial \Lambda}{\partial x}} \\ & {\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \Big (\frac {\partial U ^ {*}}{\partial \dot {x}} \Big) = - q \Big [ \frac {\partial^ {2} \Lambda}{\partial t \partial x} + \frac {\partial^ {2} \Lambda}{\partial x ^ {2}} \dot {x} + \frac {\partial^ {2} \Lambda}{\partial y \partial x} \dot {y} + \frac {\partial^ {2} \Lambda}{\partial z \partial x} \dot {z} \Big ]} \\ & {\frac {\partial U ^ {*}}{\partial x} = - q \frac {\partial^ {2} \Lambda}{\partial x \partial t} - q \Big (\dot {x} \frac {\partial^ {2} \Lambda}{\partial x ^ {2}} + \dot {y} \frac {\partial^ {2} \Lambda}{\partial x \partial y} + \dot {z} \frac {\partial^ {2} \Lambda}{\partial x \partial z} \Big)} \\ & {\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \Big (\frac {\partial U ^ {*}}{\partial \dot {x}} \Big) - \frac {\partial U ^ {*}}{\partial x} = 0} \end{array}
$$

同样可证：

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial U ^ {*}}{\partial \dot {y}}\right) - \frac {\partial U ^ {*}}{\partial y} = 0, \quad \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial U ^ {*}}{\partial \dot {z}}\right) - \frac {\partial U ^ {*}}{\partial z} = 0
$$

即由附加项 $U^{*}$ 得到的广义力 $Q_{x}^{*} = Q_{y}^{*} = Q_{z}^{*} = 0.$ 

上题两种方法给出了两个 A，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \boldsymbol {A} = \frac {1}{2} r B _ {0} \boldsymbol {e} _ {\varphi} \\ \boldsymbol {A} ^ {\prime} = - B _ {0} r \sin \varphi (\cos \varphi \boldsymbol {e} _ {r} - \sin \varphi \boldsymbol {e} _ {\varphi}). \end{array}
$$

可以找到 $\Lambda$ ，使 $A^{\prime} = A + \nabla \Lambda$ ，这个 $\Lambda$ 为

$$
\Lambda = - \frac {1}{2} B _ {0} r ^ {2} \sin \varphi \cos \varphi
$$

9.3.13 一个长度为 $b$ 、摆锤质量为 $m$ 的单摆悬于原长为 $a$ 、劲度系数为 $k$ 的弹簧上，悬点限于在竖直方向运动。摆锤处于黏性介质中，受到的阻力其大小与摆锤的速率成正比，比例系数为 $\gamma$ 。用摆的悬点在系统的平衡位置下的距离 $y$ 和摆与铅垂线的夹角 $\theta$ 为广义坐标，写出耗散函数和运动微分方程。

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/d3fb7a28bd62d2b7ccd1310ce627f2c2bda41a5813b7550a0d44f6100f4db327.jpg)


解 方法一: 取平衡位置为坐标系原点. x 轴沿水平方向, y 轴竖直向下, 如图 9.52 所示.

图9.52

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {T = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} + \dot {y} ^ {2})} \\ & {\quad = \frac {1}{2} m [ (b \dot {\theta} \cos \theta) ^ {2} + (\dot {y} - b \dot {\theta} \sin \theta) ^ {2} ]} \\ & {\quad = \frac {1}{2} m (b ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2} - 2 b \dot {y} \dot {\theta} \sin \theta)} \\ & {V = \frac {1}{2} k \left[ \left(\frac {m g}{k} + y\right) ^ {2} - \left(\frac {m g}{k}\right) ^ {2} \right] - m g [ y - b (1 - \cos \theta) ]} \\ & {\quad = \frac {1}{2} k y ^ {2} + m g b (1 - \cos \theta)} \end{array}
$$

这里取平衡位置 $y = 0, \theta = 0$ 为势能的零点，但这不是必须的，

$$
L = T - V = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {y} ^ {2} + b ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} - 2 b \dot {y} \dot {\theta} \sin \theta) - \frac {1}{2} k y ^ {2} - m g b (1 - \cos \theta)
$$

摆锤的速度

$$
\boldsymbol {v} = b \dot {\theta} \cos \theta \boldsymbol {i} + (\dot {y} - b \dot {\theta} \sin \theta) \boldsymbol {j}
$$

阻力

$$
\boldsymbol {f} = - \gamma \boldsymbol {v} = - \gamma b \dot {\theta} \cos \theta \bar {i} - \gamma (\dot {y} - b \dot {\theta} \sin \theta) \boldsymbol {j}
$$

$$
f _ {x} = - \gamma b \dot {\theta} \cos \theta
$$

$$
f _ {y} = - \gamma (\dot {y} - b \dot {\theta} \sin \theta)
$$

耗散函数

$$
R = \frac {1}{2} \gamma [ (b \dot {\theta} \cos \theta) ^ {2} + (\dot {y} - b \dot {\theta} \sin \theta) ^ {2} ] = \frac {1}{2} \gamma (\dot {y} ^ {2} + b ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} - 2 b \dot {y} \dot {\theta} \sin \theta)
$$

方法二：

$$
Q _ {y} = f _ {x} \frac {\partial X}{\partial y} + f _ {Y} \frac {\partial Y}{\partial y},
$$

$$
X = b \sin \theta , \quad Y = y + b \cos \theta
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} Q _ {y} & = - \gamma b \dot {\theta} \cos \theta \cdot 0 - \gamma (\dot {y} - b \dot {\theta} \sin \theta) \cdot 1 \\ & = - \gamma (\dot {y} - b \dot {\theta} \sin \theta) \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} Q _ {\theta} & = f _ {X} \frac {\partial X}{\partial \theta} + f _ {Y} \frac {\partial Y}{\partial \theta} \\ & = - \gamma b \dot {\theta} \cos \theta \cdot b \cos \theta - \gamma (\dot {y} - b \dot {\theta} \sin \theta) (- b \sin \theta) \\ & = \gamma b \dot {y} \sin \theta - \gamma b ^ {2} \dot {\theta} \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \mathrm{d} R & = \frac {\partial R}{\partial \dot {y}} \mathrm{d} \dot {y} + \frac {\partial R}{\partial \dot {\theta}} \mathrm{d} \dot {\theta} = - Q _ {y} \mathrm{d} \dot {y} - Q _ {\theta} \mathrm{d} \dot {\theta} \\ & = \gamma (\dot {y} - b \dot {\theta} \sin \theta) \mathrm{d} \dot {y} - (\gamma b \dot {y} \sin \theta - \gamma b ^ {2} \dot {\theta}) \mathrm{d} \dot {\theta} \\ & = \mathrm{d} \left(\frac {1}{2} \gamma \dot {y} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \gamma b ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} - \gamma b \dot {y} \dot {\theta} \sin \theta\right) \end{array}
$$

$$
R = \frac {1}{2} \gamma (\dot {y} ^ {2} + b ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} - 2 b \dot {y} \dot {\theta} \sin \theta)
$$

由

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \Big (\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {y}} \Big) - \frac {\partial L}{\partial y} = - \frac {\partial R}{\partial \dot {y}} \quad \text {或} Q _ {y}
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \bigg (\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\theta}} \bigg) - \frac {\partial L}{\partial \theta} = - \frac {\partial R}{\partial \dot {\theta}} \quad \text {或} Q _ {\theta}
$$

得

$$
m \ddot {y} - m b \ddot {\theta} \sin \theta - m b \dot {\theta} ^ {2} \cos \theta + k y = - \gamma \dot {y} + \gamma b \dot {\theta} \sin \theta
$$

$$
m b \ddot {\theta} - m \ddot {y} \sin \theta + m g \sin \theta = \gamma \dot {y} \sin \theta - \gamma b \dot {\theta}
$$

9.3.14 求 9.1.9 题所述系统的耗散函数, 并由此求 $Q_{y}, Q_{y_{3}}$ .

解

$$
R = - \int (- \alpha_ {1} \dot {y} _ {1} \mathrm{d} \dot {y} _ {1} - \alpha_ {2} \dot {y} _ {2} \mathrm{d} \dot {y} _ {2}) = \frac {1}{2} (\alpha_ {1} \dot {y} _ {1} ^ {2} + \alpha_ {2} \dot {y} _ {2} ^ {2})
$$

$$
y _ {1} = y + y _ {3}, \quad \dot {y} _ {1} = \dot {y} + \dot {y} _ {3}, \quad \dot {y} _ {2} = \dot {y} - \dot {y} _ {3}
$$

$$
R = \frac {1}{2} \alpha_ {1} (\dot {y} + \dot {y} _ {3}) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \alpha_ {2} (\dot {y} - \dot {y} _ {3}) ^ {2}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} Q _ {y} = - \frac {\partial R}{\partial \dot {y}} = - \alpha_ {1} (\dot {y} + \dot {y} _ {3}) - \alpha_ {2} (\dot {y} - \dot {y} _ {3}) \\ Q _ {y _ {3}} = - \frac {\partial R}{\partial \dot {y} _ {3}} = - \alpha_ {1} (\dot {y} + \dot {y} _ {3}) + \alpha_ {2} (\dot {y} - \dot {y} _ {3}) \end{array}
$$

9.3.15 求 9.1.12 题所述系统的耗散函数 $R(\dot{x}_1, \dot{q}_1, \dot{q}_2)$ , 并由此求 $Q_{x_1}, Q_{q_1}, Q_{q_2}$ .

解

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {R = - \int (Q _ {x _ {1}} \mathrm{d} \dot {x} _ {1} + Q _ {x _ {2}} \mathrm{d} \dot {x} _ {2} + Q _ {x _ {3}} \mathrm{d} \dot {x} _ {3})} \\ & {\quad = \int \{[ \alpha_ {1} \dot {x} _ {1} - \alpha_ {4} (\dot {x} _ {2} - \dot {x} _ {1}) ] \mathrm{d} \dot {x} _ {1} + [ \alpha_ {2} \dot {x} _ {2} + \alpha_ {4} (\dot {x} _ {2} - \dot {x} _ {1})} \\ & {\qquad - \alpha_ {5} (\dot {x} _ {3} - \dot {x} _ {2}) ] \mathrm{d} \dot {x} _ {2} + [ \alpha_ {2} \dot {x} _ {3} + \alpha_ {5} (\dot {x} _ {3} - \dot {x} _ {2}) ] \mathrm{d} \dot {x} _ {3} \}} \\ & {\quad = \int (\alpha_ {1} + \alpha_ {4}) \dot {x} _ {1} \mathrm{d} \dot {x} _ {1} + \int (\alpha_ {2} + \alpha_ {4} + \alpha_ {5}) \dot {x} _ {2} \mathrm{d} \dot {x} _ {2} + \int (\alpha_ {3} + \alpha_ {5}) \dot {x} _ {3} \mathrm{d} \dot {x} _ {3}} \\ & {\qquad + \int [ - \alpha_ {4} \dot {x} _ {2} \mathrm{d} \dot {x} _ {1} - (\alpha_ {4} \dot {x} _ {1} + \alpha_ {5} \dot {x} _ {3}) \mathrm{d} \dot {x} _ {2} - \alpha_ {5} \dot {x} _ {2} \mathrm{d} \dot {x} _ {3} ]} \\ & {\quad = \frac 12 (\alpha_ {1} + \alpha_ {4}) \dot {x} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac 12 (\alpha_ {2} + \alpha_ {4} + \alpha_ {5}) \dot {x} _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac 12 (\alpha_ {3} + \alpha_ {5}) \dot {x} _ {3} ^ {2}} \\ & {\qquad - \alpha_ {4} \dot {x} _ {1} \dot {x} _ {2} - \alpha_ {5} \dot {x} _ {2} \dot {x}, \quad \dot {x} _ {2} = \dot {x} _ {1} + \dot {q} _ {1}, \quad \dot {x} _ {3} = \dot {x} _ {1} + \dot {q} _ {1} + \dot {q} _ {2}} \\ & {\qquad , x _ {2}} = x _ {1} + q _ {1}, x _ {3}. \end{array}
$$

因为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} R (\dot {x} _ {1}, \dot {q} _ {1}, \dot {q} _ {2}) & = \frac {1}{2} (\alpha_ {1} + \alpha_ {4}) \dot {x} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} (\alpha_ {2} + \alpha_ {4} + \alpha_ {5}) (\dot {x} _ {1} + \dot {q} _ {1}) ^ {2} \\ & + \frac {1}{2} (\alpha_ {3} + \alpha_ {5}) (\ddot {x} _ {1} + \dot {q} _ {1} + \dot {q} _ {2}) ^ {2} - x _ {3} \dot {x} _ {1} (\dot {x} _ {1} + \dot {q} _ {1}) - \alpha_ {5} (\dot {x} _ {1} + \dot {q} _ {1}) (\dot {x} _ {1} + \dot {q} _ {1} + \dot {q} _ {2}) \\ Q _ {x _ {1}} & = - \frac {\partial R (\dot {x} _ {1} , \dot {q} _ {1} , \dot {q} _ {2})}{\partial \dot {x} _ {1}}, \quad Q _ {q _ {1}} = - \frac {\partial R (\dot {x} _ {1} , \dot {q} _ {1} , \dot {q} _ {2})}{\partial \dot {q} _ {1}} \\ Q _ {q _ {2}} & = - \frac {\partial R (\dot {x} _ {1} , \dot {q} _ {1} , \dot {q} _ {2})}{\partial \dot {q} _ {2}} \end{array}
$$

计算略.

9.3.16 求 9.1.19 题所述系统的耗散函数.

解 为了不与耗散函数 R 相混淆, 将圆环的半径改为 r.

$$
R = - \iint (F _ {x ^ {\prime}} \mathrm{d} \dot {x} ^ {\prime} + F _ {y ^ {\prime}} \mathrm{d} \dot {y} ^ {\prime})
$$

两个积分符号,一个是对恰当微分的积分,另一个是因为系统是连续分布的,阻力也是连续分布于各质元的,对受到阻力的所有区域积分.

今系统是半径为 r 的圆环, 9.1.19 题图 9.14 中处于 $\alpha \sim \alpha + d\alpha$ 的环元所受的摩擦力为

$$
\begin{array}{l} - f \frac {\left[ \dot {x} - r \dot {\varphi} \sin (\varphi + \alpha) i \right] + \left[ \dot {y} + r \dot {\varphi} \cos (\varphi + \alpha) \right] j}{\left\{\left[ \dot {x} - r \dot {\varphi} \sin (\varphi + \alpha) \right] ^ {2} + \left[ \dot {y} + r \dot {\varphi} \cos (\varphi + \alpha) \right] ^ {2} \right\} ^ {1 / 2}} r d \alpha \\ \dot {x} ^ {\prime} = \dot {x} - r \dot {\varphi} \sin (\varphi + \alpha) \\ \dot {y} ^ {\prime} = \dot {y} + r \dot {\varphi} \cos (\varphi + \alpha) \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} R = \int_ {0} ^ {2 \pi} \left(\int f \frac {[ \dot {x} - r \dot {\varphi} \sin (\varphi + \alpha) ] d [ \dot {x} - r \dot {\varphi} \sin (\varphi + \alpha) ]}{\{[ \dot {x} - r \dot {\varphi} \sin (\varphi + \alpha) ] ^ {2} + [ \dot {y} + r \dot {\varphi} \cos (\varphi + \alpha) ^ {2} ] \} ^ {1 / 2}} \right. \\ \left. + f \frac {[ \dot {y} + r \dot {\varphi} \cos (\varphi + \alpha) ] d [ \dot {y} + r \dot {\varphi} \cos (\varphi + \alpha) ]}{\{[ \dot {x} - r \dot {\varphi} \sin (\varphi + \alpha) ] ^ {2} + [ \dot {y} + r \dot {\varphi} \cos (\varphi + \alpha) ] ^ {2} \} ^ {1 / 2}}\right) r d \alpha \\ = r f \int_ {0} ^ {2 \pi} \int \frac {\frac {1}{2} d \{[ \dot {x} - r \dot {\varphi} \sin (\varphi + \alpha) ] ^ {2} + [ \dot {y} + r \dot {\varphi} \cos (\varphi + \alpha) ] ^ {2} \}}{\{[ \dot {x} - r \dot {\varphi} \sin (\varphi + \alpha) ] ^ {2} + [ \dot {y} + r \dot {\varphi} \cos (\varphi + \alpha) ] ^ {2} \} ^ {1 / 2}} d \alpha \\ = r f \int_ {0} ^ {2 \pi} \{[ \dot {x} - r \dot {\varphi} \sin (\varphi + \alpha) ] ^ {2} + [ \dot {y} + r \dot {\varphi} \cos (\varphi + \alpha) ] ^ {2} \} ^ {1 / 2} d \alpha \\ Q _ {x} = - \frac {\partial R (\dot {x} , \dot {y} , \dot {\varphi})}{\partial x}, Q _ {y} = - \frac {\partial R (\dot {x} , \dot {y} , \dot {\varphi})}{\partial y}, Q _ {\varphi} = - \frac {\partial R (\dot {x} , \dot {y} , \dot {\varphi})}{\partial \dot {\varphi}}. \end{array}
$$

由

可得 9.1.19 题的结果.

9.3.17 上题的圆环改为半径为 $r$ 的圆盘， $f$ 改为相接触的单位面积所受的摩擦力的大小，证明耗散函数为

$$
R = f \int_ {0} ^ {r} \int_ {0} ^ {2 \pi} \left\{\left[ \dot {x} - r ^ {\prime} \dot {\varphi} \sin (\varphi + \alpha) \right] ^ {2} + \left[ \dot {y} + r ^ {\prime} \dot {\varphi} \cos (\varphi + \alpha) \right] ^ {2} \right\} ^ {1 / 2} r ^ {\prime} d r ^ {\prime} d \alpha
$$

证明 在 $r' \sim r' + \mathrm{d}r'$ , $\alpha \sim \alpha + \mathrm{d}\alpha$ 的面元受到的摩擦力为

$$
- f \frac {\left[ \dot {x} - r ^ {\prime} \dot {\varphi} \sin (\varphi + \alpha) \right] i + \left[ \dot {y} + r ^ {\prime} \dot {\varphi} \cos (\varphi + \alpha) \right] j}{\left\{\left[ \dot {x} - r ^ {\prime} \dot {\varphi} \sin (\varphi + \alpha) \right] ^ {2} + \left[ \dot {y} + r ^ {\prime} \dot {\varphi} \cos (\varphi + \alpha) \right] ^ {2} \right\} ^ {1 / 2}} r ^ {\prime} d r ^ {\prime} d \alpha
$$

类似上题做法,可得要证明的式子.

9.3.18 假定图 9.53 中的减震器引入的力与相对速度的立方成正比, 比例系数为 b, 各对接触平面间的阻力是正比于相对速度的黏性阻力, 比例系数为 $\alpha_{1}$ 、 $\alpha_{2}$ 、 $\alpha_{3}$ . 求系统的耗散函数.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/4c153b253d3ce538c7af124e785f6589368e832987803925d54d60e784ad0036.jpg)



图9.53


解 设 $f_{1}, f_{2}, f_{3}$ 分别为 $m_{1}, m_{2}, m_{3}$ 受到的阻力，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {f _ {1} = - \alpha_ {1} \dot {x} _ {1} - \alpha_ {2} (\dot {x} _ {1} - \dot {x} _ {2}) - \alpha_ {3} (\dot {x} _ {1} - \dot {x} _ {3})} \\ & {f _ {2} = - \alpha_ {2} (\dot {x} _ {2} - \dot {x} _ {1}) - b (\dot {x} _ {2} - \dot {x} _ {3}) ^ {3}} \\ & {f _ {3} = - \alpha_ {3} (\dot {x} _ {3} - \dot {x} _ {1}) - b (\dot {x} _ {3} - \dot {x} _ {2}) ^ {3}} \\ & {\mathrm{d} R = - f _ {1} \mathrm{d} \dot {x} _ {1} - f _ {2} \mathrm{d} \dot {x} _ {2} - f _ {3} \mathrm{d} \dot {x} _ {3}} \\ & {\qquad = [ \alpha_ {1} \dot {x} _ {1} + \alpha_ {2} (\dot {x} _ {1} - \dot {x} _ {2}) + \alpha_ {3} (\dot {x} _ {1} - \dot {x} _ {3}) ] \mathrm{d} \dot {x} _ {1}} \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {+ [ \alpha_ {2} (\dot {x} _ {2} - \dot {x} _ {1}) + b (\dot {x} _ {2} - \dot {x} _ {3}) ^ {3} ] \mathrm{d} \dot {x} _ {2}} \\ & {+ [ \alpha_ {3} (\dot {x} _ {3} - \dot {x} _ {1}) + b (\dot {x} _ {3} - \dot {x} _ {2}) ^ {3} ] \mathrm{d} \dot {x} _ {3}} \\ & {= \alpha_ {1} \dot {x} _ {1} \mathrm{d} \dot {x} _ {1} + \alpha_ {2} (\dot {x} _ {2} - \dot {x} _ {1}) \mathrm{d} (\dot {x} _ {2} - \dot {x} _ {1}) + \alpha_ {3} (\dot {x} _ {3} - \dot {x} _ {1}) \mathrm{d} (\dot {x} _ {3} - \dot {x} _ {1})} \\ & {\quad + b (\dot {x} _ {3} - \dot {x} _ {2}) ^ {3} \mathrm{d} (\dot {x} _ {3} - \dot {x} _ {2})} \\ & {R = \frac {1}{2} \alpha_ {1} \dot {x} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \alpha_ {2} (\dot {x} _ {2} - \dot {x} _ {1}) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \alpha_ {3} (\dot {x} _ {3} - \dot {x} _ {1}) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{4} b (\dot {x} _ {3} - \dot {x} _ {2}) ^ {4}} \end{array}
$$

9.3.19 如图 9.54, 一个质量为 $M$ 、半径为 $R$ 的圆盘在一个光滑的水平面上滑动, 另有一个质量为 $m$ 、半径为 $r$ 的圆盘, 将其中心钉在前一圆盘上离中心距离 $b$ 处, 它可在前一圆盘上围绕该点无摩擦地转动. 选取适当的广义坐标, 写出该系统的拉格朗日函数, 给出一切可以直接写出的运动积分.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/d15eb693ca28bbfecf56800f298b8865f85fb9b8e13a74b26a560dd2b930c840.jpg)



图9.54


解 取 $x, y, \varphi, \theta$ 为广义坐标， $x, y$ 是大圆盘中心的坐标，取 $x$ 轴使 $t = 0$ 时两圆盘中心的连线方向为 $x$ 轴正向， $\varphi, \theta$ 分别表示大小两圆盘在各自质心平动参考系中的转角， $t = 0$ 时， $\varphi = \theta = 0$ ，逆时针方向为正方向。

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {T = \frac {1}{2} M (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{2} \cdot \frac {1}{2} M R ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m [ (\dot {x} - b \dot {\varphi} \sin \varphi) ^ {2}} \\ & {\qquad + (\dot {y} + b \dot {\varphi} \cos \varphi) ^ {2} ] + \frac {1}{2} \times \frac {1}{2} m r ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2}} \\ & {\qquad = \frac {1}{2} (m + M) (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{4} (M R ^ {2} + 2 m b ^ {2}) \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{4} m r ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2}} \\ & {\qquad + m b (- \dot {x} \dot {\varphi} \sin \varphi + \dot {y} \dot {\varphi} \cos \varphi).} \\ & {V = 0} \\ & {L = T - V = T} \\ & {\frac {\partial L}{\partial \theta} = 0, \quad \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\theta}} = \frac {1}{2} m r ^ {2} \dot {\theta} = \text {常量}, \quad \dot {\theta} = \text {常量}} \\ & {\frac {\partial L}{\partial x} = 0, \quad \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {x}} = (m + M) \dot {x} - m b \dot {\varphi} \sin \varphi = \text {常量}} \end{array}
$$

因为

所以

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\frac {\partial L}{\partial y} = 0, \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {y}} = (m + M) \dot {y} + m b \dot {\varphi} \cos \varphi = \text {常量}} \\ & {\frac {\partial L}{\partial t} = 0, V = 0, U = 0} \\ & {\sum_ {i} \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {q} _ {i}} \dot {q} _ {i} - L = T = \text {常量}} \end{array}
$$

9.3.20 一个均匀的实心圆柱体, 半径为 $R$ , 质量为 $M$ , 放在一个水平面上. 另一个相同的圆柱体放在它上面, 和它的最高母线相切. 两圆柱体从静止状态开始做无滑动滚动. 写出该系统的拉格朗日函数, 说明存在哪些运动积分, 并证明, 只要两圆柱保持接触, 则有

$$
\dot {\theta} ^ {2} = \frac {1 2 g (1 - \cos \theta)}{R (1 7 + 4 \cos \theta - 4 \cos^ {2} \theta)}
$$

其中 $\theta$ 是两轴所决定的平面和通过一个轴的铅垂面间的夹角.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/1620d42ba47e3f74c9bc0f5c01da3fc71fc489839bd86fb76781cf34fdd1af54.jpg)


解 图 9.55 中, $\varphi,\psi$ 分别是两个圆柱在各自的质心平动参考系中的转角,


图9.55


$$
\begin{array}{r l} T & = \frac {1}{2} M \dot {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \times \frac {1}{2} M R ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} M [ (\dot {x} + 2 R \dot {\theta} \cos \theta) ^ {2} \\ & + (2 R \dot {\theta} \sin \theta) ^ {2} ] + \frac {1}{2} \cdot \frac {1}{2} M R ^ {2} \dot {\psi} ^ {2} \end{array}
$$

两个圆柱均做纯滚动，

可得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \dot {x} - R \dot {\varphi} & = 0 \\ 2 R \dot {\theta} - R \dot {\psi} & = R \dot {\varphi} \\ \dot {x} & = R \dot {\varphi}, \quad \dot {\psi} = 2 \dot {\theta} - \dot {\varphi} \end{array}
$$

选用 $\varphi,\theta$ 为广义坐标,用上述两个约束关系,动能可改写为

$$
\begin{array}{l} T = \frac {3}{2} M R ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + 3 M R ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + 2 M R ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} \dot {\theta} \cos \theta - M R ^ {2} \dot {\theta} \dot {\varphi} \\ V = - M g \cdot 2 R (1 - \cos \theta) \\ L = T - V = \frac {3}{2} M R ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + 3 M R ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + M R ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} \dot {\theta} (2 \cos \theta - 1) \\ + 2 M g R (1 - \cos \theta) \end{array}
$$

因为 $\frac{\partial L}{\partial \varphi} = 0, \frac{\partial L}{\partial \varphi} =$ 常量，并用初始条件： $t = 0$ 时， $\dot{\theta} = 0, \dot{\varphi} = 0$ ，可得

$$
3 M R ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} + M R ^ {2} \dot {\theta} (2 \cos \theta - 1) = 0
$$

因为

$$
\frac {\partial L}{\partial t} = 0, \quad \sum_ {i} \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {q} _ {i}} \dot {q} _ {i} - L = \text {常量}
$$

所以

$$
\frac {3}{2} M R ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + 3 M R ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + M R ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} \dot {\theta} (2 \cos \theta - 1) - 2 M g R (1 - \cos \theta) = 0
$$

这里用了初始条件: t=0 时, $\dot{\varphi}=\dot{\theta}=0,\theta=0$ , 因而等号右边的常量为零.

从两个运动积分的式子解出 $\dot{\theta}^2$ 得

$$
\dot {\theta} ^ {2} = \frac {1 2 g (1 - \cos \theta)}{R (1 7 + 4 \cos \theta - 4 \cos^ {2} \theta)}
$$

9.3.21 一根质量为 $M$ 、长为 $2a$ 的均质棒 $OA$ 装在固定点的光滑枢轴上，另有一个质量为 $m$ 的珠子 $P$ 用一根自然长度为 $a$ 、劲度系数为 $k$ 的轻弹簧连于 $O$ 点，并套在棒上，可沿棒做无摩擦滑动。设 $\theta$ 是 $OA$ 与铅垂线间的夹角， $\varphi$ 是包含 $OA$ 的铅垂面与固定铅垂面间的夹角， $r$ 为 $OP$ 间的距离。用 $r, \theta, \varphi$ 为广义坐标，写出系统的拉格朗日函数，并给出两个运动常数。

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/6d24fc2b367b581b4653abf5527c1dafa0644a7fcddb4bfdca4010327129a04c.jpg)


解 取 z 轴竖直向上, x 轴水平, 随棒绕 z 轴转动, x 轴、z 轴和棒始终在同一竖直平面内, 如图 9.56 所示.

棒关于 O 点的轴转动惯量和惯量积分别为

图9.56

$$
\begin{array}{l} I _ {x x} = \frac {1}{3} M (2 a \cos \theta) ^ {2} = \frac {4}{3} M a ^ {2} \cos^ {2} \theta \\ I _ {y y} = \frac {1}{3} M (2 a) ^ {2} = \frac {4}{3} M a ^ {2} \\ I _ {z z} = \frac {1}{3} M (2 a \sin \theta) ^ {2} = \frac {4}{3} M a ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta \\ I _ {x y} = I _ {y z} = 0, \quad I _ {x z} \neq 0 \end{array}
$$

但此题因为 $\omega_{x}=0$ ，可以不必计算 $I_{xz}$ .

今

所以

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & T = \frac {1}{2} (I _ {x x} \omega_ {x} ^ {2} + I _ {y y} \omega_ {y} ^ {2} + I _ {z z} \omega_ {z} ^ {2} + 2 I _ {x z} \omega_ {x} \omega_ {z} \\ & \quad + 2 I _ {x y} \omega_ {x} \omega_ {y} + 2 I _ {y z} \omega_ {y} \omega_ {z}) + \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}) \\ & \omega_ {x} = 0, \quad \omega_ {y} = - \dot {\theta}, \quad \omega_ {z} = \dot {\varphi} \\ & T = \frac {1}{2} m \dot {r} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \left(m r ^ {2} + \frac {4}{3} M a ^ {2}\right) (\dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta) \\ & V = - M g a c o s \theta - m g r c o s \theta + \frac {1}{2} k (r - a) ^ {2} \\ & L = T - V = \frac {1}{2} m \dot {r} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \left(m r ^ {2} + \frac {4}{3} M a ^ {2}\right) (\dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta) \\ & \quad + (M a + m r) g c o s \theta - \frac {1}{2} k (r - a) ^ {2} \end{array}
$$

因为 $\frac{\partial L}{\partial p} = 0$ ，所以 $\frac{\partial L}{\partial p} =$ 常量，即

$$
\left(m r ^ {2} + \frac {4}{3} M a ^ {2}\right) \dot {\varphi} \sin^ {2} \theta = \text {常量}
$$

因为 $\frac{\partial L}{\partial t} = 0$ ，所以 $\sum_{i} \frac{\partial L}{\partial q_i} \dot{q}_i - L =$ 常量，即

$$
\frac {1}{2} m \dot {r} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \left(m r ^ {2} + \frac {4}{3} M a ^ {2}\right) (\dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta)
$$

$$
- (M a + m r) g \mathrm{cos} \theta + \frac {1}{2} k (r - a) ^ {2} = \text {常量}
$$

9.3.22 一个质量为 $m$ 的粒子在一光滑平面上运动, 它受到平面上一固定点 $P$ 的吸引力, 力的大小与离 $P$ 点的距离的平方成反比, 比例系数为 $k$ . 用极坐标写出该粒子的拉格朗日函数和运动微分方程, 并给出两个运动积分.

解 取 P 点为极坐标系原点，

$$
\boldsymbol {F} = - \frac {k}{r ^ {3}} \boldsymbol {r}
$$

取无穷远处的势能为零，

$$
V (r) = - \int_ {\infty} ^ {r} \boldsymbol {F} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r} = \int_ {r} ^ {\infty} \boldsymbol {F} \cdot \mathrm{d} \boldsymbol {r} = \int_ {r} ^ {\infty} \left(- \frac {k}{r ^ {2}}\right) \mathrm{d} r = - \frac {k}{r}
$$

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2})
$$

$$
L = T - V = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}) + \frac {k}{r}
$$

由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L}{\partial r}\right) - \frac{\partial L}{\partial r} = 0,\quad \frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L}{\dot{\partial}\varphi}\right) - \frac{\partial L}{\partial\varphi} = 0$ ，给出粒子的运动微分方程为

$$
m (\ddot {r} - r \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}) + \frac {k}{r ^ {2}} = 0
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} (m r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi}) = 0
$$

可直接写出两个运动积分.

$$
m r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} = \text { 常量 }
$$

因为

$$
\frac {\partial L}{\partial t} = 0
$$

$$
\frac {1}{2} m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}) - \frac {k}{r} = \text {常量}
$$

9.3.23 质量为 $m_{1}, m_{2}$ 的两粒子之间的作用力为有心力, 势能为 $V(r), r$ 为相对位矢.

（1）在质心平动参考系中写出此系统的拉格朗日函数，并由此证明能量和角动量守恒；证明运动总在一个平面内，满足开普勒第二定律(r 在相等的时间内扫过相等的面积)；

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/cf59669cb08f6c78e8cdaa5cf503f44090e871a2e429559f302aa87d37bb1406.jpg)



图9.57


(2) 若势能 $V = \frac{1}{2} kr^2 (k$ 是一个正的常数), 且已知总能量 $E$ 和角动量 $J$ , 求出运动过程中 $r$ 的最大值和最小值满足的表达式.

解 由于作用力总沿着两质点的连线,质点的运动将以开始时的相对速度与连线构成的平面内运动.在质心平动参考系中取平面极坐标,原点位于质心, $r_{1}$ 、 $r_{2}$ 是两质点对质心的位矢.由质心的定义,

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/f063995b135fe5605d05716678ea0eb66fb8f59a91f6cd68993b33670cec73d7.jpg)


$$
\frac {m _ {2} ^ {2}}{2 \mu} (\dot {r} _ {2} ^ {2} + r _ {2} ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} _ {2} ^ {2}) + V \left(\frac {m _ {2}}{\mu} r _ {2}\right) = E
$$

得

$$
\frac {1}{2} \mu (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi_ {2}} ^ {2}) + V (r) = E
$$

代入前已得到的 $r^2\dot{\varphi}_2 = \frac{J}{\mu}$ 及 $V(r) = \frac{1}{2} kr^2$ 

$$
\frac {1}{2} \mu \left(\dot {r} ^ {2} + \frac {J ^ {2}}{\mu^ {2} r ^ {2}}\right) + \frac {1}{2} k r ^ {2} = E
$$

当 r 处于最大值或最小值位置时, $\dot{r}=0$ . 故 $r_{max}$ 、 $r_{min}$ 满足的方程为

$$
\frac {J ^ {2}}{2 \mu r ^ {2}} + \frac {1}{2} k r ^ {2} = E
$$

或

$$
k r ^ {4} - 2 E r ^ {2} + \frac {J ^ {2}}{\mu} = 0
$$

$$
r _ {\max} = \sqrt {\frac {1}{k} \left(E + \sqrt {E ^ {2} - \frac {k}{\mu} J ^ {2}}\right)}
$$

$$
r _ {\min} = \sqrt {\frac {1}{k} \left(E - \sqrt {E ^ {2} - \frac {k}{\mu} J ^ {2}}\right)}
$$

9.3.24 给定一个一维运动的拉格朗日函数

$$
L = \mathrm{e} ^ {\gamma_ {t}} \left(\frac {1}{2} m \dot {q} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k q ^ {2}\right)
$$

其中 $\gamma, m, k$ 均是正值常量. 写出拉格朗日方程, 说明有无可直接写出的运动常数, 并描述这一运动.

若采用广义坐标 $s$ ，它与原广义坐标 $q$ 的变换关系为 $s = \mathrm{e}^{\frac{1}{2}\gamma t} q$ ，重新写出拉格朗日函数、拉格朗日方程，回答有无可直接写出的运动常数，并说明两个解之间的关系。

解

$$
\begin{array}{l} L = \mathrm{e} ^ {\gamma t} \left(\frac {1}{2} m \dot {q} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k q ^ {2}\right) \\ \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {q}} = \mathrm{e} ^ {\gamma_ {t}} m \dot {q} \\ \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {q}}\right) = \gamma \mathrm{e} ^ {\gamma_ {t}} m \dot {q} + \mathrm{e} ^ {\gamma_ {t}} m \ddot {q} \\ \frac {\partial L}{\partial q} = - k \mathrm{e} ^ {\gamma_ {t}} q \\ m \mathrm{e} ^ {\gamma_ {t}} \ddot {q} + m \gamma \mathrm{e} ^ {\gamma_ {t}} \dot {q} + k \mathrm{e} ^ {\gamma_ {t}} q = 0 \\ m \ddot {q} + m \gamma \dot {q} + k q = 0 \end{array}
$$

因 $\frac{\partial L}{\partial q} \neq 0, \frac{\partial L}{\partial t} \neq 0$ ，无直接可写出的运动常数。

从运动微分方程可看出,它是一个常系数线性齐次的常微分方程,相应的特征方程为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & m r ^ {2} + m \gamma r + k = 0 \\ & r = \frac {- m r \pm \sqrt {m ^ {2} \gamma^ {2} - 4 m k}}{2 m} \end{array}
$$

当 $\frac{\gamma}{2} < \sqrt{\frac{k}{m}}$ 时，质点作弱阻尼振动；

当 $\frac{\gamma}{2} >\sqrt{\frac{k}{m}}$ 时，质点作过阻尼振动；

当 $\frac{\gamma}{2} = \sqrt{\frac{k}{m}}$ 时，质点的运动为临界阻尼情形.

若采用广义坐标 $s, s = \mathrm{e}^{\frac{1}{2}\gamma_t}q$ ，则

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & q = \mathrm{e} ^ {- \frac {1}{2} \gamma_ {t}} s, \dot {q} = \mathrm{e} ^ {- \frac {1}{2} \gamma_ {t}} \dot {s} - \frac {1}{2} \gamma \mathrm{e} ^ {- \frac {1}{2} \gamma_ {t}} s, \\ & L = \mathrm{e} ^ {\gamma_ {t}} \left[ \frac {1}{2} m \left(\mathrm{e} ^ {- \frac {1}{2} \gamma_ {t}} \dot {s} - \frac {1}{2} \gamma \mathrm{e} ^ {- \frac {1}{2} \gamma_ {t}} s\right) ^ {2} - \frac {k}{2} (\mathrm{e} ^ {- \frac {1}{2} \gamma_ {t}} s) ^ {2} \right] \\ & = \frac {1}{2} m \left(\dot {s} - \frac {1}{2} \gamma_ {s}\right) ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k s ^ {2} \\ & \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {s}} = m (\dot {s} - \frac {1}{2} \gamma_ {s}), \quad \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {s}}\right) = m \left(\ddot {s} - \frac {1}{2} \gamma \dot {s}\right) \\ & \frac {\partial L}{\partial s} = - \frac {1}{2} \gamma m (\dot {s} - \frac {1}{2} \gamma_ {s}) - k s = - \frac {1}{2} \gamma m \dot {s} - (k - \frac {1}{4} m \gamma^ {2}) s \\ & m (\ddot {s} - \frac {1}{2} \gamma \dot {s}) + \frac {1}{2} \gamma m \dot {s} + (k - \frac {1}{4} m \gamma^ {2}) s = 0 \\ & \ddot {s} + \left(\frac {k}{m} - \frac {\gamma^ {2}}{4}\right) s = 0 \end{array}\tag{1}
$$

因 $\frac{\partial L}{\partial t} = 0$ ，可直接写出一个运动积分

$$
\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {s}} \dot {s} - L = \text {常量}
$$

即

$$
\frac {1}{2} m \dot {s} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m \Big (\frac {k}{m} - \frac {\gamma^ {2}}{4} \Big) s ^ {2} = \text {常量}
$$

由式(1)的运动微分方程可见,如 $\frac{k}{m}>\frac{\gamma^{2}}{4}$ ,即 $\frac{\gamma}{2}<\sqrt{\frac{k}{m}}$ ,用坐标s,质点作简谐振动,用坐标q,质点作弱阻尼振动.

如 $\frac{k}{m} < \frac{\gamma^2}{4}$ , 即 $\frac{\gamma}{2} > \sqrt{\frac{k}{m}}$ , 不论用坐标 $s$ 还是用坐标 $q$ , 质点的运动都是过阻尼振动.

如 $\frac{k}{m} = \frac{\gamma^2}{4}$ , 即 $\frac{\gamma}{2} = \sqrt{\frac{k}{m}}$ , 不论用坐标 $s$ 还是用坐标 $q$ , 质点的运动都是临界阻尼情形.

9.3.25 一个质量为 m、半径为 a 的均质圆环在一个质量为 M、倾角为 $\alpha$ 、置于光滑水平面上的斜面上作无滑动滚动，运动是平面的。选择表示斜面对某固定参考点的水平

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/2ab9aba9f34e2fcfaffe82ad00204152673c76fafccffc4a9333c05139b0307a.jpg)



图9.58


距离 $x$ 和表示圆环从斜面上某参考点沿斜面滚下的距离 $s$ 为广义坐标，找出四个运动常数.

解 取图 9.58 所示的 x、s 为广义坐标.

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} M \dot {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m [ (\dot {x} + \dot {s} \cos \alpha) ^ {2} + (\dot {s} \sin \alpha) ^ {2} ] + \frac {1}{2} m a ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}
$$

圆环作纯滚动，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} - a \dot {\varphi} & = 0 \\ T & = \frac {1}{2} (M + m) \dot {x} ^ {2} + m \dot {s} ^ {2} + m \dot {x} \dot {s} \cos \alpha \\ V & = - m g s \sin \alpha \\ L & = \frac {1}{2} (M + m) \dot {x} ^ {2} + m \dot {s} ^ {2} + m \dot {x} \dot {s} \cos \alpha + m g s \sin \alpha \end{array}
$$

因为

$$
\frac {\partial L}{\partial x} = 0, \quad \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {x}} = \text {常量}
$$

即

$$
(M + m) \dot {x} + m \dot {s} \cos \alpha = \text {常量}\tag{1}
$$

因为 $\frac{\partial L}{\partial t} = 0, V = 0, T = T_2$ ，即动能为广义速度的二次齐次函数，所以

即

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\sum_ {i} \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {q} _ {i}} \dot {q} _ {i} - L = T + V = \text {常量}} \\ & {\frac {1}{2} (M + m) \dot {x} ^ {2} + m \dot {s} ^ {2} + m \dot {x} \dot {s} \cos \alpha - m g s \sin \alpha = \text {常量}} \end{array}\tag{2}
$$

由拉格朗日方程可写出系统的运动微分方程为

$$
\begin{array}{l} (M + m) \ddot {x} + m \ddot {s} \cos \alpha = 0 \\ \ddot {x} \cos \alpha + 2 \ddot {s} - g \sin \alpha = 0 \end{array}
$$

两式消去 $\ddot{x}$ , 解出 $\ddot{s}$ , 得

$$
\ddot {s} = \frac {(M + m) g \sin \alpha}{2 (M + m) - m \cos^ {2} \alpha}
$$

积分上式得

$$
\dot {s} = \frac {(M + m) g t \sin \alpha}{2 (M + m) - m \cos^ {2} \alpha} + \text { 常量 }\tag{3}
$$

由式(1)、(3)，可得第四个运动常数

$$
\dot {x} = - \frac {m g t \sin \alpha \cos \alpha}{2 (M + m) - m \cos^ {2} \alpha} + \text { 常量 }\tag{4}
$$

式(1)、(2)、(3)、(4)都是第一运动积分,对式(1)、(3)、(4)均能积分得到第二运动积分.

9.3.26 证明带电粒子在稳定电磁场中的能量积分是 $T + q\phi$ ，其中 $q$ 是带电粒子所带的电荷量， $\phi$ 是电磁场的标势， $T$ 是带电粒子的动能，

证明 $T = \frac{1}{2} m(\dot{x}^2 +\dot{y}^2 +\dot{z}^2)$ 

在稳定电磁场中,带电粒子的广义势为

$$
U = q \phi - q \boldsymbol {v} \cdot \boldsymbol {A} = q \phi - q (A _ {x} \dot {x} + A _ {y} \dot {y} + A _ {z} \dot {z})
$$

其中 A 为电磁场的矢势，

因为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {L = T - U = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2}) - q \phi + q (A _ {x} \dot {x} + A _ {y} \dot {y} + A _ {z} \dot {z})} \\ & {\frac {\partial L}{\partial t} = 0, \quad \sum_ {i} \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {q} _ {i}} \dot {q} _ {i} - L = \text {常量}} \\ & {\quad \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {x}} \dot {x} + \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {y}} \dot {y} + \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {z}} \dot {z} - L} \\ & {\quad = (m \dot {x} + q A _ {x}) \dot {x} + (m \dot {y} + q A _ {y}) \dot {y} + (m \dot {z} + q A _ {z}) \dot {z}} \\ & {\quad - \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2}) + q \phi - q (A _ {x} \dot {x} + A _ {y} \dot {y} + A _ {z} \dot {z})} \\ & {\quad = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2}) + q \phi = T + q \phi} \end{array}
$$

所以能量积分(更准确地说是广义能量积分)为

$$
T + q \phi = \text {常量}
$$

9.3.27 长 $l$ 、质量为 $M$ 的均质棒在竖直的 $xz$ 平面内运动, 其一端 $A$ 限制在直线 $z = x \tan \alpha$ 上运动 ( $\alpha$ 为直线对水平的 $x$ 轴的倾角), 用图 7.59 所示的广义坐标 $s$ 和 $\theta$ , 导出运动微分方程, 并确定作平动时的 $\theta$ 值.

解 图 9.59 中 C 为棒的质心.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/0b3ef026d8cfe143ecafc5966e4abbdfe6f20d4e42bd9a632bd773f8f7f3004a.jpg)


$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {x _ {C} = s \mathrm{cos} \alpha - \frac {l}{2} \mathrm{sin} \theta} \\ & {z _ {C} = s \mathrm{sin} \alpha + \frac {l}{2} \mathrm{cos} \theta} \\ & {\dot {x} _ {C} = \dot {s} \mathrm{cos} \alpha - \frac {l}{2} \dot {\theta} \mathrm{cos} \theta} \\ & {\dot {z} _ {C} = \dot {s} \mathrm{sin} \alpha - \frac {l}{2} \dot {\theta} \mathrm{sin} \theta} \\ & {T = \frac {1}{2} M (\dot {x} _ {C} ^ {2} + \dot {z} _ {C} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{2} \cdot \frac {1}{1 2} M l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2}} \\ & {\quad = \frac {1}{2} M [ \dot {s} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{3} l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} - l \dot {s}   \dot {\theta} \mathrm{cos} (\theta - \alpha) ]} \\ & {V = - M g z _ {C} = - M g (s \mathrm{sin} \alpha + \frac {l}{2} \mathrm{cos} \theta)} \\ & {L = \frac {1}{2} M [ \dot {s} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{3} l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} - l \dot {s}   \dot {\theta} \mathrm{cos} (\theta - \alpha) ] + M g (s \mathrm{sin} \alpha + \frac {l}{2} \mathrm{cos} \theta)} \\ & {\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {s}} = M \dot {s} - \frac {1}{2} M l \dot {\theta} \mathrm{cos} (\theta - \alpha)} \end{array}
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {s}}\right) = M \ddot {s} - \frac {1}{2} M l \ddot {\theta} \cos (\theta - \alpha) + \frac {1}{2} M l \dot {\theta} ^ {2} \sin (\theta - \alpha)
$$

$$
\frac {\partial L}{\partial s} = M g \sin \alpha
$$

$$
\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\theta}} = \frac {1}{3} M l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} - \frac {1}{2} M l \dot {s} \cos (\theta - \alpha)
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\theta}}\right) = \frac {1}{3} M l ^ {2} \ddot {\theta} - \frac {1}{2} M l \ddot {s} \cos (\theta - \alpha) + \frac {1}{2} M l \dot {s} \dot {\theta} \sin (\theta - \alpha)
$$

$$
\frac {\partial L}{\partial \theta} = \frac {1}{2} M l \dot {s} \dot {\theta} \sin (\theta - \alpha) - \frac {1}{2} M g l \sin \theta
$$

由

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {q} _ {i}}\right) - \frac {\partial L}{\partial q _ {i}} = 0
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} M \ddot {s} - \frac {1}{2} M l \ddot {\theta} \cos (\theta - \alpha) + \frac {1}{2} M l \dot {\theta} ^ {2} \sin (\theta - \alpha) - M g \sin \alpha & = 0 \\ \frac {1}{3} M l ^ {2} \ddot {\theta} - \frac {1}{2} M l \ddot {s} \cos (\theta - \alpha) + \frac {1}{2} M g l \sin \theta & = 0 \end{array}
$$

纯平动时， $\dot{\theta} = 0, \ddot{\theta} = 0$ ，代入上述两式，得

$$
\begin{array}{c} \ddot {s} - g \sin \alpha = 0 \\ \ddot {s} \cos (\theta - \alpha) - g \sin \theta = 0 \end{array}
$$

用前式消去后式中的 $\ddot{s}$ , 得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\sin \alpha \cos (\theta - \alpha) - \sin \theta = 0} \\ & {\sin \alpha (\cos \theta \cos \alpha + \sin \theta \sin \alpha) - \sin \theta = 0} \\ & {\sin \alpha \cos \alpha \cos \theta - \sin \theta (1 - \sin^ {2} \alpha) = 0} \\ & {\sin \alpha \cos \theta - \sin \theta \cos \alpha = 0} \\ & {\sin (\alpha - \theta) = 0} \\ & {\qquad \qquad \qquad \qquad \qquad \qquad \theta = \alpha   \text {或}   \pi + \alpha} \end{array}
$$

所以

9.3.28 (1) 质量为 $m$ 的均质杆两端的速度分别为 $\pmb{v}_A$ 和 $\pmb{v}_B$ , 证明其动能为

$$
T = \frac {1}{6} m (\boldsymbol {v} _ {A} ^ {2} + \boldsymbol {v} _ {A} \cdot \boldsymbol {v} _ {B} + v _ {B} ^ {2})
$$

(2) 一根质量为 $m$ 、长度为 $a$ 的均质杆, 其一端限于沿一光滑的竖直轴运动, 另一端在光滑的水平面上滑动, 设 $\theta$ 是杆与竖直向下的轴间的夹角, $\varphi$ 是杆所在竖直平面的角位移, 如 $t=0$ 时, $\theta=\frac{\pi}{6}, \dot{\theta}=0, \dot{\varphi}=\Omega$ , 证明在以后的运动中, 只要杆保持与水平面接触, 有下列关系:

$$
\dot {\theta} ^ {2} = \frac {1}{1 6} \Omega^ {2} (4 - \csc^ {2} \theta) + \frac {3 g}{2 \Omega} (\sqrt {3} - 2 \cos \theta)
$$

证明 （1）用柯尼希定理写杆的动能.

$$
\boldsymbol {v} _ {C} = \frac {1}{2} (\boldsymbol {v} _ {A} + \boldsymbol {v} _ {B})
$$

设杆长为 l，绕过质心垂直于杆的轴转动的转动惯量为

$$
I = \frac {1}{1 2} m l ^ {2}
$$

杆的角速度的大小为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\omega = \frac {| \textbf {v} _ {A} - \textbf {v} _ {B} |}{l}} \\ & {T = \frac {1}{2} m v _ {c} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} I \omega^ {2}} \\ & {\quad = \frac {1}{2} m \cdot \frac {1}{2} (\textbf {v} _ {A} + \textbf {v} _ {B}) \cdot \frac {1}{2} (\textbf {v} _ {A} + \textbf {v} _ {B}) + \frac {1}{2} \cdot \frac {1}{1 2} m l ^ {2} \frac {(\textbf {v} _ {A} - \textbf {v} _ {B})}{l} \cdot \frac {(\textbf {v} _ {A} - \textbf {v} _ {B})}{l}} \\ & {\quad = \frac {1}{8} m (v _ {A} ^ {2} + 2 \textbf {v} _ {A} \cdot \textbf {v} _ {B} + \textbf {v} _ {B} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{2 4} m (v _ {A} ^ {2} - 2 \textbf {v} _ {A} \cdot \textbf {v} _ {B} + v _ {B} ^ {2})} \\ & {\quad = \frac {1}{6} m (v _ {A} ^ {2} + \textbf {v} _ {A} \cdot \textbf {v} _ {B} + v _ {B} ^ {2})} \end{array}
$$

(2) 取图 9.60 的 xyz 坐标, 用上面证明的式子计算杆的动能,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & \boldsymbol {v} _ {A} = \dot {z} _ {A} \boldsymbol {k}, \quad \boldsymbol {v} _ {B} = \dot {r} \boldsymbol {e} _ {r} + r \dot {\varphi} \boldsymbol {e} _ {\varphi} \\ & z _ {A} = a \cos \theta , \quad r = a \sin \theta \\ & \dot {r} = a \dot {\theta} \cos \theta , \quad \dot {z} _ {A} = - a \dot {\theta} \sin \theta \\ & \boldsymbol {v} _ {A} = - a \dot {\theta} \sin \theta \boldsymbol {k} \\ & \boldsymbol {v} _ {B} = a \dot {\theta} \cos \theta \boldsymbol {e} _ {r} + a \dot {\varphi} \sin \theta \boldsymbol {e} _ {\varphi} \\ & \boldsymbol {v} _ {A} \cdot \boldsymbol {v} _ {B} = 0 \\ & T = \frac {1}{6} m (\boldsymbol {v} _ {A} ^ {2} + \boldsymbol {v} _ {A} \cdot \boldsymbol {v} _ {B} + v _ {B} ^ {2}) \\ & = \frac {1}{6} m [ (- a \dot {\theta} \sin \theta) ^ {2} + (a \dot {\theta} \cos \theta) ^ {2} + (a \dot {\varphi} \sin \theta) ^ {2} ] \\ & = \frac {1}{6} m a ^ {2} (\dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta) \\ & V = m g z _ {C} = \frac {1}{2} m g a c o s \theta \\ & L = T - V = \frac {1}{6} m a ^ {2} (\dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta) - \frac {1}{2} m g a c o s \theta \end{array}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/2355eab26735c8a6169ff631075916666ba4f23a31b5399165a2290d342eab8e.jpg)



图9.60


因为 $\frac{\partial L}{\partial \varphi} = 0, \frac{\partial L}{\partial \dot{\varphi}} =$ 常量，用初始条件： $t = 0$ 时， $\theta = \frac{\pi}{6}, \dot{\varphi} = \Omega$ ，得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \frac {1}{3} m a ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} \sin^ {2} \theta & = \frac {1}{3} m a ^ {2} \Omega \sin^ {2} \frac {\pi}{6} = \frac {1}{1 2} m a ^ {2} \Omega \\ \dot {\varphi} \sin^ {2} \theta & = \frac {1}{4} \Omega \end{array}\tag{1}
$$

因为 $\frac{\partial L}{\partial t} = 0, U = 0, T = T_2$ ( $T$ 是广义速度的二次齐次函数), 用初始条件: $t = 0$ 时, $\theta = \frac{\pi}{6}, \dot{\theta} = 0, \dot{\varphi} = \Omega$ , 写 $T + U =$ 常量为

$$
\frac {1}{6} m a ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{6} m a ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta + \frac {1}{2} m g a \cos \theta
$$

$$
\begin{array}{l} = \frac {1}{6} m a ^ {2} \Omega^ {2} \sin^ {2} \frac {\pi}{6} + \frac {1}{2} m g a \cos \frac {\pi}{6} \\ = \frac {1}{2 4} m a ^ {2} \Omega^ {2} + \frac {\sqrt {3}}{4} m g a \\ 4 a \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + 4 a \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta + 1 2 g \cos \theta = a \Omega^ {2} + 6 \sqrt {3} g \end{array}\tag{2}
$$

从(1)、(2)两式可解出 $\dot{\theta}^2$ 为

$$
\dot {\theta} ^ {2} = \frac {1}{1 6} \Omega^ {2} (4 - \csc^ {2} \theta) + \frac {3 g}{2 a} (\sqrt {3} - 2 \cos \theta)
$$

9.3.29 一个质量为 $m$ 的质点, 在重力的作用下自静止竖直下落时, 受到一个可用耗散函数 $R = \frac{1}{2} \gamma v^2$ 的阻力作用, 证明质点的最大速度为 $\frac{mg}{\gamma}$ 

证明 取 $x$ 轴竖直向下，

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} m \dot {x} ^ {2}, \quad V = - m g x
$$

$$
L = T - V = \frac {1}{2} m \dot {x} ^ {2} + m g x,
$$

$$
R = \frac {1}{2} \gamma \dot {x} ^ {2}, Q = - \frac {\partial R}{\partial \dot {x}} = - \gamma \dot {x}
$$

由

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {x}}\right) - \frac {\partial L}{\partial x} = Q
$$

$$
m \ddot {x} - m g = - \gamma \dot {x}
$$

达到最大速度时， $\dot{x} = 0$ ，代入上式得

$$
\dot {x} _ {\max} = \frac {m g}{\gamma}
$$

9.3.30 自然长度为 $l_{0}$ 、劲度系数为 $k$ 的无质量弹簧一端固定，另一端和一个质量为 $m$ 的质点相连，在重力作用下，限在一个竖直平面内运动。用 $\varphi$ 和 $\lambda = \frac{r - r_0}{r_0}$ （其中 $r_0$ 是处于

平衡状态时弹簧的长度)为广义坐标.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/a4e9b401df37291adf68a8f87fc86d8f77a0bb0bd930d1416158c69eb68d4a72.jpg)


(1) 写出拉格朗日函数；

(2) 用 $\omega_{s}^{2} = \frac{k}{m}, \omega_{p}^{2} = \frac{g}{r_{0}}$ 写出拉格朗日方程；

(3) 当 $\lambda$ 和 $\varphi$ 很小, 初始条件: $t = 0$ 时, $\varphi = 0, \lambda = A, \dot{\varphi} = \omega_p B, \dot{\lambda} = 0$ , 其中 $A, B$ 是常量, 求运动的最低级近似解;


图9.61


（4）讨论运动的高一级近似，在什么条件下， $\lambda$ 的运动将发生共振？这在物理上能实现吗？

解 (1)

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2})
$$

$$
\lambda = \frac {r - r _ {0}}{r _ {0}}, \quad r = r _ {0} \lambda + r _ {0}, \quad \dot {r} = r _ {0} \dot {\lambda}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & T = \frac {1}{2} m r _ {0} ^ {2} [ \dot {\lambda} ^ {2} + (\lambda + 1) ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} ] \\ & V = - m g r \cos \varphi + m g r _ {0} + \frac {1}{2} k (r - l _ {0}) ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k (r _ {0} - l _ {0}) ^ {2} \\ & \quad = - m g (r _ {0} \lambda + r _ {0}) \cos \varphi + m g r _ {0} + \frac {1}{2} k (r _ {0} \lambda + r _ {0} - l _ {0}) ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k (r _ {0} - l _ {0}) ^ {2} \\ & \quad = - m g (r _ {0} \lambda + r _ {0}) \cos \varphi + m g r _ {0} + \frac {1}{2} k r _ {0} ^ {2} \lambda^ {2} + k r _ {0} (r _ {0} - l _ {0}) \lambda \\ & L = T - V = \frac {1}{2} m r _ {0} ^ {2} [ \dot {\lambda} ^ {2} + (\lambda + 1) ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} ] + m g (r _ {0} \lambda + r _ {0}) \cos \varphi \\ & \quad - m g r _ {0} - \frac {1}{2} k r _ {0} ^ {2} \lambda^ {2} - k r _ {0} (r _ {0} - l _ {0}) \lambda \\ & \quad = \frac {1}{2} m r _ {0} ^ {2} [ \dot {\lambda} ^ {2} + (\lambda + 1) ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} ] + m g (r _ {0} \lambda + r _ {0}) \cos \varphi - m g r _ {0} - \frac {1}{2} k r _ {0} ^ {2} \lambda^ {2} - m g r _ {0} \lambda \end{array}
$$

这里用了平衡条件：

$$
k (r _ {0} - l _ {0}) = m g
$$

$$
