\begin{array}{r l} {(2) \text {由} \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \bigg (\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\lambda}} \bigg) - \frac {\partial L}{\partial \lambda} = 0 \text {及} \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \bigg (\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\varphi}} \bigg) - \frac {\partial L}{\partial \varphi} = 0 \text {得}} \\ & {\qquad m r _ {0} ^ {2} \ddot {\lambda} + m r _ {0} ^ {2} (\lambda + 1) \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + k r _ {0} ^ {2} \lambda + m g r _ {0} (1 - \cos \varphi) = 0} \\ & {\qquad m r _ {0} ^ {2} (\lambda + 1) ^ {2} \ddot {\varphi} + 2 m r _ {0} ^ {2} (\lambda + 1) \dot {\lambda} \dot {\varphi} + m g r _ {0} (\lambda + 1) \sin \varphi = 0} \end{array}
$$

用 $\omega_s^2 = \frac{k}{m},\omega_p^2 = \frac{g}{r_0}$ ，上述方程可写为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \ddot {\lambda} - (\lambda + 1) \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \omega_ {s} ^ {2} \lambda + \omega_ {p} ^ {2} (1 - \cos \varphi) & = 0 \\ (\lambda + 1) \ddot {\varphi} + 2 \dot {\lambda} \dot {\varphi} + \omega_ {p} ^ {2} \sin \varphi & = 0 \end{array}
$$

（3）当 $\lambda, \varphi$ 都很小， $\lambda, \varphi, \dot{\lambda}, \dot{\varphi}, \ddot{\lambda}, \ddot{\varphi}$ 均为一级小量时，取最低级近似，只保留一级小量，运动微分方程简化为

$$
\begin{array}{l} \ddots \\ \dot {\lambda} + \omega_ {s} ^ {2} \lambda = 0 \\ \ddots \\ \dot {\varphi} + \omega_ {p} ^ {2} \varphi = 0 \end{array}
$$

在给定的初始条件： $\lambda=0$ 时， $\lambda=A,\dot{\lambda}=0,\varphi=0,\dot{\varphi}=\omega_{p}B$ ，可解出

$$
\begin{array}{l} \lambda = A \cos \omega_ {s} t \\ \varphi = B \sin \omega_ {p} t \end{array}
$$

(4) 求高一级近似解,保留二级小量,运动微分方程为

$$
\ddot {\lambda} + \omega_ {s} ^ {2} \lambda = \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} \omega_ {p} ^ {2} \varphi^ {2}
$$

$$
(\lambda + 1) \ddot {\varphi} + 2 \dot {\lambda} \dot {\varphi} + \omega_ {p} ^ {2} \varphi = 0
$$

讨论 $\lambda$ 的运动, 将 $\varphi$ 的最低级近似解代入

$$
\ddot {\lambda} + \omega_ {s} ^ {2} \lambda = B ^ {2} \omega_ {p} ^ {2} \cos^ {2} \omega_ {p} t - \frac {1}{2} \omega_ {p} ^ {2} B ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \omega_ {p} t
$$

用 $\cos^2\omega_p t = \frac{1}{2} (1 + \cos 2\omega_p t), \sin^2\omega_p t = \frac{1}{2} (1 - \cos 2\omega_p t)$ ，上式可改写为

$$
\ddot {\lambda} + \omega_ {s} ^ {2} \lambda = \frac {1}{4} B ^ {2} \omega_ {p} ^ {2} (1 + 3 \cos 2 \omega_ {p} t)
$$

这是一个受迫的振动方程. 当强迫力的角频率等于振动系统的固有频率即 $2\omega_{p} = \omega_{s}$ 时, $\lambda$ 的运动会发生共振.

在物理上是不可能出现共振的, 解上述微分方程, 解中将包含一项与时间成正比的项, 随着时间的推移趋于无穷大, 故这种项被称为长期项, 趋于无穷, 机械能不守恒, 而此系统在保守力场中运动, 应遵从机械能守恒, 因此在物理上不可能出现共振现象. 小的“强迫力”的存在并不改变运动是周期运动这一特性, 但其周期不再是 $\frac{2\pi}{\omega_s}$ , 而有一个小的偏离. 考虑到 $\omega = \omega_s + \delta (\delta$ 为小量), 长期项可以不出现.

9.3.31 两个质量均为 $m$ 的质点各用一根劲度系数为 $k$ 、原长为 $l$ 的弹簧连到质量为 $M$ 、长度为 $L$ 的均质细杆的中点，并限在细杆上做两根弹簧的长度保持相等的运动，细杆可以以任意方式自由运动，此系统是空间的孤立系统，列出运动微分方程并求解之（到作积分为止）。定性地描述系统的运动。

解 取静参考系,用两个坐标系,一个静止的直角坐标系 Oxyz,一个质心平动坐标系 $O'x'y'z'$ , $O'$ 是系统的质心,用 $O'$ 点的坐标 x、y、z 和一个质点在动坐标系中的球坐标 r、 $\theta$ 、 $\varphi$ 作为广义坐标,如图 9.62 所示.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/f2f6c2854a5e6c90f9787914a3141163230add49ebb4715f1d87b98fe725b793.jpg)



图9.62


计算细杆在质心平动参考系中的动能时选三个惯量主轴, 其单位矢量 $e_{1}$ 、 $e_{2}$ 、 $e_{3}$ 分别与质点 1 所在位置的 $e_{r}$ 、 $e_{\theta}$ 、 $e_{\varphi}$ 平行, 关于 $O'$ 的三个主转动惯量为

$$
I _ {1} = 0, I _ {2} = I _ {3} = \frac {1}{1 2} M L ^ {2}
$$

细杆的角速度为

$$
\omega = \dot {\varphi} \cos \theta e _ {1} - \dot {\varphi} \sin \theta e _ {2} + \dot {\theta} e _ {3}
$$

细杆的转动动能,即细杆在其质心平动参考系中的动能为

$$
\frac {1}{2} I _ {1} \omega_ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} I _ {2} \omega_ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} I _ {3} \omega_ {3} ^ {2} = \frac {1}{2 4} M L ^ {2} (\dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta)
$$

系统的动能为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} T & = \frac {1}{2} (M + 2 m) (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2}) + 2 \cdot \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {r} ^ {2} \\ & \quad + r ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta) + \frac {1}{2 4} M L ^ {2} (\dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta) \\ & = \frac {1}{2} (M + 2 m) (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2}) + m \dot {r} ^ {2} \\ & \quad + (m r ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 4} M L ^ {2}) (\dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta) \end{array}
$$

系统的势能为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & V = 2 \cdot \frac {1}{2} k (r - l) ^ {2} = k (r - l) ^ {2} \\ & L = T - V = \frac {1}{2} (M + 2 m) (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2}) + m \dot {r} ^ {2} \\ & \quad + (m r ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 4} M L ^ {2}) (\dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta) - k (r - l) ^ {2} \end{array}
$$

由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L}{\partial q_i}\right) - \frac{\partial L}{\partial q_i} = 0$ ，可得运动微分方程或其运动积分为

$$
(M + 2 m) \dot {x} = \text {常量} C _ {1}, \qquad \dot {x} = \frac {C _ {1}}{M + 2 m}
$$

$$
(M + 2 m) \dot {y} = \text {常量} C _ {2}, \qquad \dot {y} = \frac {C _ {2}}{M + 2 m}
$$

$$
(M + 2 m) \dot {z} = \text {常量} C _ {3}, \qquad \dot {z} = \frac {C _ {3}}{M + 2 m}
$$

$$
2 (m r ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 4} M L ^ {2}) \dot {\varphi} \sin^ {2} \theta = \text {常数} C _ {4}
$$

$$
\dot {\varphi} = \frac {C _ {4}}{2 (m r ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 4} M L ^ {2}) \sin^ {2} \theta}\tag{1}
$$

$$
m \ddot {r} - m r (\dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta) + k (r - l) = 0\tag{2}
$$

$$
\left(m r ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 4} M L ^ {2}\right) \ddot {\theta} + 2 m r \dot {r} \dot {\theta} - \left(m r ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 4} M L ^ {2}\right) \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \sin \theta \cos \theta = 0\tag{3}
$$

因为 $\frac{\partial L}{\partial t} = 0, V = 0, T = T_2$ （动能是广义速度的二次齐次函数），所以

$$
\begin{array}{r l} T + V & = \frac {1}{2} (M + 2 m) (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2}) + m \dot {r} ^ {2} \\ & \quad + (m r ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 4} M L ^ {2}) (\dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta) + k (r - l) ^ {2} = \text {常数} C _ {5} \end{array}
$$

因为 $\dot{x},\dot{y},\dot{z}$ 均为常量，所以

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {m \dot {r} ^ {2} + (m r ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 4} M L ^ {2}) (\dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta) = \text {常量} C _ {6}} \\ & {\quad \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta = \frac {1}{m r ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 4} M L ^ {2}} (C _ {6} - m \dot {r} ^ {2})} \end{array}\tag{4}
$$

将式(4)代入式(2)，并用 $\ddot{r} = \frac{\mathrm{d}\dot{r}}{\mathrm{d}r}\dot{r}$ ，可得

$$
\frac {1}{2} m \mathrm{d} \dot {r} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} m \frac {C _ {6} - m \dot {r} ^ {2}}{m r ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 4} M L ^ {2}} \mathrm{d} r ^ {2} = - k (r - l) \mathrm{d} r
$$

可以找到积分因子 $mr^2 + \frac{1}{24} ML^2$ ，作上式积分，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & \frac {1}{2} m (m r ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 4} M L ^ {2}) \mathrm{d} \dot {r} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} m (C _ {6} - m \dot {r} ^ {2}) \mathrm{d} r ^ {2} \\ & = - k (r - l) (m r ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2 4} M L ^ {2}) \mathrm{d} r \end{array}
$$

等号左边是 $\frac{1}{2} m\left(mr^2 +\frac{1}{24} ML^2\right)\dot{r}^2 -\frac{1}{2} mc_6r^2$ 的全微分，因原则上可从上式得到 $\dot{r}$ 与 $r$ 的函数关系，进而积出 $r = r(t)$ 

将 $r = r(t)$ 代入式(1)，积分可得 $\varphi = \varphi(t)$ ，再将 $r = r(t)$ 和 $\varphi = \varphi(t)$ 代入式(4)，可积出 $\theta = \theta(t)$ .

关于系统的运动作定性描述,可以确定的讲,质心做匀速直线运动,两质点沿杆相对于中点 $O'$ 做对称的运动,系统对 $O'$ 点的角动量是守恒的.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/1346fc0911460e1032c0c48863ed628eca8c345838b3a4f16c7bfe8b2f0beb61.jpg)


9.3.32 将一根很长的、可忽略质量的细管安上轴，可以无摩擦地绕轴在水平面内转动，一根长为 $l$ 、质量为 $M$ 的细杆无摩擦地在管内滑动（见图9.63），写出系统的拉格朗日方程；开始杆的中心位于转轴，细管以角速度 $\omega_0$ 旋转，证明杆在这个位置是不稳定的。将它稍微扰动一下，描述以后的运动，问经过很长时间之后，杆的径向速度和角速度各为多少（假定细管非常长，杆仍在管中）？

图9.63

解 取图 9.63 中的 x 和 $\varphi$ 为广义坐标，

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} M \left(\dot {x} ^ {2} + x ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}\right) + \frac {1}{2} \times \frac {1}{1 2} M l ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} = \frac {1}{2} M \dot {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} M \left(x ^ {2} + \frac {1}{1 2} M l ^ {2}\right) \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}
$$

$$
V = 0
$$

$$
L = T - V = \frac {1}{2} M \dot {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} M (x ^ {2} + \frac {1}{1 2} M l ^ {2}) \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}
$$

拉格朗日方程为

$$
\ddot {x} - x \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} = 0\tag{1}
$$

$$
M (x ^ {2} + \frac {1}{1 2} l ^ {2}) \dot {\varphi} = C (\text {常量})\tag{2}
$$

由初始条件: t=0 时, x=0, $\dot{x}=0$ , $\varphi=0$ , $\dot{\varphi}=\omega_{0}$ , 定出 $C=\frac{1}{12}Ml^{2}\omega_{0}$ , 代入式(2), 得

所以

$$
\dot {\varphi} = \frac {l ^ {2} \omega_ {0}}{1 2 x ^ {2} + l ^ {2}}\tag{3}
$$

将式(3)代入式(1)，

$$
\ddot {x} = \frac {l ^ {4} \omega_ {0} ^ {2} x}{1 2 x ^ {2} + l ^ {2}}\tag{4}
$$

用 $\ddot{x} = \frac{\mathrm{d}\dot{x}}{\mathrm{d}x}\dot{x} = \frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}\dot{x}^2}{\mathrm{d}x}$ ，上式可改写为

$$
\mathrm{d} \dot {x} ^ {2} = \frac {2 l ^ {4} \omega_ {0} ^ {2} x \mathrm{d} x}{(1 2 x ^ {2} + l ^ {2}) ^ {2}} = \frac {l ^ {4} \omega_ {0} ^ {2} \mathrm{d} x ^ {2}}{(1 2 x ^ {2} + l ^ {2}) ^ {2}}
$$

两边积分,用初始条件:x=0 时, $\dot{x}=0$ ,得

$$
\dot {x} ^ {2} = l ^ {2} \omega_ {0} ^ {2} \frac {x ^ {2}}{1 2 x ^ {2} + l ^ {2}}\tag{5}
$$

由式(1)可见,位于 $x\neq0$ 处,原静止的杆,因 $\ddot{x}\neq0$ ,将获得 $\dot{x};x<0$ 时, $\ddot{x}<0$ ,则 $\dot{x}<0$ .反之, $x>0$ 时, $\ddot{x}>0$ ,则 $\dot{x}>0$ .都将向远离平衡位置的方向运动.如在x=0处,给予一个微小的速度 $\dot{x}$ ,则x将不等于零,有上述远离平衡位置的运动,说明x=0是一个不稳定平衡位置.

在 $x = 0$ 的静止状态，给予一个微扰，可用式(3)、(5)，很长时间以后，不论 $x \to \infty$ 还是 $x \to -\infty, \dot{\varphi} \to 0, |\dot{x}| \to \frac{l\omega_0}{\sqrt{12}} = \frac{l\omega_0}{2\sqrt{3}}$ ，结论都是经过很长时间，杆的径向速度趋于 $\frac{l\omega_0}{2\sqrt{3}}$ 角速度趋于0.

9.3.33 一质量为 $M$ 的木块和一个半径为 $a$ 的无质量的竖直的圆周轨道刚性相连，一起置于光滑的水平桌面上，如图9.64所示，一个质量为 $m$ 的质点沿圆周轨道做无摩擦的运动.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/0c1ac2c27e88d6f770460d3396cee8d21e9ed08a367e0fcdd8cc9ced2577b61b.jpg)



图9.64


(1) 以 $\theta$ 为一个坐标, 写出拉格朗日函数;

(2) 找出运动微分方程；

(3) 在小角度极限下, 求 $\theta(t)$ .

解 在质点运动的竖直平面内, 沿水平方向取 $x$ 轴, 向左为 $x$ 轴正向. 以圆周轨道的中心的 $x$ 坐标与图中的 $\theta$ 为广义坐标.

(1) 

$$
\begin{array}{r l} T & = \frac {1}{2} M \dot {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m [ (\dot {x} + a \dot {\theta} \cos \theta) ^ {2} + (a \dot {\theta} \sin \theta) ^ {2} ] \\ & = \frac {1}{2} (M + m) \dot {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m (a ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + 2 a \dot {x} \dot {\theta} \cos \theta) \\ & V = - m g a \cos \theta \end{array}
$$

$$
L = T - V = \frac {1}{2} (M + m) \dot {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m \left(a ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + 2 a \dot {x} \dot {\theta} \cos \theta\right) + m g a \cos \theta
$$

(2) 

$$
\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {x}} = (M + m) \dot {x} + m a \dot {\theta} \cos \theta
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {x}}\right) = (M + m) \ddot {x} + m a \ddot {\theta} \cos \theta - m a \dot {\theta} ^ {2} \sin \theta
$$

$$
\frac {\partial L}{\partial x} = 0
$$

$$
\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\theta}} = m a ^ {2} \dot {\theta} + m a \dot {x} \cos \theta
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\theta}}\right) = m a ^ {2} \ddot {\theta} + m a \ddot {x} \cos \theta - m a \dot {x} \dot {\theta} \sin \theta
$$

$$
\frac {\partial L}{\partial \theta} = - m a \dot {x} \dot {\theta} \sin \theta - m g a \sin \theta
$$

由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L}{\partial q_i}\right) - \frac{\partial L}{\partial q_i} = 0$ 得运动微分方程为

$$
(M + m) \ddot {x} + m a \ddot {\theta} \cos \theta - m a \dot {\theta} ^ {2} \sin \theta = 0
$$

$$
a \ddot {\theta} + \ddot {x} \cos \theta + g \sin \theta = 0
$$

(3) 质点在 $\theta = 0$ 附近的运动, $\theta, \dot{\theta}, \ddot{\theta}$ 均为小量, $\cos \theta \approx 1$ , $\sin \theta \approx \theta$ .

运动微分方程可简化为

$$
(M + m) \ddot {x} + m a \ddot {\theta} = 0
$$

$$
a \ddot {\theta} + \ddot {x} + g \theta = 0
$$

消去 $\ddot{x}$ ，可得

$$
\frac {M a}{M + m} \ddot {\theta} + g \theta = 0
$$

其解为

$$
\theta = A \cos (\omega t + \alpha)
$$

其中 $A, \alpha$ 由初始条件确定，

$$
\omega = \sqrt {\frac {(M + m) g}{M a}}
$$

9.3.34 质量为 $M$ 、半径为 $R$ 的两个相同的均质圆盘用三根相同的扭杆支承，如图9.65所示。扭杆的恢复力矩为 $\tau = -k\theta (k$ 为适用于此 $l$ 和 $\theta$ 的扭转常数），圆盘可围绕扭杆的竖直轴自由转动， $\theta_{1}, \theta_{2}$ 分别表示两盘偏离平衡位置的角位移，忽略扭杆的转动惯量。初始条件为 $\theta_{1}(0) = 0, \theta_{2}(0) = 0, \dot{\theta}_{1}(0) = 0, \dot{\theta}_{2}(0) = \Omega$ （常量）。要使圆盘1得到系统的全部动能需要多长时间？可以保留隐函数形式。

解 设 I 为每个圆盘绕转轴的转动惯量, 系统的拉格朗日函数为

$$
L = \frac {1}{2} I (\dot {\theta} _ {1} ^ {2} + \dot {\theta} _ {2} ^ {2}) - \frac {1}{2} k (\theta_ {1} ^ {2} + \theta_ {2} ^ {2}) - \frac {1}{2} k (\theta_ {1} - \theta_ {2}) ^ {2}
$$

由拉格朗日方程得

$$
I \ddot {\theta} _ {1} + 2 k \theta_ {1} - k \theta_ {2} = 0\tag{1}
$$

$$
I \ddot {\theta} _ {2} + 2 k \theta_ {2} - k \theta_ {1} = 0\tag{2}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/3b549208af4b8ba2170cc614f21072ac1dc77a52dd3ac6681155da8e2b2d497c.jpg)



图9.65


式(1)+式(2)和式(1)-式(2)得

$$
\begin{array}{l} I (\ddot {\theta} _ {1} + \ddot {\theta} _ {2}) + k (\theta_ {1} + \theta_ {2}) = 0 \\ I (\ddot {\theta} _ {1} - \ddot {\theta} _ {2}) + 3 k (\theta_ {1} - \theta_ {2}) = 0 \end{array}
$$

解分别为

$$
\begin{array}{l} \theta_ {1} + \theta_ {2} = A \sin \left(\sqrt {\frac {k}{I} t + \alpha}\right) \\ \theta_ {3} - \theta_ {2} = B \sin \left(\sqrt {\frac {3 k}{I}} t + \beta\right) \end{array}
$$

初始条件为 $t = 0$ 时， $\theta_{1} + \theta_{2} = 0,\dot{\theta}_{1} + \dot{\theta}_{2} = \Omega ,\theta_{1} - \theta_{2} = 0,\dot{\theta}_{1} - \dot{\theta}_{2} = -\Omega$ ，定出

$$
A = \Omega \sqrt {\frac {I}{k}}, \quad \alpha = 0
$$

$$
B = - \Omega \sqrt {\frac {I}{3 k}}, \quad \beta = 0
$$

$$
\theta_ {1} + \theta_ {2} = \Omega \sqrt {\frac {I}{k}} \sin \left(\sqrt {\frac {k}{I}} t\right)
$$

$$
\theta_ {1} - \theta_ {2} = - \Omega \sqrt {\frac {I}{3 k}} \sin \left(\sqrt {\frac {3 k}{I}} t\right)
$$

从上述两式解出

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \theta_ {2} & = \frac {1}{2} \Omega \left[ \sqrt {\frac {I}{k}} \sin \left(\sqrt {\frac {k}{I} t}\right) + \sqrt {\frac {I}{3 k}} \sin \left(\sqrt {\frac {3 k}{I} t}\right) \right] \\ & \dot {\theta} _ {2} = \frac {1}{2} \Omega \left[ \cos \left(\sqrt {\frac {k}{I} t}\right) + \cos \left(\sqrt {\frac {3 k}{I} t}\right) \right] \end{array}
$$

当 $\dot{\theta}_2 = 0$ 时，圆盘1的动能是系统的全部动能，此时刻 $t$ 满足的关系为

$$
\cos \left(\sqrt {\frac {k}{I} t}\right) = - \cos \left(\sqrt {\frac {3 k}{I} t}\right)
$$

其中 $I = \frac{1}{2} MR^2$ 

要指出的是系统的机械能是守恒的,系统的动能不守恒,满足上述关系的 t 时刻,圆盘 1 具有系统的全部动能,既不是系统的最大动能,也不是圆盘 1 的最大动能.

9.3.35 长 $2L$ 、质量 $M$ 的均匀细杆用一根长 $l$ 、质量不计不可伸长的绳子拴在固定的钉子上。如图 9.66 所示。在杆的自由端加一水平的恒力 $F$ ，写出这个系统的拉格朗日函数和运动微分方程； $t=0$ 时，系统从下垂位置的静止状态开始运动，求在非常短的时间内（所有角度都很小）绳子和杆与竖直方向的夹角。

解 因开始时绳子和杆在竖直位置处于静止状态， $F$ 是水平的，系统将在此竖直平面内运动，取图9.67的 $xy$ 坐标，并用绳、杆与竖直线的夹角 $\theta, \varphi$ 为广义坐标，杆的质心的坐标为 $(l\sin \theta + L\sin \varphi, l\cos \theta + L\cos \varphi)$ ，质心速度为 $(l\dot{\theta}\cos \theta + L\dot{\varphi}\cos \varphi, -l\dot{\theta}\sin \theta - L\dot{\varphi}\sin \varphi), F$ 的作用点的坐标为 $(l\sin \theta + 2L\sin \varphi, l\cos \theta + 2L\cos \varphi)$ 

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/b4c50cbb4936d7640e43b0f2052210f852e613c9feab3eb6c8ba532107c039aa.jpg)



图9.66


![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/f0beee30153c062e250f76e2f62e40fb3312a647083d4e0f8fc68eea3919ee55.jpg)



图9.67


$$
\begin{array}{r l} & T = \frac {1}{2} M [ (l \dot {\theta} \cos \theta + L \dot {\varphi} \cos \varphi) ^ {2} + (- l \dot {\theta} \sin \theta - L \dot {\varphi} \sin \varphi) ^ {2} ] \\ & \quad + \frac {1}{2} \times \frac {1}{1 2} M (2 L) ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \\ & \quad = \frac {1}{2} M l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \frac {2}{3} M L ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + M l L \dot {\theta} \dot {\varphi} \cos (\theta - \varphi) \\ & V = - M g (l \cos \theta + L \cos \varphi) - F (l \cos \theta + 2 L \cos \varphi) \\ & L = T - V = \frac {1}{2} M l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \frac {2}{3} M L ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + M l L \dot {\theta} \dot {\varphi} \cos (\theta - \varphi) \\ & \quad + M g (l \cos \theta + L \cos \varphi) + F (l \sin \theta + 2 L \sin \varphi) \end{array}
$$

由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L}{\partial q_i}\right) - \frac{\partial L}{\partial q_i} = 0$ 得运动微分方程为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & M l \ddot {\theta} + M L \ddot {\varphi} \cos (\theta - \varphi) + M L \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \sin (\theta - \varphi) + M g \sin \theta - F \cos \theta = 0 \\ & \frac {4}{3} M L \ddot {\varphi} + M l \ddot {\theta} \cos (\theta - \varphi) - M l \dot {\theta} ^ {2} \sin (\theta - \varphi) + M g \sin \varphi - 2 F \cos \varphi = 0 \end{array}
$$

仅考虑在 t=0 以后非常短的时间内的运动，在此期间， $\theta$ 、 $\varphi$ 、 $\dot{\theta}$ 、 $\dot{\varphi}$ 、 $\ddot{\theta}$ 、 $\ddot{\varphi}$ 均为一级小量，略去二级及二级以上小量，运动微分方程可简化为

$$
M l \ddot {\theta} + M L \ddot {\varphi} + M g \theta - F = 0.
$$

$$
\frac {4}{3} M L \ddot {\varphi} + M l \ddot {\theta} + M g \varphi - 2 F = 0
$$

显然, $\theta=\frac{F}{Mg},\varphi=\frac{2F}{Mg}$ 为非齐次微分方程的特解.

下面求齐次微分方程

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & M l \ddot {\theta} + M L \ddot {\varphi} + M g \theta = 0 \\ & \frac {4}{3} M L \ddot {\varphi} + M l \ddot {\theta} + M g \varphi = 0 \end{array}
$$

的通解. 令

$$
\theta = u _ {1} \mathrm{e} ^ {\mathrm{i} \omega t}, \quad \varphi = u _ {2} \mathrm{e} ^ {\mathrm{i} \omega t}
$$

代入齐次微分方程得

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} (- l \omega^ {2} + g) u _ {1} - L \omega^ {2} u _ {2} = 0 \\ - l \omega^ {2} u _ {1} + \left(- \frac {4}{3} L \omega^ {2} + g\right) u _ {2} = 0 \end{array} \right.\tag{1}
$$

$u_{1}, u_{2}$ 要得非零解，必须系数行列式等于零，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\left| \begin{array}{c c} {- l \omega^ {2} + g} & {- L \omega^ {2}} \\ {- l \omega^ {2}} & {- \frac {4}{3} L \omega^ {2} + g} \end{array} \right| = 0} \\ & {\omega_ {1, 2} ^ {2} = \frac {1}{2 l L} (3 l + 4 L \pm \sqrt {9 l ^ {2} + 1 2 l L + 1 6 L ^ {2}}) g} \end{array}
$$

其中 $\omega_{1}^{2}$ 取“+”号， $\omega_{2}^{2}$ 取“-”号.

将 $\omega_1^2$ 代入方程组式(1)，解得

$$
u _ {1} ^ {(1)} = 1, \quad u _ {2} ^ {(1)} = - \frac {1}{6 L} (3 l - 4 L + \sqrt {9 l ^ {2} + 1 2 l L + 1 6 L ^ {2}})
$$

将 $\omega_{2}^{2}$ 代入方程组式(1)，解得

$$
u _ {1} ^ {(2)} = 1, u _ {2} ^ {(2)} = - \frac {1}{6 L} (3 l - 4 L - \sqrt {9 l ^ {2} + 1 2 l L + 1 6 L ^ {2}})
$$

非齐次微分方程的通解可写为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\theta = A _ {1} \cos (\omega_ {1} t + \alpha_ {1}) + A _ {2} \cos (\omega_ {2} t + \alpha_ {2}) + \frac {F}{M g}} \\ & {\varphi = - A _ {1} \frac {1}{6 L} (3 l - 4 L + \sqrt {9 l ^ {2} + 1 2 l L + 1 6 L ^ {2}}) \cos (\omega_ {1} t + \alpha_ {1})} \\ & {- A _ {2} \frac {1}{6 L} (3 l - 4 L - \sqrt {9 l ^ {2} + 1 2 l L + 1 6 L ^ {2}}) \cos (\omega_ {2} t + \alpha_ {2}) + \frac {2 F}{M g}} \end{array}
$$

由初始条件: t=0 时, $\theta=0$ , $\varphi=0$ , $\dot{\theta}=0$ , $\dot{\varphi}=0$ 定出

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\alpha_ {1} = \alpha_ {2} = 0} \\ & {A _ {1} = (8 L + 3 l - \sqrt {9 l ^ {2} + 1 2 l L + 1 6 L ^ {2}}) F / 2 M g \sqrt {9 l ^ {2} + 1 2 l L + 1 6 L ^ {2}}} \\ & {A _ {2} = - (8 L + 3 l + \sqrt {9 l ^ {2} + 1 2 l L + 1 6 L ^ {2}}) F / 2 M g \sqrt {9 l ^ {2} + 1 2 l L + 1 6 L ^ {2}}} \end{array}
$$

9.3.36 考虑一个双星系统.

(1) 用两颗星的笛卡儿坐标写出系统的拉格朗日函数；

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/af724051b0b406a6671056ef0f1038a33a98bd2af160353440f7078d0fd8100d.jpg)



图9.68


(2) 证明其势能是坐标的 $(-1)$ 次齐次函数, 即

$$
V (\alpha \boldsymbol {r} _ {1}, \alpha \boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) = \alpha^ {- 1} V (\boldsymbol {r} _ {1}, \boldsymbol {r} _ {2})
$$

其中 $\alpha$ 是任意实数；

（3）作一个变换，它保持由拉格朗日函数得到的运动微分方程不变，由此找出联系转动周期和它的轨道有关尺寸的开普勒第三定律.

解（1）设两星的质量分别为 $m_{1}, m_{2}$ ，在静止的笛卡儿坐标中的位矢分别为 $\pmb{r}_{1} = x_{1}\pmb{i} + y_{1}\pmb{j} + z_{1}\pmb{k}, \pmb{r}_{2} = x_{2}\pmb{i} + y_{2}\pmb{j} + z_{2}\pmb{k}$ ，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & T = \frac {1}{2} m _ {1} (\dot {x} _ {1} ^ {2} + \dot {y} _ {1} ^ {2} + \dot {z} _ {1} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{2} m _ {2} (\dot {x} _ {2} ^ {2} + \dot {y} _ {2} ^ {2} + \dot {z} _ {2} ^ {2}) \\ & V = - \frac {G m _ {1} m _ {2}}{\left[ (x _ {2} - x _ {1}) ^ {2} + (y _ {2} - y _ {1}) ^ {2} + (z _ {2} - z _ {1}) ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2}} \\ & L = T - V = \frac {1}{2} m _ {1} (\dot {x} _ {1} ^ {2} + \dot {y} _ {1} ^ {2} + \dot {z} _ {1} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{2} m _ {2} (\dot {x} _ {2} ^ {2} + \dot {y} _ {2} ^ {2} + \dot {z _ {2}} ^ {2}) \\ & \quad + \frac {G m _ {1} m _ {2}}{\left[ (x _ {2} - x _ {1}) ^ {2} + (y _ {2} - y _ {1}) ^ {2} + (z _ {2} - z _ {1}) ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2}} \\ & V (\alpha r _ {1}, \alpha r _ {2}) = \frac {G m _ {1} m _ {2}}{\left[ (\alpha x _ {2} - \alpha x _ {1}) ^ {2} + (\alpha y _ {2} - \alpha y _ {1}) ^ {2} + (\alpha z _ {2} - \alpha z _ {1}) ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2}} \\ & \quad = \frac {1}{\alpha} \left\{- \frac {G m _ {1} m _ {2}}{\left[ (x _ {2} - x _ {1}) ^ {2} + (y _ {2} - y _ {1}) ^ {2} + (z _ {2} - z _ {1}) ^ {2} \right] ^ {1 / 2}} \right\} \\ & \quad = \frac {1}{\alpha} V (r _ {1}, r _ {2}) \end{array}\tag{2}
$$

(3) 取双星系统的质心平动参考系, $r_{1}$ 、 $r_{2}$ 分别表示两星对质心的位矢.由质心的定义,

$$
m _ {1} \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} + m _ {2} \boldsymbol {r} _ {2} = 0
$$

设 $m_{1}$ 星对 $m_{2}$ 星的位矢为 $\pmb{r}$ , 则

$$
\begin{array}{l} \boldsymbol {r} = \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} - (- \boldsymbol {r} _ {2}) = \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} + \boldsymbol {r} _ {2} \\ \boldsymbol {r} _ {1} = \frac {m _ {2}}{m _ {1} + m _ {2}} \boldsymbol {r}, \boldsymbol {r} _ {2} = - \frac {m _ {1}}{m _ {1} + m _ {2}} \boldsymbol {r} \\ T = \frac {1}{2} m _ {1} | \dot {\boldsymbol {r}} _ {1} | ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m _ {2} | \dot {\boldsymbol {r}} _ {2} | ^ {2} \\ = \frac {1}{2} m _ {1} \left(\frac {m _ {2}}{m _ {1} + m _ {2}}\right) ^ {2} | \dot {\boldsymbol {r}} | ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m _ {2} \left(\frac {- m _ {1}}{m _ {1} + m _ {2}}\right) ^ {2} | \dot {\boldsymbol {r}} | ^ {2} \\ = \frac {1}{2} \frac {m _ {1} m _ {2}}{m _ {1} + m _ {2}} | \dot {\boldsymbol {r}} | ^ {2} \\ V = - \frac {G m _ {1} m _ {2}}{r} \\ L = T - V = \frac {1}{2} \frac {m _ {1} m _ {2}}{m _ {1} + m _ {2}} | \dot {\boldsymbol {r}} | ^ {2} + \frac {G m _ {1} m _ {2}}{r} \end{array}
$$

$$
= \frac {m _ {2}}{m _ {1} + m _ {2}} \left[ \frac {1}{2} m _ {1} | \dot {\boldsymbol {r}} | ^ {2} + \frac {G m _ {1} (m _ {1} + m _ {2})}{r} \right]
$$

作一个变换,新的拉格朗日函数与原拉格朗日函数仅差一个常数因子,写出的运动微分方程不受影响.新的拉格朗日函数为

$$
L = \frac {1}{2} m _ {1} | \dot {\boldsymbol {r}} | ^ {2} + \frac {G m _ {1} (m _ {1} + m _ {2})}{r}
$$

得到的运动微分方程相当于一个质量为 $m_{1}$ 的星在质量为 $(m_{1} + m_{2})$ 的不动的星的引力作用下的运动. 由开普勒第三定律, 双星的转动周期 $T$ 与其椭圆轨道的长半轴 $a$ 有下列关系:

$$
\frac {T ^ {2}}{a ^ {3}} = \frac {4 \pi^ {2}}{G (m _ {1} + m _ {2})}
$$

9.3.37 质量为 M、长为 l 的两均质细杆由光滑的铰链和一根线连接起来，置于光滑的地面上，处于静止状态，杆与水平地面的夹角为 $30^{\circ}$ ，如图 9.69 所示。铰链的质量可以不计，t=0 时，线被切断。求：

(1) 铰链击中地面时的速度；

(2) 铰链击中地面时的时间(写出积分式子即可, 不必求积).

解 （1）由对称考虑，铰链必竖直下落，考虑一根杆为系统，取图 9.70 中的 x、y 坐标。杆的质心的 x、y 坐标为

$$
x _ {C} = \frac {l}{2} \cos \theta , \quad y _ {C} = \frac {l}{2} \sin \theta .
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/39507ec574d8398d8cef76602229c6f70d4ef2de5f9fb88988020434869afe4c.jpg)



图9.69


![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/43460b6f703760ea90578dcb2ad0f2a23b85e96b212986e491eeb44dc1eecfff.jpg)



图9.70


取 $\theta$ 为广义坐标，

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} m \left(\dot {x} _ {C} ^ {2} + \dot {y} _ {C} ^ {2}\right) + \frac {1}{2} \cdot \frac {1}{1 2} m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} = \frac {1}{6} m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2}
$$

$$
V = m g y _ {C} = \frac {1}{2} m g l \sin \theta
$$

因为

$$
L = T - V = \frac {1}{6} m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} m g l \sin \theta
$$

$$
\frac {\partial L}{\partial t} = 0, \quad \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\theta}} \dot {\theta} - L = \text {常量}
$$

即

$$
\frac {1}{6} m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m g l \sin \theta = \frac {1}{4} m g l
$$

$$
\dot {\theta} = - \sqrt {\frac {3 g}{l} \left(\frac {1}{2} - \sin \theta\right)}
$$

铰链的 $y$ 坐标为

$$
\begin{array}{c} y = l \sin \theta \\ \dot {y} = l \dot {\theta} \cos \theta \end{array}
$$

击中地面时, $\theta=0$ ,铰链的速度为

(2) 

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\pmb {v} = \dot {\pmb {y}} \pmb {j} = (l \dot {\theta} \cos \theta) | _ {\theta = 0} \pmb {j}} \\ & {\qquad = - l \sqrt {\frac {3 g}{l} \left(\frac {1}{2} - \sin 0 ^ {\circ}\right)} \cos 0 ^ {\circ} \pmb {j} = - \sqrt {\frac {3}{2} g l} \pmb {j}} \\ & {\frac {\mathrm{d} \theta}{\mathrm{d} t} = - \sqrt {\frac {3 g}{l} \left(\frac {1}{2} - \sin \theta\right)}} \\ & {\mathrm{d} t = - \frac {1}{\sqrt {\frac {3 g}{2 l} (1 - 2 \sin \theta)}} \mathrm{d} \theta} \end{array}
$$

$t = 0$ 时， $\theta = 30^{\circ}$ ，击中地面时， $\theta = 0^{\circ}$ 

$$
t = - \int_ {3 0 ^ {\circ}} ^ {0 ^ {\circ}} \frac {\mathrm{d} \theta}{\sqrt {\frac {3 g}{2 l} (1 - 2 \sin \theta)}} = \sqrt {\frac {2 l}{3 g}} \int_ {0} ^ {3 0 ^ {\circ}} \frac {\mathrm{d} \theta}{\sqrt {1 - 2 \sin \theta}}
$$

9.3.38 球面摆由质量为 $m$ 的质点, 用一根长为 $l$ 的不可伸长的轻绳悬挂在一个固定点构成, 该质点被限制在球面上运动.

(1) 若使绳与铅直方向的夹角 $\theta_{0}$ 不变, 质点做此圆周运动的角速度多大?

(2) 在(1)问所述的圆轨道上的质点受一垂直于其速度的冲量, 结果在其轨道达最高点时绳与铅直方向成 $\theta_{1}$ 角, 写出当质点在最低点时绳与铅直方向的夹角 $\theta_{2}$ 所满足的方程 (不必解);

(3) 就在 $\theta_{0}$ 附近振动的振幅很小的情况, 求振动的角频率.

解（1）设此圆周运动的角速度为 $\omega$ ，取绕竖直轴以 $\omega$ 的角速度转动的参考系，则质点在 $\theta = \theta_0$ 处，处于平衡状态，用虚功原理，

$$
V = - m g l \cos \theta - \frac {1}{2} m l ^ {2} \omega^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta
$$

在 $\theta = \theta_0$ 处， $\frac{\partial V}{\partial\theta} = 0$ ，即

$$
\begin{array}{r l} m g l \sin \theta_ {0} - m l ^ {2} \omega^ {2} \sin \theta_ {0} \cos \theta_ {0} & = 0 \\ \omega & = \sqrt {\frac {g}{l \cos \theta_ {0}}} \end{array}
$$

(2) 取静参考系, 用球坐标 $\theta, \varphi$ 为广义坐标

因为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} L & = T - V = \frac {1}{2} m l ^ {2} (\dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta) + m g l \cos \theta \\ & \frac {\partial L}{\partial \varphi} = 0 \\ & \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\varphi}} = m l ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} \sin^ {2} \theta = m l ^ {2} \sqrt {\frac {g}{l \cos \theta_ {0}}} \sin^ {2} \theta_ {0} \end{array}\tag{1}
$$

这里考虑到冲量不影响在此位置 $(\theta = \theta_0)$ 的 $\dot{\varphi} (\dot{\varphi}$ 为(1)问中解出的 $\omega$ 

因为 $\frac{\partial L}{\partial t} = 0$ ，且 $T$ 是广义速度的二次齐次函数，

$$
T + V = \frac {1}{2} m l ^ {2} (\dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta) - m g l \cos \theta = \frac {1}{2} m l ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} _ {1} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta_ {1} - m g l \cos \theta_ {1}\tag{2}
$$

质点达最高点和最低点时用式(1)，在最低点时用式(2)，考虑到 $\dot{\theta}_{2}=0$ ，

$$
\dot {\varphi} _ {1} \sin^ {2} \theta_ {1} = \sqrt {\frac {g}{l \cos \theta_ {0}}} \sin^ {2} \theta_ {0}
$$

$$
\dot {\varphi} _ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta_ {2} = \sqrt {\frac {g}{l \cos \theta_ {0}}} \sin^ {2} \theta_ {0}
$$

$$
\frac {1}{2} l \dot {\varphi} _ {2} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta_ {2} - g \cos \theta_ {2} = \frac {1}{2} (\dot {\varphi} _ {1} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta_ {1} - g \cos \theta_ {1})
$$

从上述三式，消去 $\dot{\varphi}_1, \dot{\varphi}_2$ ，可得 $\theta_2$ 满足的方程为

$$
\frac {\sin^ {4} \theta_ {0}}{\cos \theta_ {0}} \left(\frac {1}{\sin^ {2} \theta_ {2}} - \frac {1}{\sin^ {2} \theta_ {1}}\right) = 2 (\cos \theta_ {2} - \cos \theta_ {1})
$$

$$
L = \frac {1}{2} m l ^ {2} \left(\dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta\right) + m g l \cos \theta
$$

由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L}{\partial\theta}\right) - \frac{\partial L}{\partial\theta} = 0$ 得

$$
l \ddot {\theta} - l \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \sin \theta \cos \theta + g \sin \theta = 0
$$

由 $\frac{\partial L}{\partial \varphi} = 0$ ，得 $\frac{\partial L}{\partial \dot{\varphi}} =$ 常量，

$$
\dot {\varphi} \sin^ {2} \theta = \sqrt {\frac {g}{l \cos \theta_ {0}}} \sin^ {2} \theta_ {0}
$$

用上式消去前式中的 $\dot{\varphi}$ , 得

$$
\ddot {\theta} - \frac {g}{l \cos \theta_ {0}} \frac {\sin^ {4} \theta_ {0}}{\sin^ {3} \theta} \cos \theta + \frac {g}{l} \sin \theta = 0\tag{1}
$$

把 $\sin \theta$ 和 $\cos \theta$ 在 $\theta_0$ 作泰勒展开，只保留一级小量，令 $\Delta \theta = \theta -\theta_0$ 

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\sin \theta \approx \sin \theta_ {0} + \cos \theta_ {0} \cdot \Delta \theta} \\ & {\cos \theta \approx \cos \theta_ {0} - \sin \theta_ {0} \Delta \theta} \\ & {\sin^ {- 3} \theta \approx (\sin \theta_ {0} + \cos \theta_ {0} \Delta \theta) ^ {- 3}} \\ & {\qquad = \sin^ {- 3} \theta_ {0} (1 + \operatorname{ctg} \theta_ {0} \Delta \theta) ^ {- 3} \approx \sin^ {- 3} \theta_ {0} (l - 3 \operatorname{ctg} \theta_ {0})} \end{array}
$$

代入式(1)，注意

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\ddot {\theta} = \frac {\mathrm{d} ^ {2}}{\mathrm{d} t ^ {2}} (\theta_ {0} + \Delta \theta) = \frac {\mathrm{d} ^ {2} (\Delta \theta)}{\mathrm{d} t ^ {2}}} \\ & {\frac {\mathrm{d} ^ {2} (\Delta \theta)}{\mathrm{d} t ^ {2}} + \frac {g (1 + 3 \cos^ {2} \theta_ {0})}{l \cos \theta_ {0}} \Delta \theta = 0} \end{array}
$$

质点在 $\theta=\theta_{0}$ 附近关于 $\theta$ 的小振幅振动的角频率为

$$
\omega = \sqrt {\frac {g (1 + 3 \cos^ {2} \theta_ {0})}{l \cos \theta_ {0}}}
$$

9.3.39 一弹簧摆由质量为 $m$ 的质点经原长为 $l$ 、劲度系数为 $k$ 的无质量弹簧系于固定点组成，如图9.71所示。假定系统限制在一个竖直平面内运动，导出运动微分方程，

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/6dc4289ac3414cbf338fe88dbd22efe68f548e857b0024db6b4f7e6612d08d09.jpg)


并在对平衡位置的角位移和径向位移都很小的近似下解运动微分方程.

解

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2})
$$

$$
V = \frac {1}{2} k (r - l) ^ {2} - m g r \cos \varphi
$$


图9.71


$$
L = T - V = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}) - \frac {1}{2} k (r - l) ^ {2} + m g r \cos \varphi
$$

由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L}{\partial q_i}\right) - \frac{\partial L}{\partial q_i} = 0$ 得运动微分方程

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & m \ddot {r} - m r \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + k (r - l) - m g \cos \varphi = 0 \\ & \quad r \ddot {\varphi} + 2 \dot {r} \dot {\varphi} + g \sin \varphi = 0 \end{array}
$$

在平衡位置， $\dot{r} = 0, \ddot{r} = 0, \dot{\varphi} = 0, \ddot{\varphi} = 0.$ 设平衡位置 $r = r_0, \varphi = \varphi_0$ 

$$
\begin{array}{r l} k (r _ {0} - l) - m g \cos \varphi_ {0} & = 0 \\ g \sin \varphi_ {0} & = 0 \end{array}
$$

得

$$
\varphi_ {0} = 0, \quad r _ {0} = l + \frac {m g}{k}
$$

在对平衡位置的角位移和径向位移都很小时，令 $r' = r - r_0$ ，运动微分方程可简化为

$$
m \ddot {r} ^ {\prime} + k r ^ {\prime} = 0
$$

$$
r _ {0} \ddot {\varphi} + g \varphi = 0
$$

解为

$$
r ^ {\prime} = A \cos \left(\sqrt {\frac {k}{m}} t + \alpha\right) \text {或} r = l + \frac {m g}{k} + A \cos \left(\sqrt {\frac {k}{m}} t + \alpha\right)
$$

$$
\varphi = B \cos \left(\sqrt {\frac {g}{r _ {0}}} t + \beta\right)
$$

其中 $r_0 = l + \frac{mg}{k}, A, B, \alpha, \beta$ 为积分常数，由初始条件定.

9.3.40 粒子受到力心的引力作用, 力的大小反比于它到力心的距离的三次方, 导出运动微分方程, 并解方程求轨道, 讨论轨道的性质是如何依赖于系统的参数的.

解

$$
\pmb {F} = - \frac {k}{r ^ {3}} \pmb {e} _ {r}
$$

$$
V (r) = - \frac {k}{2 r ^ {2}}
$$

$$
L = T - V = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}) + \frac {k}{2 r ^ {2}}
$$

由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L}{\dot{\partial}q_i}\right) - \frac{\partial L}{\partial q_i} = 0$ 得运动微分方程为

$$
m \ddot {r} - m r \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \frac {k}{r ^ {3}} = 0\tag{1}
$$

$$
m r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} = b (\text {常量})
$$

消去 $\dot{\varphi}$ ，可得

$$
m \ddot {r} = \frac {b ^ {2} - k m}{m r ^ {3}}
$$

令 $u = \frac{1}{r}$ ，则

$$
\dot {\varphi} = \frac {b}{m} r ^ {- 2} = \frac {b}{m} u ^ {2}
$$

$$
\dot {r} = \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {1}{u}\right) = - \frac {1}{u ^ {2}} \frac {\mathrm{d} u}{\mathrm{d} \varphi} \dot {\varphi} = - \frac {b}{m} \frac {\mathrm{d} u}{\mathrm{d} \varphi}
$$

$$
\ddot {r} = - \frac {b}{m} \cdot \frac {\mathrm{d} ^ {2} u}{\mathrm{d} \varphi^ {2}} \cdot \dot {\varphi} = - \frac {b ^ {2}}{m ^ {2}} u ^ {2} \frac {\mathrm{d} ^ {2} u}{\mathrm{d} \varphi^ {2}}
$$

将 $\ddot{r}$ 代入式(1)，得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & - \frac {b ^ {2}}{m} u ^ {2} \frac {\mathrm{d} ^ {2} u}{\mathrm{d} \varphi^ {2}} = \frac {b ^ {2} - k m}{m} u ^ {3} \\ & \frac {\mathrm{d} ^ {2} u}{\mathrm{d} \varphi^ {2}} + \frac {b ^ {2} - k m}{b ^ {2}} u = 0 \end{array}
$$

若 $b^{2} > km$ 

$$
u = \frac {1}{r _ {0}} \cos \left[ \frac {1}{b} \sqrt {b ^ {2} - k m} (\varphi - \varphi_ {0}) \right]
$$

$$
r = r _ {0} \sec \left[ \frac {1}{b} \sqrt {b ^ {2} - k m} (\varphi - \varphi_ {0}) \right]
$$

若 $b^{2} < km$ 

$$
u = \frac {1}{r _ {0}} \cosh \left[ \frac {1}{b} \sqrt {k m - b ^ {2}} (\varphi - \varphi_ {0}) \right]
$$

$$
r = r _ {0} \mathrm{sech} \left[ \frac {1}{b} \sqrt {k m - b ^ {2}} (\varphi - \varphi_ {0}) \right]
$$

两个轨道方程中的 $(r_0, \varphi_0)$ 都表示轨道上的一点.

9.3.41 一个质量为 $m$ 的粒子在有心力

$$
F (r) = - \frac {k}{r ^ {2}} + \frac {k ^ {\prime}}{r ^ {3}} \quad (k > 0)
$$

作用下做平面运动.

(1) 写出系统的拉格朗日函数；

(2) 导出运动微分方程, 并证明轨道角动量 J 是一个运动常数;

(3) 若 $J^{2}>-mk'$ ，求轨道方程.

解 (1)

$$
F (r) = - \frac {k}{r ^ {2}} + \frac {k ^ {\prime}}{r ^ {3}}
$$

$$
V (r) = - \int_ {\infty} ^ {r} F (r) \mathrm{d} r = \int_ {r} ^ {\infty} F (r) \mathrm{d} r = - \frac {k}{r} + \frac {k ^ {\prime}}{2 r ^ {2}}
$$

$$
L = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}) + \frac {k}{r} - \frac {k ^ {\prime}}{2 r ^ {2}}
$$

(2) 由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L}{\partial q_i}\right) - \frac{\partial L}{\partial q_i} = 0$ 得运动微分方程为

$$
m \ddot {r} - m r \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \frac {k}{r ^ {2}} - \frac {k ^ {\prime}}{r ^ {3}} = 0\tag{1}
$$

$$
m (r \ddot {\varphi} + 2 \dot {r} \dot {\varphi}) = 0\tag{2}
$$

式(2)也可写成

$$
\frac {m}{r} \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} (r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi}) = 0
$$

: 

$$
J = m r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} = \text {常量}
$$

(3) 令 $u = \frac{1}{r}, r = u^{-1}$ ,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \dot {r} & = - u ^ {- 2} \frac {\mathrm{d} u}{\mathrm{d} \varphi} \dot {\varphi} = - u ^ {- 2} \frac {\mathrm{d} u}{\mathrm{d} \varphi} \frac {J}{m r ^ {2}} = - \frac {J}{m} \frac {\mathrm{d} u}{\mathrm{d} \varphi} \\ \ddot {r} & = - \frac {J}{m} \frac {\mathrm{d} ^ {2} u}{\mathrm{d} \varphi^ {2}} \dot {\varphi} = - \frac {J ^ {2}}{m ^ {2}} u ^ {2} \frac {\mathrm{d} ^ {2} u}{\mathrm{d} \varphi^ {2}} \end{array}
$$

式(1)可改写为

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} ^ {2} u}{\mathrm{d} \varphi^ {2}} + \left(1 + \frac {m k ^ {\prime}}{J ^ {2}}\right) u = \frac {m k}{J ^ {2}}\tag{3}
$$

因 $J^2 > -mk', 1 + \frac{mk'}{J^2} > 0$ . 式(3)的解为

$$
u = \frac {m k}{J ^ {2} + m k ^ {\prime}} + A \cos \left(\sqrt {1 + \frac {m k ^ {\prime}}{J ^ {2}}} \varphi + \alpha\right)
$$

可选择适当的基轴，使 $\alpha=0$ ，轨道方程为

$$
\begin{array}{l} u = \frac {m k}{J ^ {2} + m k ^ {\prime}} + A \cos \left(\sqrt {1 + \frac {m k ^ {\prime}}{J ^ {2}}} \varphi\right) \\ r = \frac {1}{\frac {m k}{J ^ {2} + m k ^ {\prime}} + A \cos \left(\sqrt {1 + \frac {m k ^ {\prime}}{J ^ {2}}} \varphi\right)} \end{array}
$$

9.3.42 一个质量为 $m$ 的珠子在一个半径为 $a$ 的旋转圆环上无摩擦地滑动, 圆环绕通过竖直的直径作恒定角速度 $\omega$ 的转动, 如图 9.72 所示:

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/b42b73aef904b2a8b54af6800b7c9e1497377119aba9331d527db934d0618d74.jpg)



图9.72


(1) 写出系统的拉格朗日函数, 并找出所有的运动积分;

(2) 确定 $\omega < \omega_0$ 和 $\omega > \omega_0\left(\omega_0 = \sqrt{\frac{g}{a}}\right)$ 时珠子的平衡位置；

(3) 这些平衡位置,哪些是稳定的?哪些是不稳定的?

(4) 计算围绕稳定平衡点的小振幅振动的角频率.

解 （1）选用圆环参考系，主动力除重力外，还有惯性离轴力。在这里，科里奥利力不做虚功，作约束力处理。

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \delta W & = - m g \sin \varphi \cdot a \delta \varphi + m \omega^ {2} a \sin \varphi \cos \varphi \cdot a \delta \varphi \\ & = m (\omega^ {2} a ^ {2} \sin \varphi \cos \varphi - g a \sin \varphi) \delta \varphi \end{array}
$$

所以

$$
\begin{array}{l} Q = m (\omega^ {2} a ^ {2} \sin \varphi \cos \varphi - g a \sin \varphi) \\ V = - \int Q \mathrm{d} \varphi = - \int m (w ^ {2} a ^ {2} \sin \varphi \cos \varphi - g a \sin \varphi) \mathrm{d} \varphi \\ = - \frac {1}{2} m \omega^ {2} a ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \varphi - m g a \cos \varphi \\ T = \frac {1}{2} m a ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \\ L = T - V = \frac {1}{2} m a ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m \omega^ {2} a ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \varphi + m g a \cos \varphi \end{array}
$$

因为 $\frac{\partial L}{\partial t} = 0, \frac{\partial L}{\partial \dot{\varphi}} \dot{\varphi} - L =$ 常量，即

$$
\frac {1}{2} m a ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} m \omega^ {2} a ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \varphi - m g a \cos \varphi = \text {常量}
$$

这个运动积分是能量积分.

(2) 在平衡位置, Q=0,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} m (\omega^ {2} a ^ {2} \sin \varphi \cos \varphi - g a \sin \varphi) & = 0 \\ \sin \varphi (\omega^ {2} a \cos \varphi - g) & = 0 \end{array}
$$

当 $\omega < \sqrt{\frac{g}{a}}$ 时， $\frac{g}{a\omega^2} > 1, a^2 \cos \varphi - g \neq 0$ ，平衡位置处，

$$
\sin \varphi = 0
$$

$\varphi=0,\pi$ 为平衡位置.

当 $\omega >\sqrt{\frac{g}{a}}$ 时， $\frac{g}{a\omega^2} <  1$ ，平衡位置处，

$$
\sin \varphi = 0   \text {或}   \omega^ {2} a \mathrm{cos} \varphi - g = 0
$$

$\varphi = 0, \pi$ 和 $\arccos\left(\frac{g}{\omega^2 a}\right)$ 为平衡位置.

（3）由平衡位置处 $\frac{\mathrm{d}^2V}{\mathrm{d}\varphi^2}$ 或 $-\frac{\mathrm{d}Q}{\mathrm{d}\varphi}$ 的正负号判断平衡位置处平衡的稳定性，大于零时是稳定平衡位置，小于零则为不稳定平衡位置，

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d} ^ {2} V}{\mathrm{d} \varphi^ {2}} = - \frac {\mathrm{d} Q}{\mathrm{d} \varphi} = - m \left[ \omega^ {2} a ^ {2} (\cos^ {2} \varphi - \sin^ {2} \varphi) - g a \cos \varphi \right]
$$

$\omega < \sqrt{\frac{g}{a}}$ 时，

$$
\left. \frac {\mathrm{d} ^ {2} V}{\mathrm{d} \varphi^ {2}} \right| _ {\varphi = 0} = - m (\omega^ {2} a ^ {2} - g a) > 0
$$

$$
\left. \frac {\mathrm{d} ^ {2} V}{\mathrm{d} \varphi^ {2}} \right| _ {\varphi = \pi} = - m (\omega^ {2} a ^ {2} + g a) <   0
$$

故 $\varphi=0$ 为稳定平衡位置, $\varphi=\pi$ 为不稳定平衡位置.

$\omega>\sqrt{\frac{g}{a}}$ 时，

$$
\left. \frac {\mathrm{d} ^ {2} V}{\mathrm{d} \varphi^ {2}} \right| _ {\varphi = 0} <   0, \quad \left. \frac {\mathrm{d} ^ {2} V}{\mathrm{d} \varphi^ {2}} \right| _ {\varphi = \pi} <   0
$$

$$
\left. \frac {\mathrm{d} ^ {2} V}{\mathrm{d} \varphi^ {2}} \right| _ {\varphi = \operatorname{arc} \cos \frac {g}{\omega^ {2} a}} = - m \left(\frac {g ^ {2}}{\omega^ {2}} - \omega^ {2} a ^ {2}\right) > 0
$$

故 $\varphi = 0$ 和 $\pi$ 均为不稳定平衡位置， $\varphi = \arccos \left( \frac{g}{\omega^2 a} \right)$ 为稳定平衡位置.

(4) 由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L}{\dot{\partial}\varphi}\right) - \frac{\partial L}{\partial\varphi} = 0$ 或由能量积分对 $t$ 求导均可得

$$
a \ddot {\varphi} - a \omega^ {2} \sin \varphi \cos \varphi + g \sin \varphi = 0
$$

当 $\omega < \sqrt{\frac{g}{a}}$ 时， $\varphi = 0$ 为稳定平衡位置，在此点附近做小振动

$$
\sin \varphi \approx \varphi , \quad \cos \varphi \approx 1
$$

$$
a \ddot {\varphi} + (g - a \omega^ {2}) \varphi = 0
$$

小振动的角频率为

$$
\Omega = \sqrt {\frac {g - a \omega^ {2}}{a}}
$$

当 $\omega >\sqrt{\frac{g}{a}}$ 时， $\varphi = \arccos \left(\frac{g}{a\omega^2}\right)$ 为稳定平衡位置.

在平衡位置处，将 $\sin \varphi, \cos \varphi$ 作泰勒展开，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sin \varphi \approx \sin \varphi_ {0} + \cos \varphi_ {0} \Delta \varphi \\ \cos \varphi \approx \cos \varphi_ {0} - \sin \varphi_ {0} \Delta \varphi \end{array}
$$

其中

$$
\varphi_ {0} = \arccos \left(\frac {g}{a \omega^ {2}}\right), \Delta \varphi = \varphi - \varphi_ {0}
$$

运动微分方程可近似为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} a \frac {\mathrm{d} ^ {2} \Delta \varphi}{\mathrm{d} t ^ {2}} + & \left[ g \cos \varphi_ {0} - \frac {g}{\cos \varphi_ {0}} (2 \cos^ {2} \varphi_ {0} - 1) \right] \Delta \varphi = 0 \\ & \frac {\mathrm{d} ^ {2} \Delta \varphi}{\mathrm{d} t ^ {2}} + \left(\omega^ {2} - \frac {g ^ {2}}{a ^ {2} \omega^ {2}}\right) \Delta \varphi = 0 \end{array}
$$

小振动的角频率为

$$
\Omega = \sqrt {\omega^ {2} - \frac {g ^ {2}}{a ^ {2} \omega^ {2}}}
$$

9.3.43 质量为 $m_{1}$ 和 $m_{2}$ 的两质点被一长 l 并穿过一光滑水平面上的光滑小孔的不可伸长的轻绳连接， $m_{1}$ 在水平面上运动， $m_{2}$ 在铅垂线上运动。

(1) 要使 $m_{2}$ 在平面下 d 处保持不动, 必须给 $m_{1}$ 以什么样的初速度;

(2) 如果 $m_{2}$ 在铅直方向稍微偏离上述平衡位置, 将产生小振动, 用拉格朗日方程求振动周期.

解 (1) 必须使 $m_{1}$ 获得一个垂直于绳的初速度 $v_{0}$ , 使做此匀速率的半径为 $l - d$ 的圆

周运动所需的向心力等于作用于 $m_{2}$ 的重力，

$$
\frac {m _ {1} v _ {0} ^ {2}}{l - d} = m _ {2} g
$$

所以

$$
v _ {0} = \sqrt {\frac {m _ {2} g (l - d)}{m _ {1}}}
$$

(2) 选 $m_{1}$ 对小孔 (作为坐标系原点) 的极坐标 $r, \varphi$ 为广义坐标，

$$
L = \frac {1}{2} m _ {1} (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{2} m _ {2} \dot {r} ^ {2} + m _ {2} g (l - r)
$$

由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L}{\partial r}\right) - \frac{\partial L}{\partial r} = 0$ 得

$$
(m _ {1} + m _ {2}) \ddot {r} - m _ {1} r \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + m _ {2} g = 0\tag{1}
$$

由 $\frac{\partial L}{\partial\varphi} = 0, \frac{\partial L}{\partial\varphi} =$ 常量

$$
m _ {1} r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} = m _ {1} (l - d) v _ {0} = m _ {1} \sqrt {\frac {m _ {2} g}{m _ {1}}} (l - d) ^ {3 / 2}\tag{2}
$$

将式(2)代入式(1)，

$$
(m _ {1} + m _ {2}) \ddot {r} - \frac {m _ {2} g (l - d) ^ {3}}{r ^ {3}} + m _ {2} g = 0\tag{3}
$$

平衡位置

$$
r = r _ {0} = l - d
$$

令

$$
x = r - r _ {0} = r - (l - d)
$$

$m_{2}$ 在平衡位置附近做小振动， $m_{1}$ 在 $r = r_0$ 附近的径向运动也做小振动，

$$
\left[ \frac {(l - d)}{r} \right] ^ {3} = \left(\frac {l - d}{l - d + x}\right) ^ {3} = \left(1 + \frac {x}{l - d}\right) ^ {- 3} \approx 1 - \frac {3 x}{l - d}
$$

用此近似,式(3)近似为

$$
(m _ {1} + m _ {2}) \ddot {x} + \frac {3 m _ {2} g}{l - d} x = 0
$$

小振动的角频率 $\omega$ 和周期 T 分别为

$$
\omega = \sqrt {\frac {3 m _ {2} g}{(m _ {1} + m _ {2}) (l - d)}}
$$

$$
T = \frac {2 \pi}{\omega} = 2 \pi \sqrt {\frac {(m _ {1} + m _ {2}) (l - d)}{3 m _ {2} g}}
$$

9.3.44 一个质量为 $M$ 、半径为 $R$ 的均质圆球沿一质量为 $m$ 、倾角为 $\alpha$ 的三角块向下做纯滚动，三角块在光滑的水平面上自由运动，如图9.73所示。

(1) 求系统的拉格朗日函数, 写出拉格朗日方程;

(2) 设开始系统处于静止, 球心在水平面上 H 处, 求系统的运动.

解（1）取图9.74所示的坐标，用 $x$ 表示三角块的坐标， $\theta$ 表示球的转角，考虑到球

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/f3be9ff7daf3da94604e7125259f119dc06e3b523403adfe20b29aecb319f7e1.jpg)



图9.73


![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/f537cbd02c509834f970f9e94b099657bf62817876c7512b0712a41b8162def7.jpg)



图9.74


在三角块上做纯滚动,球心的 x、y 坐标为

$$
[ x + (\xi_ {0} + R \theta) \cos \alpha , H - R \theta \sin \alpha ]
$$

其中 $\zeta_{0}$ 是 $\theta=0$ 时，球心的 $\zeta$ 坐标， $\theta=0$ 时，球心的 y 坐标为 H.

球心的速度用 $x, y$ 分量表示为

$$
(\dot {x} + R \dot {\theta} \cos \alpha , - R \dot {\theta} \sin \alpha)
$$

系统的拉格朗日函数为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} L & = T - V = \frac {1}{2} m \dot {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} M [ (\dot {x} + R \dot {\theta} \cos \alpha) ^ {2} + (- R \dot {\theta} \sin \alpha) ^ {2} ] \\ & + \frac {1}{2} \times \frac {2}{5} M R ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} - M g (H - R \theta \sin \alpha) \\ & = \frac {1}{2} (m + M) \dot {x} ^ {2} + \frac {7}{1 0} M R ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + M R \dot {x} \dot {\theta} \cos \alpha - M g (H - R \theta \sin \alpha) \end{array}
$$

拉格朗日方程 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L}{\partial\dot{q}_i}\right) - \frac{\partial L}{\partial q_i} = 0$ 为

$$
\begin{array}{l} (m + M) \ddot {x} + M R \ddot {\theta} \cos \alpha = 0 \\ \frac {7}{5} R \ddot {\theta} + \ddot {x} \cos \alpha - g \sin \alpha = 0 \end{array}
$$

(2) 从上述两个运动微分方程可解出

$$
\ddot {\theta} = \frac {5 (m + M) g \sin \alpha}{[ 7 (m + M) - 5 M \cos^ {2} \alpha ] R}
$$

用初始条件: t=0 时, $\theta=0, \dot{\theta}=0$ , 积分得

$$
\theta = \frac {5 (m + M) g \sin \alpha}{2 [ 7 (m + M) - 5 M \cos^ {2} \alpha ] R} t ^ {2}
$$

积分第一个运动微分方程,用初始条件:t=0时, $x=0,\theta=0,\dot{x}=0,\dot{\theta}=0$ ,得

$$
(m + M) x + M R \theta \cos \alpha = 0
$$

所以

$$
x = - \frac {M R \cos \alpha}{m + M} \theta = - \frac {5 M g \sin \alpha \cos \alpha}{2 [ 7 (m + M) - 5 M \cos^ {2} \alpha ]} t ^ {2}
$$

9.3.45 质量为 $m$ 的质点被约束在一半径为 $r$ 、质量为 $M$ 的均质圆环内表面无摩擦

运动, 环在竖直的 $xy$ 平面内沿水平的 $x$ 轴做纯滚动, 如图9.75所示. $t = 0$ 时, 环是静止的, 质点在环的顶部, 具有沿 $x$ 轴方向的速度 $v_0$ , 求当质点运动到环的底部时相对于固定的 $x$ 轴的速度 $v$ , 并给出当 $\frac{m}{M} \to 0$ 及 $\frac{M}{m} \to 0$ 时的结果.

解 圆环的质心坐标为 $(x, r)$ , 质点的坐标为 $(x + r\sin \theta, r + r\cos \theta)$ , 质点的速度为

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/afc69d8dc1e7844c4a2899d877d7209daa02e6e1eb0e8a94c47ee483cd6116b3.jpg)


$$
(\dot {x} + r \dot {\theta} \cos \theta , - r \dot {\theta} \sin \theta)
$$


图9.75


系统的动能为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} T & = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + 2 r \dot {x} \dot {\theta} \cos \theta) + \frac {1}{2} M \dot {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} M r ^ {2} \left(\frac {\dot {x}}{r}\right) ^ {2} \\ & = \frac {1}{2} (m + 2 M) \dot {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m r ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + m r \dot {x} \dot {\theta} \cos \theta \end{array}
$$

系统的势能为

$$
V = m g (r + r \cos \theta)
$$

系统的拉格朗日函数为

$$
L = T - V = \frac {1}{2} (m + 2 M) \dot {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m r ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + m r \dot {x} \dot {\theta} \cos \theta - m g r (1 + \cos \theta)
$$

因为 $\frac{\partial L}{\partial x} = 0, \frac{\partial L}{\partial x} =$ 常量，即

$$
(m + 2 M) \dot {x} + m r \dot {\theta} \cos \theta = m v _ {0}\tag{1}
$$

这里用了初始条件； $t = 0$ 时， $\dot{x} = 0,\quad \theta = 0,\quad r\dot{\theta} = v_0$ 

因为 $\frac{\partial L}{\partial t} = 0, T$ 是 $\dot{x}, \dot{\theta}$ 的二次齐次函数，

$$
\sum_ {i} \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {q} _ {i}} \dot {q} _ {i} - L = T + V = \text {常量}
$$

$$
\frac {1}{2} (m + M) \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m r ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + m r \dot {x} \dot {\theta} \cos \theta + m g r (1 + \cos \theta) = \frac {1}{2} m v _ {0} ^ {2} + 2 m g r\tag{2}
$$

质点到达圆环底部时， $\theta = \pi$ ，代入式(1)、(2)两式，得到此时 $\dot{x}, \dot{\theta}$ 满足的方程：

$$
(m + 2 M) \dot {x} - m r \dot {\theta} = m v _ {0}\tag{3}
$$

$$
\frac {1}{2} (m + 2 M) \dot {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m r ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} - m r \dot {x} \dot {\theta} = \frac {1}{2} m v _ {0} ^ {2} + 2 m g r\tag{4}
$$

用式(3)消去式(4)中的 $r\dot{\theta}$ ，得

$$
M (m + 2 M) \dot {x} ^ {2} - 2 m M v _ {0} \dot {x} - 2 m ^ {2} g r = 0
$$

$$
\dot {x} = \frac {m M v _ {0} \pm m \sqrt {M ^ {2} v _ {0} ^ {2} + 2 M (m + 2 M) g r}}{M (m + 2 M)}
$$

因为从 $\theta = 0$ 到 $\theta = \pi$ ，质点对圆环的作用力的 $x$ 分量始终大于零，故到达底部时，必有 $\dot{x} > 0$ ，舍去 $\dot{x} < 0$ 的解，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \dot {x} & = \frac {m M v _ {0} + m \sqrt {M ^ {2} v _ {0} ^ {2} + 2 M (m + 2 M) g r}}{M (m + 2 M)} \\ & \quad r \dot {\theta} = \frac {1}{m} (m + 2 M) \dot {x} - v _ {0} \end{array}
$$

到达底部时,质点相对于 x 轴的速度为

$$
\begin{array}{r l}&{v = \dot {x} - r   \dot {\theta} = \dot {x} - \left[ \frac {1}{m} (m + 2 M) \dot {x} - v _ {0} \right]}\\&{\qquad = v _ {0} - \frac {2 M}{m} \dot {x} = \frac {1}{m + 2 M} \Big [ M v _ {0} - 2 \sqrt {M ^ {2} v _ {0} ^ {2} + 2 M (m + 2 M) g r} \Big ]}\\&{\text {寸}, \qquad \qquad v \rightarrow - \sqrt {v _ {0} ^ {2} + 4 g r}}\end{array}
$$

当 $\frac{m}{M} \to 0$ 时，

当 $\frac{M}{m} \to 0$ 时，

$$
V \rightarrow v _ {0}
$$

9.3.46 一质点在光滑的旋转抛物面 $r^2 = az$ 内侧滑动，证明受到的约束力的大小为

$$
\text {常量} \cdot \left(1 + \frac {4 r ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}}\right) ^ {- 3 / 2}
$$

说明约束力的方向.

证明 取柱坐标 $r, \varphi, z$ ，如图9.76所示，

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/ecb7c6ace8463b4b71dcae0af06fdef12a718019b50f6a554335b42ddca8a436.jpg)



图9.76


$$
L = T - V = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2}) - m g z
$$

(假设质点的质量为 m).

约束方程为

$$
f (r, \varphi , z) = r ^ {2} - a z = 0\tag{1}
$$

用带有未定乘子的拉格朗日方程

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {q} _ {i}}\right) - \frac {\partial L}{\partial q _ {i}} = Q _ {i} + \lambda \frac {\partial f}{\partial q _ {i}}
$$

这里 $Q_{i} = 0$ ，可得

$$
m (\ddot {r} - r \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}) = 2 \lambda r\tag{2}
$$

$$
r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} = c (\text {常量})\tag{3}
$$

$$
m \ddot {z} = - m g - \lambda a\tag{4}
$$

由式(1)，

$$
z = \frac {1}{a} r ^ {2}, \quad \dot {z} = \frac {2}{a} r r ^ {\cdot}, \quad \ddot {z} = \frac {2 r}{a} \ddot {r} + \frac {2}{a} \dot {r} ^ {2}
$$

由式(4)、(2)、上式和式(3)，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} - a \lambda & = m (\ddot {z} + g) = m \left(\frac {2 r}{a} \ddot {r} + \frac {2}{a} \dot {r} ^ {2} + g\right) = \frac {2 r}{a} (m r \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + 2 \lambda r) + \frac {2 m}{a} \dot {r} ^ {2} + m g \\ & - \left(1 + \frac {4 r ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}}\right) \lambda = \frac {2 m}{a ^ {2}} \dot {r} ^ {2} + \frac {2 m}{a ^ {2}} r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \frac {m g}{a} = \frac {2 m}{a ^ {2}} \dot {r} ^ {2} + \frac {2 m}{a ^ {2}} \frac {c ^ {2}}{r ^ {2}} + \frac {m g}{a} \end{array}\tag{5}
$$

由机械能守恒，并用式(1)、(3)，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + \frac {c ^ {2}}{r ^ {2}} + \frac {4 r ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}} \dot {r} ^ {2}) + m g \frac {r ^ {2}}{a} = E \\ & \dot {r} ^ {2} = \frac {\frac {2 E}{m} - 2 g \frac {r ^ {2}}{a} - \frac {c ^ {2}}{r ^ {2}}}{1 + \frac {4 r ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}}} \end{array}\tag{6}
$$

将式(6)代入式(5)得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {- \left(1 + \frac {4 r ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}}\right) \lambda = \frac {1}{1 + \frac {4 r ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}}} \cdot \frac {2 m}{a ^ {2}} \Big (\frac {2 E}{m} + \frac {4 c ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}} + \frac {g a}{2} \Big)} \\ & {\qquad \qquad \qquad \lambda = \text {常量} \cdot \Big (1 + \frac {4 r ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}} \Big) ^ {- 2}} \end{array}\tag{7}
$$

约束力 $F=2\lambda re_{r}-a\lambda k$ 

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {| F | = \sqrt {(2 \lambda r) ^ {2} + (- a \lambda) ^ {2}} = \lambda (a ^ {2} + 4 r ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2}} \\ & {\qquad = \text {常量} \cdot \left(1 + \frac {4 r ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}}\right) ^ {- 2} \cdot (a ^ {2} + 4 r ^ {2}) ^ {1 / 2} = \text {常量} \cdot \left(1 + \frac {4 r ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}}\right) ^ {- 3 / 2}} \end{array}
$$

注意： $\lambda<0$ （可从式(7)作此结论）.

约束力 $\pmb{F}$ 与竖直向上方向的夹角的正切为

$$
\tan \alpha = \frac {- F _ {r}}{F _ {z}} = \frac {- 2 \lambda r}{- a \lambda} = \frac {2 r}{a} = \frac {\mathrm{d} z}{\mathrm{d} r}
$$

$\frac{\mathrm{d}z}{\mathrm{d}r}$ 是 $rz$ 平面内抛物线 $z = \frac{1}{a} r^2$ 的切线的斜率. 可见, 约束力沿旋转抛物面的法线方向, 指向抛物面的旋转对称轴.

9.3.47 一个肥皂泡的半径 r 按 $r = at + b$ (其中 a、b 为常量) 的规律随时间变化, 一个质量为 m 的质点保持在表面上运动. t = 0 时, 给质点一个沿切向的速度 v, 不考虑重力的作用, 求质点的运动规律及表面对质点的作用力 (限用带未定乘子的拉格朗日方程).

解 取球坐标, 且使质点在 t=0 时位于 $\theta=\frac{\pi}{2},\varphi=0,v=b\dot{\varphi}_{0}$ .

$$
\begin{array}{l} L = T = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}) \\ \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {r}} = m \dot {r}, \quad \frac {\partial L}{\partial r} = m r \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + m r \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta \\ \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\theta}} = m r ^ {2} \dot {\theta}, \quad \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\theta}}\right) = m r ^ {2} \ddot {\theta} + 2 m \dot {r} \dot {\theta} \\ \frac {\partial L}{\partial \theta} = m r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \sin \theta \cos \theta , \quad \frac {\partial L}{\partial \varphi} = 0 \end{array}
$$

约束方程：

$$
r = a t + b, \quad \dot {r} = a, \quad \delta r = 0
$$

带未定乘子的拉格朗日方程为

$$
m \ddot {r} - m r \dot {\theta} ^ {2} - m r \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta = \lambda\tag{1}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & m r ^ {2} \ddot {\theta} + 2 m r \dot {r} \dot {\theta} - m r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \sin \theta \cos \theta = 0 \\ & m r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} \sin^ {2} \theta = m b ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \frac {\pi}{2} \cdot \frac {v}{b} \\ & r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} \sin^ {2} \theta = b v \end{array}\tag{2}
$$

即

(3) 

假设任何时刻 $\theta = \frac{\pi}{2} (t = 0$ 时， $\theta = \frac{\pi}{2})$ ，代入式(2)

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & r ^ {2} \ddot {\theta} + 2 r \dot {r} \dot {\theta} = 0 \\ & \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} (r ^ {2} \dot {\theta}) = 0 \end{array}
$$

$$
r ^ {2} \dot {\theta} = \text {常量} = b ^ {2} \cdot 0 = 0
$$

这里用了 t=0 时， $r=b,\dot{\theta}=0$ ，

$$
\dot {\theta} = 0
$$

可见在任何时刻确有 $\theta = \frac{\pi}{2}$ , 且 $\dot{\theta} = 0, \ddot{\theta} = 0$ .

将 $\theta = \frac{\pi}{2}$ 代入式(3)，

$$
\begin{array}{c} r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} = b v \\ \int_ {0} ^ {\varphi} \mathrm{d} \varphi = \int \frac {b v}{r ^ {2}} \mathrm{d} t = \int_ {0} ^ {t} \frac {b v}{(a t + b) ^ {2}} \mathrm{d} t \\ \varphi = - \left. \frac {b v}{a (a t + b)} \right| _ {0} ^ {t} = \frac {v}{a} - \frac {b v}{a (a t + b)} = \frac {v}{a} \left(1 - \frac {b}{r}\right) \end{array}\tag{4}
$$

质点的运动规律总结如下：

$$
r = a t + b, \quad \theta = \frac {\pi}{2}, \quad \varphi = \frac {v}{a} \left(1 - \frac {b}{r}\right)
$$

将式(4)代入式(1)，注意 $\ddot{r} = 0, \theta = \frac{\pi}{2}, \dot{\theta} = 0$ ，即得约束力

$$
\lambda = - m r \left(\frac {b v}{r ^ {2}}\right) ^ {2} = - \frac {m b ^ {2} v ^ {2}}{r ^ {3}}
$$

负值说明约束力为拉力.

9.3.48 一个质量为 $m$ 的质点被约束沿一弯成半径为 $a$ 的水平圆形金属丝上运动, 摩擦因数为 $\mu$ , 开始速率为 $v_{0}$ , 若不计重力的作用, 求质点的角位置与时间的关系, 以及金属丝对质点的作用力.

解

$$
\begin{array}{r l} L & = T = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}) \\ r & = a, \quad \delta r = 0 \\ f & = r - a = 0, \quad \frac {\partial f}{\partial r} = 1, \quad \frac {\partial f}{\partial \varphi} = 0 \end{array}
$$

或

由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L}{\partial q_i}\right) - \frac{\partial L}{\partial q_i} = Q_i + \lambda \frac{\partial f}{\partial q_i},$ 今 $Q_{r} = 0$ 

$$
m \ddot {r} - m r \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} = \lambda\tag{1}
$$

$$
m r ^ {2} \ddot {\varphi} + 2 m r \dot {r} \dot {\varphi} = - \mu | \lambda | r\tag{2}
$$

$$
r = a\tag{3}
$$

其中 $Q_{\varphi}=-\mu|\lambda|r$ 来自摩擦力.

由式(3)、式(1)、(2)简化为

$$
- m a \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} = \lambda
$$

$$
m a \ddot {\varphi} = - \mu | \lambda | = \mu \lambda
$$

(由前式可见, $\lambda<0$ )

消去 $\lambda$ 得

$$
\ddot {\varphi} = - \mu \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}
$$

用 $\dot{\varphi} = \frac{\mathrm{d}\dot{\varphi}}{\mathrm{d}\varphi}\dot{\varphi}$ ，可将上式积分，并用初始条件： $t = 0$ 时， $\varphi = 0,\dot{\varphi} = \frac{v_0}{a}$ 

$$
\begin{array}{l} - \int_ {\frac {v _ {0}}{a}} ^ {\dot {\varphi}} \frac {\mathrm{d} \dot {\varphi}}{\dot {\varphi}} = \int_ {0} ^ {\varphi} \mu \mathrm{d} \varphi \\ - \ln \dot {\varphi} + \ln \frac {v _ {0}}{a} = \mu \varphi \\ \dot {\varphi} = \frac {v _ {0}}{a} \mathrm{e} ^ {- \mu \varphi} \\ \int_ {0} ^ {\varphi} \mathrm{e} ^ {\mu \varphi} \mathrm{d} \varphi = \int_ {0} ^ {t} \frac {v _ {0}}{a} \mathrm{d} t \\ \varphi = \frac {1}{\mu} \ln \left(1 + \frac {\mu v _ {0}}{a} t\right) \end{array}
$$

金属丝对质点的作用力为

$$
\begin{array}{l} \boldsymbol {F} = F _ {r} \boldsymbol {e} _ {r} + F _ {\varphi} \boldsymbol {e} _ {\varphi} \\ F _ {r} = \lambda = - m a \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} = \frac {m a v _ {0} ^ {2}}{(a + \mu v _ {0} t) ^ {2}} \\ F _ {\varphi} = - \mu | \lambda | = \mu F _ {r} \end{array}
$$

9.3.49 一根光滑的金属丝弯成一螺旋线, 其方程为 $z = a\varphi, \rho = b, a, b$ 为常量, $\rho, \varphi, z$ 为柱坐标, 原点为引力中心, 引力大小与距离成正比, 比例系数为 $k$ , 有一质量为 $m$ 的珠子在金属丝上做无摩擦滑动. 求金属丝对珠子的作用力的 $\rho, \varphi, z$ 分量 (不考虑重力的作用).

解

$$
\begin{array}{r l} L & = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {\rho} ^ {2} + \rho^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2}) \\ \rho & = b, \quad \delta \rho = 0 \\ z & = a \varphi , \quad \delta z - a \delta \varphi = 0 \\ r & = \rho e _ {\rho} + z k \\ Q _ {\rho} & = - k \rho , \quad Q _ {z} = - k z \end{array}
$$

带未定乘子的拉格朗日方程为

$$
m (\ddot {\rho} - \rho \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}) = - k \rho + \lambda\tag{1}
$$

$$
m (\rho^ {2} \ddot {\varphi} + 2 \rho \dot {\rho} \dot {\varphi}) = - a \mu
$$

$$
m \ddot {z} = - k z + \mu\tag{2}
$$

(3) 

$$
\rho = b\tag{4}
$$

$$
z = a \varphi\tag{5}
$$

用式(4)、(5)，式(1)、(2)可简化为

$$
- m \frac {b}{a ^ {2}} \dot {z} ^ {2} = - k b + \lambda\tag{6}
$$

$$
m \frac {b ^ {2}}{a} \ddot {z} = - a \mu\tag{7}
$$

由式(3)、(7)消去 $\mu$ ，可得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & m \left(1 + \frac {b ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}}\right) \ddot {z} + k z = 0 \\ & z = A \cos (\omega t + \alpha) \end{array}\tag{8}
$$

其中 $\omega = a\sqrt{\frac{k}{m(a^2 + b^2)}}, A, \alpha$ 由初始条件确定，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & F _ {\rho} = \lambda = k b - \frac {m b}{a ^ {2}} \dot {z} ^ {2} \\ & F _ {\varphi} = - \frac {a}{b} \mu = - \frac {a}{b} \left(- m \frac {b ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}} \ddot {z}\right) = \frac {m b}{a} \ddot {z} \\ & F _ {z} = \mu = m \ddot {z} + k z \end{array}
$$

将式(8)代入,即可得 $F_{\rho}$ 、 $F_{\varphi}$ 、 $F_{z}$ 与t的关系.

9.3.50 一个质量为 $m$ 的质点在一个光滑的抛物面[其方程为 $x^{2} + y^{2} = a(a - z)$ , 其中 $a$ 是常量, $z$ 轴竖直向上]的顶端从静止开始下滑, 选柱坐标 $\rho, \varphi, z$ 为广义坐标, 写出含未定乘子的拉格朗日方程, 并求出约束力, 在何处质点离开抛物面?

解

$$
\begin{array}{r l} L & = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {\rho} ^ {2} + \rho^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2}) - m g z \\ f & = \rho^ {2} - a (a - z) = 0 \\ \frac {\partial f}{\partial \rho} & = 2 \rho , \quad \frac {\partial f}{\partial \varphi} = 0, \quad \frac {\partial f}{\partial z} = a \end{array}
$$

由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L}{\partial\dot{q}_i}\right) - \frac{\partial L}{\partial q_i} = Q_i + \lambda \frac{\partial f}{\partial q_i}$ , 含未定乘子的拉格朗日方程为

$$
m (\ddot {\rho} - \rho \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}) = 2 \lambda \rho\tag{1}
$$

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} (m \rho^ {2} \dot {\varphi}) = 0\tag{2}
$$

$$
m \ddot {z} = - m g + a \lambda\tag{3}
$$

$$
\rho^ {2} = a (a - z)\tag{4}
$$

积分式(2)，用初始条件：t=0 时， $\rho=0$ ，得

$$
\dot {\varphi} = 0
$$

式(1)简化为

$$
m \ddot {\rho} = 2 \lambda \rho\tag{5}
$$

由式(4)

$$
z = a - \frac {1}{a} \rho^ {2}, \quad \dot {z} = - \frac {2 \rho}{a} \dot {\rho}
$$

$$
\ddot {z} = - \frac {2 \rho}{a} \ddot {\rho} - \frac {2}{a} \dot {\rho} ^ {2}\tag{6}
$$

用式(6)，式(3)可改写为

$$
\frac {2 m}{a} \rho \ddot {\rho} + \frac {2 m}{a} \dot {\rho} ^ {2} - m g = - a \lambda\tag{7}
$$

式(5)、(7)消去 $\lambda$ ，可得

$$
\left(\frac {2}{a} \rho^ {2} + \frac {a}{2}\right) \ddot {\rho} + \frac {2}{a} \rho \dot {\rho} ^ {2} - g \rho = 0
$$

$$
\frac {1}{2} \left(\frac {2}{a} \rho^ {2} + \frac {a}{2}\right) d \dot {\rho} ^ {2} + \frac {2}{a} \rho \dot {\rho} ^ {2} d \rho - g \rho d \rho = 0
$$

$$
\mathrm{d} \left[ \frac {1}{2} \left(\frac {2}{a} \rho^ {2} + \frac {a}{2}\right) \dot {\rho} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} g \rho^ {2} \right] = 0
$$

用初始条件: t=0 时, $\rho=0,\dot{\rho}=0$ , 可得

$$
\left(\frac {2}{a} \rho^ {2} + \frac {a}{2}\right) \dot {\rho} ^ {2} = g \rho^ {2}
$$

$$
\dot {\rho} = \sqrt {2 a _ {B}} \frac {\rho}{\sqrt {4 \rho^ {2} + a ^ {2}}}\tag{8}
$$

对上式求导,可得

$$
\ddot {\rho} = \frac {2 a ^ {3} g \rho}{(4 \rho^ {2} + a ^ {2}) ^ {2}}
$$

$$
F _ {\rho} = 2 \lambda \rho = m \ddot {\rho} = \frac {2 m a ^ {3} g \rho}{\left(4 \rho^ {2} + a ^ {2}\right) ^ {2}}
$$

$$
F _ {\varphi} = 0,
$$

$$
F _ {z} = a \lambda = a \frac {F _ {\rho}}{2 \rho} = \frac {m a ^ {4} g}{\left(4 \rho^ {2} + a ^ {2}\right) ^ {2}}
$$

质点对抛物面的正压力 $N$ 是约束力的反作用力，

$$
N = \sqrt {F _ {\rho} ^ {2} + F _ {z} ^ {2} + F _ {\varphi} ^ {2}} = \frac {m a ^ {3} g}{(4 \rho^ {2} + a ^ {2}) ^ {3 / 2}} > 0
$$

不会出现 $N = 0$ 处，故质点不会离开抛物面.对式(8)积分可得 $\rho = \rho (t)$ ，因而也可得 $F_{\rho}$ $F_{z}$ 与 $t$ 的关系.

9.3.51 一个质量为 $m$ 、半径为 $a$ 的均质圆柱体 $A$ 在一个固定的、半径为 $b$ 的圆柱体 $B$ 的顶部处于静止的状态，两圆柱体的轴平行且水平，让圆柱体 $A$ 稍偏离其平衡位置做无滑动滚动下圆柱体 $B$ . 用带未定乘子的拉格朗日方程求约束力，并找出两圆柱体分离的位置.

解 取极坐标 $r, \theta$ 表示圆柱 $A$ 的轴线位置，原点在固定圆柱 $B$ 的轴线上，图9.77中 $\varphi$ 表示圆柱 $A$ 的转角，用 $r, \theta, \varphi$ 为广义坐标，

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/ec8a18a6ec8f30ce147313ee01c5cb7152e10189e1ab22178b0a3b6ccb7574ea.jpg)


$$
T = \frac {1}{2} m \left(\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2}\right) + \frac {1}{2} \times \frac {1}{2} m a ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}
$$

$$
V = m g r \cos \theta
$$

约束方程

$$
L = T - V = \frac {1}{2} m \left(\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2}\right) + \frac {1}{4} m a ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} - m g r \cos \theta
$$


图9.77


$$
f _ {1} = r - (a + b) = 0
$$

$$
f _ {2} = (a + b) \theta - a \varphi = 0
$$

后一关系来自纯滚动条件：

$$
(a + b) \dot {\theta} - a \dot {\varphi} = 0
$$

选取 $\varphi$ 的零点, $\theta=0$ 时, $\varphi=0$ , 积分即得.

由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L}{\partial q_i}\right) - \frac{\partial L}{\partial q_i} = Q_i + \lambda \frac{\partial f_1}{\partial q_i} + \mu \frac{\partial f_2}{\partial q_i}$ 及 $Q_{i} = 0$ ，可得

$$
m \left(\ddot {r} - r \dot {\theta} ^ {2}\right) + m g \cos \theta = \lambda\tag{1}
$$

$$
m \left(r ^ {2} \ddot {\theta} + 2 r \dot {r} \dot {\theta}\right) - m g r \sin \theta = (a + b) \mu\tag{2}
$$

$$
\frac {1}{2} m a ^ {2} \ddot {\varphi} = - a \mu\tag{3}
$$

$$
r = a + b\tag{4}
$$

$$
(a + b) \theta - a \varphi = 0\tag{5}
$$

用式(4)、(5)，式(1)、(2)、(3)可简化为

$$
- m (a + b) \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + m g \cos \theta = \lambda\tag{6}
$$

$$
m (a + b) \ddot {\theta} - m g \sin \theta = \mu\tag{7}
$$

$$
\frac {1}{2} m (a + b) \ddot {\theta} = - \mu\tag{8}
$$

由式(7)、(8)消去 $\mu$ ，得

$$
\frac {3}{2} (a + b) \ddot {\theta} = g \sin \dot {\theta}
$$

$$
\frac {2}{3} (a + b) \dot {\theta} \mathrm{d} \dot {\theta} = g \sin \theta \mathrm{d} \theta
$$

积分上式，用初始条件 $\theta=0$ 时， $\dot{\theta}=0$ ，

$$
\frac {3}{4} (a + b) \dot {\theta} ^ {2} = g (1 - \cos \theta)
$$

$$
\dot {\theta} ^ {2} = \frac {4 g}{3 (a + b)} (1 - \cos \theta)\tag{9}
$$

$$
F _ {r} = \lambda = m g \cos \theta - m (a + b) \dot {\theta} ^ {2} = \frac {1}{3} m g (7 \cos \theta - 4)
$$

式(9)两边对 t 求导，

$$
2 \dot {\theta} \ddot {\theta} = \frac {4 g}{3 (a + b)} \sin \theta \dot {\theta}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & \ddot {\theta} = \frac {2 g}{3 (a + b)} \sin \theta \\ & F _ {\theta} = \mu = m (a + b) \ddot {\theta} - m g \sin \theta = - \frac {1}{3} m g \sin \theta \end{array}
$$

对式(9)积分可得 $\theta=\theta(t)$ ，从而也可得到 $F_{r}, F_{\theta}$ 与 t 的关系.

两圆柱体分离时, $F_{r}=0$ ,

$$
7 \cos \theta - 4 = 0
$$

可见，在 $\theta = \arccos \left(\frac{4}{7}\right)$ 处两圆柱体分离.

9.3.52 一个质量为 $m$ 、半径为 $a$ 的均质圆盘，受到约束，保持盘面铅直地在一完全粗糙的水平面上滚动，受到一个力 $f$ 作用，作用线通过盘心， $f = f_{1}i + f_{2}j, i, j$ 分别是水平面上的 $x, y$ 轴的单位矢量， $z$ 轴垂直于水平面。取固连于圆盘的坐标系 $x', y', z'$ ，原点位于盘心 $C, x'y'$ 轴在盘面上， $z'$ 轴垂直于盘面，取 $z', z$ 轴的夹角为 $\theta, \varphi$ 是 $i$ 和 $k \times k'(k'$ 是 $z'$ 轴的单位矢量）间的夹角， $\psi$ 是 $i'$ 和 $k \times k'$ 的夹角， $\theta, \varphi, \psi$ 是欧拉角，选质心 $C$ 的 $x, y$ 坐标和 $\varphi, \psi$ 为广义坐标，写出盘面的运动微分方程。

解

$$
\begin{array}{r l} L & = T = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{2} I _ {1} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} I _ {3} \dot {\psi} ^ {2} \\ & = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{8} m a ^ {2} (\dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + 2 \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}) \end{array}
$$

圆盘做纯滚动，盘面与水平面接触点的速度为零，

$$
\dot {x} \boldsymbol {i} + \dot {y} \boldsymbol {j} + \dot {\psi} \boldsymbol {k} ^ {\prime} \times (- a \boldsymbol {k}) = 0
$$

图 9.78 中 xy 平面是水平面, z 轴垂直纸面向上, $\varphi$ 是 i 和 $k \times k'$ 间的夹角, 如图中所示,

$$
\boldsymbol {k} ^ {\prime} = \sin \varphi \boldsymbol {i} - \cos \varphi \boldsymbol {j}
$$

所以

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/0a0b471c40e3e7b3c96fc661a955f3ac8bacff98f7f3dee2b54f76c3645632db.jpg)



图9.78


$$
Q _ {x} = f _ {1}, \quad Q _ {y} = f _ {2}, \quad Q _ {\varphi} = Q _ {\psi} = 0
$$

由带未定乘子的拉格朗日方程得运动微分方程为

$$
m \ddot {x} = f _ {1} + \lambda_ {1}
$$

$$
m \ddot {y} = f _ {2} + \lambda_ {2}
$$

$$
\frac {1}{4} m a ^ {2} \ddot {\varphi} = 0
$$

$$
\frac {1}{2} m a ^ {2} \ddot {\psi} = \lambda_ {1} a \cos \varphi + \lambda_ {2} a \sin \varphi
$$

加上两个约束方程

$$
\begin{array}{l} \dot {x} + a \dot {\psi} \cos \varphi = 0 \\ \dot {y} + a \dot {\psi} \sin \varphi = 0 \end{array}
$$

9.3.53 一个质量为 $m$ 的质点, 受到一个力 $F = F_{x}i + F_{y}j + F_{z}k$ 作用以及一个非完整约束 $\dot{x} - t\dot{y} = a$ , 其中 $a$ 为常量, 求运动微分方程.

解

$$
L = T = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + y ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2})
$$

$$
Q _ {x} = F _ {x}, Q _ {y} = F _ {y}, Q _ {z} = F _ {z}
$$

$$
\dot {x} - t \dot {y} = a
$$

$$
\mathrm{d} x - t \mathrm{d} y = a \mathrm{d} t
$$

$$
\delta x - t \delta y = 0
$$

运动微分方程为

$$
m \ddot {x} = F _ {x} + \lambda\tag{1}
$$

$$
m \ddot {y} = F _ {y} - \lambda t\tag{2}
$$

$$
m \ddot {z} = F _ {z}\tag{3}
$$

$$
\dot {x} - t \dot {y} = a\tag{4}
$$

由前两式消去 $\lambda$ ，可得

$$
m t \ddot {x} + m \ddot {y} = F _ {x} t + F _ {y}\tag{5}
$$

运动微分方程可用式(5)、(3)和(4).

## 9.4 冲击运动 机电模拟

9.4.1 用拉格朗日方程解7.4.33题.

解 用 $y_{A}, y_{B}$ 分别表示 $A$ 杆、 $B$ 杆质心的 $y$ 坐标， $\dot{\varphi}_{A}, \dot{\varphi}_{B}$ 分别是 $A$ 杆、 $B$ 杆绕质心的角速度，正方向规定如图9.79所示。

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/0e3512f61bc0ed2811eb1e5a63d91764c7155f43da9d381a3dad80387fdcc971.jpg)



图9.79


$A$ 杆和 $B$ 杆的拉格朗日函数分别为

$$
L _ {A} = \frac {1}{2} \dot {y} _ {A} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \times \frac {1}{1 2} \dot {\varphi} _ {A} ^ {2} = \frac {1}{2} \dot {y} _ {A} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 4} \dot {\varphi} _ {A} ^ {2},
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} L _ {B} & = \frac {1}{2} \times 2 \dot {y} _ {B} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \times \frac {1}{1 2} \times 2 \dot {\varphi} _ {B} ^ {2} \\ & = \dot {y} _ {B} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{1 2} \dot {\varphi} _ {B} ^ {2} \end{array}
$$

$$
p _ {y _ {A}} = \frac {\partial L _ {A}}{\partial \dot {y} _ {A}} = \dot {y} _ {A}, \quad p _ {\varphi_ {A}} = \frac {\partial L _ {A}}{\partial \dot {\varphi} _ {A}} = \frac {1}{1 2} \dot {\varphi} _ {A}
$$

$$
p _ {y _ {B}} = \frac {\partial L _ {B}}{\partial \dot {y} _ {B}} = 2 \dot {y} _ {B}, \quad p _ {\varphi_ {B}} = \frac {\partial L _ {B}}{\partial \dot {\varphi} _ {B}} = \frac {1}{6} \dot {\varphi} _ {B}
$$

碰撞期间,两杆受到的冲量 I 如图 9.79 所示,

$$
y _ {P} = y _ {B} + \frac {1}{2} \varphi_ {B}, \quad y _ {Q} = y _ {A} - \frac {1}{2} \varphi_ {A}
$$

$$
K y _ {B} = I \frac {\partial y _ {P}}{\partial y _ {B}} = I, \quad K _ {\varphi_ {B}} = I \frac {\partial y _ {P}}{\partial \varphi_ {B}} = \frac {1}{2} I
$$

$$
K _ {y _ {A}} = - I \frac {\partial y _ {Q}}{\partial y _ {A}} = - I, \quad K _ {\varphi_ {A}} = - I \frac {\partial y _ {Q}}{\partial \varphi_ {A}} = \frac {1}{2} I
$$

由冲击运动的拉格朗日方程

$$
\Delta p _ {i} = K _ {i}
$$

$$
2 \dot {y} _ {B} - 0 = I, \quad \frac {1}{6} \dot {\varphi} _ {B} - 0 = \frac {1}{2} I
$$

$$
\dot {y} _ {A} - 1 0 = - I, \quad \frac {1}{1 2} \dot {\varphi} _ {A} - 0 = \frac {1}{2} I
$$

由于作弹性碰撞,机械能守恒,碰撞前后势能没变化,故动能相等.

$$
\frac {1}{2} \dot {y} _ {A} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \times \frac {1}{1 2} \dot {\varphi} _ {A} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \times 2 \dot {y} _ {B} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \times \frac {1}{1 2} \times 2 \dot {\varphi} _ {B} ^ {2} = \frac {1}{2} \times 1 0 ^ {2}
$$

从上述五个式子可以解出 $I, \dot{y}_A, \dot{\varphi}_A, \dot{y}_B, \dot{\varphi}_B$ ，得

$$
\dot {y} _ {A} = \frac {2 0}{3} \mathrm{ms} ^ {- 1}, \quad \dot {\varphi} _ {A} = 2 0 \mathrm{rads} ^ {- 1}
$$

$$
\dot {y} _ {B} = \frac {5}{3} \mathrm{ms} ^ {- 1}, \quad \dot {\varphi} _ {B} = 1 0 \mathrm{rads} ^ {- 1}
$$

9.4.2 用拉格朗日方程解7.4.34题.

解 先考虑球与台阶的碰撞过程,取球心的坐标 x、y 和绕球心的转角 $\varphi$ (顺时针方向为正) 为广义坐标,如图 9.80 所示,图中还画出了在碰撞过程中球受到台阶的冲量 $I_{x}$ 、 $I_{y}$ .

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/6493bbbf097ce77ce1d97f7b8b9d6872e19ecd97bd7bc000c04d74230875fff3.jpg)



图9.80


设球的质量为 $m$ 

$$
L = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{2} \cdot \frac {2}{5} m a ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} - m g y
$$

受到冲力的球上 A 点的 x、y 坐标为

$$
x _ {A} = x + a \cos \varphi
$$

$$
y _ {A} = y - a \sin \varphi
$$

$$
\delta x _ {A} = \delta x - a \sin \varphi \delta \varphi
$$

$$
\delta y _ {A} = \delta y - a \cos \varphi \delta \varphi
$$

$$
K _ {x} \delta x + K _ {y} \delta y + K _ {\varphi} \delta \varphi = I _ {x} \delta x _ {A} + I _ {y} \delta y _ {A}
$$

$$
= I _ {x} \delta x + I _ {y} \delta y - a (I _ {x} \sin \varphi + I _ {y} \cos \varphi) \delta \varphi
$$

所以

$$
K _ {x} = I _ {x}, \quad K _ {y} = I _ {y}
$$

$$
K _ {\varphi} = - a (I _ {x} \sin \varphi + I _ {y} \cos \varphi)
$$

$$
p _ {x} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {x}} = m \dot {x}, \quad p _ {y} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {y}} = m \dot {y}
$$

$$
p _ {\varphi} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\varphi}} = \frac {2}{5} m a ^ {2} \dot {\varphi}
$$

碰撞前 $p_{x0} = mv, \quad p_{y0} = 0, \quad p_{\varphi 0} = \frac{2}{5} mav$ ，这里用了纯滚动条件：

$$
v - a \dot {\varphi} _ {0} = 0, \quad \dot {\varphi} _ {0} = \frac {v}{a}
$$

由 $\Delta p_{i} = K_{i}$ 

$$
m \dot {x} - m v = I _ {x}\tag{1}
$$

$$
m \dot {y} - 0 = I _ {y}\tag{2}
$$

$$
\frac {2}{5} m a ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} - \frac {2}{5} m a v = - a (I _ {x} \sin \varphi + I _ {y} \cos \varphi)\tag{3}
$$

碰撞期间位置变化可以忽略, $\Delta q_{i}=0$ ,所以

$$
\sin \varphi = \frac {a - h}{a}\tag{4}
$$

$$
\cos \varphi = \sqrt {1 - \sin^ {2} \varphi} = \frac {1}{a} \sqrt {2 a h - h ^ {2}}\tag{5}
$$

再考虑做完全非弹性碰撞,碰撞后球上 A 点速度为零,

$$
\dot {x} \boldsymbol {i} + \dot {y} \boldsymbol {j} + (- \dot {\varphi}) \boldsymbol {k} \times (a \cos \varphi \boldsymbol {i} - a \sin \varphi \boldsymbol {j}) = 0
$$

由此可得

$$
\dot {x} = a \dot {\varphi} \sin \varphi\tag{6}
$$

$$
\dot {y} = a \dot {\varphi} \cos \varphi\tag{7}
$$

从上述七个式子可解出碰撞后的 $\dot{\varphi}$ 为

$$
\dot {\varphi} = \frac {7 a - 5 h}{7 a ^ {2}} v
$$

再考虑碰撞后跃上台阶的过程,这是个定轴转动,拉格朗日函数为

$$
L = \frac {1}{2} \left(\frac {2}{5} m a ^ {2} + m a ^ {2}\right) \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} - m g a \sin \varphi
$$

由

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\varphi}}\right) - \frac {\partial L}{\partial \varphi} = 0
$$

可得

$$
\frac {7}{5} a \ddot {\varphi} = - g \cos \varphi
$$

$$
\frac {7}{5} a \dot {\varphi} d \dot {\varphi} = - g \cos \varphi d \varphi
$$

做上述积分时,积分下限分别为 $\varphi=\arcsin\left(\frac{a-h}{a}\right)$ , $\dot{\varphi}=\frac{7a-5h}{7a^{2}}v$ , 积分上限是刚能跃上台阶的要求: $\varphi=\frac{\pi}{2}$ 时, $\dot{\varphi}=0$ ,

$$
\left. \frac {7}{1 0} a \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \right| _ {\dot {\varphi} = \frac {7 a - 5 h}{7 a ^ {2}} v} ^ {\dot {\varphi} = 0} = - g \sin \varphi \Bigg | _ {\varphi = \arcsin \left(\frac {a - h}{a}\right)} ^ {\varphi = \frac {\pi}{2}}
$$

可得

$$
\begin{array}{l} \frac {7 a - 5 h}{7 a ^ {2}} v = \sqrt {\frac {1 0 g h}{7 a ^ {2}}} \\ v = \frac {a}{7 a - 5 h} \sqrt {7 0 g h} \end{array}
$$

可见，当 $v > \frac{a}{7a - 5h}\sqrt{70gh}$ 时，球能跃上台阶.

9.4.3 一个由质量为 $m$ 和 $M$ 的两个质点用一根不计质量、长 $a$ 的棒构成的哑铃开始处于静止状态，突然在棒上距 $Mb$ 处受到一个垂直于棒的冲量 $I$ ，求作用后哑铃的运动状态。

解 系统的质心位于棒上离 M 的距离 $\frac{ma}{m+M}$ 处，绕通过质心垂直于棒的轴转动的转动惯量为

$$
M \left(\frac {m a}{m + M}\right) ^ {2} + m \left(\frac {M a}{m + M}\right) ^ {2} = \frac {m M}{m + M} a ^ {2}
$$

取质心的坐标 x、y 和棒与 y 轴的夹角 $\varphi$ 为广义坐标，y 轴沿静止时棒的方向，x 轴沿冲量方向，如图 9.81 所示，C 为质心，P 是冲量作用点，

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/7eb5dff57a00686a51947de93becf6736ee81112407cc429ab6684664a3788e1.jpg)


$$
\begin{array}{r l} L & = T = \frac {1}{2} (m + M) (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{2} \cdot \frac {m M}{m + M} a ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \\ & p _ {x} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {x}} = (m + M) \dot {x} \\ & p _ {y} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {y}} = (m + M) \dot {y} \\ & p _ {\varphi} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\varphi}} = \frac {m M}{m + M} a ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} \\ & x _ {P} = x + \left(b - \frac {m a}{m + M}\right) \sin \varphi \\ & \delta x _ {P} = \delta x + \left(b - \frac {m a}{m + M}\right) \cos \varphi \delta \varphi \\ & I \delta x _ {p} = I \delta x + I \left(b - \frac {m a}{m + M}\right) \cos \varphi \delta \varphi \end{array}
$$

图9.81

$$
K _ {x} = I, \quad K _ {y} = 0
$$

$$
K _ {\varphi} = I \left(b - \frac {m a}{m + M}\right) \cos \varphi \Big | _ {\varphi = 0} = I \left(b - \frac {m a}{m + M}\right)
$$

由 $\Delta p_{i} = K_{i}$ 

$$
\Delta p _ {x} = (m + M) \dot {x} = I
$$

$$
\Delta P _ {y} = (m + M) \dot {y} = 0
$$

$$
\Delta p _ {\varphi} = \frac {m M}{m + M} a ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} = \left(b - \frac {m a}{m + M}\right) I
$$

得

$$
\dot {x} = \frac {I}{m + M}, \quad \dot {y} = 0
$$

$$
\dot {\varphi} = \frac {(m + M) b - m a}{m M a ^ {2}} I
$$

9.4.4 质量为 $m_{1}$ 与 $m_{2}$ 的两粒子被一劲度系数为 k 的轻弹簧连接, 静止在光滑的水平面上.

(1) 突然沿从 $m_{1}$ 到 $m_{2}$ 的方向给予 $m_{1}$ 一个冲量 I，问 $m_{2}$ 在碰撞后第一次到达静止之前运行多远；

(2) 是否可能给 $m_{1}$ 一冲量使系统由静止变成无振动的转动？

解（1）沿从 $m_{1}$ 到 $m_{2}$ 的方向取 $x_{1}$ 轴和 $x_{2}$ 轴，原点分别取在两粒子的平衡位置，

$$
L = \frac {1}{2} m _ {1} \dot {x} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m _ {2} \dot {x} _ {2} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k (x _ {2} - x _ {1}) ^ {2}
$$

$$
p _ {1} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {x} _ {1}} = m _ {1} \dot {x} _ {1}, \quad p _ {2} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {x} _ {2}} = m _ {2} \dot {x} _ {2}
$$

$$
k _ {1} = I, \quad k _ {2} = 0
$$

$$
\Delta p _ {1} = m _ {1} \dot {x} _ {1} = I, \quad \dot {x} _ {1} = \frac {I}{m _ {1}}
$$

$$
\Delta p _ {2} = m _ {2} \dot {x} _ {2} = 0, \quad \dot {x} _ {2} = 0
$$

因为

$$
\Delta x _ {1} = 0, \quad \Delta x _ {2} = 0
$$

所以

$$
x _ {1} = 0, \quad x _ {2} = 0
$$

这是冲量作用后系统的运动状态,是以后运动的初始条件,即 t=0 时, $x_{1}=x_{2}=0,\dot{x}_{1}=\frac{I}{m_{1}}.\dot{x}_{2}=0.$ 

以后的运动微分方程由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L}{\partial x_i}\right) - \frac{\partial L}{\partial x_i} = 0$ 得

$$
m _ {1} \ddot {x} _ {1} - k (x _ {2} - x _ {1}) = 0\tag{1}
$$

$$
m _ {2} \ddot {x} _ {2} + k (x _ {2} - x _ {2}) = 0\tag{2}
$$

式(1)+式(2)得

$$
m _ {1} \ddot {x} _ {1} + m _ {2} \ddot {x} _ {2} = 0
$$

积分上式,用初始条件,可得

$$
\begin{array}{l} m _ {1} \dot {x} _ {1} + m _ {2} \dot {x} _ {2} = I \\ m _ {1} x _ {1} + m _ {2} x _ {2} = I t \end{array}\tag{3}
$$

式(2) $\times m_{1}$ —式(1) $\times m_{2}$ ，可得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & \frac {m _ {1} m _ {2}}{m _ {1} + m _ {2}} (\ddot {x} _ {2} - \ddot {x} _ {1}) + k (x _ {2} - x _ {1}) = 0 \\ & \qquad x _ {2} - x _ {1} = A \sin (\omega t + \alpha) \end{array}
$$

其中

$$
\omega = \sqrt {\frac {k (m _ {1} + m _ {2})}{m _ {1} m _ {2}}}
$$

由初始条件: t=0 时, $x_{1}=0$ , $x_{2}=0$ , $\dot{x}_{1}=\frac{I}{m_{1}}$ , $\dot{x}_{2}=0$ , 定出

$$
\alpha = 0, \quad A = - \frac {I}{m _ {1} \omega}
$$

$$
x _ {2} - x _ {1} = - \frac {I}{m _ {1} \omega} \sin \omega t\tag{4}
$$

式(3)+式(4) $\times m_{1}$ ，可得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} x _ {2} & = \frac {I t}{m _ {1} + m _ {2}} - \frac {I}{(m _ {1} + m _ {2}) \omega} \sin \omega t \\ \dot {x} _ {2} & = \frac {I}{m _ {1} + m _ {2}} - \frac {I}{m _ {1} + m _ {2}} \cos \omega t \end{array}
$$

碰撞后 $m_{2}$ 瞬时静止时有 $\dot{x}_2 = 0$ ，即

$$
\cos \omega t = 1
$$

除 t=0 外, 第一次静止时刻为

$$
t _ {1} = \frac {2 \pi}{\omega} = 2 \pi \sqrt {\frac {m _ {1} m _ {2}}{k (m _ {1} + m _ {2})}}
$$

$m_{2}$ 在 t=0-t=t₁ 期间运行的距离为

$$
x _ {2} (t _ {1}) = \frac {2 \pi I}{m _ {1} + m _ {2}} \sqrt {\frac {m _ {1} m _ {2}}{k (m _ {1} + m _ {2})}}
$$

(2) 不可能, 因为要给 $m_{1}$ 一冲量使系统由静止变成无振动的转动, 必须所给的冲量与弹簧垂直. 若冲量为 $I, m_{1}$ 获得的速度为 $\frac{I}{m_{1}}, m_{2}$ 的速度仍为零. 假定此后 $m_{1}$ 围绕 $m_{2}$ 作无振动的转动, 取 $m_{2}$ 平动参考系, 作为两体问题, $m_{1}$ 的质量改用折合质量, 做此转动需要向心力为

$$
\left. \frac {m _ {1} m _ {2}}{m _ {1} + m _ {2}} \left(\frac {I}{m _ {1}}\right) ^ {2} \right/ _ {l}
$$

其中 l 是弹簧的自然长度, 可弹簧仍处于原长状态, 对 $m_{1}$ 无作用力, 不能提供所需的向心力, 因此给 $m_{1}$ 一冲量使系统由静止变成无振动的转动是不可能的.

9.4.5 两根长度为 $a$ 、质量为 $m$ 的棒 $AB$ 和 $BC$ , 在 $B$ 点无摩擦地连接, 放在光滑的水平桌面上, 开始两棒(即点 A、B、C)共线, 在 A 点施以与 ABC 线垂直的冲量 I, 求冲击后的瞬时两棒的运动状态.

解 选 $x$ 轴沿 $t = 0$ 时 $CBA$ 的方向, $y$ 轴沿 $\pmb{I}$ 的作用线方向. 取 $B$ 点的坐标 $x, y$ 及 $AB$ 棒、 $BC$ 棒分别与 $x$ 轴的夹角 $\theta_{1}, \theta_{2}$ 为广义坐标, 如图9.82所示.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/77e92e302e0545df6045ef07e3710e59edc654609483d5a112c40da49552ef5d.jpg)



图9.82


AB 棒质心的坐标为

$$
\begin{array}{l} x _ {1} = x + \frac {a}{2} \cos \theta_ {1} \\ y _ {1} = y + \frac {a}{2} \sin \theta_ {1} \end{array}
$$

BC 棒质心的坐标为

$$
x _ {2} = x - \frac {a}{2} \cos \theta_ {2}, \quad y _ {2} = y - \frac {a}{2} \sin \theta_ {2}
$$

两棒的质心速度分别为

$$
\begin{array}{l l} \dot {x} _ {1} = \dot {x} - \frac {a}{2} \dot {\theta} _ {1} \sin \theta_ {1}, & \dot {y} _ {1} = \dot {y} + \frac {a}{2} \dot {\theta} _ {1} \cos \theta_ {1} \\ \dot {x} _ {2} = \dot {x} + \frac {a}{2} \dot {\theta} _ {2} \sin \theta_ {2}, & \dot {y} _ {2} = \dot {y} - \frac {a}{2} \dot {\theta} _ {2} \cos \theta_ {2} \end{array}
$$

系统的拉格朗日函数为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} L & = T = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} _ {1} ^ {2} + \dot {y} _ {1} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{2} \times \frac {1}{1 2} m a ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} _ {2} ^ {2} + \dot {y} _ {2} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{2} \times \frac {1}{1 2} m a ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {2} ^ {2} \\ & = m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{6} m a ^ {2} (\dot {\theta} _ {1} ^ {2} + \dot {\theta} _ {2} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{2} m a \dot {x} (- \dot {\theta} _ {1} \sin \theta_ {1} + \dot {\theta} _ {2} \sin \theta_ {2}) \\ & + \frac {1}{2} m a \dot {y} (\dot {\theta} _ {1} \cos \theta_ {1} - \dot {\theta} _ {2} \cos \theta_ {2}) \end{array}
$$

冲量 $I$ 做的“虚功”为

$$
I \delta y _ {A} = I \delta (y + a \sin \theta_ {1}) = I (\delta y + a \cos \theta_ {1} \delta \theta_ {1})
$$

冲量 $I$ 的广义冲量为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & K _ {x} = 0, \quad K _ {y} = I \\ & K _ {\theta_ {1}} = a I \cos \theta_ {1} | _ {\theta_ {1} = 0} = a I, \quad K _ {\theta_ {2}} = 0 \end{array}
$$

由冲击运动的拉格朗日方程

$$
\Delta \left(\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {q} _ {i}}\right) = K _ {i}
$$

可得

$$
2 m \dot {x} + \frac {1}{2} m a (- \dot {\theta} _ {1} \sin \theta_ {1} + \dot {\theta} _ {2} \sin \theta_ {2}) | _ {\theta_ {1} = \theta_ {2} = 0} = 0
$$

$$
2 m \dot {y} + \frac {1}{2} m a (\dot {\theta} _ {1} \cos \theta_ {1} - \dot {\theta} _ {2} \cos \theta_ {2}) | _ {\theta_ {1} = \theta_ {2} = 0} = I
$$

$$
\frac {1}{3} m a ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {1} - \frac {1}{2} m a \dot {x} \sin \theta_ {1} | _ {\theta_ {1} = 0} + \frac {1}{2} m a \dot {y} \cos \theta_ {1} | _ {\theta_ {1} = 0} = a I
$$

$$
\frac {1}{3} m a ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m a \dot {x} \sin \theta_ {2} | _ {\theta_ {2} = 0} - \frac {1}{2} m a \dot {y} \cos \theta_ {2} | _ {\theta_ {2} = 0} = 0
$$

即

$$
2 m \dot {x} = 0
$$

$$
2 m \dot {y} + \frac {1}{2} m a (\dot {\theta} _ {1} - \dot {\theta} _ {2}) = I
$$

$$
\frac {1}{3} m a ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {1} + \frac {1}{2} m a \dot {y} = a I
$$

$$
\frac {1}{3} n a ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {2} - \frac {1}{2} m a \dot {y} = 0
$$

解得

$$
\dot {x} = 0, \quad \dot {y} = - \frac {I}{m}
$$

$$
\dot {\theta} _ {1} = \frac {9 I}{2 m a}, \quad \dot {\theta} _ {2} = - \frac {3 I}{2 m a}
$$

AB棒的质心速度为

$$
\dot {x} _ {1} = \dot {x} - \frac {a}{2} \left. \dot {\theta} _ {1} \sin \theta_ {1} \right| _ {\theta_ {1} = 0} = 0
$$

$$
\dot {y} _ {1} = \dot {y} + \left. \frac {a}{2} \dot {\theta} _ {1} \cos \theta_ {1} \right| _ {\theta_ {1} = 0} = \frac {5 I}{4 m}
$$

BC 棒的质心速度为

$$
\dot {x} _ {2} = \dot {x} + \frac {a}{2} \dot {\theta} _ {2} \sin \theta_ {2} \Big | _ {\theta_ {2} = 0} = 0
$$

$$
\dot {y} _ {2} = \dot {y} - \frac {a}{2} \left. \dot {\theta} _ {2} \cos \theta_ {2} \right| _ {\theta_ {2} = 0} = - \frac {I}{4 m}
$$

AB 棒、BC 棒的角速度分别为上述的 $\dot{\theta}_{1}$ 和 $\dot{\theta}_{2}$ .

9.4.6 质量均为 $m$ 、长度均为 $2a$ 的两根均质棒 $PQ, QR$ 在 $Q$ 点光滑铰连，开始置于光滑的桌面上成一直线，在 $PQ$ 的 $P$ 端作用一冲量 $I$ ，其方向沿垂直于 $PQ$ 的水平方向。求两棒在作用后各自质心的初速度和初角速度。

解 此题与上题基本相同, 只是棒长改为 $2a$ , 用上题的图, $x_{1}, y_{1}$ 表示 $PQ$ 棒的质心坐标, $x_{2}, y_{2}$ 表示 $QR$ 棒的质心坐标. 可用上题结果, 因结果中 $\dot{x}_{1}, \dot{y}_{1}, \dot{x}_{2}, \dot{y}_{2}, \dot{\theta}_{1}, \dot{\theta}_{2}$ 均与棒长无关, 故结果与上题完全相同.

由于在 x 方向未受冲量, 对两棒系统或对两棒分别为系统, 均是这种情况, 用矢量力

学， $x$ 方向动量守恒，且为完整约束，可减少自由度，即仅有三个自由度。

9.4.7 一个质量为 $m$ 、半径为 $a$ 的均质圆环可围绕边缘上一点 $O$ 自由转动, 开始处于静止状态, 突然在边缘上另一点 $P$ 作用一个冲量 $I$ , 其方向垂直于圆环所在平面. 证明: 由此冲量产生的角速度大小不会超过 $\frac{3I}{\sqrt{2}ma}$ , 并求产生这么大的角速度 $OP$ 的距离.

解 取固定坐标系 Oxyz，在冲量作用时，x 轴通过 O 点的直径，I 沿 z 轴方向，如图 9.83 所示，图中用 ⊙ 表示 I 的方向垂直纸面向上.

设冲量作用,使圆环获得的角速度表成

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/67af7c6a5fae6edd4b614877599261949ad58732a2282f1b658f768e60cae070.jpg)



图9.83


$$
\omega = \dot {\varphi} i + \dot {\theta} j + \dot {\psi} k
$$

用 $\varphi, \theta, \psi$ 为广义坐标，绕 $x, y, z$ 轴的转动惯量分别为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & I _ {1} = \frac {1}{2} m a ^ {2}, I _ {2} = \frac {1}{2} m a ^ {2} + m a ^ {2} = \frac {3}{2} m a ^ {2} \\ & I _ {3} = I _ {1} + I _ {2} = 2 m a ^ {2} \\ & L = T = \frac {1}{2} (I _ {1} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + I _ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + I _ {3} \dot {\psi} ^ {2}) \\ & = \frac {1}{2} (\frac {1}{2} m a ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \frac {3}{2} m a ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + 2 m a ^ {2} \dot {\psi} ^ {2}) \end{array}
$$

冲量作用期间,位形未变,势能没有变化,写冲击期间的拉格朗日方程,拉格朗日函数中可以不写势能,

$$
\begin{array}{l} p _ {\varphi} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\varphi}} = \frac {1}{2} m a ^ {2} \dot {\varphi}, \quad p _ {\theta} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\theta}} = \frac {3}{2} m a ^ {2} \dot {\theta} \\ p _ {\psi} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\psi}} = 2 m a ^ {2} \dot {\psi} \end{array}
$$

在图示位置,即冲量作用时的位置,

$$
\delta z _ {P} = 2 a \cos \alpha \sin \alpha \delta \varphi - 2 a \cos \alpha \cdot \cos \alpha \delta \theta
$$

冲量做的“虚功”为

$$
I \delta z _ {P} = 2 a I \cos \alpha \sin \alpha \delta \varphi - 2 a I \cos^ {2} \alpha \delta \theta
$$

广义冲量为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & K _ {\varphi} = 2 a I \cos \alpha \sin \alpha \\ & K _ {\theta} = - 2 a I \cos^ {2} \alpha \\ & K _ {\psi} = 0 \end{array}
$$

由冲击运动的拉格朗日方程 $\Delta p_{i}=K_{i}$ 得

$$
\frac {1}{2} m a ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} = 2 a I \cos \alpha \sin \alpha
$$

$$
\frac {3}{2} m a ^ {2} \dot {\theta} = - 2 a I \cos^ {2} \alpha
$$

所以

$$
\begin{array}{r l} 2 m a ^ {2} \dot {\psi} & = 0 \\ \dot {\varphi} & = \frac {4 I \cos \alpha \sin \alpha}{m a} \end{array}
$$

$$
