\begin{array}{l} \dot {\theta} = - \frac {4 I \cos^ {2} \alpha}{3 m a} \\ \dot {\psi} = 0 \\ \omega = \frac {4 I \cos \alpha \sin \alpha}{m a} i - \frac {4 I \cos^ {2} \alpha}{3 m a} j \\ \omega^ {2} = \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \dot {\psi} ^ {2} = \frac {1 6 I ^ {2}}{9 m ^ {2} a ^ {2}} (9 \cos^ {2} \alpha - 8 \cos^ {4} \alpha) \end{array}
$$

$\omega$ 取极值的条件是 $\frac{\mathrm{d}\omega^2}{\mathrm{d}\alpha} = 0$ ，得

$$
\sin \alpha \cos \alpha (1 8 - 3 2 \cos^ {2} \alpha) = 0
$$

$\alpha$ 可取 $-\frac{\pi}{2} \to \frac{\pi}{2}$ ，在此范围内， $\alpha = 0, \pm \arccos \left(\frac{3}{4}\right), \frac{\pi}{2}, \omega$ 为极值，分别代入 $\omega^2$ 的式子，可知，当

$$
\alpha = \pm \arccos \left(\frac {3}{4}\right)
$$

时，

$$
\omega^ {2} = \omega_ {\max} ^ {2} = \frac {9 I ^ {2}}{2 m ^ {2} a ^ {2}}
$$

$$
\omega_ {\max} = \frac {3 I}{\sqrt {2} m a}
$$

此时

$$
O P = 2 a \cos \left[ \pm \arccos \left(\frac {3}{4}\right) \right] = \frac {3}{2} a
$$

9.4.8 由长度为 $2l$ 、质量为 $m$ 的四根杆用光滑铰链连接成的构件 $ABCD$ ，开始成正方形静置于光滑的水平面上，取图9.84所示的坐标，质心位于坐标原点， $AB, CD$ 与 $x$ 轴平行， $AD, BC$ 与 $y$ 轴平行，今在 $A$ 点沿 $AD$ 方向作用一冲量 $I$ ，求作用后构件的运动状态。

解 用构件质心 M 的 x、y 坐标、BC 杆与 x 轴的夹角 $\theta_{1}$ 、AB 杆与 x 轴的夹角 $\theta_{2}$ 作广义坐标，E、F、G、H 分别是 AB、BC、CD、AD 四杆的质心，如图 9.85 所示.

图9.85


$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\pmb {r} _ {E} = (x - l \cos \theta_ {1}) \pmb {i} + (y - l \sin \theta_ {1}) \pmb {j}} \\ & {\pmb {r} _ {F} = (x - l \cos \theta_ {2}) \pmb {i} + (y - l \sin \theta_ {2}) \pmb {j}} \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \boldsymbol {r} _ {G} & = (x + l \cos \theta_ {1}) \boldsymbol {i} + (y + l \sin \theta_ {1}) \boldsymbol {j} \\ \boldsymbol {r} _ {H} & = (x + l \cos \theta_ {2}) \boldsymbol {i} + (y + l \sin \theta_ {2}) \boldsymbol {j} \\ \boldsymbol {v} _ {E} & = (\dot {x} + l \dot {\theta} _ {1} \sin \theta_ {1}) \boldsymbol {i} + (\dot {y} - l \dot {\theta} _ {1} \cos \theta_ {1}) \boldsymbol {j} \\ \boldsymbol {v} _ {F} & = (\dot {x} + l \dot {\theta} _ {2} \sin \theta_ {2}) \boldsymbol {i} + (\dot {y} - l \dot {\theta} _ {2} \cos \theta_ {2}) \boldsymbol {j} \\ \boldsymbol {v} _ {G} & = (\dot {x} - l \dot {\theta} _ {1} \sin \theta_ {1}) \boldsymbol {i} + (\dot {y} + l \dot {\theta} _ {1} \cos \theta_ {1}) \boldsymbol {j} \\ \boldsymbol {v} _ {H} & = (\dot {x} - l \dot {\theta} _ {2} \sin \theta_ {2}) \boldsymbol {i} + (\dot {y} + l \dot {\theta} _ {2} \cos \theta_ {2}) \boldsymbol {j} \end{array}
$$

四根杆的动能分别为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & T _ {A B} = \frac {1}{2} m v _ {E} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \cdot \frac {1}{3} m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {2} ^ {2} \\ & \quad = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {1} ^ {2} + 2 l \dot {x} \dot {\theta} _ {1} \sin \theta_ {1} + \dot {y} ^ {2} - 2 l \dot {y} \dot {\theta} _ {1} \cos \theta_ {1}) + \frac {1}{6} m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {2} ^ {2} \\ & T _ {B C} = \frac {1}{2} m v _ {F} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \cdot \frac {1}{3} m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {1} ^ {2} \\ & \quad = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2} + l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {2} ^ {2} + 2 l \dot {x} \dot {\theta} _ {2} \sin \theta_ {2} - 2 l \dot {y} \dot {\theta} _ {2} \cos \theta_ {2}) + \frac {1}{6} m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {1} ^ {2} \\ & T _ {C D} = \frac {1}{2} m v _ {G} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \cdot \frac {1}{3} m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {2} ^ {2} \\ & \quad = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2} + l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {1} ^ {2} - 2 l \dot {x} \dot {\theta} _ {1} \sin \theta_ {1} + 2 l \dot {y} \dot {\theta} _ {1} \cos \theta_ {1}) + \frac {1}{6} m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {2} ^ {2} \\ & T _ {A D} = \frac {1}{2} m v _ {H} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \cdot \frac {1}{3} m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {1} ^ {2} \\ & \quad = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2} + l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {2} ^ {2} - 2 l \dot {x} \dot {\theta} _ {2} \sin \theta_ {2} + 2 l \dot {y} \dot {\theta} _ {2} \cos \theta_ {2}) + \frac {1}{6} m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {1} ^ {2} \\ & L = T = T _ {A B} + T _ {B C} + T _ {C D} + T _ {A D} = 2 m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2}) + \frac {4}{3} m l ^ {2} (\dot {\theta} _ {1} ^ {2} + \dot {\theta} _ {2} ^ {2}) \\ & p _ {x} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {x}} = 4 m \dot {x}, \quad p _ {y} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {y}} = 4 m \dot {y}. \\ & p _ {\theta_ {1}} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\theta_ {1}}} = \frac {8}{3} m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta_ {1}}, \quad p _ {\theta_ {2}} = \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\theta_ {2}}} = \frac {8}{3} m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta_ {2}} \\ & y _ {A} = y _ {E} + l s i n \theta_ {2} = y - l s i n \theta_ {1} + l s i n \theta_ {2}. \\ & K _ {x} = I \frac {\partial y _ {A}}{\partial x} = 0 \\ & K _ {y} = I \frac {\partial y _ {A}}{\partial y} = I \\ & K _ {\theta_ {1}} = I \left. \frac {\partial y _ {A}}{\partial \theta_ {1}} \right| _ {\theta_ {1} = \frac {\pi}{2}} = - I l c o s \theta_ {1} | _ {\theta_ {1} = \frac {\pi}{2}} = 0 \\ & K _ {\theta_ {2}} = I \left. \frac {\partial y _ {A}}{\partial \theta_ {2}} \right| _ {\theta_ {2} = \pi} = I l c o s \theta_ {2} | _ {\theta_ {2} = \pi} = - I l. \end{array}
$$

由冲击运动的拉格朗日方程 $\Delta p_{i}=K_{i}$ ,

$$
\Delta p _ {x} = 4 m \dot {x} = 0
$$

$$
\Delta p _ {y} = 4 m \dot {y} = I
$$

$$
\Delta p _ {\theta_ {1}} = \frac {8}{3} m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {1} = 0
$$

$$
\Delta p _ {\theta_ {2}} = \frac {8}{3} m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {2} = - I l
$$

所以

$$
\dot {x} = 0, \quad \dot {y} = \frac {I}{4 m}, \quad \dot {\theta} _ {1} = 0, \quad \dot {\theta} _ {2} = - \frac {3 I}{8 m l}
$$

9.4.9 分别用基尔霍夫定律和拉格朗日方程得到图 9.86 所示电路的电路方程.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/22511f14f00e5bfde3d93e2b8215d1b696c591151245d064437b35c6ece959fa.jpg)



图9.86


解 用基尔霍夫定律，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & L (\ddot {q} _ {1} - \ddot {q} _ {2}) + R _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} = \varepsilon_ {1} - \varepsilon_ {2} \\ & L (\ddot {q} _ {2} - \ddot {q} _ {1}) + \frac {1}{C} q _ {2} + R _ {2} \dot {q} _ {2} = \varepsilon_ {2} \end{array}
$$

用拉格朗日方程，

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} L (\dot {q} _ {1} - \dot {q} _ {2}) ^ {2}, \quad V = \frac {1}{2 C} q _ {2} ^ {2}
$$

$$
\widetilde {L} = T - V = \frac {1}{2} L (\dot {q} _ {1} - \dot {q} _ {2}) ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2 C} q _ {2} ^ {2}
$$

这里拉格朗日函数改用 $\widetilde{L}$ , 以免与自感 $L$ 发生混淆, 下面瑞利耗散函数改用 $\widetilde{R}$ , 以免与电阻 $R$ 发生混淆,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \widetilde {R} & = \frac {1}{2} R _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} R _ {2} \dot {q} _ {2} ^ {2} \\ Q _ {1} & = \varepsilon_ {1} - \varepsilon_ {2}, \quad Q _ {2} = \varepsilon_ {2} \\ \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial \widetilde {L}}{\partial \dot {q} _ {i}}\right) - \frac {\partial \widetilde {L}}{\partial q _ {i}} & = Q _ {i} - \frac {\partial \widetilde {R}}{\partial \dot {q} _ {i}} \end{array}
$$

日

得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & L (\ddot {q} _ {1} - \ddot {q} _ {2}) = \varepsilon_ {1} - \varepsilon_ {2} - R _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} \\ & - L (\ddot {q} _ {1} - \ddot {q} _ {2}) + \frac {1}{C} q _ {2} = \varepsilon_ {2} - R _ {2} \dot {q} _ {2} \end{array}
$$

与基尔霍夫定律得到的电路方程是一样的.

9.4.10 用拉格朗日方程得到图 9.87 所示电路的电路方程.

提示:电流源可作为一个约束 $\dot{q}=i$ 来处理.

解 用基尔霍夫定律，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \frac {1}{C} q _ {1} + R _ {1} (\dot {q} _ {1} - \dot {q}) + R _ {2} (\dot {q} _ {1} - \dot {q} _ {2}) & = 0 \\ R _ {2} (\dot {q} _ {2} - \dot {q} _ {1}) + R _ {3} \dot {q} _ {2} & = - \varepsilon \\ \dot {q} & = i \end{array}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/732704368a1b332c24906354f388186463c6aab7ca603d787502bc1826b13bf9.jpg)



图9.87


用拉格朗日方程，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & T = 0, \quad V = \frac {1}{2 C} q _ {1} ^ {2} \\ & \widetilde {L} = - V = - \frac {1}{2 C} q _ {1} ^ {2} \\ & Q _ {1} = 0, \quad Q _ {2} = - \varepsilon \\ & \widetilde {R} = \frac {1}{2} R _ {1} (\dot {q} _ {1} - \dot {q}) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} R _ {2} (\dot {q} _ {2} - \dot {q} _ {1}) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} R _ {3} \dot {q} _ {2} ^ {2} \\ & \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial \widetilde {L}}{\partial \dot {q} _ {i}}\right) - \frac {\partial \widetilde {L}}{\partial q _ {i}} = Q _ {i} - \frac {\partial \widetilde {R}}{\partial \dot {q} _ {i}} \end{array}
$$

由

得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \frac {1}{C} q _ {1} & = - R _ {1} (\dot {q} _ {1} - \dot {q}) + R _ {2} (\dot {q} _ {2} - \dot {q} _ {1}) \\ 0 & = - R _ {2} (\dot {q} _ {2} - \dot {q} _ {1}) - R _ {3} \dot {q} _ {2} - \varepsilon \\ \dot {q} & = i \end{array}
$$

9.4.11 用拉格朗日方程写出图 9.88 所示电路的电路方程.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/0392b882eabafd4e7e0bcd9efb499e05e493f21cd60ab73a120c3724d108b6ab.jpg)



图9.88


解 用基尔霍夫定律，

$$
R _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} + L _ {1} \ddot {q} _ {1} + \frac {1}{C _ {1}} (q _ {1} - q _ {2}) + L _ {2} \ddot {q} _ {1} + R _ {2} \dot {q} _ {1} + M \ddot {q} _ {2} = \varepsilon
$$

$$
L _ {3} \ddot {q} _ {2} + L _ {4} \ddot {q} _ {2} + M \ddot {q} _ {1} + \frac {1}{C _ {1}} (q _ {2} - q _ {1}) + \frac {1}{C _ {2}} q _ {2} + (R _ {3} + R _ {4}) \dot {q} _ {2} = 0
$$

用拉格朗日方程

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} L _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} L _ {2} \dot {q} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} L _ {3} \dot {q} _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} L _ {4} \dot {q} _ {2} ^ {2} + M \dot {q} _ {1} \dot {q} _ {2}
$$

$$
V = \frac {1}{2 C _ {1}} (q _ {1} - q _ {2}) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 C _ {2}} q _ {2} ^ {2}
$$

$$
\widetilde {L} = T - V = \frac {1}{2} L _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} L _ {2} \dot {q} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} L _ {3} \dot {q} _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} L _ {4} \dot {q} _ {2} ^ {2} + M \dot {q} _ {1} \dot {q} _ {2} - \frac {1}{2 C _ {1}} (q _ {1} - q _ {2}) ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2 C _ {2}} q _ {2} ^ {2}
$$

$$
\widetilde {R} = \frac {1}{2} R _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} R _ {2} \dot {q} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} R _ {3} \dot {q} _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} R _ {4} \dot {q} _ {2} ^ {2}
$$

$$
Q _ {1} = \varepsilon , \quad Q _ {2} = 0
$$

由

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial \widetilde {L}}{\partial \dot {q} _ {i}}\right) - \frac {\partial \widetilde {L}}{\partial q _ {i}} = Q _ {i} - \frac {\partial \widetilde {R}}{\partial \dot {q} _ {i}}
$$

可得

$$
L _ {1} \ddot {q} _ {1} + L _ {2} \ddot {q} _ {1} + M \ddot {q} _ {2} + \frac {1}{C _ {1}} (q _ {1} - q _ {2}) = \varepsilon - (R _ {1} + R _ {2}) \dot {q} _ {1}
$$

$$
(L _ {3} + L _ {4}) \ddot {q} _ {2} + M \ddot {q} _ {1} + \frac {1}{C _ {1}} (q _ {2} - q _ {1}) + \frac {1}{C _ {2}} q _ {2} = - (R _ {3} + R _ {4}) \dot {q} _ {2}
$$

9.4.12 用拉格朗日方程写出图 9.89 电路(各线圈有互感 $M_{12}, M_{23}, M_{13}$ , 图中难于表明)的电路方程.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/164e721a748bf1662dffc0fdcc7f3bde0c551ebb954623dda27874a522b7f1bf.jpg)



图9.89


解 电路包含两个回路, 只有两个自由度, 有约束关系: $\dot{q}_1 = \dot{q}_2 + \dot{q}_3$ 或 $q_1 = q_2 + q_3$ . 取 $q_1, q_2$ 为广义坐标,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & T = \frac {1}{2} (L _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} ^ {2} + L _ {2} \dot {q} _ {2} ^ {2} + L _ {3} \dot {q} _ {3} ^ {2} + 2 M _ {1 2} \dot {q} _ {1} \dot {q} _ {2} + 2 M _ {2 3} \dot {q} _ {2} \dot {q} _ {3} + 2 M _ {1 3} \dot {q} _ {1} \dot {q} _ {3}) \\ & \quad = \frac {1}{2} [ L _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} ^ {2} + L _ {2} \dot {q} _ {2} ^ {2} + L _ {3} (\dot {q} _ {1} - \dot {q} _ {2}) ^ {2} + 2 M _ {1 2} \dot {q} _ {1} \dot {q} _ {2} + 2 M _ {2 3} \dot {q} _ {2} (\dot {q} _ {1} - \dot {q} _ {2}) + 2 M _ {1 3} \dot {q} _ {1} (\dot {q} _ {1} - \dot {q} _ {2}) ] \\ & V = \frac {1}{2} \left[ \frac {q _ {1} ^ {2}}{C _ {1}} + \frac {q _ {2} ^ {2}}{C _ {2}} + \frac {(q _ {1} - q _ {2}) ^ {2}}{C _ {3}} \right] - E _ {1} q _ {1} + E _ {2} q _ {2} + E _ {3} (q _ {1} - q _ {2}) \\ & \widetilde {R} = \frac {1}{2} [ R _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} ^ {2} + R _ {2} \dot {q} _ {2} ^ {2} + R _ {3} (\dot {q} _ {1} - \dot {q} _ {2}) ^ {2} ] \\ & Q _ {1} = - \frac {\partial \widetilde {R}}{\partial \dot {q} _ {1}} = - R _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} - R _ {3} (\dot {q} _ {1} - \dot {q} _ {2}) = - (R _ {1} + R _ {3}) \dot {q} _ {1} + R _ {3} \dot {q} _ {2} \end{array}
$$

$$
Q _ {2} = - \frac {\partial \widetilde {R}}{\partial \dot {q} _ {2}} = - R _ {2} \dot {q} _ {2} + R _ {3} (\dot {q} _ {1} - \dot {q} _ {2}) = R _ {3} \dot {q} _ {1} - (R _ {2} + R _ {3}) \dot {q} _ {2}
$$

这里用引入势能的办法处理直流电源,当然也可以像前几题那样用广义力处理.对于交流电源,也可引入显含时间的势能来处理,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\widetilde {L} = T - V = \frac {1}{2} [ L _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} ^ {2} + L _ {2} \dot {q} _ {2} ^ {2} + L _ {3} (\dot {q} _ {1} - \dot {q} _ {2}) ^ {2} + 2 M _ {1 2} \dot {q} _ {1} \dot {q} _ {2} + 2 M _ {2 3} \dot {q} _ {2} (\dot {q} _ {1} - \dot {q} _ {2})} \\ & {\quad + 2 M _ {1 3} \dot {q} _ {1} (\dot {q} _ {1} - \dot {q} _ {2}) ] - \frac {1}{2} \Big [ \frac {q _ {1} ^ {2}}{C _ {1}} + \frac {q _ {2} ^ {2}}{C _ {2}} + \frac {(q _ {1} - q _ {2}) ^ {2}}{C _ {3}} \Big ] + E _ {1} q _ {1} - E _ {2} q _ {2} - E _ {3} (q _ {1} - q _ {2})} \\ & {\text {由}} \\ & {\quad \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \Big (\frac {\partial \widetilde {L}}{\partial \dot {q} _ {i}} \Big) - \frac {\partial \widetilde {L}}{\partial q _ {i}} = Q _ {i}} \\ & {\text {得}} \\ & {\quad (L _ {1} + L _ {3} + 2 M _ {1 3}) \ddot {q} _ {1} + (M _ {1 2} + M _ {2 3} - L _ {3} - M _ {1 3}) \ddot {q} _ {2}} \\ & {\quad + \Big (\frac {1}{C _ {1}} + \frac {1}{C _ {3}} \Big) q _ {1} - \frac {1}{C _ {3}} q _ {2} - E _ {1} + E _ {3} = - (R _ {1} + R _ {3}) \dot {q} _ {1} + R _ {3} \dot {q} _ {2}} \\ & {\quad (L _ {2} + L _ {3} - 2 M _ {2 3}) \ddot {q} _ {2} + (M _ {1 2} - L _ {3} + M _ {2 3} - M _ {1 3}) \ddot {q} _ {1}} \\ & {\quad + \Big (\frac {1}{C _ {2}} + \frac {1}{C _ {3}} \Big) q _ {2} - \frac {1}{C _ {3}} q _ {1} + E _ {2} - E _ {3} = R _ {3} \dot {q} _ {1} - (R _ {2} + R _ {3}) \dot {q} _ {2}} \end{array}
$$

9.4.13 图 9.90 所示的电路中包含两个相同的二极管, 假定 $E_{3} = A \mid \dot{q}_{3}^{b-1} \mid \dot{q}_{3}$ , 其中 $A, b$ 是常量, $E_{2} = E_{0} \sin \omega t$ , 各线圈有互感 $M_{12}, M_{13}, M_{23}$ . 用拉格朗日方程写出电路方程.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/6509c70e141861af21da9f1f701fddad06697ab792a7e46cd1fbc2055b0e19e1.jpg)



图9.90


解 只有两个自由度，用 $q_{1}, q_{2}$ 为广义坐标， $\dot{q}_{3} = \dot{q}_{2} - \dot{q}_{1}$ ，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {T = \frac {1}{2} [ L _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} ^ {2} + L _ {2} \dot {q} _ {2} ^ {2} + L _ {3} (\dot {q} _ {2} - \dot {q} _ {1}) ^ {2} + 2 M _ {1 2} \dot {q} _ {1} \dot {q} _ {2}} \\ & {\quad + 2 M _ {1 3} \dot {q} _ {1} (\dot {q} _ {2} - \dot {q} _ {1}) + 2 M _ {2 3} \dot {q} _ {2} (\dot {q} _ {2} - \dot {q} _ {1}) ]} \\ & {V = \frac {q _ {1} ^ {2}}{2 C} - E _ {1} q _ {1} + E _ {0} q _ {2} \sin \omega t} \\ & {\widetilde {L} = T - V = \frac {1}{2} [ L _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} ^ {2} + L _ {2} \dot {q} _ {2} ^ {2} + L _ {3} (\dot {q} _ {2} - \dot {q} _ {1}) ^ {2} + 2 M _ {1 2} \dot {q} _ {1} \dot {q}   _ {2}} \end{array}
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {+ 2 M _ {1 3} q _ {1} (\dot {q} _ {2} - \dot {q} _ {1}) + 2 M _ {2 3} \dot {q} _ {2} (\dot {q} _ {2} - \dot {q} _ {1}) ] - \frac {q _ {1} ^ {2}}{2 C} + E _ {1} q _ {1} - E q _ {2} \sin \omega t} \\ {\delta W = - R _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} \delta q _ {1} - R _ {2} \dot {q} _ {2} \delta q _ {2} - R _ {3} (\dot {q} _ {2} - \dot {q} _ {1}) (\delta q _ {2} - \delta q _ {1})} \\ & {\quad + A | (\dot {q} _ {2} - \dot {q} _ {1}) ^ {b - 1} | (\dot {q} _ {2} - \dot {q} _ {1}) (\delta q _ {2} - \delta q _ {1})} \\ & {\quad = [ - R _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} + R _ {3} (\dot {q} _ {2} - \dot {q} _ {1}) - A | (\dot {q} _ {2} - \dot {q} _ {1}) ^ {b - 1} | (\dot {q} _ {2} - \dot {q} _ {1}) ] \delta q _ {1}} \\ & {\quad + [ - R _ {2} \dot {q} _ {2} - R _ {3} (\dot {q} _ {2} - \dot {q} _ {1}) + A | (\dot {q} _ {2} - \dot {q} _ {1}) | ^ {b - 1} (\dot {q} _ {2} - \dot {q} _ {1}) ] \delta q _ {2}} \\ & {Q _ {1} = - R _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} + R _ {3} (\dot {q} _ {2} - \dot {q} _ {1}) - A | (\dot {q} _ {2} - \dot {q} _ {1}) ^ {b - 1} | (\dot {q} _ {2} - \dot {q} _ {1})} \\ & {Q _ {2} = - R _ {2} \dot {q} _ {2} - R _ {3} (\dot {q} _ {2} - \dot {q} _ {1}) + A | (\dot {q} _ {2} - \dot {q} _ {1}) ^ {b - 1} | (\dot {q} _ {2} - \dot {q} _ {1})} \\ & {\quad \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \Big (\frac {\partial \widetilde L}{\partial \dot {\boldsymbol q} _ {\boldsymbol i}} \Big) - \frac {\partial \widetilde L}{\partial \boldsymbol q _ {\boldsymbol i}} = Q _ {\boldsymbol i}} \end{array}
$$

由

得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & (L _ {1} + L _ {3} - 2 M _ {1 3}) \ddot {q} _ {1} + (M _ {1 2} - L _ {3} + M _ {1 3} - M _ {2 3}) \ddot {q} _ {2} + \frac {q _ {1}}{C} - E _ {1} \\ & \quad = - R _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} + R _ {3} (\dot {q} _ {2} - \dot {q} _ {1}) - A | (\dot {q} _ {2} - \dot {q} _ {1}) ^ {b - 1} | (\dot {q} _ {2} - \dot {q} _ {1}) \\ & (L _ {2} + L _ {3} + 2 M _ {2 3}) \ddot {q} _ {2} + (M _ {1 2} - L _ {3} + M _ {1 3} - M _ {2 3}) \ddot {q} _ {2} + E _ {0} \sin \omega t \\ & \quad = - R _ {2} \dot {q} _ {2} - R _ {3} (\dot {q} _ {2} - \dot {q} _ {1}) + A | (\dot {q} _ {2} - \dot {q} _ {1}) ^ {b - 1} | (\dot {q} _ {2} - \dot {q} _ {1}) \end{array}
$$

9.4.14 图 9.91 所示的系统中电容器 C 的上极板悬在劲度系数为 k 的线圈弹簧的下端(弹簧上端固定)，质量为 m，上极板在重力、弹簧力和极板间的电场力作用下在竖直方向运动，下极板是固定的，电容器的电容 $C=\frac{A}{s-x}$ ，其中 A 是与极板面积及所用的单位有关的常数，s 是电容器未充电情况下上极板处于平衡位置两极板的间距。用拉格朗日方程给出运动微分方程。

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/e8058c440111db5a43d3cbc61a54e5d95ecc05992e4f24bc14232dc75078f8c6.jpg)



图9.91


解 系统有两个自由度,取 q 和 x 为广义坐标.

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} L \dot {q} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m \dot {x} ^ {2}
$$

$$
V = \frac {1}{2 C} q ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k x ^ {2} - q E _ {0} \sin \omega t
$$

$$
= \frac {s - x}{2 A} q ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k x ^ {2} - q E _ {0} \sin \omega t
$$

注意: x=0 时作用于上极板的弹簧力与重力相抵消, 因此 $\frac{1}{2}kx^{2}$ 已是弹簧力和重力的合力的势能,

$$
\widetilde {L} = \frac {1}{2} L \dot {q} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m \dot {x} ^ {2} - \frac {s - x}{2 A} q ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k x ^ {2} + q E _ {0} \sin \omega t
$$

$$
Q _ {q} = - R \dot {q}, Q _ {x} = 0
$$

由

得

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial \widetilde {L}}{\partial \dot {q} _ {i}}\right) - \frac {\partial \widetilde {L}}{\partial q _ {i}} = Q _ {i}
$$

$$
L \ddot {q} + \frac {s - x}{A} q - E _ {0} \sin \omega t = - R \dot {q}
$$

$$
m \ddot {x} + k x - \frac {1}{2 A} q ^ {2} = 0
$$

说明一下两式中各项的物理意义: $L\ddot{q}$ 是电感两端的电压, $\frac{s-x}{A}q$ 是电容器两极板间的电压, $R\dot{q}$ 为电阻两端的电压, $\frac{1}{2A}q^{2}$ 是电容器两极板间的吸引力.

9.4.15 图 9.92 中可变电容器的两极板可沿竖直直线 ab 运动, 无转动, 极板受弹簧力、重力和两极板间电场力的作用, $l_{10}$ 、 $l_{20}$ 是极板不带电时两极板处于平衡时两弹簧的长度, s、A 均为常量, $m_{1}$ 、 $m_{2}$ 是两极板的质量. 求系统的拉格朗日函数及运动微分方程.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/13f3a9d4a4f3077f84f506b034a88671a6078ea0cd14063abd8084c5a370ebca.jpg)



图9.92


解

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} m _ {1} \dot {x} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m _ {2} \dot {x} _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} L \dot {q} ^ {2}
$$

$$
V = \frac {1}{2} k _ {1} x _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k _ {2} x _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} q ^ {2} \frac {s - x _ {1} - x _ {2}}{A} - E q
$$

$$
\widetilde {L} = \frac {1}{2} m _ {1} \dot {x} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m _ {2} \dot {x} _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} L \dot {q} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k _ {1} x _ {1} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k _ {2} x _ {2} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} q ^ {2} \frac {s - x _ {1} - x _ {2}}{A} + E q
$$

$$
Q _ {x _ {1}} = 0, \quad Q _ {x _ {2}} = 0, \quad Q _ {q} = - R \dot {q}
$$

由

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial \widetilde {L}}{\partial \dot {q} _ {i}}\right) - \frac {\partial \widetilde {L}}{\partial q _ {i}} = Q _ {i}
$$

得

$$
m _ {1} \ddot {x} _ {1} + k _ {1} x _ {1} - \frac {q ^ {2}}{2 A} = 0
$$

$$
m _ {2} \ddot {x} _ {2} + k _ {2} x _ {2} - \frac {q ^ {2}}{2 A} = 0
$$

$$
L \ddot {q} + \frac {s - x _ {1} - x _ {2}}{A} q - E = - R \dot {q}
$$

9.4.16 图 9.93 中半圆柱 A 通过一根扭转常数为 k (转单位角度时受到恢复力矩为 k) 的细弹性棒钉在位于通过柱心的固定的垂直轴 O 上, 可在固定的半圆柱面 B 内转动, 构成可变电容器, 假定其电容为 $C = C_{0} \left(1 - \frac{\theta}{\pi}\right)$ , $\theta = \theta_{0}$ 时, 弹性棒未被扭曲, 写出拉格朗日函数和运动微分方程.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/11d4a8b9e16d93ed9a5decb292a87c214653af9be7e16a4236419861dfd4301e.jpg)



图9.93


解

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} L \dot {q} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} I \theta^ {2}
$$

其中 I 是半圆柱 B 绕 O 轴的转动惯量

$$
\begin{array}{r l} V & = \frac {1}{2} k (\theta - \theta_ {0}) ^ {2} - \frac {q ^ {2}}{2 C _ {0} (1 - \theta / \pi)} - E q \\ \widetilde {L} & = \frac {1}{2} L \dot {q} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} I \dot {\theta} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k (\theta - \theta_ {0}) ^ {2} - \frac {q ^ {2}}{2 C _ {0} (1 - \theta / \pi)} + E q \\ Q _ {q} & = - R \dot {q}, \quad Q _ {\theta} = 0 \\ \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial \widetilde {L}}{\partial \dot {q} _ {i}}\right) - \frac {\partial \widetilde {L}}{\partial q _ {i}} & = Q _ {i} \end{array}
$$

由

得运动微分方程为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & L \ddot {q} + \frac {1}{C _ {0} (1 - \theta / \pi)} q - E = - R \dot {q} \\ & I \ddot {\theta} + k (\theta - \theta_ {0}) + \frac {1}{2 \pi C _ {0} (1 - \theta / \pi) ^ {2}} q ^ {2} = 0 \end{array}
$$

9.4.17 可变电容器与墙式电流计如图 9.94 所示连接，设电流计中的磁场沿径向，磁感应强度为 B，线圈有 n 匝，每匝面积为 $A_{1}$ ，线圈绕轴的转动惯量为 $I, k_{1}$ 是扭转弹簧的扭转常数, 可变电容器的电容 $C = \frac{A_2}{d - x}, A_2, d$ 均为常量, $l_0$ 是极板不带电时, 质量为 $m$ 的极板平衡时的弹簧长度, $n$ 匝线圈的自感系数为 $L_1$ , 写出系统的拉格朗日函数和运动微分方程.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/84b8a0f9e72310bc804872faaab1c0c533238689098e05ea229c4146c017f195.jpg)



图9.94


解 此系统有四个自由度, 用 $\theta, q_{1}, q_{2}, x$ 作为广义坐标. $\dot{q}_{3} = \dot{q}_{2} - \dot{q}_{1}$ , 电流计线圈当有电流 $\dot{q}_{1}$ 通过时, 受到磁场的力矩为 $nBA_{1}\dot{q}_{1}$ ,

$$
\begin{array}{l} T = \frac {1}{2} I \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} L _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} L _ {2} \dot {q} _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m \dot {x} ^ {2} \\ V = - E _ {1} q _ {1} - E _ {2} (q _ {2} - q _ {1}) - n B A _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} \theta + \frac {1}{2} k _ {1} \theta^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k _ {2} x ^ {2} + \frac {(q _ {2} - q _ {1}) ^ {2}}{2 A _ {2}} (d - x) \\ \widetilde {L} = \frac {1}{2} I \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} L _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} L _ {2} \dot {q} _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m \dot {x} ^ {2} + E _ {1} q _ {1} + E _ {2} (q _ {2} - q _ {1}) \\ + n B A _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} \theta - \frac {1}{2} k _ {1} \theta^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k _ {2} x ^ {2} - \frac {(q _ {2} - q _ {1}) ^ {2}}{2 A _ {2}} (d - x). \end{array}
$$

$$
\delta W = - R _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} \delta q _ {1} - R _ {2} \dot {q} _ {2} \delta q _ {2}
$$

所以

$$
Q _ {\theta} = 0, \quad Q _ {q _ {1}} = - R _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1}, \quad Q _ {q _ {2}} = - R \dot {q} _ {2}, \quad Q _ {x} = 0
$$

$$
\frac {\partial \widetilde {L}}{\partial \dot {\theta}} = I \dot {\theta}, \quad \frac {\partial \widetilde {L}}{\partial \theta} = n B A _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} - k _ {1} \theta
$$

$$
\frac {\partial \widetilde {L}}{\partial \dot {q} _ {1}} = L _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} + n B A _ {1} \theta , \quad \frac {\partial L}{\partial q _ {1}} = E _ {1} - E _ {2} + \frac {q _ {2} - q _ {1}}{A _ {2}} (d - x)
$$

$$
\frac {\partial \widetilde {L}}{\partial \dot {q} _ {2}} = L _ {2} \dot {q} _ {2},
$$

$$
\frac {\partial \widetilde {L}}{\partial q _ {2}} = E _ {2} - \frac {1}{A _ {2}} (q _ {2} - q _ {1}) (d - x)
$$

$$
\frac {\partial \widetilde {L}}{\partial \dot {x}} = m \dot {x},
$$

$$
\frac {\partial L}{\partial x} = - k _ {2} x + \frac {1}{2 A _ {2}} (q _ {2} - q _ {1}) ^ {2}
$$

由

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial \widetilde {L}}{\partial \dot {q} _ {i}}\right) - \frac {\partial \widetilde {L}}{\partial q _ {i}} = Q _ {i}
$$

可得运动微分方程为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & I \ddot {\theta} + k _ {1} \theta - n B A _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} = 0 \\ & L _ {1} \ddot {q} _ {1} + n B A _ {1} \dot {\theta} - E _ {1} + E _ {2} - \frac {1}{A _ {2}} (q _ {2} - q _ {1}) (d - x) = - R _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} \\ & L _ {2} \ddot {q} _ {2} - E _ {2} + \frac {1}{A _ {2}} (q _ {2} - q _ {1}) (d - x) = - R \dot {q} _ {2} \\ & m \ddot {x} + k _ {2} x - \frac {1}{2 A _ {2}} (q _ {2} - q _ {1}) ^ {2} = 0 \end{array}
$$

9.4.18 半径为 r 的圆盘的中心安装在水平的弹性棒的一端, 弹性棒的另一端是固定的, 圆盘转动 $\theta$ 角时, 弹性棒给予圆盘的力矩为 $-k_{1}\theta$ , 图 9.95 中弹簧的劲度系数为 $k_{2}$ , $m_{1}$ 、 $m_{2}$ 是两个金属圆柱的质量, 用绝缘的不可伸长的轻绳和弹簧连接并跨在圆盘上, 圆盘转动时, 圆盘与绳子间无相对滑动, 圆盘绕弹性棒的转动惯量为 I, 圆柱体可分别在两个固定的导电圆筒内作竖直运动, 圆柱体与圆筒构成可变电容器

$$
\begin{array}{l l} C _ {1} = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} C _ {1 0} \left(1 - \frac {y _ {1}}{l _ {1}}\right) & y _ {1} \geqslant 0 \\ C _ {1 0} & y _ {1} <   0 \end{array} \right. \\ C _ {2} = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} C _ {2 0} \left(1 - \frac {y _ {2}}{l _ {2}}\right) & y _ {2} \geqslant 0 \\ C _ {2 0} & y _ {2} <   0 \end{array} \right. \end{array}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/c50cba47dfb2531838324ce8774a6422baac8a58e989f3d447bb67f441ac02a9.jpg)



图9.95


由互感将两个电路耦合，用 $q_{1}, q_{2}, y_{1}, y_{2}$ 为广义坐标，写出系统的动能、势能和运动微分方程.

解 圆盘与绳子间无相对滑动，

$$
r \dot {\theta} = \dot {y} _ {2}, \quad \dot {\theta} = \frac {1}{r} \dot {y} _ {2}
$$

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} m _ {1} \dot {y} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m _ {2} \dot {y} _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} I \left(\frac {\dot {y} _ {2}}{r}\right) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} L _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} L _ {2} \dot {q} _ {2} ^ {2} + M \dot {q} _ {1} \dot {q} _ {2}
$$

设弹簧的伸长量为 $l$ ，原长为 $l_{0}$ ，绳长为 $L$ ，由于绳子不可伸长， $L$ 为常量，

$$
L + y _ {1} + y _ {2} + l _ {0} + l = \text {常量}
$$

令 $y_{1} + y_{2} + l = b$ 为常量， $l = b - (y_{1} + y_{2})$ ，弹簧的势能为

$$
\frac {1}{2} k _ {2} l ^ {2} = \frac {1}{2} k _ {2} (b - y _ {1} - y _ {2}) ^ {2}
$$

$$
V = \frac {1}{2 C _ {1}} q _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 C _ {2}} q _ {2} ^ {2} - E _ {1} q _ {1} - E _ {2} q _ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k _ {1} \left(\frac {y _ {2}}{r}\right) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k _ {2} (b - y _ {1} - y _ {2}) ^ {2} + m _ {1} g y _ {1} + m _ {2} g y _ {2}
$$

这里用了 $\theta = \frac{y_2}{r}$ ，是将绳子跨在圆盘上时，当 $y_2 = 0$ 时，弹性棒未扭转。

注意: $C_{1}$ 与 $y_{1}$ 有关, $C_{2}$ 与 $y_{2}$ 有关.

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \widetilde {L} & = \frac {1}{2} m _ {1} \dot {y} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m _ {2} \dot {y} _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \frac {I}{r ^ {2}} \dot {y} _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} L _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} L _ {2} \dot {q} _ {2} ^ {2} + M \dot {q} _ {1} \dot {q} _ {2} - \frac {1}{2 C _ {1}} q _ {1} ^ {2} \\ & - \frac {1}{2 C _ {2}} q _ {2} ^ {2} + E _ {1} q _ {1} + E _ {2} q _ {2} - \frac {1}{2} \frac {k _ {1}}{r ^ {2}} y _ {2} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k _ {2} (b - y _ {1} - y _ {2}) ^ {2} - m _ {1} g y _ {1} - m _ {2} g y _ {2} \\ & \frac {\partial \widetilde {L}}{\partial \dot {q} _ {1}} = L _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} + M \dot {q} _ {2}, \quad \frac {\partial \widetilde {L}}{\partial q _ {1}} = - \frac {q _ {1}}{C _ {1}} + E _ {1} \\ & \frac {\partial \widetilde {L}}{\partial \dot {q} _ {2}} = L _ {2} \dot {q} _ {2} + M \dot {q} _ {1}, \quad \frac {\partial \widetilde {L}}{\partial q _ {2}} = - \frac {q _ {2}}{C _ {2}} + E _ {2} \end{array}
$$

$$
\frac {\partial \widetilde {L}}{\partial \dot {y} _ {1}} = m _ {1} \dot {y} _ {1},
$$

$$
\frac {\partial \widetilde {L}}{\partial y _ {1}} = \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} k _ {2} (b - y _ {1} - y _ {2}) - m _ {1} g - \frac {l _ {1} q _ {1} ^ {2}}{2 C _ {1 0} (l _ {1} - y _ {1}) ^ {2}} & y _ {1} \geqslant 0 \\ k _ {2} (b - y _ {1} - y _ {2}) - m _ {1} g & y _ {1} \leqslant 0 \end{array} \right.
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & \frac {\partial \widetilde {L}}{\partial \dot {y} _ {2}} = m _ {2} \dot {y} _ {2} + \frac {I}{r ^ {2}} \dot {y} _ {2} \\ \frac {\partial \widetilde {L}}{\partial y _ {2}} = & \left\{ \begin{array}{l l} k _ {2} (b - y _ {1} - y _ {2}) - m _ {2} g - \frac {k _ {1}}{r ^ {2}} y _ {2} - \frac {l _ {2} q _ {2} ^ {2}}{2 C _ {2 0} (l _ {1} - y _ {2}) ^ {2}} & y _ {2} > 0 \\ k _ {2} (b - y _ {1} - y _ {2}) - m _ {2} g - \frac {k _ {1}}{r ^ {2}} y _ {2} & y _ {2} \leqslant 0 \end{array} \right. \\ & Q _ {q _ {1}} = - R _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1}, \quad Q _ {q _ {2}} = - R _ {2} \dot {q} _ {2}, \quad Q _ {y _ {1}} = 0, \quad Q _ {y _ {2}} = 0 \end{array}
$$

运动微分方程为

$$
L _ {1} \ddot {q} _ {1} + M \ddot {q} _ {2} + \frac {l _ {1} q _ {1}}{C _ {1 0} (l _ {1} - y _ {1})} - E _ {1} = - R _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} \quad y _ {1} > 0
$$

$$
L _ {1} \ddot {q} _ {1} + M \ddot {q} _ {2} + \frac {1}{C _ {1 0}} q _ {1} - E _ {1} = - R _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} \quad y _ {1} \leqslant 0
$$

$$
L _ {2} \ddot {q} _ {2} + M \ddot {q} _ {1} + \frac {l _ {2} q _ {2}}{C _ {2 0} (l _ {2} - y _ {2})} - E _ {2} = - R _ {2} \dot {q} _ {2}
$$

$$
y _ {2} > 0
$$

$$
L _ {2} \ddot {q} _ {2} + M \ddot {q} _ {1} + \frac {q _ {2}}{C _ {2 0}} - E _ {2} = - R _ {2} \dot {q} _ {2}
$$

$$
y _ {2} \leqslant 0
$$

$$
m _ {1} \ddot {y} _ {1} - k _ {2} (b - y _ {1} - y _ {2}) + m _ {1} g + \frac {l _ {1} q _ {1} ^ {2}}{2 C _ {1 0} (l _ {1} - y _ {1}) ^ {2}} = 0
$$

$$
y _ {1} > 0
$$

$$
m _ {1} \ddot {y} _ {1} - k _ {2} (b - y _ {1} - y _ {2}) + m _ {1} g = 0
$$

$$
y _ {1} \leqslant 0
$$

$$
m _ {2} \ddot {y} _ {2} + \frac {I}{r ^ {2}} \ddot {y} _ {2} - k _ {2} (b - y _ {1} - y _ {2}) + m _ {2} g - \frac {k _ {1}}{r ^ {2}} y _ {2} + \frac {l _ {2} q _ {2} ^ {2}}{2 C _ {2 0} (l _ {2} - y _ {2}) ^ {2}} = 0, y _ {2} > 0
$$

$$
m _ {2} \ddot {y} _ {2} + \frac {I}{r ^ {2}} \ddot {y} _ {2} - k _ {2} (b - y _ {1} - y _ {2}) + m _ {2} g - \frac {k _ {1}}{r ^ {2}} y _ {2} = 0
$$

$$
y _ {2} \leqslant 0
$$

其中 $E_{1}=E_{0}\sin\omega t.$ 

9.4.19 图 9.96(a)、(b)、(c) 分别画了三个系统. 在 (a) 中, E 提供恒定的电压, 并假定线圈间无互感；在(b)中， $F$ 是恒定的外力，每个木块受到黏性力 $-\alpha_{i} \dot{x}_{i}$ ；在(c)中， $\tau$ 是恒定的外力矩，每个圆盘受到制动器的黏性力矩 $-b_{i} r_{i} \dot{\theta}_{i}, k_{1}, k_{2}$ 是弹簧的扭转常数。写出三个系统的拉格朗日函数，并证明：适当选择坐标，对应的物理量取相同的值，三个系统的运动微分方程相同。

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/7401f689d4eaf2058aa4aaeb8e3b965ac1aa601ffef2450e5c6e3d71ea1b3d67.jpg)



(a)


![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/fe540b171fe6c23a5f34003f50e2b9603bf342235adf6dcccc985e71f591796c.jpg)



(b)


![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/697e895609ea659a12deb53483d09ed3a34b7a11cbbf49618e70aca250412468.jpg)



(c)



图9.96


解 对于图 9.96(a) 所示的系统，

$$
T _ {\mathrm{a}} = \frac {1}{2} L _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} L _ {2} \dot {q} _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} L _ {2} \dot {q} _ {3} ^ {2}
$$

$$
V _ {\mathrm{a}} = \frac {1}{2 C _ {1}} q _ {4} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 C _ {2}} q _ {5} ^ {2} = \frac {1}{2 C _ {1}} (q _ {1} - q _ {2}) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2 C _ {2}} (q _ {2} - q _ {3}) ^ {2} - E q _ {1}
$$

$$
\widetilde {L} _ {a} = \frac {1}{2} L _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} L _ {2} \dot {q} _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} L _ {3} \dot {q} _ {3} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2 C _ {1}} (q _ {1} - q _ {2}) ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2 C _ {2}} (q _ {2} - q _ {3}) ^ {2} + E q _ {1}
$$

$$
\widetilde {R} _ {\mathrm{a}} = \frac {1}{2} R _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} R _ {2} \dot {q} _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} R _ {3} \dot {q} _ {3} ^ {2}
$$

运动微分方程为

$$
L _ {1} \ddot {q} _ {1} + \frac {1}{C _ {1}} q _ {1} - \frac {1}{C _ {1}} q _ {2} - E = - R _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1}
$$

$$
L _ {2} \ddot {q} _ {2} - \frac {1}{C _ {1}} q _ {1} + \left(\frac {1}{C _ {1}} + \frac {1}{C _ {2}}\right) q _ {2} - \frac {1}{C _ {2}} q _ {3} = - R _ {2} \dot {q} _ {2}
$$

$$
L _ {3} \ddot {q} _ {3} - \frac {1}{C _ {2}} q _ {2} + \frac {1}{C _ {2}} q _ {3} = - R _ {3} \dot {q} _ {3}.
$$

对于图 9.96(b) 所示的系统, 设劲度系数为 $k_{1}, k_{2}$ 的两弹簧的原长分别为 $l_{10}, l_{20}$ ,

$$
T _ {\mathrm{b}} = \frac {1}{2} m _ {1} \dot {x} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m _ {2} \dot {x} _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m _ {3} \dot {x} _ {3} ^ {2}
$$

$$
V _ {\mathrm{b}} = \frac {1}{2} k _ {1} (x _ {2} - x _ {1} - l _ {1 0}) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k _ {2} (x _ {3} - x _ {2} - l _ {2 0}) ^ {2} - F x _ {1}
$$

$$
L _ {\mathrm{b}} = \frac {1}{2} m _ {1} \dot {x} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m _ {2} \dot {x} _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m _ {3} \dot {x} _ {3} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k _ {1} (x _ {2} - x _ {1} - l _ {1 0}) ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k _ {2} (x _ {3} - x _ {2} - l _ {2 0}) ^ {2} + F x
$$

$$
R _ {\mathrm{b}} = \frac {1}{2} \alpha_ {1} \dot {x} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \alpha_ {2} \dot {x} _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \alpha_ {3} \dot {x} _ {3} ^ {2}
$$

运动微分方程为

$$
m _ {1} \ddot {x} _ {1} - k _ {1} (x _ {2} - x _ {1} - l _ {1 0}) - F = - \alpha_ {1} \dot {x} _ {1}
$$

$$
m _ {2} \ddot {x} _ {2} + k _ {1} (x _ {2} - x _ {1} - l _ {1 0}) - k _ {2} (x _ {3} - x _ {2} - l _ {2 0}) = - \alpha_ {2} \dot {x} _ {2}
$$

$$
m _ {3} \ddot {x} _ {3} + k _ {2} (x _ {3} - x _ {2} - l _ {2 0}) = - \alpha_ {3} \dot {x} _ {3}
$$

对于图 9.96(c) 所示的系统，

$$
T _ {\mathrm{C}} = \frac {1}{2} I _ {1} \dot {\theta} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} I _ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} I _ {3} \dot {\theta} _ {3} ^ {2}
$$

$$
V _ {\mathrm{C}} = \frac {1}{2} k _ {1} (\theta_ {2} - \theta_ {1}) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k _ {2} (\theta_ {3} - \theta_ {2}) ^ {2} - \tau \theta_ {1},
$$

$$
L _ {\mathrm{C}} = \frac {1}{2} (I _ {1} \dot {\theta} _ {1} ^ {2} + I _ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {2} ^ {2} + I _ {3} \dot {\theta} _ {3} ^ {2}) - \frac {1}{2} k _ {1} (\theta_ {2} - \theta_ {1}) ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k _ {2} (\theta_ {3} - \theta_ {2}) ^ {2} + \tau \theta_ {1}
$$

$$
R _ {\mathrm{C}} = \frac {1}{2} b _ {1} r _ {1} \dot {\theta} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} b _ {2} r _ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} b _ {3} r _ {3} \dot {\theta} _ {3} ^ {2}
$$

运动微分方程为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & I _ {1} \ddot {\theta} _ {1} + k _ {1} \theta_ {1} - k _ {1} \theta_ {2} - \tau = - b _ {1} r _ {1} \dot {\theta} _ {1} \\ & I _ {2} \ddot {\theta} _ {2} - k _ {1} \theta_ {1} + (k _ {1} + k _ {2}) \theta_ {2} - k _ {2} \theta_ {3} = - b _ {2} r _ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {2} \\ & I _ {3} \ddot {\theta} _ {3} - k _ {2} \theta_ {2} + k _ {2} \theta_ {3} = - b _ {3} r _ {3} \dot {\theta} _ {3} \end{array}
$$

若三个系统所取的广义坐标的零点均取在平衡位置，即令 $q_{i}^{\prime} = q_{i} - q_{i0}, x_{i}^{\prime} = x_{i} - x_{i0}, \theta_{i}^{\prime} = \theta_{i} - \theta_{i0}, (i = 1,2,3), q_{i0}$ 是图9.96(a)系统平衡时，第 $i$ 个电容带正电的极板上的电荷， $x_{i0}$ 是图9.96(b)系统平衡时，第 $i$ 个木块的坐标， $\theta_{i0}$ 是图9.96(c)系统平衡时，第 $i$ 个圆盘的转角。它们满足的方程，可由相应的运动微分方程令广义坐标对时间的一阶、二阶导数等于零获得，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & \left\{ \begin{array}{l} \frac {q _ {1 0}}{C _ {1}} - \frac {q _ {2 0}}{C _ {1}} - E = 0 \\ - \frac {q _ {1 0}}{C _ {1}} + \left(\frac {1}{C _ {1}} + \frac {1}{C _ {2}}\right) q _ {2 0} - \frac {q _ {3 0}}{C _ {2}} = 0 \\ - \frac {q _ {2 0}}{C _ {2}} + \frac {q _ {3 0}}{C _ {2}} = 0 \end{array} \right. \\ & \left\{ \begin{array}{l} - k _ {1} (x _ {2 0} - x _ {1 0} - l _ {1 0}) - F = 0 \\ k _ {1} (x _ {2 0} - x _ {1 0} - l _ {1 0}) - k _ {2} (x _ {3 0} - x _ {2 0} - l _ {2 0}) = 0 \\ k _ {2} (x _ {3 0} - x _ {2 0} - l _ {2 0}) = 0 \end{array} \right. \\ & \left\{ \begin{array}{l} k _ {1} \theta_ {1 0} - k _ {1} \theta_ {2 0} - \tau = 0 \\ - k _ {1} \theta_ {1 0} + (k _ {1} + k _ {2}) \theta_ {2 0} - k _ {2} \theta_ {3 0} = 0 \\ - k _ {2} \theta_ {2 0} + k _ {2} \theta_ {3 0} = 0 \end{array} \right. \end{array}
$$

三个系统的运动微分方程可改写为

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} L _ {1} \ddot {q} _ {1} ^ {\prime} + \frac {1}{C _ {1}} q _ {1} ^ {\prime} - \frac {1}{C _ {1}} q _ {2} ^ {\prime} = - R _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} ^ {\prime} \\ L _ {2} \ddot {q} _ {2} ^ {\prime} - \frac {1}{C _ {2}} q _ {1} ^ {\prime} + \left(\frac {1}{C _ {1}} + \frac {1}{C _ {2}}\right) q _ {2} ^ {\prime} - \frac {1}{C _ {2}} q _ {3} ^ {\prime} = - R _ {2} \dot {q} _ {2} ^ {\prime} \\ L _ {3} \ddot {q} _ {3} ^ {\prime} - \frac {1}{C _ {2}} q _ {2} ^ {\prime} + \frac {1}{C _ {2}} q _ {3} ^ {\prime} = - R _ {3} \dot {q} _ {3} ^ {\prime} \\ \left\{ \begin{array}{l} m _ {1} \ddot {x} _ {1} ^ {\prime} + k _ {1} x _ {1} ^ {\prime} - k _ {1} x _ {2} ^ {\prime} = - a _ {1} \dot {x} _ {1} ^ {\prime} \\ m _ {2} \ddot {x} _ {2} ^ {\prime} - k _ {1} x _ {1} ^ {\prime} + (k _ {1} + k _ {2}) x _ {2} ^ {\prime} - k _ {2} x _ {3} ^ {\prime} = - a _ {2} \dot {x} _ {2} ^ {\prime} \\ m _ {3} \ddot {x} _ {3} ^ {\prime} - k _ {2} x _ {2} ^ {\prime} + k _ {2} x _ {3} ^ {\prime} = - a _ {3} \dot {x} _ {3} ^ {\prime} \end{array} \right. \\ \left\{ \begin{array}{l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l l} I _ {1} \ddot {\theta_ {1}} ^ {\prime} + k _ {1} \theta_ {1} ^ {\prime} - k _ {1} \theta_ {2} ^ {\prime} = - b _ {1} r _ {1} \dot {\theta_ {1}} ^ {\prime} \\ I _ {2} \ddot {\theta_ {2}} ^ {\prime} - k _ {1} \theta_ {1} ^ {\prime} + (k _ {1} + k _ {2}) \theta_ {2} ^ {\prime} - k _ {2} \theta_ {3} ^ {\prime} = - b _ {2} r _ {2} \dot {\theta_ {2}} ^ {\prime} \\ I _ {3} \ddot {\theta_ {3}} ^ {\prime} - k _ {3} \dot {\theta_ {2}} ^ {\prime} + k _ {2} \theta_ {3} ^ {\prime} = - b _ {3} r _ {3} \dot {\theta_ {3}} ^ {\prime}. \end{array} \right.
$$

只要 $L_{i} = m_{i} = I_{i},\frac{1}{C_{i}} = k_{i},R_{i} = \alpha_{i} = b_{i}r_{i},(i = 1,2,3)$ ，则 $q_{i}^{\prime},x_{i}^{\prime},\theta_{i}^{\prime}(i = 1,2,3)$ 遵从完全相同的微分方程.

9.4.20 列出图 9.97(a)、(b) 系统的运动微分方程, 质量为 $m_{1}$ 、 $m_{2}$ 的两木块底面受到黏性阻力, 黏度分别为 $\alpha_{1}$ 、 $\alpha_{2}$ , 减震器也提供一个黏性阻力, 黏度为 $\alpha_{3}$ , 证明两个系统可以是等效的.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/7001237b2dd770dd47af9d8009f0d98f676bc83a51f9055c88c762ab3045eb1a.jpg)



(a)


![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/3559c0a1f77f84c68d19b364df2df49a4dcd0193d7477aab0205be158417562f.jpg)



(b)



图9.97


证明 设图 9.97(a) 系统无 F 时，在 $x_{1}=x_{10}, x_{2}=x_{20}$ 是系统的平衡位置，取此位置为势能的零点，则

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & V _ {\mathrm{a}} = \frac {1}{2} k _ {1} (x _ {1} - x _ {1 0}) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k _ {2} (x _ {2} - x _ {2 0}) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k _ {3} [ (x _ {2} - x _ {2 0}) - (x _ {1} - x _ {1 0}) ] ^ {2} \\ & L _ {\mathrm{a}} = \frac {1}{2} m _ {1} \dot {x} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m _ {2} \dot {x} _ {2} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k _ {1} (x _ {1} - x _ {1 0}) ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k _ {2} (x _ {2} - x _ {2 0}) ^ {2} \\ & \quad - \frac {1}{2} k _ {3} [ (x _ {2} - x _ {2 0}) - (x _ {1} - x _ {1 0}) ] ^ {2} \end{array}
$$

令 $x_{1}^{\prime} = x_{1} - x_{10}, x_{2}^{\prime} = x_{2} - x_{20}$ ，则

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {L _ {a} = \frac {1}{2} m _ {1} \dot {x} _ {1} ^ {\prime 2} + \frac {1}{2} m _ {2} \dot {x} _ {2} ^ {\prime 2} - \frac {1}{2} k _ {1} x _ {1} ^ {\prime 2} - \frac {1}{2} k _ {2} x _ {2} ^ {\prime 2} - \frac {1}{2} k _ {3} (x _ {2} ^ {\prime} - x _ {1} ^ {\prime}) ^ {2}} \\ & {\delta W = F _ {0} \sin \omega t \delta x _ {1} - \alpha_ {1} \dot {x} _ {1} \delta x _ {1} - \alpha_ {2} \dot {x} _ {2} \delta x _ {2} + \alpha_ {3} (\dot {x} _ {2} - \dot {x} _ {1}) \delta x _ {1} - \alpha_ {3} (\dot {x} _ {2} - \dot {x} _ {1}) \delta x _ {2}} \\ & {\qquad = F _ {0} \sin \omega t \delta x _ {1} ^ {\prime} - \alpha_ {1} \dot {x} _ {1} ^ {\prime} \delta x _ {1} ^ {\prime} + \alpha_ {3} (\dot {x} _ {2} ^ {\prime} - \dot {x} _ {1} ^ {\prime}) \delta x _ {1} ^ {\prime} - \alpha_ {2} \dot {x} _ {2} ^ {\prime} \delta x _ {2} ^ {\prime}} \\ & {\qquad - \alpha_ {3} (\dot {x} _ {2} ^ {\prime} - \dot {x} _ {1} ^ {\prime}) \delta x _ {2} ^ {\prime}} \\ & {Q _ {1} = F _ {0} \sin \omega t - \alpha_ {1} \dot {x} _ {1} ^ {\prime} + \alpha_ {3} (\dot {x} _ {2} ^ {\prime} - \dot {x} _ {1} ^ {\prime})} \\ & {Q _ {2} = - \alpha_ {2} \dot {x} _ {2} ^ {\prime} - \alpha_ {3} (\dot {x} _ {2} ^ {\prime} - \dot {x} _ {1} ^ {\prime})} \\ & {\widetilde L _ {\mathrm{b}} = \frac {1}{2} L _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} L _ {2} \dot {q} _ {2} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2 C _ {1}} q _ {1} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2 C _ {2}} q _ {2} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2 C _ {2}} (q _ {1} - q _ {2}) ^ {2}} \\ & {\delta W = - E _ {0} \sin \omega t \delta q _ {1} - R _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} \delta q _ {1} - R _ {2} \dot {q} _ {2} \delta q _ {2} - R _ {3} \dot {q} _ {3} \delta q _ {3}} \\ & {\qquad = - E _ {0} \sin \omega t \delta q _ {1} - R _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} \delta q _ {1} - R _ {2} \dot {q} _ {2} \delta q _ {2} - R _ {3} (\dot {q} _ {2} - \dot {q} _ {1}) (\delta q _ {2} - \delta q _ {1})} \\ & {Q _ {1} = - E _ {0} \sin \omega t - R _ {1} \dot {q} _ {1} + R _ {3} (\dot {q} _ {2} - \dot {q} _ {1})} \\ & {Q _ {2} = - R _ {2} \dot {q} _ {2} - R _ {3} (\dot {q} _ {2} - \dot {q} _ {1})} \end{array}
$$

比较两个系统的拉格朗日函数和广义力可见，只要 $m_{1} = L_{1}, m_{2} = L_{2}, k_{1} = \frac{1}{C_{1}}, k_{2} = \frac{1}{C_{1}}, k_{3} = \frac{1}{C_{3}}, F_{0} = -E_{0}, \alpha_{1} = R_{1}, \alpha_{2} = R_{2}, \alpha_{3} = R_{3}, x_{1}^{\prime}$ 与 $q_{1}, x_{2}^{\prime}$ 与 $q_{2}$ 有完全相同的运动微分方程，这样，两个系统就完全等效了.

## 第十章 有限多自由度系统的小振动

## 10.1 自由的小振动

10.1.1 一个刚性结构由三根无质量的杆联结在一起，并贴着质量均为 $m$ 的两个点质量组成， $AB = BC = L$ ， $BD = l$ ， $\angle ABD = \angle DBC = \theta$ ，如图10.1那样支承在固定点 $D$ 上，并以小振幅前后摆动。求振动的角频率，要做这样的振动对 $l$ 有何要求？

解 系统的质心位于 AC 的中点, 在 BD 的延长线上, 系统的重力势能等于位于质心的具有系统质量的质点的重力势能.

$$
V = 2 m (L \cos \theta - l) (1 - \cos \varphi) g \approx m g (L \cos \theta - l) \varphi^ {2}
$$

其中 $\varphi$ 是偏离平衡位置 $(\varphi = 0)$ 的角位移， $\varphi$ 是 $BD$ 与竖直线间的夹角，最后的式子已考虑了小振幅振动， $\varphi$ 是小量， $1 - \cos \varphi \approx \frac{1}{2} \varphi^2$ .

$$
A D = C D = \sqrt {L ^ {2} + l ^ {2} - 2 L l \cos \theta}
$$

系统是个刚性结构,位于 A、C 的两质点对 D 点的角速度等于质心对 D 点的角速度 $\dot{\varphi}$ , 系统的动能为

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/333c4624261bc527f828e1e1e8c3d4bcd2509efae56e621916780c7480bd2c4d.jpg)


$$
T = 2 \times \frac {1}{2} m (A D \dot {\varphi}) ^ {2} = m \left(L ^ {2} + l ^ {2} - 2 L l \cos \theta\right) \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}
$$

图10.1

系统的拉格朗日函数为

由

$$
\begin{array}{r l} L & = m (L ^ {2} + l ^ {2} - 2 L l \cos \theta) \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} - m g (L \cos \theta - l) \varphi^ {2} \\ & \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\varphi}}\right) - \frac {\partial L}{\partial \varphi} = 0 \end{array}
$$

得运动微分方程为

$$
(L ^ {2} + l ^ {2} - 2 L l \cos \theta) \ddot {\varphi} + (L \cos \theta - l) g \varphi = 0
$$

小幅振动的角频率为

$$
\omega = \left[ \frac {(L \cos \theta - l) g}{L ^ {2} + l ^ {2} - 2 L l \cos \theta} \right] ^ {1 / 2}
$$

在平衡位置, $\frac{\partial V}{\partial\varphi}=0$ ,由此得 $\varphi=0$ .

要在平衡位置附近做小振动,必须是稳定的平衡位置,要求在平衡位置有 $\frac{\partial^{2}V}{\partial\varphi^{2}}>0$ ,

$$
\frac {\partial^ {2} V}{\partial \varphi^ {2}} = 2 m g (L \cos \theta - l) > 0
$$

要求 $l$ 满足的条件是

$$
l <   L \cos \theta
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/9cdc9ace590b91f50ef3744341ed811c25cf023e1a6c7d11268b5417db118e53.jpg)


10.1.2 质量为 $m_{1}$ 的质点用长为 $l_{1}$ 的、不可伸长的轻绳系于固定点 O，另一质量为 $m_{2}$ 的质点用长为 $l_{2}$ 不可伸长的轻绳系于前一质点上，求此系统做小振动的简正模式和相应的角频率.

解 取图 10.2 的坐标, $\theta$ 、 $\varphi$ 为广义坐标,

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & x _ {1} = l _ {1} \sin \theta , y _ {1} = l _ {1} \cos \theta \\ & \quad x _ {2} = l _ {1} \sin \theta + l _ {2} \sin \varphi \\ & \quad y _ {2} = l _ {1} \cos \theta + l _ {2} \cos \varphi \end{array}
$$


图10.2


系统的动能(做小振动近似)为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} T & = \frac {1}{2} m _ {1} (\dot {x} _ {1} ^ {2} + \dot {y} _ {1} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{2} m _ {2} (\dot {x} _ {2} ^ {2} + \dot {y} _ {2} ^ {2}) \\ & = \frac {1}{2} (m _ {1} + m _ {2}) l _ {1} ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m _ {2} l _ {2} ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + m _ {2} l _ {1} l _ {2} \dot {\theta} \dot {\varphi} \cos (\varphi - \theta) \\ & \approx \frac {1}{2} (m _ {1} + m _ {2}) l _ {1} ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m _ {2} l _ {2} ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + m _ {2} l _ {1} l _ {2} \dot {\theta} \dot {\varphi} \end{array}
$$

系统的势能(做小振动近似)为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} V & = - m _ {1} g y _ {1} - m _ {2} g y _ {2} \\ & = - (m _ {1} + m _ {2}) g l _ {1} \cos \theta - m _ {2} g l _ {2} \cos \varphi \\ & \approx V _ {0} + \frac {1}{2} (m _ {1} + m _ {2}) g l _ {1} \theta^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m _ {2} g l _ {2} \varphi^ {2} \end{array}
$$

其中 $V_{0}$ 是常量，势能的零点可以任意选取， $V_{0}$ 值无关重要，故不必具体写出.

惯性矩阵和刚度矩阵分别为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \boldsymbol {M} & = \left( \begin{array}{c c} (m _ {1} + m _ {2}) l _ {1} ^ {2} & m _ {2} l _ {1} l _ {2} \\ m _ {2} l _ {1} l _ {2} & m _ {2} l _ {2} ^ {2} \end{array} \right) \\ \boldsymbol {K} & = \left( \begin{array}{c c} (m _ {1} + m _ {2}) g l _ {1} & 0 \\ 0 & m _ {2} g l _ {2} \end{array} \right) \end{array}
$$

系统有两个自由度,有两个简正频率,

即

$$
\begin{array}{l} \theta = u _ {1} \cos (\omega t + \alpha), \quad \varphi = u _ {2} \cos (\omega t + \alpha) \\ (K - \omega^ {2} M) \cdot u = 0 \\ \left( \begin{array}{c c} (m _ {1} + m _ {2}) l _ {1} (g - l _ {1} \omega^ {2}) & - m _ {2} l _ {1} l _ {2} \omega^ {2} \\ - m _ {2} l _ {1} l _ {2} \omega^ {2} & m _ {2} l _ {2} (g - l _ {2} \omega^ {2}) \end{array} \right) \binom {u _ {1}} {u _ {2}} = 0 \end{array}\tag{1}
$$

$u_{1}$ 、 $u_{2}$ 要得非零解，必须

$$
\left| \begin{array}{c c} (m _ {1} + m _ {2}) l _ {1} (g - l _ {1} \omega^ {2}) & - m _ {2} l _ {1} l _ {2} \omega^ {2} \\ - m _ {2} l _ {1} l _ {2} \omega^ {2} & m _ {2} l _ {2} (g - l _ {2} \omega^ {2}) \end{array} \right| = 0
$$

可得

$$
\begin{array}{c}{m _ {1} l _ {1} l _ {2} \omega^ {4} - (m _ {1} + m _ {2}) (l _ {1} + l _ {2}) g \omega^ {2} + (m _ {1} + m _ {2}) g ^ {2} = 0}\\{\omega_ {1}, \omega_ {2} = \left\{ \right.\frac {g}{2 m _ {1} l _ {1} l _ {2}} \Big [ (m _ {1} + m _ {2}) (l _ {1} + l _ {2}) \pm \sqrt {(m _ {1} + m _ {2}) \big [ m _ {2} (l _ {1} + l _ {2}) ^ {2} + m _ {1} (l _ {1} - l _ {2}) ^ {2} \big ]} \left. \right]\left. \right\} ^ {1 / 2}}\end{array}
$$

其中 $\omega_{1}$ 的式子用式中的“+”号， $\omega_{2}$ 的式子用式中的“-”号.

用 $\omega_{1}$ 代入(1)式，解出的 $u_{1}, u_{2}$ 标为 $u_{1}^{(1)}, u_{2}^{(1)}$ 

$$
\frac {u _ {1} ^ {(1)}}{u _ {2} ^ {(1)}} = \frac {1}{2 l _ {1}} \left[ l _ {1} - l _ {2} - \sqrt {\frac {m _ {2} (l _ {1} + l _ {2}) ^ {2} + m _ {1} (l _ {1} - l _ {2}) ^ {2}}{m _ {1} + m _ {2}}} \right]
$$

用 $\omega_{2}$ 代入(1)式，得

$$
\frac {u _ {1} ^ {(2)}}{u _ {2} ^ {(2)}} = \frac {1}{2 l _ {1}} \left[ l _ {1} - l _ {2} + \sqrt {\frac {m _ {2} (l _ {1} + l _ {2}) ^ {2} + m _ {1} (l _ {1} - l _ {2}) ^ {2}}{m _ {1} + m _ {2}}} \right]
$$

可以考虑以下极限情况得到熟知的结果,作为上述计算的正确性的一种检验.

当 $m_{1}\rightarrow 0$ 时，

$$
\omega = \omega_ {2} \rightarrow \sqrt {\frac {g}{\left(l _ {1} + l _ {2}\right)}}
$$

当 $l_{1}\rightarrow 0$ 时，

$$
\omega = \omega_ {2} \rightarrow \sqrt {\frac {g}{l _ {2}}}
$$

当 $l_{2}\rightarrow 0$ 时，

$$
\omega = \omega_ {2} \rightarrow \sqrt {\frac {g}{l _ {1}}}
$$

$\omega_{1}$ 均为无穷大, 系统不运动, 这也是合理的, 因为上述几个极限情况, 都只有一个自由度, 只存在一个简正频率.

10.1.3 半径为 r 的均质球在一个半径为 R 的固定球壳内表面滚动, 试求该球在平衡位置附近做小振动的周期.

解 设球的质量为 m，

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} \left(\frac {2}{5} m r ^ {2} + m r ^ {2}\right) \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}
$$

由于纯滚动，

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/1947f75ad42c2a233a08329689b45e82d294c1f2579680aea344a20352fab519.jpg)



图10.3


由

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & (R - r) \dot {\theta} - r \dot {\varphi} = 0 \\ & \dot {\varphi} = \frac {R - r}{r} \dot {\theta} \\ & T = \frac {7}{1 0} m r ^ {2} \left(\frac {R - r}{r} \dot {\theta}\right) ^ {2} = \frac {7}{1 0} m (R - r) ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} \\ & V = m g (R - r) (1 - \cos \theta) \approx \frac {1}{2} m g (R - r) \theta^ {2} \\ & L = T - V = \frac {7}{1 0} m (R - r) ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} m g (R - r) \theta^ {2} \\ & \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\theta}}\right) - \frac {\partial L}{\partial \theta} = 0 \\ & \frac {7}{5} m (R - r) ^ {2} \ddot {\theta} + m g (R - r) \theta = 0 \\ & \omega = \sqrt {\frac {5 g}{7 (R - r)}} \\ & T = \frac {2 \pi}{\omega} = 2 \pi \sqrt {\frac {7 (R - r)}{5 g}} \end{array}
$$

得

10.1.4 前题所述的双摆,能否在一定条件下,系统的运动为一个单摆?

解 由前题解出的简正频率和振动模式,可以写出系统运动的通解如下:

其中

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\theta = \frac {1}{2 l _ {1}} \Bigg [ l _ {1} - l _ {2} - \sqrt {\frac {m _ {2} (l _ {1} + l _ {2}) ^ {2} + m _ {1} (l _ {1} - l _ {2}) ^ {2}}{m _ {1} + m _ {2}}} \Bigg ] A \cos (\omega_ {1} t + \alpha_ {1})} \\ & {\qquad + \frac {1}{2 l _ {1}} \Bigg [ l _ {1} - l _ {2} + \sqrt {\frac {m _ {2} (l _ {1} + l _ {2}) ^ {2} + m _ {1} (l _ {1} - l _ {2}) ^ {2}}{m _ {1} + m _ {2}}} \Bigg ] B \cos (\omega_ {2} t + \alpha_ {2})} \\ & {\varphi = A \cos (\omega_ {1} t + \alpha_ {1}) + B \cos (\omega_ {2} t + \alpha_ {2})} \\ & {\omega_ {1} = \Bigg \{\frac {g}{2 m _ {1} l _ {1} l _ {2}} \big [ (m _ {1} + m _ {2}) (l _ {1} + l _ {2})} \\ & {\qquad + \sqrt {(m _ {1} + m _ {2}) [ m _ {2} (l _ {1} + l _ {2}) ^ {2} + m _ {1} (l _ {1} - l _ {2}) ^ {2} ]} \Bigg ] \Bigg \} ^ {\frac {1}{2}}} \\ & {\omega_ {2} = \Bigg \{\frac {g}{2 m _ {1} l _ {1} l _ {2}} \big [ (m _ {1} + m _ {2}) (l _ {1} + l _ {2})} \\ & {\qquad - \sqrt {(m _ {1} + m _ {2}) [ m _ {2} (l _ {1} + l _ {2}) ^ {2} + m _ {1} (l _ {1} - l _ {2}) ^ {2} ]} \Bigg ] \Bigg \} ^ {\frac {1}{2}}} \end{array}
$$

要使系统像一个单摆那样运动,需 $\theta=\varphi$ .

有两种情况, $\theta=\varphi:$ 

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\mathrm{(a)} \frac {1}{2 l _ {1}} \Big [ l _ {1} - l _ {2} - \sqrt {\frac {m _ {2} (l _ {1} + l _ {2}) ^ {2} + m _ {1} (l _ {1} - l _ {2}) ^ {2}}{m _ {1} + m _ {2}}} \Big ] = 1, \text {且} B = 0;} \\ & {\mathrm{(b)} \frac {1}{2 l _ {1}} \Big [ l _ {1} - l _ {2} + \sqrt {\frac {m _ {2} (l _ {1} + l _ {2}) ^ {2} + m _ {1} (l _ {1} - l _ {2}) ^ {2}}{m _ {1} + m _ {2}}} \Big ] = 1, \text {且} A = 0.} \end{array}
$$

第一种情况不可能,因为

$$
- \sqrt {\frac {m _ {2} (l _ {1} + l _ {2}) ^ {2} + m _ {1} (l _ {1} - l _ {2}) ^ {2}}{m _ {1} + m _ {2}}} = l _ {1} + l _ {2}
$$

不能成立,等号左边为负值,右边为正值,不可能相等.

第二种情况是可能的,但要求 $l_{1}l_{2}=0$ ,即 $l_{1}=0$ 或 $l_{2}=0$ ,这两种情况实际上就是一个单摆,因此可作出结论:双摆系统在任何情况下都不可能像单摆那样运动.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/da90e53f99a7acfcde16e405c4743bec80ac7c0e8112161d446f6cf1de9f1ee1.jpg)



图10.4


10.1.5 一个长度为 $4l$ 、质量为 $m$ 的单摆悬挂在另一个长度为 $3l$ 、质量为 $m$ 的单摆下面，如图10.4所示。这系统可能做这样的绕平衡位置的小振动：下面单摆上的某一点无水平位移，找出这一点的位置。

解 分别用 $(x_{1},y_{1})$ 、 $(x_{2},y_{2})$ 表示上、下两个质点的坐标.

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & x _ {1} = 3 l \sin \theta , y _ {1} = 3 l \cos \theta \\ & x _ {2} = 3 l \sin \theta + 4 l \sin \varphi \\ & y _ {2} = 3 l \cos \theta + 4 l \cos \varphi \\ T = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} _ {1} ^ {2} + \dot {y} _ {1} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} _ {2} ^ {2} + \dot {y} _ {2} ^ {2}) \end{array}
$$

$$
= \frac {1}{2} m \left[ 1 8 l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + 1 6 l ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + 2 4 l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} \dot {\varphi} \cos (\theta - \varphi) \right]
$$

$$
V = - m g y _ {1} - m g y _ {2} = - m g (6 l \cos \theta + 4 l \cos \varphi)
$$

$$
L = \frac {1}{2} m l ^ {2} \left[ 1 8 \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + 1 6 \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + 2 4 \dot {\theta} \dot {\varphi} \cos (\theta - \varphi) \right] + m g l (6 \cos \theta + 4 \cos \varphi)
$$

由

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {q} _ {i}}\right) - \frac {\partial L}{\partial q _ {i}} = 0
$$

得

$$
3 \ddot {\theta} + 2 \ddot {\varphi} \cos (\theta - \varphi) - 2 \dot {\varphi} (\dot {\theta} - \dot {\varphi}) \sin (\theta - \varphi) + \frac {g}{l} \sin \theta = 0
$$

$$
4 \ddot {\varphi} + 3 \ddot {\theta} \cos (\theta - \varphi) - 3 \dot {\theta} (\dot {\theta} - \dot {\varphi}) \sin (\theta - \varphi) + \frac {g}{l} \sin \varphi = 0
$$

对于小振动，上两式只保留一级小量，

$$
3 \ddot {\theta} + 2 \ddot {\varphi} + \frac {g}{l} \theta = 0
$$

$$
3 \ddot {\theta} + 4 \ddot {\varphi} + \frac {g}{l} \varphi = 0
$$

试用

$$
\theta = \theta_ {0} \mathrm{e} ^ {\mathrm{i} \omega t}, \quad \varphi = \varphi_ {0} \mathrm{e} ^ {\mathrm{i} \omega t}
$$

代入上述两方程,得

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} \left(\frac {g}{l} - 3 \omega^ {2}\right) \theta_ {0} - 2 \omega^ {2} \varphi_ {0} = 0 \\ - 3 \omega^ {2} \theta_ {0} + \left(\frac {g}{l} - 4 \omega^ {2}\right) \varphi_ {0} = 0 \end{array} \right.\tag{1}
$$

特征方程为

$$
\left| \begin{array}{c c} \frac {g}{l} - 3 \omega^ {2} & - 2 \omega^ {2} \\ - 3 \omega^ {2} & \frac {g}{l} - 4 \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right| = 0
$$

解出简正频率，

$$
\omega_ {1} = \sqrt {\frac {g}{l}}, \quad \omega_ {2} = \sqrt {\frac {g}{6 l}}
$$

将 $\omega_{1},\omega_{2}$ 分别代入(1)式，得

$$
\varphi_ {0} ^ {(1)} = - \theta_ {0} ^ {(1)}, \quad \varphi_ {0} ^ {(2)} = \frac {3}{2} \theta_ {0} ^ {(2)}
$$

$\theta, \varphi$ 的通解可写为

$$
\theta = A \cos (\omega_ {1} t + \alpha_ {1}) + B \cos (\omega_ {2} t + \alpha_ {2})
$$

$$
\varphi = - A \cos (\omega_ {1} t + \alpha_ {1}) + \frac {3}{2} B \cos (\omega_ {2} t + \alpha_ {2})
$$

设下面的摆绳上离上面的质点的距离为 s 的一点在小振动中不动, 则

$$
\begin{array}{r l} 3 l \theta + x \varphi & = 3 l [ A \cos (\omega_ {1} t + \alpha_ {1}) + B \cos (\omega_ {2} t + \alpha_ {2}) ] \\ & + s [ - A \cos (\omega_ {1} t + \alpha_ {1}) + \frac {3}{2} B \cos (\omega_ {2} t + \alpha_ {2}) ] = 0 \end{array}
$$

对一切 $t$ 都成立.

如两种振动模式都被激发,要求 s 满足

$$
3 l - s = 0, \quad 3 l + \frac {3}{2} s = 0
$$

两式同时满足是不可能的,说明只能有一种振动模式被激发.若这一点必须在绳上,则要求s在 $[0,4l]$ 区间内,则要求满足3l-s=0,s=3l.这是只有第一种振动模式被激发的情况;若不要s在 $[0,4l]$ 区间内,可要求 $3l+\frac{3}{2}s=0,s=-2l$ ,这一点在下面那段绳的向上的延长线上离上面质点距离2l处,这是只有第二种振动模式被激发的情况.

10.1.6 $\mathrm{CO}_{2}$ 的简单经典模型是由三个质点组成的线状结构，用弹簧力代替原子间的作用力，两个弹簧自然长度为 $l$ ，劲度系数为 $k$ ，只允许沿连线方向运动. $\mathbf{C}^{4+}$ 的质量为 $M,\mathrm{O}^{2-}$ 的质量为 $m$ 

(1) 这个系统有多少个振动自由度?

(2) 写出各质点的运动微分方程；

(3) 求简正频率和振动模式(振幅比).

解 （1）系统有三个自由度，但只有两个振动自由度.

(2) 用 $x_{1}, x_{2}, x_{3}$ 分别表示 $\mathrm{O}^{2-}, \mathrm{C}^{4+}, \mathrm{O}^{2-}$ 对原平衡位置的位移，

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} m \dot {x} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} M \dot {x} _ {2} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m \dot {x} _ {3} ^ {2}
$$

$$
V = \frac {1}{2} k (x _ {2} - x _ {1}) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} k (x _ {3} - x _ {2}) ^ {2}
$$

$$
L = T - V = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} _ {1} ^ {2} + \dot {x} _ {3} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{2} M \dot {x} _ {2} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k (x _ {2} - x _ {1}) ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k (x _ {3} - x _ {2}) ^ {2}
$$

由

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {x} _ {i}}\right) - \frac {\partial L}{\partial x _ {i}} = 0
$$

得运动微分方程为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & m \ddot {x} _ {1} - k (x _ {2} - x _ {1}) = 0 \\ & M \ddot {x} _ {2} - k (x _ {1} - 2 x _ {2} + x _ {3}) = 0 \\ & m \ddot {x} _ {3} + k (x _ {3} - x _ {2}) = 0 \end{array}
$$

(3) 令 $x_{i} = A_{i}\mathrm{e}^{\mathrm{i}\omega t}$ , 代入微分方程组, 可得

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} (k - m \omega^ {2}) A _ {1} - k A _ {2} = 0 \\ - k A _ {1} + (2 k - M \omega^ {2}) A _ {2} - k A _ {3} = 0 \\ - k A _ {2} + (k - m \omega^ {2}) A _ {3} = 0 \end{array} \right.\tag{1}
$$

$A_{1}, A_{2}, A_{3}$ 有非零解的条件为

$$
\left| \begin{array}{c c c} k - m \omega^ {2} & - k & 0 \\ - k & 2 k - M \omega^ {2} & - k \\ 0 & - k & k - m \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right| = 0
$$

解得

$$
\omega_ {1} = \sqrt {\frac {k}{m}}, \quad \omega_ {2} = \sqrt {\frac {2 m + M}{m M}} k, \quad \omega_ {3} = 0
$$

其中 $\omega_{1}$ 、 $\omega_{2}$ 是所要求的简正频率； $\omega_{3}=0$ ，不是振动，是系统整体的平动。

依次将 $\omega_{1}$ 、 $\omega_{2}$ 代入方程组(1)，可解出相应的振幅比：

$$
\frac {A _ {2} ^ {(1)}}{A _ {1} ^ {(1)}} = 0, \quad \frac {A _ {3} ^ {(1)}}{A _ {1} ^ {(1)}} = - 1
$$

$$
\frac {A _ {2} ^ {(2)}}{A _ {1} ^ {(2)}} = - \frac {2 m}{M}, \quad \frac {A _ {3} ^ {(2)}}{A _ {1} ^ {(2)}} = 1
$$

或写作

$$
\omega_ {1}: (1, 0, - 1), \quad \omega_ {2}: \left(1, - \frac {2 m}{M}, 1\right)
$$

10.1.7 若上题中系统处于平衡位置,突然左边的质点受到一冲量 p 的作用,求此后该质点的运动.

解 由上题求出的振动模式,可写出三个质点运动的通解为

$$
\begin{array}{l} {x _ {1} = C \cos (\omega_ {1} t + \alpha_ {1}) + D \cos (\omega_ {2} t + \alpha_ {2}) + a t + b} \\ {x _ {2} = - \frac {2 m}{M} D \cos (\omega_ {2} t + \alpha_ {2}) + a t + b} \\ {x _ {3} = - C \cos (\omega_ {1} t + \alpha_ {1}) + D \cos (\omega_ {2} t + \alpha_ {2}) + a t + b} \end{array}
$$

其中 $at + b$ 是整体做平动的特解.

C、D、 $\alpha_{1}$ 、 $\alpha_{2}$ 、a、b 均为待定常量，由初始条件：t=0 时， $x_{1}=x_{2}=x_{3}=0,\dot{x}_{1}=\frac{p}{m},\dot{x}_{2}=\dot{x}_{3}=0$ 确定，可得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} C = \frac {p}{2 m \omega_ {1}}, & D = \frac {p M}{2 m (M + 2 m) \omega_ {2}}, \quad \alpha_ {1} = \alpha_ {2} = - \frac {\pi}{2} \\ & a = \frac {p}{M + 2 m}, \quad b = 0 \end{array}
$$

左边质点的运动方程为

$$
x _ {1} = \frac {p}{2 m \omega_ {1}} \sin \omega_ {1} t + \frac {p M}{2 m (M + 2 m) \omega_ {2}} \sin \omega_ {2} t + \frac {p t}{M + 2 m}
$$

10.1.8 一根长 L、截面积 A、质量 M 的均质圆木垂直地浮在水中，并用劲度系数为 k 的轻弹簧与均质的、质量为 M、长度为 2L 的杠杆相连，杠杆的枢轴装在其中心，圆木限于做竖直运动，弹簧为自然长度时杠杆处于水平的平衡位置，如图 10.5 所示，水的密度为 $\rho$ .

(1) 求杠杆小角位移时的简正模(频率和位移比);

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/b802ba453f0034feaae1fea2889929232753f447d5824a5c09d41db57fbf613c.jpg)


(2) 讨论劲度系数 k 很大时的极限情况.

解（1）取杠杆与水平线的夹角为 $\theta$ （顺时针方向

图10.5

为正)和圆木的竖直坐标 $x$ (向下为正)为广义坐标, 均选系统的平衡位置为广义坐标的零点.

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} \times \frac {1}{3} M L ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} M \dot {x} ^ {2}
$$

$$
V = \frac {1}{2} k (x - L \theta) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \rho g A x ^ {2}
$$

注意重力已与平衡时的浮力相抵消, 因而 $\frac{1}{2}\rho gAx^{2}$ 实际上已包括了重力势能和浮力势能.
改用 $L\theta$ 和 x 为广义坐标. 惯性矩阵和刚度矩阵分别为

$$
\boldsymbol {M} = \left( \begin{array}{c c} \frac {1}{3} M & 0 \\ 0 & M \end{array} \right), \quad \boldsymbol {K} = \left( \begin{array}{c c} k & - k \\ - k & k + \rho g A \end{array} \right)
$$

$$
| \boldsymbol {H} | = | \boldsymbol {K} - \omega^ {2} \boldsymbol {M} | = \left| \begin{array}{c c} k - \frac {1}{3} M \omega^ {2} & - k \\ - k & k + \rho g A - M \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right| = 0
$$

$$
k ^ {2} + k \rho g A - \left(\frac {4}{3} k M + \frac {1}{3} M \rho g A\right) \omega^ {2} + \frac {1}{3} M ^ {2} \omega^ {4} - k ^ {2} = 0
$$

$$
M ^ {2} \omega^ {4} - (4 k M + M \rho g A) \omega^ {2} + 3 k \rho g A = 0
$$

$$
\omega_ {1} ^ {2} = \frac {1}{2 M} \left[ 4 k + \rho g A + \sqrt {(4 k + \rho g A) ^ {2} - 1 2 k \rho g A} \right]
$$

$$
\omega_ {2} ^ {2} = \frac {1}{2 M} \left[ 4 k + \rho g A - \sqrt {(4 k + \rho g A) ^ {2} - 1 2 k \rho g A} \right]
$$

将 $\omega_1^2, \omega_2^2$ 分别代入 $H \cdot u = 0$ , 解出

$$
\frac {u _ {2} ^ {(1)}}{u _ {1} ^ {(1)}} = \frac {1}{3} - \frac {1}{6 k} \left[ \rho g A + \sqrt {(4 k + \rho g A) ^ {2} - 1 2 k \rho g A} \right]
$$

$$
\frac {u _ {2} ^ {(2)}}{u _ {1} ^ {(2)}} = \frac {1}{3} - \frac {1}{6 k} \left[ \rho g A - \sqrt {(4 k + \rho g A) ^ {2} - 1 2 k \rho g A} \right]
$$

(2) $k$ 很大时

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \sqrt {(4 k + \rho g A) ^ {2} - 1 2 k \rho g A} & = \sqrt {1 6 k ^ {2} - 4 k \rho g A} \\ & = 4 k \sqrt {1 - \frac {\rho g A}{4 k}} \approx 4 k - \frac {1}{2} \rho g A \end{array}
$$

$$
\omega_ {1} ^ {2} \approx \frac {1}{2 M} \Big (4 k + \rho g A + 4 k - \frac {1}{2} \rho g A \Big) \approx \frac {4 k}{M}
$$

$$
\omega_ {1} = 2 \sqrt {\frac {k}{M}}
$$

$$
\frac {u _ {2} ^ {(1)}}{u _ {1} ^ {(1)}} = \frac {1}{3} - \frac {1}{6 k} \left(\rho g A + 4 k - \frac {1}{2} \rho g A\right) \approx - \frac {1}{3}
$$

$$
\omega_ {2} ^ {2} \approx \frac {1}{2 M} \Big (4 k + \rho g A - 4 k + \frac {1}{2} \rho g A \Big) = \frac {3 \rho g A}{4 M}
$$

$$
\omega_ {2} = \sqrt {3 \rho g A / 4 M}
$$

$$
\frac {u _ {2} ^ {(2)}}{u _ {1} ^ {(2)}} = \frac {1}{3} - \frac {1}{6 k} (\rho g A - 4 k + \frac {1}{2} \rho g A) \approx 1
$$

$\omega_{1}$ 的极限情况是弹簧力比重力、浮力的合力大得多，但 k 还不是无限大的情况，这时仅弹簧力起作用，

$$
| \boldsymbol {H} | \approx \left| \begin{array}{c c} k - \frac {1}{3} M \omega^ {2} & - k \\ - k & k - M \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right| = 0
$$

得出 $\omega_{1} = 2\sqrt{\frac{k}{M}}.$ 

$\omega_{2}$ 的极限情况可认为 $k \to \infty$ ，弹簧基本上保持自然长度，好像弹簧如同一个不可伸长的轻绳，可得 $\omega_{2} = \sqrt{3\rho gA / 4M}$ （这时 $x = L\theta$ ，只有一个自由度， $T = \frac{1}{2} \times \frac{4}{3} M \dot{x}^{2}$ ）

10.1.9 用一根劲度系数为 k 的弹簧, 把一个质量为 m 的木块拴在质量为 M 的楔上, 楔的斜面与水平面成 $\alpha$ 角, 楔又在水平面上滑动, 如图 10.6 所示, 所有的接触面都是光滑的.

（1）已知弹簧的原长为 d，求木块和楔都处于静止时的弹簧长度 $s_{0}$ ;

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/0f70c10ee54d28fb0ee1a0a2add2909b8072146815c95a844aab712d4f469c8f.jpg)



图10.6


(2) 以楔的坐标 x 和弹簧长度 s 为广义坐标, 写出系统的拉格朗日函数和运动微分方程;

(3) 求振动的角频率.

解 （1）木块处于平衡时，沿斜面的合力为零，

(2) 

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & m g \sin \alpha - k (s _ {0} - d) = 0 \\ & s _ {0} = \frac {1}{k} m g \sin \alpha + d \\ & T = \frac {1}{2} M \dot {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} + \dot {s} \cos \alpha) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {s} \sin \alpha) ^ {2} \\ & = \frac {1}{2} (M + m) \dot {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m \dot {s} ^ {2} + m \dot {x} \dot {s} \cos \alpha \\ & V = \frac {1}{2} k (s - d) ^ {2} - m g s \sin \alpha \\ & L = \frac {1}{2} (M + m) \dot {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m \dot {s} ^ {2} + m \dot {x} \dot {s} \cos \alpha - \frac {1}{2} k (s - d) ^ {2} + m g s \sin \alpha \\ & \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {q} _ {i}}\right) - \frac {\partial L}{\partial q _ {i}} = 0 \end{array}
$$

由

得运动微分方程为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & (M + m) \ddot {x} + m \ddot {s} \cos \alpha = 0 \\ & m \ddot {s} + m \ddot {x} \cos \alpha + k (s - d) - m g \sin \alpha = 0 \end{array}
$$

(3) 用前式消去后式中的 $\ddot{x}$ ，可得

$$
\frac {m (M + m \sin^ {2} \alpha)}{M + m} \ddot {s} + k (s - d) - m g \sin \alpha = 0
$$

令 $s' = s - d - \frac{mg\sin\alpha}{k}$ ，上式可改写为

$$
\frac {m (M + m \sin^ {2} \alpha)}{M + m} \ddot {s} ^ {\prime} + k s ^ {\prime} = 0
$$

振动的角频率为

$$
\omega = \sqrt {\frac {k (M + m)}{m (M + m \sin^ {2} \alpha)}}
$$

10.1.10 一个粒子在由式

$$
z = \frac {1}{2} b (x ^ {2} + y ^ {2})
$$

(其中 b 为常量, z 轴沿竖直方向)给出的轴对称容器的内壁上做无摩擦运动, 如图 10.9 所示.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/4040d0b576fd71af103287936734389162de7bed23ca73f499921dccb8cf9bdb.jpg)


(1) 粒子在高度 $z = z_{0}$ 处的圆轨道中运动, 求质量为 m 的粒子的能量和对 z 轴的角动量;

(2) 微微向下拨动在水平圆轨道上运动的粒子, 求围绕原来轨道的小振幅振动的角频率.


图10.7


解 用柱坐标,题目给出了约束关系:

$$
z = \frac {1}{2} b (x ^ {2} + y ^ {2}) = \frac {1}{2} b r ^ {2}
$$

用 $r, \varphi$ 为广义坐标，

由

$$
\begin{array}{r l} L & = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2}) - m g z \\ & = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + b ^ {2} r ^ {2} \dot {r} ^ {2}) - \frac {1}{2} m b g r ^ {2} \\ & \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {r}}\right) - \frac {\partial L}{\partial r} = 0 \\ & \text {;} \ddot {r} + b ^ {2} r \dot {r} ^ {2} - r \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + b g r = 0 \end{array}
$$

得

(1) 

在 $z = z_0$ 处做水平的圆周运动， $\dot{r} = 0,\ddot{r} = 0$ ，代入上式可得

$$
\dot {\varphi} _ {0} = \sqrt {b g}
$$

由约束关系，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {r = r _ {0} = \sqrt {\frac {2 z _ {0}}{b}}} \\ & {E = T + V = \frac {1}{2} m r _ {0} ^ {2} \dot {\varphi_ {0}} ^ {2} + m g z _ {0} = 2 m g z _ {0}} \\ & {\qquad J = m r _ {0} ^ {2} \dot {\varphi_ {0}} = 2 m z _ {0} \sqrt {\frac {g}{b}}} \\ & {\frac {\partial L}{\partial \varphi} = 0, \quad \frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\varphi}} = m r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} = \text {常量}.} \end{array}
$$

因为

所以

$$
r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} = r _ {0} ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} _ {0} = 2 z _ {0} \sqrt {\frac {g}{b}}\tag{2}
$$

用(2)式消去(1)式中的 $\dot{\varphi}$ ,

$$
(1 + b ^ {2} r ^ {2}) \ddot {r} + b ^ {2} r \dot {r} ^ {2} - \frac {4 z _ {0} ^ {2} g}{b r ^ {3}} + b g r = 0\tag{3}
$$

令 $r = r_0 + \delta r, \delta r$ 为一级小量，只保留一级小量，并注意 $\frac{4z_0^2}{r_i^4} = b^2$ ，(3)式可近似为

$$
(1 + b ^ {2} r _ {0} ^ {2}) \frac {\mathrm{d} ^ {2}}{\mathrm{d} t ^ {2}} (\delta r) + 4 b g \delta r = 0
$$

在 $r$ 方向的小振动角频率为

$$
\omega = \sqrt {\frac {4 b g}{1 + b ^ {2} r _ {0} ^ {2}}} = \sqrt {\frac {4 b g}{1 + 2 b z _ {0}}}
$$

10.1.11 一个质量为 M、半径为 R 的环被悬挂在位于环上一点的枢轴上，环可围绕枢轴在它自己的垂直平面内自由转动，一个质量为 m 的珠子在环上做无摩擦滑动。

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/99496141a30b1bf65d8dc1732ba6b03c7981689e50b55ad38920408c128b8585.jpg)


(1) 选适当的广义坐标, 写出此系统的拉格朗日函数;

(2) 对于 $m, M$ 的一般情况及 $m \gg M$ 和 $m \ll M$ 两种极限情况求小振动的简正频率.

图10.8

解 （1）用图 10.8 中的 $\theta, \varphi$ 为广义坐标，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & T = \frac {1}{2} \times 2 M R ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m [ (R \dot {\theta}) ^ {2} + (R \dot {\varphi}) ^ {2} + 2 R ^ {2} \dot {\theta} \dot {\varphi} \cos (\varphi - \theta) ] \\ & V = M g R (1 - \cos \theta) + m g R [ (1 - \cos \theta) ] + 1 (1 - \cos \varphi) ] \\ & L = \frac {1}{2} (2 M + m) R ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m R ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + m R ^ {2} \dot {\theta} \dot {\varphi} \cos (\varphi - \theta) \\ & - (M + m) g R (1 - \cos \theta) - m g R (1 - \cos \varphi) \end{array}
$$

(2) 在平衡位置 $\theta=0, \varphi=0$ 附近的小振动，T、V 可作近似，保留到二级小量，

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} (2 M + m) R ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m R ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + m R ^ {2} \dot {\theta} \dot {\varphi}
$$

$$
V = \frac {1}{2} (M + m) g R \theta^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m g R \varphi^ {2}
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {M} = \left( \begin{array}{c c} (2 M + m) R ^ {2} & m R ^ {2} \\ m R ^ {2} & m R ^ {2} \end{array} \right)
$$

$$
\boldsymbol {K} = \left( \begin{array}{c c} (M + m) g R & 0 \\ 0 & m g R \end{array} \right)
$$

$$
| \boldsymbol {H} | = \left| \begin{array}{c c} (M + m) g R - \omega^ {2} (2 M + m) R ^ {2} & - m R ^ {2} \omega^ {2} \\ - m R ^ {2} \omega^ {2} & m g R ^ {2} - m R ^ {2} \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right| = 0
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & 2 M R ^ {4} \omega^ {4} - (3 M + 2 m) g R ^ {3} \omega^ {2} + (M + m) g ^ {2} R ^ {2} = 0 \\ & \quad [ M R \omega^ {2} - (M + m) g ] (2 R \omega^ {2} - g) = 0 \end{array}
$$

$$
\omega_ {1} = \sqrt {\frac {g}{2 R}}, \quad \omega_ {2} = \sqrt {\frac {(M + m) g}{M R}}
$$

当 $m \gg M$ 时，

$$
\omega_ {1} = \sqrt {\frac {g}{2 R}}, \quad \omega_ {2} = \sqrt {\frac {m g}{M R}}
$$

当 $m \ll M$ 时，

$$
\omega_ {1} = \sqrt {\frac {g}{2 R}}, \quad \omega_ {2} = \sqrt {\frac {g}{R}}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/16d2db768d2c7e83d7585c71f40ced2d1f9ba9619b3882393f63362ffc945d0f.jpg)



图10.9


10.1.12 一个质量为 $m$ 、带电量为 $q$ 的小物体，被限制在张角为 $2\alpha$ 的圆锥面内侧做无摩擦运动，一个 $-q$ 的电荷固定在圆锥的顶点上，不计重力，图10.9中虚线是运动物体在圆锥内表面的稳定轨道，绕 $z$ 轴的角速度为 $\dot{\varphi}_0$ ，求围绕此稳定轨道小振动的角频率，假定 $v \ll c$ （光速）所以辐射是可以忽略的。

解 选取柱坐标中的 $r, \varphi$ 为广义坐标，有关系 $z = r \cot \alpha$ 

$$
\begin{array}{r l} T & = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2}) = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {r} ^ {2} \csc^ {2} \alpha + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}) \\ V & = q \left(- \frac {q}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r \csc \alpha}\right) = - \frac {q ^ {2} \sin \alpha}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r} \\ L & = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {r} ^ {2} \csc^ {2} \alpha + r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2}) + \frac {q ^ {2} \sin \alpha}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r} \end{array}
$$

由

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {q} _ {i}}\right) - \frac {\partial L}{\partial q _ {i}} = 0
$$

得

$$
m \ddot {r} \csc^ {2} \alpha - m r \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \frac {q ^ {2} \sin \alpha}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r ^ {2}} = 0\tag{1}
$$

$$
m r ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} = J (\text {常量})\tag{2}
$$

对于平衡轨道， $\ddot{r}=0, r=r_{0}, \dot{\varphi}=\dot{\varphi}_{0}$ ，由(1)式得

$$
m r _ {0} \dot {\varphi} _ {0} ^ {2} = \frac {q ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \cdot \frac {\sin \alpha}{r _ {0} ^ {2}}\tag{3}
$$

用(2)、(3)式,可把(1)式改写为

$$
m \ddot {r} + \frac {q ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r ^ {2}} \sin^ {3} \alpha \left(1 - \frac {r _ {0}}{r}\right) = 0\tag{4}
$$

考虑在平衡轨道 $r = r_0$ 附近的小振动，令 $r = r_0 + x, x \ll r_0$ 为一级小量。保留一级小量，(4)式可近似为

$$
\begin{array}{l} m \ddot {x} + \left(\frac {q ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r _ {0} ^ {3}} \sin^ {3} \alpha\right) x = 0 \\ \omega^ {2} = \frac {q ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} m r _ {0} ^ {3}} \sin^ {3} \alpha = \dot {\varphi} _ {0} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \alpha \end{array}
$$

(这里用了(3)式)

$$
\omega = \dot {\varphi} _ {0} \sin \alpha
$$

10.1.13 一个转动惯量(对其对称轴)为 I 的飞轮，在水平面内绕固定的过圆心 O 与圆平面垂直的对称轴转动，一个质量为 m 的小球可以自由地沿半径方向的一条轴滑动，小球用一根劲度系数为 k、自然长度为 l 的轻弹簧连接在轮的中心，如图 10.10 所示，求：

(1) 飞轮以恒定角速度 $\Omega_{0}$ 转动与弹簧有稳定长度 $r_{0}$ 之间的关系；

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/2aed541fcf4a8b437a0e7a6397379ab2a68af5251221700b45e8dfd09bb1b11f.jpg)


(2) 用弹簧长度 r、飞轮的转角 $\theta$ 为广义坐标, 写出系


图10.10


统的运动微分方程；

(3) 飞轮的角速度在 $\Omega_{0}$ 附近, 小球离轮心的距离在 $r_{0}$ 附近, 即系统在 (1) 的稳定运动附近做小振动的角频率.

解 (1)

$$
k \left(r _ {0} - l\right) = m r _ {0} \Omega_ {0} ^ {2}
$$

$$
r _ {0} = \frac {k l}{k - m \Omega_ {0} ^ {2}}
$$

(2) 

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} I \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {r} ^ {2} + r ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2})
$$

$$
V = \frac {1}{2} k (r - l) ^ {2}
$$

$$
L = \frac {1}{2} (I + m r ^ {2}) \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m \dot {r} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k (r - l) ^ {2}
$$

由

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {q} _ {i}}\right) - \frac {\partial L}{\partial q _ {i}} = 0
$$

得运动微分方程为

$$
\begin{array}{l} m \dot {r} - m r \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + k (r - l) = 0 \\ (I + m r ^ {2}) \dot {\theta} = (I + m r _ {0} ^ {2}) \Omega_ {0} \end{array}
$$

其中 $r_0, \Omega_0$ 是两个常量，由初始条件给出，因为不一定是稳定运动，不要求有(1)问得出的关系.

(3) 在(1)问的稳定运动附近做小振动, 此时 $r_0$ 和 $\Omega_0$ 之间有(1)给出的关系.

用运动微分方程的第二个式子消去第一个式子中的 $\dot{\theta}$ ，得

$$
m \ddot {r} - m r \left[ \left(\frac {I + m r _ {0} ^ {2}}{I + m r ^ {2}}\right) \Omega_ {0} \right] ^ {2} + k (r - l) = 0
$$

在(1)问的稳定运动附近做小振动,令 $x=r-r_{0},x$ 为一级小量,

$$
\begin{array}{l} (r _ {0} + x) \left[ \frac {I + m r _ {0} ^ {2}}{I + m (r _ {0} + x) ^ {2}} \right] ^ {2} \Omega_ {0} ^ {2} \\ \approx (r _ {0} + x) \left[ \frac {I + m r _ {0} ^ {2}}{I + m r _ {0} ^ {2} + 2 m r _ {0} x} \right] ^ {2} \Omega_ {0} ^ {2} \\ = (r _ {0} + x) \left(1 + \frac {2 m r _ {0} x}{I + m r _ {0} ^ {2}}\right) ^ {- 2} \Omega_ {0} ^ {2} \\ \approx (r _ {0} + x) \left(1 - \frac {4 m r _ {0} x}{I + m r _ {0} ^ {2}}\right) \Omega_ {0} ^ {2} \end{array}
$$

$$
m \ddot {x} - m (r _ {0} + x) \left(1 - \frac {4 m r _ {0} x}{I + m r _ {0} ^ {2}}\right) \Omega_ {0} ^ {2} + k (r _ {0} - l + x) = 0
$$

用 $mr_0\Omega_0^2 = k(r_0 - l)$ ，上式可改写为

$$
m \ddot {x} + \frac {4 m ^ {2} r _ {0} ^ {2} \Omega_ {0} ^ {2}}{I + m r _ {0} ^ {2}} x - m \Omega_ {0} ^ {2} x + k x = 0
$$

$$
m \ddot {x} + \left(k + \frac {3 m ^ {2} r _ {0} ^ {2} - m I}{I + m r _ {0} ^ {2}} \Omega_ {0} ^ {2}\right) x = 0
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/8dc72895ff634b2a7ca466a3a5e5031699db0a0a6e65b0a86a109725ac4e0a50.jpg)


$$
\omega = \sqrt {\frac {k}{m} + \frac {3 m r _ {0} ^ {2} - I}{I + m r _ {0} ^ {2}}} \Omega_ {0} ^ {2}
$$

10.1.14 一质量为 $m$ 的珠子被限制在半径为 $b$ 的光滑圆环上运动, 该圆环以固定的角速度绕同它的直径相重合的固定的竖直轴旋转.


图10.11


(1) 写出拉格朗日函数和珠子的运动微分方程；

(2) 求临界角速度 $\Omega, \omega < \Omega$ 时，圆环的底部是珠子的一个稳定平衡位置；

(3) 当 $\omega > \Omega$ 时, 求珠子的稳定平衡位置.

解 （1）用静参考系，取图 10.11 中的 $\theta$ 为广义坐标，

由

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {T = \frac {1}{2} m (b ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + b ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta \omega^ {2})} \\ & {V = m g b \cos \theta} \\ & {L = \frac {1}{2} m b ^ {2} (\dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \omega^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta) - m g b \cos \theta} \\ & {\qquad \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \bigg (\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\theta}} \bigg) - \frac {\partial L}{\partial \theta} = 0} \end{array}
$$

得运动微分方程为

$$
b \ddot {\theta} - b \omega^ {2} \sin \theta \cos \theta - g \sin \theta = 0
$$

(2) 当珠子在圆环底部附近运动时, $\theta = \pi + \theta'$ , $\theta'$ 为小量,

$$
\sin \theta = \sin (\pi + \theta^ {\prime}) \approx \sin \pi + \cos (\pi + \theta^ {\prime}) | _ {\theta^ {\prime} = 0} \theta^ {\prime} = - \theta^ {\prime}
$$

$$
\cos \theta = \cos (\pi + \theta^ {\prime}) \approx \cos \pi - \sin (\pi + \theta^ {\prime}) | _ {\theta^ {\prime} = 0} \theta^ {\prime} = - 1
$$

运动微分方程可近似为

$$
b \ddot {\theta} ^ {\prime} + (g - b \omega^ {2}) \theta^ {\prime} = 0
$$

圆环底部是珠子的一个稳定平衡位置,需在 $\theta=\pi$ 附近,也即在 $\theta'=0$ 附近的运动必须是小振动,要求 $\frac{g-b\omega^{2}}{b}>0$ ,由此可求出临界角速度 $\Omega$ ,

$$
g - b \Omega^ {2} = 0, \quad \Omega = \sqrt {\frac {g}{b}}
$$

(3) 当 $\omega > \Omega$ 时，

$$
b \ddot {\theta} - b \omega^ {2} \sin \theta \cos \theta - g \sin \theta = 0
$$

处于平衡位置时, $\ddot{\theta}=0$ ,

$$
\sin \theta (b \omega^ {2} \cos \theta + g) = 0
$$

$\sin \theta_0 = 0, \theta_0 = 0$ 为平衡位置，但如(2)中所述， $\omega > \Omega$ 时，它不是稳定平衡位置，

$$
b \omega^ {2} \cos \theta_ {0} + g = 0
$$

$$
\theta_ {0} = \operatorname{arc} \cos \left(- \frac {g}{b \omega^ {2}}\right)
$$

是平衡位置, $\theta_{0}$ 在 $\left(\frac{\pi}{2},\pi\right)$ 区间内.

为考察在此位置的平衡的稳定性，令 $\theta = \theta_0 + \theta'$ ， $\theta'$ 为小量，将 $\sin \theta, \cos \theta$ 均在 $\theta = \theta_0$ 处作泰勒展开，只保留一级小量，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sin \theta = \sin \theta_ {0} + \cos \theta_ {0} \theta^ {\prime} \\ \cos \theta = \cos \theta_ {0} - \sin \theta_ {0} \theta^ {\prime} \end{array}
$$

在 $\theta = \theta_0$ 附近运动的运动微分方程可近似为

$$
\ddot {\theta} ^ {\prime} + \omega^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta_ {0} \theta^ {\prime} = 0
$$

$\omega^2\sin^2\theta_0 > 0$ ，故 $\theta_0 = \operatorname {arc}\cos \left(-\frac{g}{b\omega^2}\right)$ 是稳定平衡位置.

10.1.15 质量为 $m$ 的粒子在竖直平面内的光滑的抛物线 $z = \frac{1}{a} x^2 (a$ 为常量， $z$ 轴竖直向上)上运动.

(1) 写出拉格朗日函数；

(2) 给出关于平衡位置做小振动的运动微分方程并求解.

解（1）用 $x$ 为广义坐标，

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {z} ^ {2}) = \frac {1}{2} m \left(1 + \frac {4 x ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}}\right) \dot {x} ^ {2}
$$

$$
V = m g z = \frac {1}{a} m g x ^ {2}
$$

$$
L = \frac {1}{2} m \left(1 + \frac {4 x ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}}\right) \dot {x} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{a} m g x ^ {2}
$$

(2) 由

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {x}}\right) - \frac {\partial L}{\partial x} = 0
$$

得运动微分方程为

$$
\left(1 + \frac {4 x ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}}\right) \ddot {x} + \frac {2 g}{a} x = 0
$$

平衡位置处， $\ddot{x} = 0$ 或 $\frac{\partial V}{\partial x} = 0$ ，可得 $x = 0$ 为平衡位置，且 $\left.\frac{\partial^2V}{\partial x^2}\right|_{x=0} = \frac{2}{a} mg > 0, x = 0$ 是稳定平衡位置。

在平衡位置附近, 因为 x、 $\ddot{x}$ 均为一级小量, 仅保留一级小量, 运动微分方程可近似为

$$
\dot {x} + \frac {2 g}{a} x = 0
$$

解出

$$
x = A \cos \left(\sqrt {\frac {2 g}{a}} t + \alpha\right)
$$

其中 A、 $\alpha$ 为积分常数，由初条件确定.

10.1.16 长 $\frac{3}{2} l$ 、质量为 $m$ 的均质棒，用一根长 $l$ 、可不计其质量的弦线悬于固定点，如图 10.12 所示。求在平面内小振动的简正频率和振动模式。

解 取图中 $\varphi, \theta$ 为广义坐标, 棒的质心坐标为

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/168742559858a195c998fcd4fdd4442086da3ed4b40a7765a74644f076ee9999.jpg)



图10.12


$$
x = l \cos \varphi + \frac {3}{4} l \cos \theta
$$

$$
y = l \sin \varphi + \frac {3}{4} l \sin \theta
$$

质心速度为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\dot {x} = - l \dot {\varphi} \sin \varphi - \frac {3}{4} l \dot {\theta} \sin \theta} \\ & {\dot {y} = l \dot {\varphi} \cos \varphi + \frac {3}{4} l \dot {\theta} \cos \theta} \\ & {T = \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + \dot {y} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{2} \cdot \frac {1}{1 2} m \left(\frac {3}{2} l\right) ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2}} \\ & {\quad = \frac {1}{2} m l ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \frac {3}{8} m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \frac {3}{4} m l ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} \dot {\theta} \cos (\theta - \varphi)} \\ & {V = - m g x = - m g l \left(\cos \varphi + \frac {3}{4} \cos \theta\right)} \\ & {L = \frac {1}{2} m l ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \frac {3}{8} m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \frac {3}{4} m l ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} \dot {\theta} \cos (\theta - \varphi) + m g \left(\cos \varphi + \frac {3}{4} \cos \theta\right)} \end{array}
$$

在平衡位置处， $\frac{\partial V}{\partial\varphi} = 0,\frac{\partial V}{\partial\theta} = 0$ ，可见 $\varphi = 0, \theta = 0$ 为平衡位置.

在平衡位置 $\theta=0,\varphi=0$ 附近做小振动，

$$
\cos \varphi \approx 1 - \frac {1}{2} \varphi^ {2}, \quad \cos \theta \approx 1 - \frac {1}{2} \theta^ {2}, \quad \cos (\theta - \varphi) \approx 1 - \frac {1}{2} (\theta - \varphi) ^ {2}
$$

$\dot{\theta}$ 、 $\dot{\varphi}$ 均为一级小量，保留到二级小量，拉格朗日函数可近似为

$$
L = \frac {1}{2} m l ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} + \frac {3}{8} m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + \frac {3}{4} m l ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} \dot {\theta} - \frac {1}{2} m g l \varphi^ {2} - \frac {3}{8} m g l \theta^ {2} + \frac {7}{4} m g l
$$

由

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {q} _ {i}}\right) - \frac {\partial L}{\partial q _ {i}} = 0
$$

得

$$
l \ddot {\varphi} + \frac {3}{4} l \ddot {\theta} + g \varphi = 0
$$

$$
l \ddot {\varphi} + l \ddot {\theta} + g \theta = 0
$$

特征方程

$$
\left| \begin{array}{c c} g - l \omega^ {2} & - \frac {3}{4} l \omega^ {2} \\ - l \omega^ {2} & g - l \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right| = 0
$$

解出简正频率为

$$
\omega_ {1} = (\sqrt {3} + 1) \sqrt {\frac {q}{l}}
$$

$$
\omega_ {2} = (\sqrt {3} - 1) \sqrt {\frac {q}{l}}
$$

将 $\omega_{1},\omega_{2}$ 分别代入

$$
\left( \begin{array}{c c} g - l \omega^ {2} & - \frac {3}{4} l \omega^ {2} \\ - l \omega^ {2} & g - l \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right) \binom {u _ {1}} {u _ {2}} = 0
$$

得

$$
\omega_ {1}: u _ {1} ^ {(1)} = - \frac {\sqrt {3}}{2}, \quad u _ {2} ^ {(1)} = 1
$$

$$
\omega_ {2}: u _ {1} ^ {(2)} = \frac {\sqrt {3}}{2}, \quad u _ {2} ^ {(2)} = 1
$$

上述振动模式可如图 10.13 所示.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/6a2f9f3800e89b09e90a5cdd3d395878f04150b4a3d42ac4ddd024483fae6bd6.jpg)



图10.13


10.1.17 飞球节速器由连在长 l 的臂上的两个质量 m 和质量 M 组成, 如图 10.14 所示. 该组合件绕竖直轴转动, 质量 M 可沿该轴无摩擦上下滑动, 忽略各臂质量、空气阻力, 并假定 M 的直径很小. 假定轴以恒定角速度 $\omega_{0}$ 转动.

(1) 计算质量 M 的平衡高度；

(2) 求质量 M 在平衡位置附近小振动的角频率；

(3) 现假定轴被允许自由转动, 小振动的角频率是否改变? 若变, 计算新的值.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/2e001ba01a237b7dc5ee2a37a32ee747bcf46857c36e3be8bb3f1aeccd0a2dfb.jpg)


解 图 10.14 中 x 轴总在四臂所张的平面内, x 轴随组合件一起绕 z 轴转动, 用一臂与轴的夹角 $\theta$ 为广义坐标, 用 $x_{m}$ 、 $z_{m}$ 表示质量 m 的坐标, 用 $z_{M}$ 表示质量 M 的坐标,

图10.14

(1) 

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & x _ {m} = l \sin \theta , \quad z _ {m} = - l \cos \theta \\ & z _ {M} = - 2 l \cos \theta \\ & T = 2 \times \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} _ {m} ^ {2} + \dot {z} _ {m} ^ {2} + x _ {m} ^ {2} \omega_ {0} ^ {2}) + \frac {1}{2} M \dot {z} _ {M} ^ {2} \\ & = m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + m l ^ {2} \omega_ {0} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta + 2 M l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta \\ & V = 2 m g z _ {m} + M g z _ {M} = (- 2 m g l - 2 M g l) \cos \theta \\ & L = (m l ^ {2} + 2 M l ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta) \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + m l ^ {2} \omega_ {0} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta + 2 (m + M) g l \cos \theta \\ & \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\theta}}\right) - \frac {\partial L}{\partial \theta} = 0 \end{array}
$$

由

得

$$
(m + 2 M \sin^ {2} \theta) l \ddot {\theta} + 2 M l \sin \theta \cos \theta \dot {\theta} ^ {2} - m l \omega_ {0} ^ {2} \sin \theta \cos \theta + (m + M) g \sin \theta = 0
$$

质量 M 处于平衡位置时, $\dot{\theta}=0, \ddot{\theta}=0$ ,

$$
m l \omega_ {0} ^ {2} \sin \theta_ {0} \cos \theta_ {0} = (m + M) g \sin \theta_ {0}
$$

$$
\sin \theta_ {0} = 0, \text {或} \cos \theta_ {0} = \frac {(m + M) g}{m l \omega_ {0} ^ {2}}
$$

质量 $M$ 的平衡高度分别为

$$
z _ {M} = - 2 l, \text {或} - \frac {2 (m + M) g}{m \omega_ {0} ^ {2}}
$$

注意:这里求质量 M 的平衡位置不能用这里的 V, 由 $\frac{\partial V}{\partial \theta}=0$ 得出. 因为这个 V 是用静参考系写出的, 系统并不处于平衡, 如改用绕轴以 $\omega_{0}$ 的角速度转动的参考系, M 的平衡位置也是系统的平衡位置, 可用 $\frac{\partial V}{\partial \theta}=0$ , 不过这个 V 应改为

$$
V = - 2 (m + M) g l \cos \theta - m l ^ {2} \omega_ {0} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta
$$

后面新加的项是惯性离轴力势能.

(2) 在 $\sin\theta_{0}=0$ ，即 $\theta_{0}=0$ 这个平衡位置附近，运动微分方程可近似为

$$
m l \ddot {\theta} + [ (m + M) g - m l \omega_ {0} ^ {2} ] \theta = 0
$$

如 $(m+M)g-ml\omega_{0}^{2}>0$ ，则 $\theta=0$ 是一个稳定平衡位置。在这个位置给一微扰，质量M不会远离平衡位置，但 $\theta$ 只能向正值方向运动，不能向负值方向运动。从 $\theta>0$ 回到 $\theta=0$ 后再怎样运动，题目未给出足够的条件无法讨论。因此我们讨论 $(m+M)g-ml\omega_{0}^{2}<0$ 的情况， $\theta=0$ 是一个不稳定平衡位置。存在着另一个平衡位置，那里 $\cos\theta_{0}=\frac{(m+M)g}{ml\omega_{0}^{2}}(<1)$ ，它是个稳定平衡位置。下面讨论在这个平衡位置附近的小振动。令 $\theta'=\theta-\theta_{0}$ ，用

$$
\begin{array}{l} \sin \theta \approx \sin \theta_ {0} + \cos \theta_ {0} \theta^ {\prime} \\ \cos \theta \approx \cos \theta_ {0} - \sin \theta_ {0} \theta^ {\prime} \end{array}
$$

代入运动微分方程,只保留一级小量,得

$$
(m + 2 M \sin^ {2} \theta_ {0}) l \ddot {\theta} ^ {\prime} + m l \omega_ {0} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta_ {0} \theta^ {\prime} = 0
$$

质量 M 在平衡位置附近小振动的角频率为

其中

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \Omega & = \sqrt {\frac {m \omega_ {0} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta_ {0}}{m + 2 M \sin^ {2} \theta_ {0}}} \\ \sin^ {2} \theta_ {0} & = 1 - \left[ \frac {(m + M) g}{m l \omega_ {0} ^ {2}} \right] ^ {2} \end{array}
$$

(3) 转轴并不受约束, 可以自由转动, 用 $\dot{\varphi}$ 表示转动的角速度, 系统的自由度增为两个,

$$
L = (m l ^ {2} + 2 M l ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta) \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + m l ^ {2} \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \theta + 2 (m + M) g l \cos \theta
$$
