因为 $\frac{\partial L}{\partial \varphi} = 0, \frac{\partial L}{\partial \dot{\varphi}} =$ 常量，可得

$$
\dot {\varphi} \sin^ {2} \theta = \omega_ {0} \sin^ {2} \theta_ {0}\tag{1}
$$

由

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\theta}}\right) - \frac {\partial L}{\partial \theta} = 0
$$

得

$$
(m + 2 M \sin^ {2} \theta) l \ddot {\theta} + 2 M l \sin \theta \cos \theta \dot {\theta} ^ {2} - m l \dot {\varphi} ^ {2} \sin \theta \cos \theta + (m + M) g \sin \theta = 0\tag{2}
$$

用(1)式消去(2)式中的 $\dot{\varphi}$ ，得

$$
(m + 2 M \sin^ {2} \theta) l \ddot {\theta} + 2 M l \sin \theta \cos \theta \dot {\theta} ^ {2} - \frac {m l \omega_ {0} ^ {2} \sin^ {4} \theta_ {0} \cos \theta}{\sin^ {3} \theta} + (m + M) g \sin \theta = 0\tag{3}
$$

在质量 $M$ 的平衡位置， $\theta_0 = \arccos \left[\frac{(m + M)g}{ml\omega_0^2}\right]$ ，对 $\cos \theta, \sin \theta$ 作泰勒展开，代入(3)式，只保留一级小量，得

$$
(m + 2 M \sin^ {2} \theta_ {0}) l \ddot {\theta} ^ {\prime} + (m + M) g \frac {1 + 3 \cos^ {2} \theta_ {0}}{\cos \theta_ {0}} \theta^ {\prime} = 0
$$

用 $\cos \theta_0 = \frac{(m + M)g}{ml\omega_0^2}$ ，上式也可改写为

$$
(m + 2 M \sin^ {2} \theta_ {0}) \ddot {\theta} ^ {\prime} + m \omega_ {0} ^ {2} (1 + 3 \cos^ {2} \theta_ {0}) \theta^ {\prime} = 0
$$

小振动的角频率为

$$
\Omega = \omega_ {0} \sqrt {\frac {m (1 + 3 \cos^ {2} \theta_ {0})}{m + 2 M \sin^ {2} \theta_ {0}}}
$$

可见,有保持角速度为 $\omega_{0}$ 的约束还是在角速度为 $\omega_{0}$ 时不受约束,质量 M 在其平衡位置的小振动的角频率是不同的.

10.1.18 两个长度均为 $l$ 、质量均为 $m$ 的单摆悬在同一水平线上，用一根劲度系数为 $k$ 的无质量的弹簧将两质点连起来，弹簧的自然长度等于两悬点间的距离。求系统在平衡位置附近做小振动的简正频率和简正坐标。若开始时两质点处于平衡位置，左边的质点由于受到一个冲量获得一个向右的水平速度 $v_0$ ，用简正坐标表达系统的运动规律。

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/9e21af8012f6556b673df2cc8c235edd967f389d0374aac2dbe135be407f13a1.jpg)


解 取图 10.15 的 $\theta_{1}, \theta_{2}$ 为广义坐标. 设两悬点间距为 a,


图10.15


$$
\begin{array}{r l} & T = \frac {1}{2} m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {2} ^ {2} \\ & V = \frac {1}{2} k \left\{\left[ a + l \sin \theta_ {2} - l \sin \theta_ {1}\right) ^ {2} + (- l \cos \theta_ {1} + l \cos \theta_ {2}) ^ {2} \right] ^ {\frac {1}{2}} - a \Bigg \} ^ {2} \\ & \quad + m g [ l (1 - \cos \theta_ {1}) + l (1 - \cos \theta_ {2}) ] \\ & \approx \frac {1}{2} k l ^ {2} (\theta_ {2} - \theta_ {1}) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m g l (\theta_ {1} ^ {2} + \theta_ {2} ^ {2}) \\ & = \frac {1}{2} (k l ^ {2} + m g l) \theta_ {1} ^ {2} - k l ^ {2} \theta_ {1} \theta_ {2} + \frac {1}{2} (k l ^ {2} + m g l) \theta_ {2} ^ {2} \\ & M = \left( \begin{array}{c c} m l ^ {2} & 0 \\ 0 & m l ^ {2} \end{array} \right), K = \left( \begin{array}{c c} k l ^ {2} + m g l & - k l ^ {2} \\ - k l ^ {2} & k l ^ {2} + m g l \end{array} \right) \\ | H | = | K - \omega^ {2} M | = & \left| \begin{array}{c c} k l ^ {2} + m g l - m l ^ {2} \omega^ {2} & - k l ^ {2} \\ - k l ^ {2} & k l ^ {2} + m g l - m l ^ {2} \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right| = 0 \\ & \omega_ {1} = \sqrt {\frac {g}{l}}, \qquad \omega_ {2} = \sqrt {\frac {g}{l} + \frac {2 k}{m}} \end{array}
$$

可解出

将 $\omega_{1}$ 代入 $H \cdot u = 0$ ,

$$
\left( \begin{array}{c c} k l & - k l \\ - k l & k l \end{array} \right) \binom {u _ {1} ^ {(1)}} {u _ {2} ^ {(1)}} = 0
$$

取 $u_{1}^{(1)} = 1$ ，则 $u_{2}^{(1)} = 1$ 

将 $\omega_{2}$ 代入 $H \cdot u = 0$ ,

$$
\left( \begin{array}{l l} - k l & - k l \\ - k l & - k l \end{array} \right) \binom {u _ {1} ^ {(2)}} {u _ {2} ^ {(2)}} = 0
$$

取 $u_{1}^{(2)} = 1$ ，则 $u_{2}^{(2)} = -1$ 

两个特解为

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} \theta_ {1} ^ {(1)} = \cos \left(\sqrt {\frac {g}{l}} t + \alpha_ {1}\right) \\ \theta_ {2} ^ {(1)} = \cos \left(\sqrt {\frac {g}{l}} t + \alpha_ {1}\right) \\ \left\{ \begin{array}{l} \theta_ {1} ^ {(2)} = \cos \left(\sqrt {\frac {g}{l}} + \frac {2 k}{m} t + \alpha_ {2}\right) \\ \theta_ {2} ^ {(2)} = - \cos \left(\sqrt {\frac {g}{l}} + \frac {2 k}{m} t + \alpha_ {2}\right) \end{array} \right. \end{array} \right.
$$

通解为

$$
\begin{array}{l} \theta_ {1} = C _ {1} \cos \left(\sqrt {\frac {g}{l}} t + \alpha_ {1}\right) + C _ {2} \cos \left(\sqrt {\frac {g}{l} + \frac {2 k}{m}} t + \alpha_ {2}\right) \\ \theta_ {2} = C _ {1} \cos \left(\sqrt {\frac {g}{l}} t + \alpha_ {1}\right) - C _ {2} \cos \left(\sqrt {\frac {g}{l} + \frac {2 k}{m}} t + \alpha_ {2}\right) \end{array}
$$

初始条件: t=0 时, $\theta_{1}=0$ , $\theta_{2}=0$ , $\dot{\theta}_{1}=\frac{v_{0}}{l}$ , $\dot{\theta}_{2}=0$ , 定出

$$
\begin{array}{r l} \alpha_ {1} & = \alpha_ {2} = \frac {\pi}{2}, \quad C _ {1} = - \frac {v _ {0}}{2 l} \sqrt {\frac {l}{g}} \text {或} - \frac {v _ {0}}{2 \omega_ {1} l} \\ & \mathcal {C} _ {2} = - \frac {v _ {0}}{2 l} \frac {1}{\sqrt {\frac {g}{l} + \frac {2 k}{m}}} \text {或} - \frac {v _ {0}}{2 \omega_ {2} l} \\ & \theta_ {1} = \frac {v _ {0}}{2 \omega_ {1} l} \sin \omega_ {1} t + \frac {v _ {0}}{2 \omega_ {2} l} \sin \omega_ {2} t \\ & \theta_ {2} = \frac {v _ {0}}{2 \omega_ {1} l} \sin \omega_ {1} t - \frac {v _ {0}}{2 \omega_ {1} l} \sin \omega_ {2} t \end{array}
$$

将 $u_{1}^{(1)}, u_{2}^{(1)}, u_{1}^{(2)}, u_{2}^{(2)}$ 代入简正坐标 $\xi_{1}, \xi_{2}$ 与原用坐标 $\theta_{1}, \theta_{2}$ 的关系式，

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\binom {\xi_ {1}} {\xi_ {2}} = \left( \begin{array}{c c} u _ {1} ^ {(1)} & u _ {2} ^ {(1)} \\ u _ {1} ^ {(2)} & u _ {2} ^ {(2)} \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{c c} m _ {1 1} & m _ {1 2} \\ m _ {2 1} & m _ {2 2} \end{array} \right) \binom {\theta_ {1}} {\theta_ {2}}} \\ & {\qquad = \left( \begin{array}{c c} 1 & 1 \\ 1 & - 1 \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{c c} m l ^ {2} & 0 \\ 0 & m l ^ {2} \end{array} \right) \binom {\theta_ {1}} {\theta_ {2}} = m l ^ {2} \binom {\theta_ {1} + \theta_ {2}} {\theta_ {1} - \theta_ {2}}} \\ & {\qquad \xi_ {1} = m l ^ {2} (\theta_ {1} + \theta_ {2}), \quad \xi_ {2} = m l ^ {2} (\theta_ {1} - \theta_ {2})} \end{array}
$$

简正坐标的常数倍仍是简正坐标,因此可用

$$
\boldsymbol {\xi} _ {1} = \theta_ {1} + \theta_ {2}, \quad \boldsymbol {\xi} _ {2} = \theta_ {1} - \theta_ {2}
$$

用此简正坐标表达的满足初始条件的解为

$$
\xi_ {1} = \frac {v _ {0}}{\omega_ {1} l} \sin \omega_ {1} t, \quad \xi_ {2} = \frac {v _ {0}}{\omega_ {2} l} \sin \omega_ {2} t
$$

10.1.19 两个质量分别为 $M, m$ , 且 $M > m$ 的质点被两根长 $l$ 不可伸长的轻绳挂在支架上, 用一根劲度系数为 $k$ , 原长等于两支点间距的轻弹簧将两质点相连, 如图 10.16 所示. 求沿两质点连线在平衡位置附近做小振动的简正频率, 给出在每个模式中 $m$ 和 $M$ 的运动之间的关系, 写出通解. 若 $t = 0$ 时, $m$ 静止在它的平衡位置, $M$ 从一个初始正位移处静止释放. 系统的总能量为 $E_0$ (取平衡位置为

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/b6431f99940ac76051c7ae2ece9f2fc06b70ce2c0277dd60e8f4ba60d49b66c6.jpg)



图10.16


势能的零点）、弹簧很弱，且 $M = 2m$ ，求以后运动期间被 $m$ 获得的最大能量.说明在哪里用了弹簧很弱的条件.

解

由

得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {L = \frac {1}{2} m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} M l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {2} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k l ^ {2} (\theta_ {2} - \theta_ {1}) ^ {2} - m g l (1 - \cos \theta_ {1}) - M g l (1 - \cos \theta_ {2})} \\ & {\quad \approx \frac {1}{2} m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} M l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {2} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} (k l ^ {2} + m g l) \theta_ {1} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} (k l ^ {2} + m g l) \theta_ {2} ^ {2} + k l ^ {2} \theta_ {1} \theta_ {2}} \\ & {\qquad \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {\theta} _ {i}}\right) - \frac {\partial L}{\partial \theta_ {i}} = 0} \\ & {\qquad m l \ddot {\theta} _ {1} + (k l + m g) \theta_ {1} - k l \theta_ {2} = 0} \\ & {\qquad M l \ddot {\theta} _ {2} + (k l + M g) \theta_ {2} - k l \theta_ {1} = 0} \end{array}
$$

简正频率 $\omega$ 满足的方程为

解出：

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\left| \begin{array}{c c} k l + m g - m l \omega^ {2} & - k l \\ - k l & k l + M g - M l \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right| = 0} \\ & {\omega_ {1} = \sqrt {\frac {g}{l}}} \\ & {\omega_ {1} = \sqrt {\frac {m M g + k l (m + M)}{m M l}}} \\ & {(k l + m g - m l \omega^ {2}) u _ {1} - k l u _ {2} = 0} \end{array}
$$

分别代入

令 $u_{1}^{(1)} = 1, u_{1}^{(2)} = 1$ ，可得 $u_{2}^{(1)} = 1, u_{2}^{(2)} = -\frac{m}{M}$ .

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/1c2d9d0c23d0023d211fa3590524e52070adbf20a251dd620fd8f5399a6212f3.jpg)



图10.17


振动模式可用图 10.17 表示; 第一种振动模式, 简正频率为 $\omega_{1}, m$ 和 M 同相位且振幅相同; 第二种振动模式, 简正频率为 $\omega_{2}, m$ 和 M 的振动相位相反, 振幅也不同, m 的振幅与 M 的振幅之比为

$$
1: \frac {m}{M}.
$$

通解为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\theta_ {1} = C _ {1} u _ {1} ^ {(1)} \cos (\omega_ {1} t + \alpha_ {1}) + C _ {2} u _ {1} ^ {(2)} \cos (\omega_ {2} t + \alpha_ {2})} \\ & {\qquad = C _ {1} \cos \left(\sqrt {\frac {g}{l}} t + \alpha_ {1}\right) + C _ {2} \cos \left(\sqrt {\frac {m M g + k l (m + M)}{m M l}} t + \alpha_ {2}\right)} \\ & {\theta_ {2} = C _ {1} u _ {2} ^ {(1)} \cos (\omega_ {1} t + \alpha_ {1}) + C _ {2} u _ {2} ^ {(2)} \cos (\omega_ {2} t + \alpha_ {2})} \\ & {\qquad = C _ {1} \cos \left(\sqrt {\frac {g}{l}} t + \alpha_ {1}\right) - \frac {m}{M} C _ {2} \cos \left(\sqrt {\frac {m M g + k l (m + M)}{m M l}} t + \alpha_ {2}\right)} \end{array}
$$

对于下列初始条件：

$$
t = 0 \text {时}, \theta_ {1} = 0, \quad \dot {\theta} _ {1} = 0, \quad \theta_ {2} = \theta_ {2 0}, \quad \dot {\theta} _ {2} = 0
$$

其中 $\theta_{20}$ 满足下列方程, 取正根:

$$
\frac {1}{2} k l ^ {2} \theta_ {2 0} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} M g l \theta_ {2 0} ^ {2} = E _ {0}
$$

所以

$$
\theta_ {2 0} = \sqrt {\frac {2 E _ {0}}{k l ^ {2} + M g l}}
$$

确定 $C_1, C_2, \alpha_1, \alpha_2$ 的方程为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & C _ {1} \cos \alpha_ {1} + C _ {2} \cos \alpha_ {2} = 0 \\ & - C _ {1} \omega_ {1} \sin \alpha_ {1} - C _ {2} \omega_ {2} \sin \alpha_ {2} = 0 \\ & C _ {1} \cos \alpha_ {1} - \frac {m}{M} C _ {2} \cos \alpha_ {2} = \theta_ {2 0} \\ & - C _ {1} \omega_ {1} \sin \alpha_ {1} + \frac {m}{M} C _ {2} \omega_ {2} \sin \alpha_ {2} = 0 \end{array}
$$

解出

$$
\alpha_ {1} = 0, \quad \alpha_ {2} = 0
$$

$$
C _ {1} = \frac {M}{m + M} \theta_ {2 0}, \quad C _ {2} = - \frac {M}{m + M} \theta_ {2 0}
$$

用 $M = 2m$ 这个条件：

$$
C _ {1} = \frac {2}{3} \theta_ {2 0}, \quad C _ {2} = - \frac {2}{3} \theta_ {2 0},
$$

$$
\theta_ {1} = \frac {2}{3} \theta_ {2 0} (\cos \omega_ {1} t - \cos \omega_ {2} t)
$$

$$
\theta_ {2} = \frac {2}{3} \theta_ {2 0} \left(\cos \omega_ {1} t + \frac {1}{2} \cos \omega_ {2} t\right)
$$

其中

$$
\omega_ {1} = \sqrt {\frac {g}{l}}, \quad \omega_ {2} = \sqrt {\frac {2 m g + 3 k l}{2 m l}}, \quad \theta_ {2 0} = \sqrt {\frac {2 E _ {0}}{k l ^ {2} + 2 m g l}}
$$

质点 $m$ 的能量为

$$
E _ {1} = \frac {1}{2} m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m g l \theta_ {1} ^ {2}
$$

弹簧很弱意味着 $kl \ll mg$ ，

$$
\begin{array}{l} \omega_ {2} = \sqrt {\frac {2 m g + 3 k l}{2 m l}} = \sqrt {\frac {g}{l} \left(1 + \frac {3 k l}{2 m g}\right)} \approx \sqrt {\frac {g}{l}} \left(1 + \frac {3 k l}{4 m g}\right) = \omega_ {1} (1 + \delta) \\ \theta_ {2 0} = \sqrt {\frac {2 E _ {0}}{2 m g l + k l ^ {2}}} = \sqrt {\frac {2 E _ {0}}{2 m g l}} \left(1 + \frac {k l}{2 m g}\right) ^ {- \frac {1}{2}} \approx \sqrt {\frac {E _ {0}}{m g l}} \left(1 - \frac {k l}{4 m g}\right) = \sqrt {\frac {E _ {0}}{m g l}} \left(1 - \frac {1}{3} \delta\right) \end{array}
$$

其中 $\delta = \frac{3kl}{4mg} \ll 1$ .

用弱弹簧近似后,经计算可得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} {E _ {1} = \frac {2}{9} m g l \theta_ {2 0} ^ {2} [ 1 + (1 + \delta) ^ {2} \sin^ {2} \omega_ {2} t + \cos^ {2} \omega_ {2} t ]} \\ & {- 2 (1 + \delta) \sin \omega_ {1} t + \sin \omega_ {2} t - 2 \cos \omega_ {1} t \cos \omega_ {2} t ]} \\ & {\approx \frac {4}{9} E _ {0} \left\{1 - \cos [ (\omega_ {1} - \omega_ {2}) t ] \right\} \qquad (\text {略去一切小量})} \end{array}
$$

显然质点 $m$ 的最大能量为

$$
E _ {1 \max} = \frac {4}{9} E _ {0} [ 1 - (- 1) ] = \frac {8}{9} E _ {0}
$$

10.1.20 质量均为 m、荷电均为 e 的两金属小球，用同样长度为 l 的、不可伸长的轻绝缘绳系在同一水平线上，两悬点相距为 a。让两球在其平衡位置附近做小振动，求简正频率（设电场力比重力小得多）。

解

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & T = \frac {1}{2} m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m l \dot {\theta} _ {2} ^ {2} \\ & V = m g l (1 - \cos \theta_ {1}) + m g l (1 - \cos \theta_ {2}) + \frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r ^ {\prime}} - \frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} a} \end{array}
$$

其中 $r' = \{(a + l\sin \theta_2 - l\sin \theta_1)^2 + [l(1 - \cos \theta_1) - l(1 - \cos \theta_2)]^2\}^{1/2}$ 

$$
\approx a \left[ 1 + \frac {l}{a} (\theta_ {2} - \theta_ {1}) + \frac {\lambda^ {2}}{a ^ {2}} (\theta_ {2} - \theta_ {1}) ^ {2} \right]
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\mathcal {L} ^ {- 1} = \frac {1}{a} \Big [ 1 - \frac {l}{a} (\theta_ {2} - \theta_ {1}) + \frac {2 l ^ {2}}{a ^ {2}} (\theta_ {2} - \theta_ {1}) ^ {2} \Big ]} \\ & {\quad V \approx \frac {1}{2} m g l \theta_ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m g l \theta_ {2} ^ {2} - \frac {e ^ {2} l}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} a ^ {2}} (\theta_ {2} - \theta_ {1}) + \frac {e ^ {2} l ^ {2}}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} a ^ {3}} (\theta_ {2} - \theta_ {1}) ^ {2}} \\ & {\qquad = \Big (\frac {1}{2} m g l + \frac {e ^ {2} l ^ {2}}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} a ^ {3}} \Big) (\theta_ {1} ^ {2} + \theta_ {2} ^ {2}) - \frac {e ^ {2} l ^ {2}}{\pi \varepsilon_ {0} a ^ {3}} \theta_ {1} \theta_ {2} - \frac {e ^ {2} l}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} a ^ {2}} (\theta_ {2} - \theta_ {1})} \\ & {\quad L = \frac {1}{2} m l ^ {2}   \dot {\theta} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m l ^ {2}   \dot {\theta} _ {2} ^ {2} - \Big (\frac {1}{2} m g l + \frac {e ^ {2} l ^ {2}}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} a ^ {3}} \Big) (\theta_ {1} ^ {2} + \theta_ {2} ^ {2}) + \frac {e ^ {2} l ^ {2}}{\pi \varepsilon_ {0} a ^ {3}} \theta_ {1} \theta_ {2} + \frac {e ^ {2} l}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} a ^ {2}} (\theta_ {2} - \theta_ {1})} \\ & {\textbf {\text {由}}} \\ & {\qquad \frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \Big (\frac {\partial L}{\partial   \dot {\theta} _ {i}} \Big) - \frac {\partial L}{\partial \theta_ {i}} = 0} \end{array}
$$

得

$$
\dot {\theta} _ {1} + \left(\frac {g}{L} + \frac {e ^ {2}}{\pi \varepsilon_ {0} m a ^ {3}}\right) \theta_ {1} - \frac {e ^ {2}}{\pi \varepsilon_ {0} m a ^ {3}} \theta_ {2} + \frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} m l a ^ {2}} = 0
$$

$$
\dot {\theta} _ {2} + \left(\frac {g}{l} + \frac {e ^ {2}}{\pi \varepsilon_ {0} m a ^ {3}}\right) \theta_ {2} - \frac {e ^ {2}}{\pi \varepsilon_ {0} m a ^ {3}} \theta_ {1} - \frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} m l a ^ {2}} = 0
$$

在势能函数中出现 $\theta_{1},\theta_{2}$ 的一次方项，是二阶微分方程中出现非齐次项的原因，而这种情况的产生是因为 $\theta_{1} = 0,\theta_{2} = 0$ 不是平衡位置，平衡位置 $\theta_{10},\theta_{20}$ 是这个非齐次方程的一个特定的特解，它们满足下列代数方程：

$$
\left(\frac {g}{l} + \frac {e ^ {2}}{\pi \varepsilon_ {0} m a ^ {3}}\right) \theta_ {1 0} - \frac {e ^ {2}}{\pi \varepsilon_ {0} m a ^ {3}} \theta_ {2 0} + \frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} m l a ^ {2}} = 0
$$

$$
\left(\frac {g}{l} + \frac {e ^ {2}}{\pi \varepsilon_ {0} m a ^ {3}}\right) \theta_ {2 0} - \frac {e ^ {2}}{\pi \varepsilon_ {0} m a ^ {3}} \theta_ {1 0} - \frac {e ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} m l a ^ {2}} = 0
$$

由对称性可知， $\theta_{20} = -\theta_{10}$ ，可解出

$$
\theta_ {2 0} = - \theta_ {1 0} = \frac {e ^ {2} a}{4 (\pi \varepsilon_ {0} m a ^ {3} g + 2 e ^ {2} l)}
$$

如取 $\theta_1' = \theta_1 - \theta_{10}, \theta_2' = \theta_2 - \theta_{20}$ ，二阶微分方程将是齐次的。不论用 $\theta_1, \theta_2$ 还是用 $\theta_1', \theta_2'$ 作广义坐标，不仅简正频率相同，而且简正频率满足的特征方程也是相同的，特征方程为

$$
\left| \begin{array}{c c} \frac {g}{l} + A - \omega^ {2} & - A \\ - A & \frac {g}{l} + A - \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right| = 0
$$

其中

$$
A = \frac {e ^ {2}}{\pi \varepsilon_ {0} m a ^ {3}}
$$

解出

$$
\omega_ {1} = \sqrt {\frac {g}{l}}, \quad \omega_ {2} = \sqrt {\frac {g}{l} + 2 A} = \sqrt {\frac {g}{l} + \frac {2 e ^ {2}}{\pi \varepsilon_ {0} m a ^ {3}}}
$$

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/83a6e983b2d68bbd2e090d1e165b5e878799f50df120e771a5a1fb8ec8f1bf08.jpg)



图10.18


10.1.21 质量为 M 的物体被限制在水平的 x 轴上无摩擦地滑动, 质量为 m 的质点通过一根无质量的、长度为 b 的、不可伸长的线和 M 联结, m 限在竖直的 xy 平面内运动, 如图 10.18 所示.

(1) 写出系统的拉格朗日函数；

(2) 就小振动情形. 求简正坐标, 并加以说明;

(3) 求用简正坐标表达的系统的运动学方程.

解（1）用 $x, \theta$ 为广义坐标，

$$
T = \frac {1}{2} M \dot {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m [ (\dot {x} + b \dot {\theta} \cos \theta) ^ {2} + (b \dot {\theta} \sin \theta) ^ {2} ]
$$

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & = \frac {1}{2} (M + m) \dot {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m b ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + m b \dot {x} \dot {\theta} \cos \theta \\ & V = m g b (1 - \cos \theta) \\ & L = \frac {1}{2} (M + m) \dot {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m b ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + m b \dot {x} \dot {\theta} \cos \theta - m g b (1 - \cos \theta) \end{array}
$$

(2) 对于小振动情形，

$$
L = \frac {1}{2} (M + m) \dot {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m b ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + m b \dot {x} \dot {\theta} - \frac {1}{2} m g b \theta^ {2}
$$

由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L}{\partial\dot{q}_i}\right) - \frac{\partial L}{\partial q_i} = 0$ 得运动微分方程为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} (M + m) \dot {x} + m b \dot {\theta} & = C (\text {常量}) \\ \ddot {x} + b \ddot {\theta} + g \theta & = 0 \end{array}\tag{1}
$$

(2) 

若令 $\eta = x + \frac{mb}{m + M}\theta$ ，则(1)式可改写为

$$
(m + M) \dot {\eta} = C \quad \text {或} \quad \ddot {\eta} = 0
$$

$\eta$ 是一个简正坐标, 它的物理意义是系统的质心的 $x$ 坐标, 质心在 $x$ 方向做速度为 $\frac{C}{m + M}$ 的匀速运动.

由 $\ddot{\eta} = 0, \ddot{x} = -\frac{mb}{m + M}\ddot{\theta}$ ，将它代入(2)式，(2)式变成

$$
\frac {M b}{m + M} \ddot {\theta} + g \theta = 0
$$

可见, $\theta$ 就是另一个简正坐标.

$\theta$ 表示的运动是简谐振动, 简正频率为

$$
\omega = \sqrt {\frac {(m + M) g}{M b}}
$$

(3) $\dot{\eta} = \frac{C}{m + M}, \eta = \frac{C}{m + M} t + D,$ 

$$
\theta = A \cos \left(\sqrt {\frac {(m + M) g}{M b}} t + \alpha\right)
$$

C、D、A 和 $\alpha$ 均为积分常数，由初始条件确定.

10.1.22 由质量为 m 的质点和长为 l 的轻绳组成的单摆, 悬挂在质量为 M 的物体上, 该物体置在光滑的水平面上, 并用一根劲度系数为 k 的水平轻弹簧连到一固定点, 如图 10.19 所示.

(1) 写出系统的拉格朗日函数；

(2) 求系统做小振动的角频率.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/ed7bd1911190a61ba3e86c292d8cd8228d0b63cf25cec03123986d21aab5d366.jpg)


解（1） $x$ 轴的原点取在弹簧为原长时物体所在位置，


图10.19


$$
\begin{array}{r l} & T = \frac {1}{2} M \dot {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m (\dot {x} ^ {2} + l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + 2 l \dot {x} \dot {\theta} \cos \theta) \\ & V = \frac {1}{2} k x ^ {2} + m g l (1 - \cos \theta) \\ & L = \frac {1}{2} (M + m) \dot {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + m l \dot {x} \dot {\theta} \cos \theta - \frac {1}{2} k x ^ {2} - m g l (1 - \cos \theta) \end{array}
$$

(2) 做小振动, $\theta$ 、x、 $\dot{\theta}$ 和 $\dot{x}$ 均为小量,

$$
L \approx \frac {1}{2} (M + m) \dot {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} + m l \dot {x} \dot {\theta} - \frac {1}{2} k x ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} m g l \theta^ {2}
$$

由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L}{\partial\dot{q}_i}\right) - \frac{\partial L}{\partial q_i} = 0$ 得

$$
(M + m) \ddot {x} + m l \ddot {\theta} + k x = 0
$$

$$
m l \ddot {\theta} + m \ddot {x} + m g \theta = 0
$$

设

$$
x = u _ {1} \cos (\omega t + \alpha), l \theta = u _ {2} \cos (\omega t + \alpha)
$$

代入微分方程组,可得

$$
\left( \begin{array}{c c} k - (M + m) \omega^ {2} & - m \omega^ {2} \\ - m \omega^ {2} & \frac {m g}{l} - m \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right) \left( \begin{array}{l} u _ {1} \\ u _ {2} \end{array} \right) = 0
$$

$u_{1}$ 、 $u_{2}$ 要得非零解，必须系数行列式等于零，

解出

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/b354dd6d482ee08b87f4a96a49bcde26bcb41a74a14df94a5f7cbe85ce894f77.jpg)


$$
\begin{array}{r l} & \left| \begin{array}{c c} k - (M + m) \omega^ {2} & - m \omega^ {2} \\ - m \omega^ {2} & \frac {m g}{l} - m \omega^ {2} \end{array} \right| = 0 \\ & M \omega^ {4} - \left[ k + (M + m) \frac {g}{l} \right] \omega^ {2} + \frac {k g}{l} = 0 \\ & \omega_ {1} = \sqrt {\frac {(M + m) g + k l + \sqrt {[ (M + m) g + k l ] ^ {2} - 4 k M g l}}{2 M l}} \\ & \omega_ {2} = \sqrt {\frac {(M + m) g + k l - \sqrt {[ (M + m) g + k l ] ^ {2} - 4 k M g l}}{2 M l}} \end{array}
$$

10.1.23 质量为 $2m$ 和 $m$ 的两个质点，用两根劲度系数均为 $k$ 的轻弹簧悬挂在固定的支架上，如图10.20所示，只考虑竖直方向上的运动.

(1) 求系统的简正频率, 并描述每种模式的运动;


图10.20


(2) 将质量为 $2m$ 的质点从平衡位置缓慢地向下移动一段距离 $l$ , 然后放开, 让系统自由振动, 求质量为 $m$ 的质点的运动.

解 （1）取竖直向下的 y 轴， $y_{1}$ 、 $y_{2}$ 分别表示上、下两个质点的位置，原点均取在悬点，再设上、下两个弹簧的原长为 $l_{1}$ 、 $l_{2}$ ，则

$$
\begin{array}{r l} L & = T - V = \frac {1}{2} \times 2 m \dot {y} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m \dot {y} _ {2} ^ {2} + 2 m g y _ {1} + m g y _ {2} \\ & - \frac {1}{2} k (y _ {1} - l _ {1}) ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k (y _ {2} - y _ {l} - l _ {2}) ^ {2} \\ & = \frac {1}{2} m (2 \dot {y} _ {1} ^ {2} + \dot {y} _ {2} ^ {2}) + m g (2 y _ {1} + y _ {2}) - \frac {1}{2} k (y _ {1} - l _ {1}) ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k (y _ {2} - y _ {1} - l _ {2}) ^ {2} \end{array}
$$

由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L}{\partial\dot{y}_i}\right) - \frac{\partial L}{\partial y_i} = 0$ 得

$$
\left\{ \begin{array}{l} 2 m \ddot {y} _ {1} + 2 k y _ {1} - k y _ {2} - 2 m g - k l _ {1} + k l _ {2} = 0 \\ m \ddot {y} _ {2} + k y _ {2} - k y _ {1} - m g - k l _ {2} = 0 \end{array} \right.
$$

令 $y_{1} = y_{1}^{\prime} + \eta_{1},y_{2} = y_{2}^{\prime} + \eta_{2}$ ，使

$$
\begin{array}{r l} 2 k \eta_ {1} - k \eta_ {2} - 2 m g - k l _ {1} + k l _ {2} & = 0 \\ k \eta_ {2} - k \eta_ {1} - m g - k l _ {2} & = 0 \end{array}
$$

即 $\eta_{1} = \frac{1}{k} (3mg + kl_{1}),\eta_{2} = \frac{1}{k} (4mg + kl_{1} + kl_{2})$ 

则运动微分方程可改写为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & 2 m \ddot {y} _ {1} ^ {\prime} + 2 k y _ {1} ^ {\prime} - k y _ {2} ^ {\prime} = 0 \\ & m \ddot {y} _ {2} ^ {\prime} + k y _ {2} ^ {\prime} - k y _ {1} ^ {\prime} = 0 \end{array}
$$

$y_{1}=\eta_{1},y_{2}=\eta_{2}$ 或 $y_{1}^{\prime}=0,y_{2}^{\prime}=0$ 为系统的平衡位置.

令 $y_1' = u_1 e^{i\omega t}, y_2' = u_2 e^{i\omega t}$ ，可得

特征方程为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\left( \begin{array}{c c} {2 k - 2 m \omega^ {2}} & {- k} \\ {- k} & {k - m \omega^ {2}} \end{array} \right) \binom {u _ {1}} {u _ {2}} = 0} \\ & {\left| \begin{array}{c c} {2 k - 2 m \omega^ {2}} & {- k} \\ {- k} & {k - m \omega^ {2}} \end{array} \right| = 0} \end{array}
$$

解出

$$
\omega_ {1} = \sqrt {\left(1 + \frac {\sqrt {2}}{2}\right) \frac {k}{m}}, \quad \omega_ {2} = \sqrt {\left(1 - \frac {\sqrt {2}}{2}\right) \frac {k}{m}}
$$

将 $\omega_{1},\omega_{2}$ 分别代入关于 $u_{1},u_{2}$ 的方程组，令 $u_{1}^{(1)} = 1,u_{1}^{(2)} = 1$ ，可得 $u_{2}^{(1)} = -\sqrt{2},u_{2}^{(2)} =$ $\sqrt{2}$ 

振动模式可表示为

$$
\omega_ {1}: \binom{1}{- \sqrt {2}}, \quad \omega_ {2}: \binom{1}{\sqrt {2}}
$$

描述如下: 按简正频率为 $\omega_{1}$ 的模式振动, 上、下两质点的振动相位相反, 振幅比为 $1:\sqrt{2}$ ; 按简正频率为 $\omega_{2}$ 的模式振动, 上、下两质点的振动相位相同, 振幅比也是 $1:\sqrt{2}$ .

## (2) 系统的通解为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & y _ {1} ^ {\prime} = A \cos (\omega_ {1} t + \alpha_ {1}) + B \cos (\omega_ {2} t + \alpha_ {2}) \\ & y _ {2} ^ {\prime} = - \sqrt {2} A \cos (\omega_ {1} t + \alpha_ {1}) + \sqrt {2} B \cos (\omega_ {2} t + \alpha_ {2}) \\ & , \quad y _ {1} ^ {\prime} = y _ {2} ^ {\prime} = l, \dot {y} _ {1} ^ {\prime} = \dot {y} _ {2} ^ {\prime} = 0 \end{array}
$$

初始条件: t=0 时,

定出

$$
\alpha_ {1} = \alpha_ {2} = 0, \quad A = \frac {1}{2} \left(1 - \frac {\sqrt {2}}{2}\right) l, \quad B = \frac {1}{2} \left(1 + \frac {\sqrt {2}}{2}\right) l
$$

下面的质点的运动方程为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} y _ {2} & = y _ {2} ^ {\prime} + \eta_ {2} = \frac {1}{2} (- \sqrt {2} + 1) l \cos \left[ \sqrt {\left(1 + \frac {\sqrt {2}}{2}\right) \frac {k}{m} t} \right] \\ & + \frac {1}{2} (\sqrt {2} + 1) l \cos \left[ \sqrt {\left(1 - \frac {\sqrt {2}}{2}\right) \frac {k}{m} t} \right] + \frac {4 m g}{k} + l _ {1} + l _ {2} \end{array}
$$

10.1.24 对于如图 10.21 所示的、两个质量为 m 的质点、用三根无质量的、劲度系数分别为 b 和 $\lambda'$ 的弹簧连接组成的系统，如果这两个质点从图上所标出的对称的初始位置静止释放，求此振动周期。

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/3ddcfb75140a1d65555c7111b95ac67bbb9b4dc7a23c70715959097b83dd8136.jpg)



图10.21


如只考虑两质点沿连线方向的运动, 这个系统有两个自由度. 取 $x_{1}, x_{2}$ 分别表示左、右两个质点的位置, 原点均取在平衡位置, $x_{1}$ 向左为正方向, $x_{2}$ 向右为正方向,

$$
L = \frac {1}{2} m \dot {x} _ {1} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m \dot {x} _ {2} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k x _ {1} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k x _ {2} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} k ^ {\prime} (x _ {1} + x _ {2}) ^ {2}
$$

由

$$
\frac {\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} \left(\frac {\partial L}{\partial \dot {x} _ {i}}\right) - \frac {\partial L}{\partial x _ {i}} = 0
$$

得

$$
m \ddot {x} _ {1} + k x _ {1} + k ^ {\prime} (x _ {1} + x _ {2}) = 0\tag{1}
$$

$$
m \ddot {x} _ {2} + k x _ {2} + k ^ {\prime} (x _ {1} + x _ {2}) = 0\tag{2}
$$

由系统和所给初始条件的对称性, 只有一种振动模式被激发. 用上述的 $x_{1}, x_{2}$ 坐标, 两质点始终有 $x_{1} = x_{2}$ , 即两质点的振动振幅和相位都相同的振动模式被激发.

(1)、(2)式可改写为

$$
\begin{array}{l} m \ddot {x} _ {1} + (2 k ^ {\prime} + k) x _ {1} = 0 \\ m \ddot {x} _ {2} + (2 k ^ {\prime} + k) x _ {2} = 0 \end{array}
$$

立即可得振动的角频率和周期分别为

$$
\omega = \sqrt {\frac {2 k ^ {\prime} + k}{m}}, \quad T = 2 \pi \sqrt {\frac {m}{2 k ^ {\prime} + k}}
$$

10.1.25 考虑一质量为 $m$ 的粒子在二维势场

$$
V (x, y) = - \frac {1}{2} k x ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \lambda_ {0} x ^ {2} y ^ {2} + \frac {1}{4} \lambda_ {1} x ^ {4}
$$

$(k, \lambda_{0}, \lambda_{1} > 0, \text{均为常量})$ 中的运动，求：

(1) 在什么点 $(x_0, y_0)$ 质点处于稳定平衡？

(2) 给出围绕这个平衡位置做小振动的拉格朗日函数；

(3) 求(2)问所述振动的简正频率.

解（1）由 $\frac{\partial V}{\partial x} = 0$ 及 $\frac{\partial V}{\partial y} = 0$ 可得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} - k x + \lambda_ {0} x y ^ {2} + \lambda_ {1} x ^ {3} & = 0 \\ \lambda_ {0} x ^ {2} y & = 0 \end{array}
$$

以下几组 $(x, y)$ 均能满足这两个方程，均为平衡位置.

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {\left\{x _ {1 0} = \sqrt {\frac {k}{\lambda_ {1}}}, \quad \left\{x _ {2 0} = - \sqrt {\frac {k}{\lambda_ {1}}}, \quad \left\{x _ {3 0} = 0, \right. \right. \right.} \\ & {\left. y _ {1 0} = 0; \quad y _ {2 0} = 0; \quad y _ {3 0} \text {任意}. \right.} \\ & {\qquad \frac {\partial^ {2} V}{\partial x ^ {2}} (\mathrm{d} x) ^ {2} + 2   \frac {\partial^ {2} V}{\partial x \partial y} \mathrm{d} x \mathrm{d} y + \frac {\partial^ {2} V}{\partial y ^ {2}} (\mathrm{d} y) ^ {2}} \\ & {= (- k + \lambda_ {0} y ^ {2} + 3 \lambda_ {1} x ^ {2}) (\mathrm{d} x) ^ {2} + 4 \lambda_ {0} x y \mathrm{d} x \mathrm{d} y + \lambda_ {0} x ^ {2} (\mathrm{d} y) ^ {2}} \end{array}
$$

将 $(x_{10},y_{10})$ 或 $(x_{20},y_{20})$ 分别代入上式，均得

$$
\frac {\partial^ {2} V}{\partial x ^ {2}} (\mathrm{d} x) ^ {2} + 2 \frac {\partial^ {2} V}{\partial x \partial y} \mathrm{d} x \mathrm{d} y + \frac {\partial^ {2} V}{\partial y ^ {2}} (\mathrm{d} y) ^ {2} = 2 k (\mathrm{d} x) ^ {2} + \frac {k \lambda_ {0}}{\lambda_ {1}} (\mathrm{d} y) ^ {2} > 0
$$

可见,这两个位置都是稳定平衡位置.

将 $(x_{30},y_{30})$ 代入，

$$
\frac {\partial^ {2} V}{\partial x ^ {2}} (\mathrm{d} x) ^ {2} + 2 \frac {\partial^ {2} V}{\partial x \partial y} \mathrm{d} x \mathrm{d} y + \frac {\partial^ {2} V}{\partial y ^ {2}} (\mathrm{d} y) ^ {2} = (- k + \lambda_ {0} y ^ {2}) (\mathrm{d} x) ^ {2}
$$

可见，当 $-k + \lambda_0y_{30}^2 > 0$ ，即 $y_{30} > \sqrt{\frac{k}{\lambda_0}}$ ，(0, $y_{30}$ )的那些点都是“稳定平衡位置”， $y_{30} < -\sqrt{\frac{k}{\lambda_0}}$ ，(0, $y_{30}$ )的那些点也都是“稳定平衡位置”。而当 $-k + \lambda_0y_{30}^2 < 0$ ，即 $-\sqrt{\frac{k}{\lambda_0}} < y_{30} < \sqrt{\frac{k}{\lambda_0}}$ 的那些点(0, $y_{30}$ )是不稳定的平衡位置，在(0, $y_{30}$ )对于 $y_{30}$ 不限，沿 $y$ 轴均是随遇平衡的。

(2) 考虑 $(x_{10}, y_{10})$ 这个平衡位置，将 $V(x, y)$ 在 $(x_{10}, y_{10})$ 点作泰勒展开，令 $x' = x - \sqrt{\frac{k}{\lambda_1}}$ ， $y' = y$ ，则

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {V (x ^ {\prime}, y ^ {\prime}) = - \frac {1}{2} k \Big (x ^ {\prime} + \sqrt {\frac {k}{\lambda_ {1}}} ^ {2} \Big) + \frac {1}{2} \lambda_ {0} \Big (x ^ {\prime} + \sqrt {\frac {k}{\lambda_ {1}}} \Big) ^ {2} y ^ {\prime 2} + \frac {1}{4} \lambda_ {1} \Big (x ^ {\prime} + \sqrt {\frac {k}{\lambda_ {1}}} \Big) ^ {4}} \\ & {\qquad \approx - \frac {1}{2} k \cdot \frac {k}{\lambda_ {1}} + \frac {1}{2} \lambda_ {0} \frac {k}{\lambda_ {1}} \cdot 0 + \frac {1}{4} \lambda_ {1} \Big (\frac {k}{\lambda_ {1}} \Big) ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} \Big [ \Big (- k + 3 \lambda_ {1} \frac {k}{\lambda_ {1}} \Big) x ^ {\prime 2} + \frac {\lambda}{\lambda_ {1}} k y ^ {2} \Big ]} \\ & {\qquad = - \frac {k ^ {2}}{4 \lambda_ {1}} + k x ^ {\prime 2} + \frac {k \lambda_ {0}}{2 \lambda_ {1}} y ^ {\prime 2}} \\ & {\qquad L = \frac {1}{2} m   \dot {x} ^ {\prime 2} + \frac {1}{2} m   \dot {y} ^ {\prime 2} - k x ^ {\prime 2} - \frac {k \lambda_ {0}}{2 \lambda_ {1}} y ^ {\prime 2} + \frac {k ^ {2}}{4 \lambda_ {1}}} \\ & {\text {或} \qquad L = \frac {1}{2} m   \dot {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m   \dot {y} ^ {2} - k \Big (x - \sqrt {\frac {k}{\lambda_ {1}}} \Big) ^ {2} - \frac {k \lambda_ {0}}{2 \lambda_ {1}} y ^ {2} + \frac {k ^ {2}}{4 \lambda_ {1}}} \end{array}
$$

考虑 $(x_{20}, y_{20})$ 这个平衡位置，将 $V(x, y)$ 在 $(x_{20}, y_{20})$ 处作泰勒展开，也保留到二级小量。令 $x' = x + \sqrt{\frac{k}{\lambda_1}}, y' = y$ 。和上面一样。计算

$$
V \left(x ^ {\prime}, y ^ {\prime}\right) = V (0, 0) + \frac {1}{2} \left[ \frac {\partial^ {2} V}{\partial x ^ {\prime 2}} \right| _ {(0, 0)} x ^ {\prime 2} + 2 \left. \frac {\partial^ {2} V}{\partial x ^ {\prime} \partial y ^ {\prime}} \right| _ {(0, 0)} x ^ {\prime} y ^ {\prime} + \left. \frac {\partial^ {2} V}{\partial y ^ {\prime 2}} \right| _ {(0, 0)} y ^ {\prime 2} \Bigg ]
$$

可得

$$
L = \frac {1}{2} m \dot {x} ^ {\prime 2} + \frac {1}{2} m \dot {y} ^ {\prime 2} - k x ^ {\prime 2} - \frac {k \lambda_ {0}}{2 \lambda_ {1}} y ^ {\prime 2} + \frac {k ^ {2}}{4 \lambda_ {1}}
$$

或

$$
L = \frac {1}{2} m \dot {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m \dot {y} ^ {2} - k \left(x + \sqrt {\frac {k}{\lambda_ {1}}}\right) ^ {2} - \frac {k \lambda_ {0}}{2 \lambda_ {1}} y ^ {2} + \frac {k ^ {2}}{4 \lambda_ {1}}
$$

考虑 $(0, y_{30})$ ，其中 $y_{30} > \sqrt{\frac{k}{\lambda_0}}$ 或 $y_{30} < -\sqrt{\frac{k}{\lambda_0}}$ 这些平衡位置， $V(x, y)$ 在这些位置作泰勒展开，保留到二级小量，可得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} V (x, y) & = \frac {1}{2} (- k + \lambda_ {0} y _ {3 0} ^ {2}) x ^ {2} \\ L & = \frac {1}{2} m \dot {x} ^ {2} + \frac {1}{2} m \dot {y} ^ {2} - \frac {1}{2} (- k + \lambda_ {0} y _ {3 0} ^ {2}) x ^ {2} \end{array}
$$

(3) 对于在稳定平衡位置 $\left(\sqrt{\frac{k}{\lambda_{1}}},0\right)$ 或 $\left(-\sqrt{\frac{k}{\lambda_{1}}},0\right)$ 附近的小振动，有完全相同的拉格

朗日方程，

$$
m \ddot {x} ^ {\prime} + 2 k x ^ {\prime} = 0
$$

$$
m \ddot {y} ^ {\prime} + \frac {k \lambda_ {0}}{\lambda_ {1}} y ^ {\prime} = 0
$$

均有两个简正频率，沿 $x$ 方向振动， $\omega_{1} = \sqrt{\frac{2k}{m}}$ ，沿 $y$ 方向振动， $\omega_{2} = \sqrt{\frac{k\lambda_{0}}{\lambda_{1}}}$ .沿其他方向，是 $x$ 方向角频率为 $\omega_{1}$ 的简谐振动与 $y$ 方向角频率为 $\omega_{2}$ 的简谐振动的合成运动.

对于第三组稳定平衡位置附近的小振动，

$$
\begin{array}{c} {m   \ddot {x} + (-   k   +   \lambda_ {0} y _ {3 0} ^ {2}) x = 0} \\ {\dot {y} = \text {常量}} \end{array}
$$

有一个简正频率: $\omega = \sqrt{\frac{-k + \lambda_0 y_{30}^2}{m}}$ , 其中 $y_{30} > \sqrt{\frac{k}{\lambda_0}}$ 或 $y_{30} < -\sqrt{\frac{k}{\lambda_0}}$ . 但这小振动仅限于在与 $x$ 轴平行的方向, 因此严格地讲, 这些点不能算作稳定平衡位置.

![image](https://cdn-mineru.openxlab.org.cn/result/2026-11-28/f4e2c433-6a1c-45c7-8e7f-62b075fde331/684dcefceee07ba1ecd4747273f1607b3446278aac5a870e84b32140f7ba5e6c.jpg)



图10.2


10.1.26 一个质量为 m、半径为 r 的小球，用一根长为 l 的、绝缘的、不可伸长的轻绳挂在电容器两极板的中间，两极板接地，间距为 L，球的电势为 V，平衡时，球位于距两极板等距离处，绳子在铅垂方向，电势满足什么条件时，球可在平衡位置附近做小振动，求振动的角频率，为了简化计算，可作适当的近似.

提示: 可用两个镜像电荷代替两个接地

的极板,而不是用两串镜像电荷来代替两个接地的极板.

解 小球所带的电荷为

$$
q = 4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r V
$$

用两个镜像电荷代替两个接地的极板来考虑对小球的作用.

取水平的 $x$ 轴，原点取在平衡位置，表示小球位置的坐标 $x, \theta$ 只有一个是独立的，小球位于 $x$ （或 $\theta$ ）位置时，两个镜像电荷分别位于 $x$ 坐标为 $L - x$ 及 $-L - x$ 处，与小球的距离分别为 $L - 2x$ 及 $L + 2x$ ，如图10.22所示。用 $\theta$ 作为广义坐标，

$$
x = l \sin \theta , \quad \dot {x} = l \dot {\theta} \cos \theta
$$

小球的动能 $T$ 和势能 $U$ 分别为

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & T = \frac {1}{2} m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} ^ {2} \\ U = - \frac {q ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \left(\frac {1}{L - 2 x} + \frac {1}{L + 2 x} - \frac {2}{L}\right) + m g l (1 - \cos \theta) \\ = - \frac {q ^ {2}}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \left(\frac {L}{L ^ {2} - 4 x ^ {2}} - \frac {1}{L}\right) + m g l (1 - \cos \theta) \\ = - \frac {q ^ {2}}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \cdot \frac {1}{L} \left[ \left(1 - \frac {4 x ^ {2}}{L ^ {2}}\right) ^ {- 1} - 1 \right] + m g l (1 - \cos \theta) \end{array}
$$

$$
\approx - \frac {q ^ {2}}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \cdot \frac {4 x ^ {2}}{L ^ {3}} + m g l \cdot \frac {1}{2} \theta^ {2} \approx \left(\frac {1}{2} m g l - \frac {2 q ^ {2} l ^ {2}}{\pi \varepsilon_ {0} L ^ {3}}\right) \theta^ {2} = \left(\frac {1}{2} m g l - \frac {3 2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r ^ {2} V ^ {2} l ^ {2}}{L ^ {3}}\right) \theta^ {2}
$$

可在平衡位置附近做小振动的条件是 $\frac{\partial^2 U}{\partial \theta^2}\bigg|_{\theta = 0} > 0$ 

即

$$
\frac {1}{2} m g l - \frac {3 2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r ^ {2} V ^ {2} l ^ {2}}{L ^ {3}} > 0
$$

因此要求电势 V 满足的条件是

$$
V <   \left(\frac {m g L ^ {3}}{6 4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r ^ {2} l}\right) ^ {1 / 2}
$$

在平衡位置附近做小振动的运动微分方程，由 $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\left(\frac{\partial L}{\partial\dot{\theta}}\right) - \frac{\partial L}{\partial\theta} = 0$ （其中 $L = T - U$ )得

$$
\begin{array}{r l} m l ^ {2} \dot {\theta} + \left(m g l - \frac {6 4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r ^ {2} V ^ {2} l ^ {2}}{L ^ {3}}\right) \theta & = 0 \\ \omega = \left(\frac {g}{l} - \frac {6 4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r ^ {2} V ^ {2}}{m L ^ {3}}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \end{array}
$$

说明:此题更严格的解法,当然是用两串镜像电荷而不是只用两个镜像电荷.

$$
\begin{array}{r l} & {U = - \frac {q ^ {2}}{4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} \Big [ \frac {1}{(2 n - 1) L - 2 x} + \frac {1}{(2 n - 1) L + 2 x} - \frac {2}{(2 n - 1) L} \Big ] + m g (1 - \cos \theta)} \\ & {\quad = - \frac {q ^ {2}}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} \frac {1}{(2 n - 1) L} \Big [ 1 + \frac {4 x ^ {2}}{(2 n - 1) ^ {2} L ^ {2}} - 1 \Big ] + m g (1 - \cos \theta)} \\ & {\quad \approx - \frac {q ^ {2}}{2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0}} \frac {4 x ^ {2}}{L ^ {3}} \sum_ {n = 1} ^ {\infty} \frac {1}{(2 n - 1) ^ {3}} + \frac {1}{2} m g l \theta^ {2}} \\ & {\quad \approx \Big (\frac {1}{2} m g l - \frac {3 2 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r ^ {2} V ^ {2} l ^ {2}}{L ^ {3}} \alpha \Big) \theta^ {2}} \end{array}
$$

其中 $\alpha = \sum_{n=1}^{\infty} \frac{1}{(2n-1)^3}$ 

可在平衡位置附近做小振动,要求电势满足的条件改为

$$
V <   \left(\frac {m g L ^ {3}}{6 4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r ^ {2} l}\right) ^ {1 / 2} \cdot \alpha^ {- 1 / 2}
$$

小振动的角频率改为

$$
\omega = \left(\frac {g}{l} - \frac {6 4 \pi \varepsilon_ {0} r ^ {2} V ^ {2}}{m L ^ {3}} \alpha\right) ^ {1 / 2}
$$

$\alpha$ 这个无穷级数 $\sum_{n=1}^{\infty} \frac{1}{(2n-1)^3}$ 收敛是很快的, 只取一项 $(n=1)$ 时, $\alpha=1$ , 取两项时 $(n=1, 2), \alpha=1.037$ , 取三项时, $\alpha=1.045$ , 取四项时, $\alpha=1.0479$ , 取五项时, $\alpha=1.0492$ .

可见,用两个镜像电荷而不用两串镜像电荷所引起的误差是相当小的.

10.1.27 三个自然长度为 $\sqrt{2}$ 、劲度系数为 $k$ 的轻弹簧，与一质量为 $m$ 的质点相连，三个弹簧的另一端分别固定在 $(-1,1)$ 、(1,1)和 $(-1,-1)$ 处，质点 $m$ 仅在水平且光滑的 $xy$ 平面内运动.

(1) 写出系统的拉格朗日函数；
